let soundPrefs = { enabled: true, volume: 0.6 };
try{
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(LS_SOUND_PREFS);
  if(raw){ const p = JSON.parse(raw); soundPrefs = Object.assign(soundPrefs, p); }
}catch(e){}

let audioCtxGlobal = null;
function getAudioCtx(){
  if(!audioCtxGlobal){
    try{
      audioCtxGlobal = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    }catch(e){ return null; }
  }
  if(audioCtxGlobal.state === 'suspended'){
    try{ audioCtxGlobal.resume(); }catch(e){}
  }
  return audioCtxGlobal;
}

function playTone(freqs, dur, type, gap){
  if(!soundPrefs.enabled) return;
  const ctx = getAudioCtx();
  if(!ctx) return;
  const vol = soundPrefs.volume;
  const now = ctx.currentTime;
  const g = gap || 0.08;
  freqs.forEach((f, i) => {
    const o = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gn = ctx.createGain();
    o.connect(gn); gn.connect(ctx.destination);
    o.type = type || 'sine';
    o.frequency.value = f;
    const t = now + i * g;
    gn.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
    gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + 0.02);
    gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    o.start(t);
    o.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
  });
}

const SOUNDS = {
  success:    () => playTone([659, 784, 988, 1319], 0.22, 'sine', 0.07),
  ok:         () => playTone([880, 1320], 0.18, 'sine', 0.09),
  error:      () => playTone([392, 262], 0.22, 'triangle', 0.12),
  warn:       () => playTone([587, 494], 0.2, 'triangle', 0.12),
  message:    () => playTone([1047, 1319, 1568], 0.15, 'sine', 0.06),
  notify:     () => playTone([880, 1175], 0.14, 'sine', 0.08),
  newStudent: () => playTone([523, 659, 784, 1047], 0.2, 'sine', 0.09),
  newPayment: () => playTone([784, 1047, 1319], 0.24, 'triangle', 0.1),
  celebrate:  () => playTone([523, 659, 784, 1047, 1319], 0.25, 'sine', 0.09),
  click:      () => playTone([660], 0.06, 'sine', 0),
  newTask:    () => playTone([698, 880, 1047], 0.18, 'sine', 0.08),
  autoSubmit: () => playTone([440, 587, 784], 0.3, 'triangle', 0.1)
};

function playSound(key){
  try{
    const fn = SOUNDS[key] || SOUNDS.ok;
    fn();
  }catch(e){}
}
function playDing(){ playSound('ok'); }

function toggleSoundPref(){
  soundPrefs.enabled = !soundPrefs.enabled;
  try{ localStorage.setItem(LS_SOUND_PREFS, JSON.stringify(soundPrefs)); }catch(e){}
  if(soundPrefs.enabled) playSound('success');
  document.querySelectorAll('.sound-toggle').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('on', soundPrefs.enabled);
    const lbl = el.querySelector('.st-lbl');
    if(lbl) lbl.textContent = soundPrefs.enabled ? 'الأصوات: مفعلة' : 'الأصوات: معطلة';
  });
  toast(soundPrefs.enabled ? '🔊 تم تفعيل الأصوات' : '🔇 تم تعطيل الأصوات', soundPrefs.enabled ? 'ok' : 'warn');
}

function soundToggleHTML(){
  return `<button class="sound-toggle ${soundPrefs.enabled?'on':''}" onclick="toggleSoundPref()" title="تفعيل/تعطيل الأصوات">
    <span class="st-ico">${soundPrefs.enabled ? '🔊' : '🔇'}</span>
    <span class="st-lbl">${soundPrefs.enabled ? 'الأصوات: مفعلة' : 'الأصوات: معطلة'}</span>
  </button>`;
}

function uid(){ return Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7); }
function pad2(n){ return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }
function esc(s){
  return String(s === null || s === undefined ? '' : s)
    .replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
}
function val(id){ const el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value.trim() : ''; }

function normalizeDigits(str){
  if(str === null || str === undefined) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/[٠-٩]/g, d => String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)))
    .replace(/[۰-۹]/g, d => String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d)))
    .replace(/[\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E\u2066-\u2069]/g, '')
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '');
}
function cleanPhone(input){
  return normalizeDigits(input).replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
}

function toast(msg, type){
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  const ico = type === 'err' ? '✕' : type === 'warn' ? '!' : type === 'gold' ? '★' : type === 'purple' ? '◆' : type === 'ok' ? '✓' : '✓';
  t.className = 'toast' + (type ? ' ' + type : '');
  t.innerHTML = `<span class="t-ico">${ico}</span><span>${esc(msg)}</span>`;
  t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 3200);
}

function fmtBytes(bytes){
  if(!bytes || bytes < 0) return '0 ب';
  const units = ['ب','ك.ب','م.ب','ج.ب'];
  let i = 0, n = bytes;
  while(n >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1){ n /= 1024; i++; }
  return n.toFixed(2) + ' ' + units[i];
}

function fmtDate(d){
  if(!d) return '—';
  const dt = new Date(d);
  if(isNaN(dt)) return esc(d);
  return dt.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'});
}
function fmtDateWithDay(d){
  if(!d) return '—';
  const dt = new Date(d);
  if(isNaN(dt)) return esc(d);
  return DAYS_JS[dt.getDay()] + '، ' + dt.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'});
}
function todayISO(){ return new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); }
function fmtTime12(t){
  if(!t || typeof t !== 'string' || !t.includes(':')) return '—';
  const parts = t.split(':');
  const h = parseInt(parts[0], 10);
  const m = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
  if(isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return '—';
  const period = h >= 12 ? 'م' : 'ص';
  const h12 = h % 12 || 12;
  return `${h12}:${pad2(m)} ${period}`;
}
function fmtDateTime12(startDate, startTime){
  if(!startDate) return null;
  return fmtDateWithDay(startDate) + (startTime ? ' — ' + fmtTime12(startTime) : '');
}
function getTodayParts(){
  const d = new Date();
  return { dayName: DAYS_JS[d.getDay()], dateStr: d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'}) };
}
function getNowTime12(){
  const d = new Date();
  const h = d.getHours();
  const m = d.getMinutes();
  const period = h >= 12 ? 'م' : 'ص';
  const h12 = h % 12 || 12;
  return `${h12}:${pad2(m)} ${period}`;
}
function toHijri(date){
  try{
    const d = date instanceof Date ? date : new Date(date);
    if(isNaN(d)) return '';
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic', { year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric' }).format(d);
  }catch(e){ return ''; }
}
function todayHeaderHTML(variant){
  const { dayName, dateStr } = getTodayParts();
  const cls = variant === 'student' ? 'today-bar student' : 'today-bar';
  return `<div class="${cls}">
    <span class="tb-ico">📅</span>
    <div class="tb-txt">
      <span class="tb-day">${esc(dayName)}</span>
      <span class="tb-date">${esc(dateStr)}</span>
    </div>
    <span class="tb-time"><span class="tb-clock">🕐</span><span class="live-clock">${getNowTime12()}</span></span>
    <span class="tb-tag">اليوم</span>
  </div>`;
}

function combineToTimestamp(dateStr, timeStr){
  if(!dateStr) return null;
  const t = timeStr || '00:00';
  try{
    const d = new Date(dateStr + 'T' + t + ':00');
    if(isNaN(d)) return null;
    return d.getTime();
  }catch(e){ return null; }
}

function fmtCountdown(ms){
  if(ms <= 0) return null;
  const total = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
  return {
    days: Math.floor(total / 86400),
    hours: Math.floor((total % 86400) / 3600),
    mins: Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60),
    secs: total % 60
  };
}

function formatCountdownMMSS(totalSec){
  if(totalSec < 0) totalSec = 0;
  const h = Math.floor(totalSec / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
  const s = totalSec % 60;
  if(h > 0) return `${pad2(h)}:${pad2(m)}:${pad2(s)}`;
  return `${pad2(m)}:${pad2(s)}`;
}

/* ============ الأفاتار والشعار ============ */
function avatar(name, size = 46){
  const colors = ['#0f766e','#2563eb','#7c3aed','#db2777','#ea580c','#0891b2','#65a30d','#d97706','#dc2626','#be185d'];
  const idx = [...(name||'؟')].reduce((a,c)=>a+c.charCodeAt(0),0) % colors.length;
  const initial = (name||'').trim().charAt(0) || '؟';
  return `<div style="width:${size}px;height:${size}px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,${colors[idx]},${colors[idx]}cc);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-weight:900;font-size:${Math.round(size*0.42)}px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 4px 12px ${colors[idx]}55">${esc(initial)}</div>`;
}
function logoSVG(size = 48){
  return `<svg class="logo-svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="${size}" height="${size}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <rect width="100" height="100" rx="26" fill="url(#logoBg)"/>
    <rect width="100" height="100" rx="26" fill="url(#logoShine)"/>
    <path d="M22 33 L50 19 L78 33 L50 47 Z" fill="url(#logoAccent)"/>
    <path d="M22 33 L50 19 L78 33" fill="none" stroke="#fde68a" stroke-width="1.5" opacity="0.7"/>
    <rect x="46" y="47" width="8" height="4" rx="1.5" fill="url(#logoAccent)" opacity="0.85"/>
    <text x="50" y="84" text-anchor="middle" font-size="38" font-weight="900" fill="#ffffff" font-family="Cairo, sans-serif" style="paint-order:stroke">ص</text>
  </svg>`;
}
function brandBlockHTML(size = 40){
  const img = db && db.settings && db.settings.teacherImage;
  if(img) return `<img src="${esc(img)}" class="brand-photo" alt="شعار" style="width:${size}px;height:${size}px">`;
  return `<span class="brand-logo">${logoSVG(size)}</span>`;
}
function notifIconURI(){
  const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100"><defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#2dd4bf"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#115e59"/></linearGradient><linearGradient id="a" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fcd34d"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#d97706"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="100" height="100" rx="24" fill="url(#g)"/><path d="M22 33 L50 19 L78 33 L50 47 Z" fill="url(#a)"/><text x="50" y="84" text-anchor="middle" font-size="38" font-weight="900" fill="#fff" font-family="sans-serif">ص</text></svg>`;
  return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(svg);
}

/* ============ حفظ بيانات الدخول ============ */
function b64encode(str){
  try{ return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(str))); }catch(e){ return ''; }
}
function b64decode(str){
  try{ return decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(str))); }catch(e){ return ''; }
}
function saveLoginCreds(role, phone, password){
  try{ localStorage.setItem('salah_creds_' + role, b64encode(JSON.stringify({phone, password}))); }catch(e){}
}
function loadLoginCreds(role){
  try{
    const raw = localStorage.getItem('salah_creds_' + role);
    if(!raw) return null;
    const data = JSON.parse(b64decode(raw));
    if(data && data.phone && data.password) return data;
    return null;
  }catch(e){ return null; }
}
function clearLoginCreds(role){
  try{ localStorage.removeItem('salah_creds_' + role); }catch(e){}
}
function maskPhone(p){
  p = String(p||'');
  if(p.length < 6) return '••••••';
  return p.slice(0, 3) + '•'.repeat(Math.max(4, p.length - 6)) + p.slice(-3);
}
function waIntlNumber(phone){
  let p = cleanPhone(phone);
  if(!p) return '';
  if(p.startsWith('0')) p = '2' + p;
  return p;
}

/* ============ تحويل dataURI إلى Blob ============ */
function dataURItoBlob(dataURI){
  try{
    const parts = dataURI.split(',');
    const meta = parts[0];
    const data = parts[1];
    const mimeMatch = meta.match(/:(.*?);/);
    const mime = mimeMatch ? mimeMatch[1] : 'application/pdf';
    const binary = atob(data);
    const len = binary.length;
    const buffer = new Uint8Array(len);
    for(let i = 0; i < len; i++) buffer[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    return new Blob([buffer], {type: mime});
  }catch(e){
    console.warn('dataURItoBlob error:', e);
    return null;
  }
}

/* ============ بناء روابط Embed ============ */
function buildEmbedURL(url, type){
  if(!url) return '';
  let u;
  try{ u = new URL(url); } catch(e){
    if(/^[\w-]{11}$/.test(url)) return `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${url}?rel=0&modestbranding=1&playsinline=1&fs=1`;
    return url;
  }
  const host = u.hostname.replace(/^www\./,'').toLowerCase();

  if(host.includes('youtube.com') || host.includes('youtu.be')){
    let vid = u.searchParams.get('v');
    if(!vid && u.pathname.includes('/shorts/')) vid = u.pathname.split('/shorts/')[1]?.split('/')[0];
    if(!vid && u.pathname.includes('/embed/'))  vid = u.pathname.split('/embed/')[1]?.split('/')[0];
    if(!vid && u.pathname.includes('/live/'))   vid = u.pathname.split('/live/')[1]?.split('/')[0];
    if(!vid && host === 'youtu.be')             vid = u.pathname.slice(1).split('/')[0];
    if(vid){
      return `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${vid}?rel=0&modestbranding=1&playsinline=1&fs=1&autoplay=0`;
    }
  }

  if(host.includes('vimeo.com')){
    const m = u.pathname.match(/\/(\d+)/);
    if(m) return `https://player.vimeo.com/video/${m[1]}?title=0&byline=0&portrait=0`;
  }

  if(host.includes('drive.google.com')){
    const m = u.pathname.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);
    if(m) return `https://drive.google.com/file/d/${m[1]}/preview`;
  }

  if(host.includes('docs.google.com')){
    let cleaned = url.replace(/\/(edit|view)(\?.*)?$/, '/preview');
    if(!cleaned.includes('/preview')) cleaned = cleaned.replace(/\/?$/, '/preview');
    return cleaned;
  }

  if(host.includes('meet.google.com') || host.includes('zoom.us') ||
     host.includes('teams.microsoft.com') || host.includes('web.whatsapp.com')){
    return '';
  }

  return url;
}

/* ============ normalizeDB ============ */
function normalizeDB(d){
  if(!d || typeof d !== 'object') return defaultDB();
  const def = defaultDB();
  const out = {
    settings: Object.assign({}, def.settings, d.settings || {}),
    students: Array.isArray(d.students) ? d.students : [],
    lessons: Array.isArray(d.lessons) ? d.lessons : [],
    notes: Array.isArray(d.notes) ? d.notes : [],
    liveSessions: Array.isArray(d.liveSessions) ? d.liveSessions : [],
    schedules: Array.isArray(d.schedules) ? d.schedules : [],
    library: {
      homework: Array.isArray(d.library && d.library.homework) ? d.library.homework : [],
      exams: Array.isArray(d.library && d.library.exams) ? d.library.exams : []
    },
    waLog: Array.isArray(d.waLog) ? d.waLog : []
  };
  if(!out.settings.loginTexts || typeof out.settings.loginTexts !== 'object'){
    out.settings.loginTexts = Object.assign({}, def.settings.loginTexts);
  } else {
    out.settings.loginTexts = Object.assign({}, def.settings.loginTexts, out.settings.loginTexts);
  }
  if(!out.settings.platformIntro || typeof out.settings.platformIntro !== 'object'){
    out.settings.platformIntro = Object.assign({}, def.settings.platformIntro);
  }
  if(typeof out.settings.currency !== 'string') out.settings.currency = 'EGP';
  if(typeof out.settings.showAutoCollect !== 'boolean') out.settings.showAutoCollect = true;
  if(!out.settings.prices) out.settings.prices = def.settings.prices;
  if(!out.settings.prices.plans || !out.settings.prices.plans.length) out.settings.prices.plans = def.settings.prices.plans;
  if(!out.settings.prices.sessionTypes) out.settings.prices.sessionTypes = def.settings.prices.sessionTypes;
  if(typeof out.settings.teacherImage !== 'string') out.settings.teacherImage = '';
  if(typeof out.settings.contactPhone !== 'string' || !out.settings.contactPhone){
    out.settings.contactPhone = out.settings.teacherPhone || DEFAULT_TEACHER_PHONE;
  }
  if(typeof out.settings.teacherSenderPhone !== 'string' || !out.settings.teacherSenderPhone){
    out.settings.teacherSenderPhone = out.settings.teacherPhone || DEFAULT_TEACHER_PHONE;
  }
  if(typeof out.settings.parentApprovalPassword !== 'string'){
    out.settings.parentApprovalPassword = '';
  }
  if(!out.settings.whatsappApi || typeof out.settings.whatsappApi !== 'object'){
    out.settings.whatsappApi = Object.assign({}, def.settings.whatsappApi);
  } else {
    out.settings.whatsappApi = Object.assign({}, def.settings.whatsappApi, out.settings.whatsappApi);
  }
  out.notes = out.notes.map(n => ({
    ...n,
    type: n.type || (n.url && !n.pdfData && !n.pdfUrl ? 'link' : 'pdf'),
    folder: typeof n.folder === 'string' ? n.folder : '',
    id: n.id || uid(),
    createdAt: n.createdAt || Date.now()
  }));
  out.lessons = out.lessons.map(l => ({
    ...l,
    folder: typeof l.folder === 'string' ? l.folder : '',
    id: l.id || uid(),
    createdAt: l.createdAt || Date.now()
  }));
  out.liveSessions = out.liveSessions.map(lv => ({
    ...lv,
    folder: typeof lv.folder === 'string' ? lv.folder : '',
    id: lv.id || uid(),
    createdAt: lv.createdAt || Date.now()
  }));
  out.students = out.students.map(s => ({
    id: s.id || uid(),
    name: s.name || 'بدون اسم',
    phone: s.phone || '',
    password: s.password || '',
    stage: s.stage || '',
    track: s.track || 'عام',
    parentPhone: s.parentPhone || '',
    notes: s.notes || '',
    privateNotes: Array.isArray(s.privateNotes) ? s.privateNotes : [],
    parentApprovalPassword: typeof s.parentApprovalPassword === 'string' ? s.parentApprovalPassword : '',
    status: s.status || 'pending',
    plan: s.plan || null,
    payment: s.payment || null,
    paymentHistory: Array.isArray(s.paymentHistory) ? s.paymentHistory : [],
    homework: Array.isArray(s.homework) ? s.homework : [],
    exams: Array.isArray(s.exams) ? s.exams : [],
    evaluations: Array.isArray(s.evaluations) ? s.evaluations : [],
    autoEvaluations: Array.isArray(s.autoEvaluations) ? s.autoEvaluations : [],
    schedule: Array.isArray(s.schedule) ? s.schedule : [],
    notifications: Array.isArray(s.notifications) ? s.notifications : [],
    certificates: Array.isArray(s.certificates) ? s.certificates : [],
    activityLog: Array.isArray(s.activityLog) ? s.activityLog : [],
    progressLog: Array.isArray(s.progressLog) ? s.progressLog : [],
    subscription: s.subscription || null,
    phoneVerified: s.phoneVerified === true,
    verifiedAt: s.verifiedAt || null,
    enrolledAt: s.enrolledAt || Date.now(),
    expiresAt: s.expiresAt || null,
    createdAt: s.createdAt || Date.now()
  }));
  return out;
}

/* ============ تحميل من الكاش ============ */
function loadFromCache(){
  try{
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY);
    if(raw){
      const d = JSON.parse(raw);
      if(d && d.students && d.settings) return normalizeDB(d);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return null;
}
db = loadFromCache() || defaultDB();

/* ============ toggle password visibility ============ */
function togglePasswordVisibility(id, btn){
  const inp = document.getElementById(id);
  if(!inp) return;
  if(inp.type === 'password'){
    inp.type = 'text';
    if(btn){ btn.textContent = '🙈'; btn.classList.add('showing'); }
  } else {
    inp.type = 'password';
    if(btn){ btn.textContent = '👁️'; btn.classList.remove('showing'); }
  }
}

/* ============ Firebase init ============ */
function initFirebase(){
  if(typeof firebase === 'undefined' || !firebase.initializeApp){
    console.warn('Firebase SDK not loaded');
    updateSyncBadge();
    return;
  }
  try{
    if(!firebase.apps.length) firebase.initializeApp({
      databaseURL: FIREBASE_URL,
      storageBucket: "salah-arabic-platform.appspot.com"
    });
    rtdb = firebase.database();
    storage = firebase.storage();
    dbRef = rtdb.ref(DB_PATH);

    try{ rtdb.goOnline(); }catch(e){}

    rtdb.ref('.info/connected').on('value', snap => {
      const connected = snap.val() === true;
      if(connected !== firebaseConnected){
        firebaseConnected = connected;
        if(connected) firebaseReady = true;
        updateSyncBadge();
      }
    });

    dbRef.on('value', snap => {
      const data = snap.val();
      firebaseReady = true;
      firebaseConnected = true;

      if(!data){
        if(!hasSeeded){
          hasSeeded = true;
          const payload = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(db));
          lastWriteSig = JSON.stringify(payload);
          dbRef.set(payload).then(() => {
            updateSyncBadge();
          }).catch(e => {
            hasSeeded = false;
            console.warn('Seed failed:', e);
            toast('فشل حفظ البيانات على Firebase — تحقق من قواعد الأمان','err');
          });
        }
        return;
      }

      const sig = JSON.stringify(data);

      if(sig === lastWriteSig){
        updateSyncBadge();
        return;
      }

      if(isApplyingRemote) return;

      isApplyingRemote = true;
      db = normalizeDB(data);
      lastWriteSig = sig;
      try{ localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, sig); }catch(e){}
      hasSeeded = true;

      const active = document.activeElement;
      const isTyping = active && (active.tagName === 'INPUT' || active.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || active.tagName === 'SELECT');
      if(isTyping) pendingRender = true; else render();
      updateSyncBadge();
      setTimeout(()=>{ isApplyingRemote = false; }, 50);
      setTimeout(migrateBase64PDFsToStorage, 800);
    }, err => {
      console.warn('Firebase read error:', err);
      firebaseConnected = false;
      updateSyncBadge();
      const msg = String(err && err.message || '');
      if(msg.includes('permission') || msg.includes('denied')){
        toast('صلاحيات Firebase تمنع القراءة — فعّل القراءة/الكتابة في قواعد الأمان','err');
      }
    });

    if(reconnectIntervalHandle) clearInterval(reconnectIntervalHandle);
    reconnectIntervalHandle = setInterval(() => {
      if(!firebaseConnected){
        try{
          if(rtdb){
            rtdb.goOffline();
            setTimeout(()=>{ try{ rtdb.goOnline(); }catch(e){} }, 120);
          }
        }catch(e){}
        updateSyncBadge();
      }
    }, 8000);

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if(document.visibilityState === 'visible'){
        try{
          if(rtdb && !firebaseConnected){
            rtdb.goOffline();
            setTimeout(()=>{ try{ rtdb.goOnline(); }catch(e){} }, 120);
          }
        }catch(e){}
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('online', () => {
      try{ if(rtdb) rtdb.goOnline(); }catch(e){}
    });

  }catch(e){
    console.error('Firebase init failed:', e);
    updateSyncBadge();
  }
}

/* ============ حفظ DB ============ */
function saveDB(){
  try{ localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(db)); }catch(e){}

  if(isApplyingRemote) return;
  if(!dbRef) return;

  clearTimeout(saveTimeout);
  updateSyncBadge('syncing');
  saveTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    if(!dbRef) return;
    const payload = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(db));
    const sig = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const sizeMB = new Blob([sig]).size / (1024 * 1024);
    lastWriteSig = sig;

    if(sizeMB < 6){
      dbRef.set(payload).then(() => {
        firebaseReady = true;
        firebaseConnected = true;
        updateSyncBadge();
      }).catch(e => {
        console.warn('Full write failed:', e);
        firebaseConnected = false;
        updateSyncBadge();
        const msg = String(e && e.message || '');
        if(msg.includes('permission') || msg.includes('denied')){
          toast('صلاحيات Firebase تمنع الكتابة — تحقق من قواعد الأمان','err');
        }
      });
    } else {
      const writes = [
        dbRef.child('settings').set(db.settings || {}),
        dbRef.child('students').set(db.students || []),
        dbRef.child('lessons').set(db.lessons || []),
        dbRef.child('liveSessions').set(db.liveSessions || []),
        dbRef.child('schedules').set(db.schedules || []),
        dbRef.child('library').set(db.library || { homework: [], exams: [] }),
        dbRef.child('waLog').set(db.waLog || [])
      ];
      Promise.all(writes).then(() => {
        updateSyncBadge();
      }).catch(e => {
        console.warn('Sectioned write failed:', e);
        firebaseConnected = false;
        updateSyncBadge();
      });
    }
  }, 30);
}

function updateSyncBadge(force){
  const els = document.querySelectorAll('.sync-status');
  let cls = 'sync-status', txt = 'متزامن';
  if(force === 'syncing'){
    cls += ' syncing'; txt = 'جاري الحفظ...';
  } else if(firebaseConnected){
    cls += ' online'; txt = 'متزامن';
  } else if(!firebaseReady && Date.now() - connectionInitTime < 8000){
    cls += ' syncing'; txt = 'جاري الاتصال...';
  } else {
    cls += ' offline'; txt = 'غير متصل';
  }
  els.forEach(el => {
    el.className = cls;
    el.innerHTML = `<span class="dot"></span><span>${txt}</span>`;
    if(!firebaseConnected) el.setAttribute('onclick','retryFirebase()');
    else el.removeAttribute('onclick');
  });
}

function retryFirebase(){
  toast('جارٍ إعادة الاتصال...','warn');
  connectionInitTime = Date.now();
  try{
    if(rtdb){
      rtdb.goOffline();
      setTimeout(()=>{ try{ rtdb.goOnline(); }catch(e){} }, 150);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  setTimeout(() => {
    if(firebaseConnected){
      toast('تم الاتصال بنجاح ✓','ok');
      playSound('ok');
    } else {
      toast('ما زال غير متصل — تحقق من الإنترنت','err');
    }
    updateSyncBadge();
  }, 2000);
}

/* ============ PDF Migration ============ */
let migrationRunning = false;
async function migrateBase64PDFsToStorage(){
  if(migrationRunning) return;
  if(!storage) return;
  if(!db.notes || !db.notes.length) return;

  const needsMigration = db.notes.filter(n => n.pdfData && !n.pdfUrl && n.type === 'pdf');
  if(!needsMigration.length) return;

  migrationRunning = true;
  let migrated = 0;

  for(const note of needsMigration){
    try {
      const blob = dataURItoBlob(note.pdfData);
      if(!blob) continue;

      const noteId = note.id || ('note_' + uid());
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_PDF_FOLDER + '/' + noteId);
      await storageRef.put(blob);
      const url = await storageRef.getDownloadURL();

      note.id = noteId;
      note.pdfUrl = url;
      note.pdfName = note.pdfName || 'ملف.pdf';
      note.pdfSize = blob.size;
      delete note.pdfData;
      migrated++;
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('Migration failed for note:', note.id, e);
    }
  }

  if(migrated > 0){
    saveDB();
    toast(`تم ترحيل ${migrated} مذكرة إلى السحابة ✓`, 'ok');
  }

  migrationRunning = false;
}

/* ============ الإشعارات النظامية ============ */
async function requestNotifPermission(){
  if(!('Notification' in window)) return false;
  if(Notification.permission === 'granted') return true;
  if(Notification.permission === 'denied') return false;
  try{ return (await Notification.requestPermission()) === 'granted'; }catch(e){ return false; }
}
function pushSystemNotification(title, body){
  if(typeof Notification === 'undefined' || Notification.permission !== 'granted') return;
  try{
    const n = new Notification(title, { body, icon: notifIconURI(), badge: notifIconURI(), dir:'rtl', lang:'ar', tag:'salah-'+Date.now() });
    n.onclick = () => { try { window.focus(); n.close(); } catch(e){} };
  }catch(e){}
  if(navigator.vibrate) try{ navigator.vibrate([120, 60, 120]); }catch(e){}
}

/* ============ سجل الأنشطة ============ */
function logStudentActivity(studentId, type, title, meta){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return;
  if(!Array.isArray(s.activityLog)) s.activityLog = [];
  s.activityLog.push({
    id: uid(),
    type: type || 'other',
    title: title || '',
    meta: meta || '',
    at: Date.now()
  });
  if(s.activityLog.length > 500) s.activityLog = s.activityLog.slice(-500);
}
function getActivityTypeInfo(type){
  return ACTIVITY_TYPES[type] || ACTIVITY_TYPES.other;
}
function logStudentProgress(studentId, kind, item, score, total){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return;
  if(!Array.isArray(s.progressLog)) s.progressLog = [];
  const pct = total ? Math.round((score / total) * 100) : 0;
  s.progressLog.push({
    id: uid(),
    kind,
    itemId: item.id,
    title: item.title || '',
    score,
    total,
    pct,
    at: Date.now()
  });
  if(s.progressLog.length > 300) s.progressLog = s.progressLog.slice(-300);
}

/* ============ سجل الواتساب ============ */
function logWAMessage(phone, text, meta){
  if(!db.waLog) db.waLog = [];
  const m = meta || {};
  db.waLog.push({
    id: uid(),
    phone: cleanPhone(phone),
    text: text || '',
    recipientName: m.recipientName || '',
    recipientType: m.recipientType || 'student',
    category: m.category || 'custom',
    title: m.title || '',
    studentId: m.studentId || null,
    sentAt: Date.now()
  });
  if(db.waLog.length > 500) db.waLog = db.waLog.slice(-500);
  saveDB();
}
function waCatLabel(k){
  const c = WA_CATEGORIES.find(x => x.key === k);
  return c ? c.label : 'مخصصة';
}
function waCatIcon(k){
  const c = WA_CATEGORIES.find(x => x.key === k);
  return c ? c.icon : '💬';
}
function waCatColor(k){
  const c = WA_CATEGORIES.find(x => x.key === k);
  return c ? c.color : '#64748b';
}

/* ============ إرسال واتساب ============ */
function sendWhatsApp(phone, text, logMeta){
  const p = cleanPhone(phone);
  if(!p){ toast('لا يوجد رقم هاتف','warn'); return; }
  const intl = p.startsWith('0') ? '2' + p : p;
  const url = `https://wa.me/${intl}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
  try{ window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener'); }catch(e){}
  if(logMeta) logWAMessage(p, text, logMeta);
  playSound('message');
}
function teacherWhatsAppStudent(student, text){
  if(!student || !student.phone){ toast('لا يوجد رقم هاتف للطالب','warn'); return; }
  sendWhatsApp(student.phone, text, { recipientName: student.name, recipientType: 'student', category: 'custom', studentId: student.id });
}

function buildTaskWAMessage(kind, item, teacherName){
  const now = new Date();
  const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {weekday:'long', year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'});
  const timeStr = getNowTime12();
  let title = kind === 'homework' ? 'واجب جديد' : kind === 'exam' ? 'اختبار جديد' : kind === 'eval' ? 'تقييم جديد' : kind === 'certificate' ? 'شهادة جديدة' : kind === 'note' ? 'مذكرة جديدة' : kind === 'live' ? 'محاضرة أونلاين جديدة' : 'درس جديد';
  let icon = kind === 'homework' ? '📝' : kind === 'exam' ? '📊' : kind === 'eval' ? '🎯' : kind === 'certificate' ? '🎓' : kind === 'note' ? '📄' : kind === 'live' ? '🎥' : '📹';

  let msg = `${icon} *${title}*\n`;
  msg += `من منصة الأستاذ ${teacherName} للغة العربية\n`;
  msg += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n`;
  msg += `📌 *العنوان:* ${item.title || item.session || 'بدون عنوان'}\n`;
  if(item.folder) msg += `📁 *المجلد:* ${item.folder}\n`;
  if(item.desc || item.notes) msg += `📋 *التفاصيل:* ${item.desc || item.notes}\n`;
  if(item.due) msg += `📅 *تاريخ التسليم:* ${fmtDateWithDay(item.due)}\n`;
  if(item.startDate) msg += `⏰ *موعد الفتح:* ${fmtDateTime12(item.startDate, item.startTime)}\n`;
  if(item.closeDate) msg += `🔒 *موعد الإغلاق:* ${fmtDateTime12(item.closeDate, item.closeTime)}\n`;
  if(item.durationMinutes && item.durationMinutes > 0) msg += `⏱️ *المدة الزمنية:* ${item.durationMinutes} دقيقة\n`;
  if(item.maxScore || item.total) msg += `⭐ *الدرجة:* ${item.maxScore || item.total}\n`;
  if(item.grade) msg += `🏅 *التقدير:* ${item.grade}\n`;
  msg += `\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  msg += `🕐 تاريخ الإرسال: ${dateStr} — ${timeStr}\n\n`;
  msg += `يرجى الدخول إلى المنصة والاطلاع على المحتوى في الوقت المحدد.\n\n`;
  msg += `بالتوفيق 🌟`;
  return msg;
}

function sendTaskWhatsApp(student, kind, item){
  if(!student){ return; }
  const teacherName = db.settings.teacherName || 'صلاح عطية';
  const msg = buildTaskWAMessage(kind, item, teacherName);
  const sendToStudent = ui.sendWA.toStudent && student.phone;
  const sendToParent = ui.sendWA.toParent && student.parentPhone;
  if(!sendToStudent && !sendToParent){
    toast('لم يتم تحديد مستقبل للرسالة','warn');
    return;
  }
  if(sendToStudent){
    setTimeout(() => {
      sendWhatsApp(student.phone, msg, {
        recipientName: student.name,
        recipientType: 'student',
        category: kind,
        title: item.title || item.session || '',
        studentId: student.id
      });
    }, 200);
  }
  if(sendToParent){
    setTimeout(() => {
      sendWhatsApp(student.parentPhone, msg, {
        recipientName: student.name + ' — ولي الأمر',
        recipientType: 'parent',
        category: kind,
        title: item.title || item.session || '',
        studentId: student.id
      });
    }, 800);
  }
  toast(`تم فتح واتساب للمستقبل${sendToStudent && sendToParent ? 'ين' : ''} ✓`, 'gold');
}

/* ============ البحث عن الطلاب ============ */
function getStudent(id){ return db.students.find(s => s.id === id); }
function getStudentByPhone(phone){
  const p = cleanPhone(phone);
  return db.students.find(s => cleanPhone(s.phone) === p);
}
function genTempPassword(){
  return 'Stu@' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random()*9000);
}
function getAllStages(){
  const set = new Set();
  db.students.forEach(s => { if(s.stage && s.stage.trim()) set.add(s.stage.trim()); });
  STAGES.forEach(s => set.add(s));
  return Array.from(set);
}
function getAllFolders(kind){
  const set = new Set();
  if(kind === 'note'){
    db.notes.forEach(n => { if(n.folder && n.folder.trim()) set.add(n.folder.trim()); });
  } else if(kind === 'lesson'){
    db.lessons.forEach(l => { if(l.folder && l.folder.trim()) set.add(l.folder.trim()); });
  } else if(kind === 'live'){
    db.liveSessions.forEach(lv => { if(lv.folder && lv.folder.trim()) set.add(lv.folder.trim()); });
  } else if(kind === 'all'){
    db.notes.forEach(n => { if(n.folder && n.folder.trim()) set.add(n.folder.trim()); });
    db.lessons.forEach(l => { if(l.folder && l.folder.trim()) set.add(l.folder.trim()); });
    db.liveSessions.forEach(lv => { if(lv.folder && lv.folder.trim()) set.add(lv.folder.trim()); });
  }
  return Array.from(set).sort();
}
function sessionTypeLabel(key){
  const t = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === key);
  if(t) return t.label;
  if(key === 'online') return '🎥 محاضرة أونلاين';
  return 'محاضرة';
}
function sessionTypeColor(key){
  const t = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === key);
  if(t) return t.color;
  if(key === 'online') return '#2563eb';
  return '#64748b';
}

/* ============ isItemAvailable ============ */
function isItemAvailable(item){
  if(!item.startAt) return true;
  return Date.now() >= item.startAt;
}

/* ============ نظام المدة الزمنية والإغلاق (جديد - من المنصة الأولى) ============ */
function isItemClosed(item){
  if(!item) return false;
  if(item.closedAt) return true;
  const closeAt = item.closeAt || (item.closeDate ? combineToTimestamp(item.closeDate, item.closeTime || '23:59') : null);
  if(closeAt && Date.now() >= closeAt) return true;
  return false;
}

function getQuizStartedAt(itemId){
  try{
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('quiz_start_' + itemId);
    return raw ? parseInt(raw, 10) : null;
  }catch(e){ return null; }
}
function setQuizStartedAt(itemId){
  try{ sessionStorage.setItem('quiz_start_' + itemId, String(Date.now())); }catch(e){}
}
function clearQuizStartedAt(itemId){
  try{ sessionStorage.removeItem('quiz_start_' + itemId); }catch(e){}
}
function setQuizStartedAtIfNeeded(itemId, durationMinutes){
  if(!durationMinutes || durationMinutes <= 0) return;
  const existing = getQuizStartedAt(itemId);
  if(existing){
    const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - existing) / 1000);
    const total = durationMinutes * 60;
    if(elapsed >= total) setQuizStartedAt(itemId);
  } else {
    setQuizStartedAt(itemId);
  }
}
function getQuizRemainingSeconds(item){
  if(!item || !item.durationMinutes) return null;
  const startedAt = getQuizStartedAt(item.id);
  if(!startedAt) return null;
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000);
  const total = item.durationMinutes * 60;
  return Math.max(0, total - elapsed);
}

function checkAutoClose(item, studentId){
  if(!item || item.closedAt) return false;
  const closeAt = item.closeAt || (item.closeDate ? combineToTimestamp(item.closeDate, item.closeTime || '23:59') : null);
  if(closeAt && Date.now() >= closeAt){
    item.closedAt = Date.now();
    item.closedReason = 'end_date';
    item.updatedAt = Date.now();
    const s = getStudent(studentId);
    if(s) s.updatedAt = Date.now();
    saveDB();
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function isItemSolved(item){
  if(!item) return false;
  if(item.type === 'questions'){
    return item.submitted === true;
  }
  if(item.type === 'link'){
    return !!(item.submission && (item.submission.image ||
      (item.submission.studentScore !== undefined && item.submission.studentScore !== null)));
  }
  return (item.score !== '' && item.score !== null && item.score !== undefined);
}

function itemStatusBadgeHTML(item){
  if(!item) return '';
  const closed = isItemClosed(item);
  if(!closed) return '';
  const solved = isItemSolved(item);
  if(solved){
    return `<span class="item-status-badge solved">🔒 ✅ تم الحل</span>`;
  }
  return `<span class="item-status-badge unsolved">🔒 ❌ لم يتم الحل</span>`;
}

function itemMetaChipsHTML(item){
  let html = '';
  if(item.durationMinutes && item.durationMinutes > 0){
    html += `<span class="duration-chip">⏱️ مدة: ${item.durationMinutes} دقيقة</span>`;
  }
  if(item.closeAt || item.closeDate){
    let dateStr = '', timeStr = '';
    if(item.closeDate){
      dateStr = fmtDateWithDay(item.closeDate);
      timeStr = item.closeTime ? fmtTime12(item.closeTime) : '';
    } else if(item.closeAt){
      const d = new Date(item.closeAt);
      dateStr = fmtDateWithDay(d.toISOString().slice(0,10));
      const h = d.getHours();
      const m = d.getMinutes();
      const period = h >= 12 ? 'م' : 'ص';
      const h12 = h % 12 || 12;
      timeStr = `${h12}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')} ${period}`;
    }
    html += `<span class="close-chip">📅 إغلاق: ${dateStr}${timeStr ? ' — ⏰ ' + timeStr : ''}</span>`;
  }
  if(item.autoSubmitted){
    html += `<span class="auto-submit-chip">⚡ تسليم تلقائي</span>`;
  }
  return html;
}

function closedBannerHTML(item, variant){
  const reason = item.closedReason || '';
  let cls = 'manual', ico = '🔒', title = 'تم إغلاق هذا العنصر', sub = 'تواصل مع المعلم لإعادة الفتح إذا لزم';
  if(reason === 'timeout'){
    cls = 'timeout'; ico = '⏰';
    title = 'انتهت المدة الزمنية';
    sub = 'تم التسليم تلقائيًا واعتماد الدرجة في سجلك';
  } else if(reason === 'end_date'){
    cls = 'end-date'; ico = '📅';
    title = 'انتهى موعد الإغلاق المحدد من المعلم';
    sub = 'لا يمكن الحل بعد الآن — تواصل مع المعلم لإعادة الفتح';
  } else if(reason === 'manual_close'){
    cls = 'manual'; ico = '🔒';
    title = 'تم إغلاقه من المعلم';
    sub = 'تواصل مع المعلم لإعادة الفتح إذا لزم';
  }
  const closeAt = item.closeAt || (item.closeDate ? combineToTimestamp(item.closeDate, item.closeTime || '23:59') : null);
  const dateStr = closeAt ? fmtDateWithDay(new Date(closeAt).toISOString().slice(0,10)) : '';
  return `
    <div class="closed-banner ${cls}">
      <div class="cb-ico">${ico}</div>
      <div class="cb-txt">
        ${title}
        <small>${sub}${dateStr ? ' • تاريخ الإغلاق: ' + dateStr : ''}</small>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

/* ============ التسليم التلقائي عند انتهاء الوقت ============ */
function autoSubmitForTimeout(kind, itemId){
  if(ui.screen !== 'student' || !ui.studentToken) return;
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
  if(!s) return;
  const item = kind === 'hw'
    ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId)
    : s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if(!item || item.submitted || !item.questions || !item.questions.length) return;

  const answers = {};
  item.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(q.type === 'mcq' || q.type === 'tf'){
      const checked = document.querySelector(`input[name="q_${q.id}"]:checked`);
      if(checked) answers[q.id] = checked.value;
      else if(window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id] !== undefined) answers[q.id] = window._quizAnswers[q.id];
    } else {
      const inp = document.getElementById('ans_' + q.id);
      if(inp) answers[q.id] = inp.value.trim();
      else answers[q.id] = (window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id]) || '';
    }
  });

  let earned = 0, total = 0;
  item.questions.forEach(q => {
    total += q.points || 1;
    if(isAnswerCorrect(q, answers[q.id])) earned += q.points || 1;
  });

  item.answers = answers;
  item.submitted = true;
  item.submittedAt = Date.now();
  item.score = earned;
  item.total = total;
  item.autoSubmitted = true;
  item.closedAt = item.closedAt || Date.now();
  item.closedReason = item.closedReason || 'timeout';
  item.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();

  createAutoEvaluation(s.id, {
    type: kind === 'hw' ? 'homework' : 'exam',
    itemId: item.id,
    itemTitle: item.title,
    subject: item.subject || '',
    score: earned,
    total: total,
    date: todayISO()
  });

  logStudentActivity(s.id, kind === 'hw' ? 'homework' : 'exam',
    `انتهى وقت ${kind === 'hw' ? 'الواجب' : 'الاختبار'} وتم التسليم تلقائيًا: "${item.title}"`,
    `النتيجة: ${earned}/${total}`);

  clearQuizStartedAt(item.id);

  if(kind === 'hw'){ ui.quizHwId = null; } else { ui.quizExamId = null; }
  ui.quizStepIndex = 0;
  window._quizAnswers = {};

  saveDB();
  render();
  toast(`⏰ انتهى الوقت — تم التسليم تلقائيًا: ${earned}/${total}`, 'warn');
  playSound('autoSubmit');
}

/* ============ ticker للتحقق من انتهاء الوقت ============ */
let quizTimerTicker = null;
function startQuizTimerTicker(){
  if(quizTimerTicker) clearInterval(quizTimerTicker);
  quizTimerTicker = setInterval(() => {
    if(ui.screen !== 'student') return;
    const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
    if(!s) return;

    let activeItem = null, kind = null;
    if(ui.quizHwId){
      activeItem = s.homework.find(x => x.id === ui.quizHwId);
      kind = 'hw';
    } else if(ui.quizExamId){
      activeItem = s.exams.find(x => x.id === ui.quizExamId);
      kind = 'exam';
    }
    if(!activeItem || !activeItem.durationMinutes || activeItem.submitted) return;

    const remaining = getQuizRemainingSeconds(activeItem);
    if(remaining === null) return;
    if(remaining <= 0){
      autoSubmitForTimeout(kind, activeItem.id);
      return;
    }
    const el = document.getElementById('quizTimerValue');
    if(el){
      el.textContent = formatCountdownMMSS(remaining);
      const chip = el.closest('.quiz-timer');
      if(chip){
        chip.classList.toggle('danger', remaining <= 60);
        chip.classList.toggle('warning', remaining > 60 && remaining <= 180);
      }
    }
  }, 1000);
}

/* ============ Countdown Ticker ============ */
let countdownTicker = null;
function startCountdownTicker(){
  if(countdownTicker) clearInterval(countdownTicker);
  countdownTicker = setInterval(() => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.countdown-display[data-target]').forEach(disp => {
      const target = parseInt(disp.dataset.target, 10);
      if(!target) return;
      const ms = target - Date.now();
      if(ms <= 0){ return; }
      const p = fmtCountdown(ms);
      if(!p) return;
      const dEl = disp.querySelector('[data-cd="d"]');
      const hEl = disp.querySelector('[data-cd="h"]');
      const mEl = disp.querySelector('[data-cd="m"]');
      const sEl = disp.querySelector('[data-cd="s"]');
      if(dEl) dEl.textContent = pad2(p.days);
      if(hEl) hEl.textContent = pad2(p.hours);
      if(mEl) mEl.textContent = pad2(p.mins);
      if(sEl) sEl.textContent = pad2(p.secs);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.mini-countdown-chip[data-target]').forEach(chip => {
      const target = parseInt(chip.dataset.target, 10);
      if(!target) return;
      const ms = target - Date.now();
      const lbl = chip.querySelector('.mc-lbl');
      if(ms <= 0){
        chip.classList.add('ready');
        if(lbl) lbl.textContent = 'متاح الآن';
      } else {
        chip.classList.remove('ready');
        const p = fmtCountdown(ms);
        if(lbl && p) lbl.textContent = `${pad2(p.days)}ي ${pad2(p.hours)}:${pad2(p.mins)}:${pad2(p.secs)}`;
      }
    });
  }, 1000);
}

let liveClockTicker = null;
function startLiveClock(){
  if(liveClockTicker) clearInterval(liveClockTicker);
  liveClockTicker = setInterval(() => {
    const now = getNowTime12();
    document.querySelectorAll('.live-clock').forEach(el => el.textContent = now);
  }, 1000);
}

/* ============ Countdown Box HTML ============ */
function countdownPartsHTML(targetTs){
  if(!targetTs) return '';
  const ms = targetTs - Date.now();
  if(ms <= 0) return '';
  const p = fmtCountdown(ms);
  if(!p) return '';
  return `
    <div class="countdown-display">
      <div class="cd-unit"><span class="cd-num" data-cd="d">${pad2(p.days)}</span><span class="cd-lbl">يوم</span></div>
      <div class="cd-unit"><span class="cd-num" data-cd="h">${pad2(p.hours)}</span><span class="cd-lbl">ساعة</span></div>
      <div class="cd-unit"><span class="cd-num" data-cd="m">${pad2(p.mins)}</span><span class="cd-lbl">دقيقة</span></div>
      <div class="cd-unit"><span class="cd-num" data-cd="s">${pad2(p.secs)}</span><span class="cd-lbl">ثانية</span></div>
    </div>`;
}
function countdownBoxHTML(item, variant){
  const ts = item.startAt;
  if(!ts) return '';
  const available = isItemAvailable(item);
  if(available) return '';
  const dateTimeStr = fmtDateTime12(item.startDate, item.startTime);
  const label = variant === 'student' ? 'يُفتح تلقائيًا بعد' : 'الفتح التلقائي بعد';
  return `
  <div class="countdown-box">
    <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#78350f;text-align:center;margin-bottom:8px">⏳ ${label}</div>
    <div class="countdown-display" data-target="${ts}">
      ${countdownPartsHTML(ts).replace('class="countdown-display"','').replace(/^<div[^>]*>|<\/div>$/g,'')}
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#92400e;margin-top:8px">📅 يفتح: <b>${esc(dateTimeStr)}</b></div>
  </div>`;
}
function miniCountdownChipHTML(item){
  const ts = item.startAt;
  if(!ts) return '';
  const available = isItemAvailable(item);
  if(available) return '';
  return `<span class="mini-countdown-chip" data-target="${ts}"><span class="mc-ico">⏰</span><span class="mc-lbl">—</span></span>`;
}
function scheduleInfoBoxHTML(item){
  const ts = item.startAt;
  const available = !ts ? true : Date.now() >= ts;
  const dateTimeStr = ts ? fmtDateTime12(item.startDate, item.startTime) : '';

  let closeInfoHTML = '';
  if(item.closeAt || item.closeDate){
    const closeAt = item.closeAt || combineToTimestamp(item.closeDate, item.closeTime || '23:59');
    if(closeAt){
      const closeD = new Date(closeAt);
      const closeDateStr = fmtDateWithDay(closeD.toISOString().slice(0,10));
      const closeTimeStr = item.closeTime ? fmtTime12(item.closeTime) : (() => {
        const h = closeD.getHours();
        const m = closeD.getMinutes();
        const period = h >= 12 ? 'م' : 'ص';
        const h12 = h % 12 || 12;
        return `${h12}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')} ${period}`;
      })();
      closeInfoHTML = `
        <div style="margin-top:10px;padding:10px 12px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef2f2,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fecaca;border-radius:11px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px">
          <span style="font-size:18px">🔒</span>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:2px">موعد الإغلاق التلقائي</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:#7f1d1d">${closeDateStr}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;color:#dc2626;margin-top:2px">⏰ الساعة ${closeTimeStr}</div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
  }

  if(!ts && !closeInfoHTML) return '';

  if(!available){
    return `
    <div class="schedule-info-box">
      <div class="sib-ico">🕐</div>
      <div class="sib-body">
        <div class="sib-title">موعد الفتح</div>
        <div class="sib-date">${esc(dateTimeStr)}</div>
        <div class="sib-sub">🔒 لم يُفتح بعد</div>
        ${closeInfoHTML}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  return `
  <div class="schedule-info-box">
    <div class="sib-ico">🕐</div>
    <div class="sib-body">
      <div class="sib-title">موعد الفتح</div>
      <div class="sib-date">${esc(dateTimeStr)}</div>
      <div class="sib-sub">✓ تم فتحه — متاح الآن</div>
      ${closeInfoHTML}
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ Modal functions ============ */
function openModal(html){
  document.getElementById('modalRoot').innerHTML =
    `<div class="modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeModal()"><div class="modal">${html}</div></div>`;
}
function openModalWide(html){
  document.getElementById('modalRoot').innerHTML =
    `<div class="modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeModal()"><div class="modal modal-wide">${html}</div></div>`;
}
function closeModal(){ document.getElementById('modalRoot').innerHTML = ''; }

function go(screen){ ui.screen = screen; render(); }
function logout(){
  ui.screen='home'; ui.openStudentId=null; ui.studentToken=null;
  ui.selectedPlanId = null;
  try{ localStorage.removeItem(LS_TEACHER_TOKEN); }catch(e){}
  try{ localStorage.removeItem(LS_STUDENT_TOKEN); }catch(e){}
  render();
  playSound('click');
}

/* ============ Teacher Login ============ */
function teacherLogin(){
  const phone = cleanPhone(val('tPhone'));
  const pass = val('tPass');
  if(!phone || !pass){ toast('أدخل رقم الهاتف وكلمة المرور','warn'); return; }
  if(phone === cleanPhone(db.settings.teacherPhone) && pass === db.settings.teacherPassword){
    try{
      localStorage.setItem(LS_TEACHER_TOKEN, 'ok');
      localStorage.setItem(LS_TEACHER_PHONE, phone);
    }catch(e){}
    saveLoginCreds('teacher', phone, pass);
    ui.screen = 'teacher';
    render();
    toast('مرحبًا أستاذ ' + db.settings.teacherName + ' 👋','gold');
    playSound('celebrate');
  } else {
    toast('بيانات الدخول غير صحيحة','err');
    playSound('error');
  }
}
function quickTeacherLogin(){
  const creds = loadLoginCreds('teacher');
  if(!creds){ toast('لا توجد بيانات محفوظة','warn'); return; }
  const phone = cleanPhone(creds.phone);
  const pass = creds.password;
  if(phone === cleanPhone(db.settings.teacherPhone) && pass === db.settings.teacherPassword){
    try{
      localStorage.setItem(LS_TEACHER_TOKEN, 'ok');
      localStorage.setItem(LS_TEACHER_PHONE, phone);
    }catch(e){}
    ui.screen = 'teacher';
    render();
    toast('مرحبًا أستاذ ' + db.settings.teacherName + ' 👋','gold');
    playSound('celebrate');
  } else {
    toast('البيانات المحفوظة غير صحيحة','err');
    clearLoginCreds('teacher');
    render();
  }
}
function clearTeacherCreds(){
  if(!confirm('مسح بيانات الدخول المحفوظة؟')) return;
  clearLoginCreds('teacher');
  try{ localStorage.removeItem(LS_TEACHER_PHONE); }catch(e){}
  toast('تم مسح البيانات المحفوظة ✓','gold');
  render();
}

/* ============ Student Login ============ */
function studentLogin(){
  const phone = cleanPhone(val('sPhone'));
  const pass = val('sPass');
  if(!phone || !pass){ toast('أدخل رقم الهاتف وكلمة المرور','warn'); return; }
  const s = getStudentByPhone(phone);
  if(!s){ toast('لا يوجد حساب بهذا الرقم','err'); playSound('error'); return; }
  if(s.password !== pass){ toast('كلمة المرور غير صحيحة','err'); playSound('error'); return; }
  if(s.status === 'suspended'){ toast('حسابك موقوف — تواصل مع المعلم','warn'); return; }
  try{
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_TOKEN, s.id);
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_PHONE, phone);
  }catch(e){}
  saveLoginCreds('student', phone, pass);
  ui.studentToken = s.id;
  ui.screen = 'student';
  ui.stuTab = 'overview';
  logStudentActivity(s.id, 'login', 'تسجيل دخول', '');
  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('أهلًا بك ' + s.name + ' 👋','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}
function quickStudentLogin(){
  const creds = loadLoginCreds('student');
  if(!creds){ toast('لا توجد بيانات محفوظة','warn'); return; }
  const phone = cleanPhone(creds.phone);
  const pass = creds.password;
  const s = getStudentByPhone(phone);
  if(!s || s.password !== pass){
    toast('البيانات المحفوظة غير صحيحة','err');
    clearLoginCreds('student');
    render();
    return;
  }
  if(s.status === 'suspended'){ toast('حسابك موقوف','warn'); return; }
  try{
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_TOKEN, s.id);
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_PHONE, phone);
  }catch(e){}
  ui.studentToken = s.id;
  ui.screen = 'student';
  ui.stuTab = 'overview';
  logStudentActivity(s.id, 'login', 'تسجيل دخول', '');
  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('أهلًا بك ' + s.name + ' 👋','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}
function clearStudentCreds(){
  if(!confirm('مسح بيانات الدخول المحفوظة؟')) return;
  clearLoginCreds('student');
  try{ localStorage.removeItem(LS_STUDENT_PHONE); }catch(e){}
  toast('تم مسح البيانات المحفوظة ✓','gold');
  render();
}

/* ============ OTP ============ */
let otpTimerHandle = null;

function generateOtp(){
  return String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));
}

async function sendWhatsAppViaAPI(phone, message){
  const api = (db.settings && db.settings.whatsappApi) || {};
  const provider = api.provider || 'none';

  if(!api.enabled || provider === 'none'){
    return { ok: false, error: 'API غير مهيأ', notConfigured: true };
  }

  const cleanP = cleanPhone(phone);
  if(!cleanP) return { ok: false, error: 'رقم غير صالح' };
  const intlPhone = cleanP.startsWith('0') ? '2' + cleanP : cleanP;

  if(provider === 'ultramsg' && api.ultramsgInstance && api.ultramsgToken){
    try{
      const url = `https://api.ultramsg.com/${api.ultramsgInstance}/messages/chat`;
      const body = new URLSearchParams();
      body.append('token', api.ultramsgToken);
      body.append('to', '+' + intlPhone);
      body.append('body', message);
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},
        body: body.toString()
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if(res.ok && !data.error) return { ok: true, provider: 'ultramsg', data };
      return { ok: false, error: (data.error && (data.error.message || data.error)) || 'فشل الإرسال' };
    }catch(e){ return { ok: false, error: 'تعذر الاتصال بـ UltraMsg: ' + e.message }; }
  }

  if(provider === 'meta' && api.metaPhoneId && api.metaToken){
    try{
      const url = `https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/${api.metaPhoneId}/messages`;
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + api.metaToken,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          messaging_product: 'whatsapp',
          recipient_type: 'individual',
          to: intlPhone,
          type: 'text',
          text: { preview_url: false, body: message }
        })
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if(res.ok && !data.error) return { ok: true, provider: 'meta', data };
      return { ok: false, error: (data.error && data.error.message) || 'فشل الإرسال' };
    }catch(e){ return { ok: false, error: 'تعذر الاتصال بـ Meta API: ' + e.message }; }
  }

  if(provider === 'callmebot' && api.callmebotKey){
    try{
      const url = `https://api.callmebot.com/whatsapp.php?phone=%2B${intlPhone}&text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}&apikey=${encodeURIComponent(api.callmebotKey)}`;
      const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'GET' });
      if(res.ok) return { ok: true, provider: 'callmebot' };
      return { ok: false, error: 'فشل الإرسال عبر CallMeBot' };
    }catch(e){ return { ok: false, error: 'تعذر الاتصال بـ CallMeBot: ' + e.message }; }
  }

  return { ok: false, error: 'إعدادات المزود غير مكتملة' };
}

async function sendOtpCode(phone, name, code){
  const teacherName = (db && db.settings && db.settings.teacherName) || 'صلاح عطية';
  const senderPhone = (db && db.settings && (db.settings.teacherSenderPhone || db.settings.teacherPhone)) || DEFAULT_TEACHER_PHONE;

  const msg =
`🔐 *رمز التحقق الخاص بك*
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

مرحبًا *${name}* 👋

رمز التحقق لتأكيد حسابك في
منصة الأستاذ *${teacherName}*
للغة العربية:

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
     🔢  *${code}*
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

⏱️ الرمز صالح لمدة *60 ثانية* فقط.
🔒 لا تشارك هذا الرمز مع أي شخص.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📞 للاستفسار: ${senderPhone}
🌟 بالتوفيق في رحلتك التعليمية`;

  const p = cleanPhone(phone);
  if(!p) return { ok: false, error: 'رقم غير صالح' };

  const apiResult = await sendWhatsAppViaAPI(p, msg);

  if(apiResult.ok){
    logWAMessage(p, msg, {
      recipientName: name,
      recipientType: 'student',
      category: 'otp',
      title: '🔐 رمز تحقق OTP (تلقائي)',
      studentId: null
    });
    return { ok: true, auto: true, provider: apiResult.provider };
  }

  const intl = p.startsWith('0') ? '2' + p : p;
  const url = `https://wa.me/${intl}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`;
  try{ window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener'); }catch(e){}

  logWAMessage(p, msg, {
    recipientName: name,
    recipientType: 'student',
    category: 'otp',
    title: '🔐 رمز تحقق OTP (يدوي)',
    studentId: null
  });

  return { ok: true, auto: false, error: apiResult.error };
}

function startOtpRegistration(){
  const name = val('regName');
  const phone = cleanPhone(val('regPhone'));
  const parentPhone = cleanPhone(val('regParent'));
  const stage = val('regStage');
  const track = val('regTrack') || 'عام';
  const password = val('regPass');

  if(!name){ toast('أدخل اسم الطالب الكامل','warn'); return; }
  if(!phone){ toast('أدخل رقم هاتف الطالب','warn'); return; }
  if(phone.length < 10){ toast('رقم هاتف الطالب غير صحيح','warn'); return; }
  if(!parentPhone){ toast('أدخل رقم ولي الأمر','warn'); return; }
  if(parentPhone.length < 10){ toast('رقم ولي الأمر غير صحيح','warn'); return; }
  if(!stage){ toast('اختر المرحلة الدراسية','warn'); return; }
  if(!password){ toast('أدخل كلمة المرور','warn'); return; }
  if(password.length < 4){ toast('كلمة المرور 4 أحرف على الأقل','warn'); return; }
  if(getStudentByPhone(phone)){ toast('هذا الرقم مسجل بالفعل — سجّل الدخول','warn'); return; }

  ui.pendingRegData = { name: name.trim(), phone, parentPhone, stage, track, password };
  ui.otpCode = generateOtp();
  ui.otpExpiresAt = Date.now() + OTP_VALIDITY_MS;
  ui.otpAttempts = 0;
  ui.otpVerified = false;
  ui.otpResendCount = 0;
  ui.regStep = 'otp';

  render();

  setTimeout(async () => {
    const result = await sendOtpCode(phone, name.trim(), ui.otpCode);

    if(result.auto){
      toast('✅ تم إرسال رمز التحقق تلقائيًا عبر واتساب 📱','gold');
    } else if(result.ok && result.error){
      toast('⚠️ تعذر الإرسال التلقائي — أرسل الرمز من واتساب المفتوح','warn');
    } else if(result.ok){
      toast('تم فتح واتساب — أرسل الرمز للطالب','gold');
    } else {
      toast('تعذر إرسال الرمز — تحقق من الاتصال','err');
    }

    setTimeout(() => {
      const first = document.getElementById('otp_0');
      if(first) first.focus();
    }, 250);
  }, 350);

  startOtpTimer();
  playSound('notify');
}

function startOtpTimer(){
  if(otpTimerHandle){ clearInterval(otpTimerHandle); otpTimerHandle = null; }
  otpTimerHandle = setInterval(() => {
    const el = document.getElementById('otpTimerNum');
    const bar = document.getElementById('otpProgressFill');
    const wrap = document.getElementById('otpProgress');
    const timerWrap = document.getElementById('otpTimerWrap');
    const resendBtn = document.getElementById('otpResendBtn');
    if(!el) return;
    const remaining = ui.otpExpiresAt - Date.now();
    if(remaining <= 0){
      el.textContent = '00:00';
      if(timerWrap) timerWrap.classList.add('expired');
      if(bar) bar.style.width = '0%';
      if(resendBtn){
        resendBtn.disabled = false;
        resendBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة إرسال الرمز';
      }
      if(otpTimerHandle){ clearInterval(otpTimerHandle); otpTimerHandle = null; }
      return;
    }
    const secs = Math.ceil(remaining / 1000);
    const mm = String(Math.floor(secs / 60)).padStart(2,'0');
    const ss = String(secs % 60).padStart(2,'0');
    el.textContent = mm + ':' + ss;
    const pct = (remaining / OTP_VALIDITY_MS) * 100;
    if(bar){
      bar.style.width = pct + '%';
      if(wrap){
        wrap.classList.remove('warn','danger');
        if(pct < 20) wrap.classList.add('danger');
        else if(pct < 50) wrap.classList.add('warn');
      }
    }
    if(resendBtn){
      if(remaining > 0){
        resendBtn.disabled = true;
        resendBtn.textContent = `🔄 إعادة الإرسال بعد ${secs} ث`;
      } else {
        resendBtn.disabled = false;
        resendBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة إرسال الرمز';
      }
    }
  }, 500);
}

function handleOtpInput(el, idx){
  let v = normalizeDigits(el.value).replace(/[^\d]/g,'');
  if(v.length > 1) v = v.slice(-1);
  el.value = v;
  el.classList.remove('error');
  if(v){
    el.classList.add('filled');
    if(idx < OTP_LENGTH - 1){
      const next = document.getElementById('otp_' + (idx+1));
      if(next) next.focus();
    } else {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const code = getEnteredOtp();
        if(code.length === OTP_LENGTH) verifyOtp();
      }, 180);
    }
  } else {
    el.classList.remove('filled');
  }
  checkOtpComplete();
}

function handleOtpKey(el, idx, e){
  if(e.key === 'Backspace' && !el.value && idx > 0){
    const prev = document.getElementById('otp_' + (idx-1));
    if(prev){ prev.focus(); prev.value = ''; prev.classList.remove('filled'); }
    e.preventDefault();
    checkOtpComplete();
    return;
  }
  if(e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && idx > 0){
    const prev = document.getElementById('otp_' + (idx-1));
    if(prev) prev.focus();
  }
  if(e.key === 'ArrowRight' && idx < OTP_LENGTH - 1){
    const next = document.getElementById('otp_' + (idx+1));
    if(next) next.focus();
  }
}

function handleOtpPaste(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text');
  const digits = normalizeDigits(String(text)).replace(/[^\d]/g,'').slice(0, OTP_LENGTH);
  if(!digits) return;
  for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
    const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
    if(inp){
      inp.value = digits[i] || '';
      if(digits[i]) inp.classList.add('filled');
      else inp.classList.remove('filled');
    }
  }
  checkOtpComplete();
  if(digits.length === OTP_LENGTH) setTimeout(verifyOtp, 180);
}

function checkOtpComplete(){
  let full = '';
  for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
    const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
    if(inp) full += inp.value;
  }
  const btn = document.getElementById('otpVerifyBtn');
  if(btn){
    if(full.length === OTP_LENGTH && !ui.otpVerified) btn.removeAttribute('disabled');
    else btn.setAttribute('disabled','');
  }
}

function getEnteredOtp(){
  let code = '';
  for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
    const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
    if(inp) code += normalizeDigits(inp.value);
  }
  return code;
}

function markOtpError(){
  for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
    const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
    if(inp){
      inp.classList.add('error');
      inp.classList.remove('filled');
      inp.value = '';
    }
  }
  const first = document.getElementById('otp_0');
  if(first) setTimeout(() => first.focus(), 120);
  checkOtpComplete();
}

function verifyOtp(){
  if(ui.otpVerified) return;
  const code = getEnteredOtp();
  if(code.length !== OTP_LENGTH){
    toast(`أدخل الرمز المكون من ${OTP_LENGTH} أرقام`,'warn'); return;
  }

  const st = document.getElementById('otpStatus');
  if(st){
    st.style.display = 'flex';
    st.className = 'otp-status checking';
    st.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner"></span> جاري التحقق...';
  }

  setTimeout(() => {
    if(Date.now() > ui.otpExpiresAt){
      markOtpError();
      if(st){ st.className = 'otp-status err'; st.innerHTML = '⏰ انتهت صلاحية الرمز'; }
      toast('انتهت صلاحية الرمز','err');
      playSound('error');
      return;
    }
    if(code !== ui.otpCode){
      ui.otpAttempts++;
      markOtpError();
      if(ui.otpAttempts >= OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS){
        if(st){ st.className = 'otp-status err'; st.innerHTML = '🚫 تجاوزت عدد المحاولات'; }
        toast('تجاوزت عدد المحاولات','err');
        for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
          const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
          if(inp) inp.disabled = true;
        }
        ui.otpExpiresAt = 0;
        const resendBtn = document.getElementById('otpResendBtn');
        if(resendBtn){ resendBtn.disabled = false; resendBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة إرسال الرمز'; }
      } else {
        const remain = OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS - ui.otpAttempts;
        if(st){ st.className = 'otp-status err'; st.innerHTML = `❌ الرمز غير صحيح — متبقي ${remain}`; }
        toast(`الرمز غير صحيح — متبقي ${remain}`,'err');
      }
      playSound('error');
      return;
    }

    ui.otpVerified = true;
    for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
      const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
      if(inp){
        inp.classList.remove('error');
        inp.classList.add('success');
        inp.disabled = true;
      }
    }
    if(otpTimerHandle){ clearInterval(otpTimerHandle); otpTimerHandle = null; }
    if(st){ st.className = 'otp-status ok'; st.innerHTML = '✅ تم التحقق بنجاح — جاري إنشاء الحساب...'; }
    const btn = document.getElementById('otpVerifyBtn');
    if(btn) btn.setAttribute('disabled','');

    playSound('success');
    toast('تم التحقق من الرقم ✓','gold');

    setTimeout(() => finalizeRegistration(), 900);
  }, 500);
}

async function resendOtp(){
  if(Date.now() < ui.otpExpiresAt){
    const remaining = Math.ceil((ui.otpExpiresAt - Date.now()) / 1000);
    toast(`انتظر ${remaining} ثانية قبل إعادة الإرسال`,'warn');
    return;
  }
  const p = ui.pendingRegData;
  if(!p){ toast('انتهت الجلسة — أعد التسجيل','err'); cancelOtp(); return; }

  ui.otpCode = generateOtp();
  ui.otpExpiresAt = Date.now() + OTP_VALIDITY_MS;
  ui.otpAttempts = 0;
  ui.otpResendCount++;

  for(let i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++){
    const inp = document.getElementById('otp_' + i);
    if(inp){
      inp.disabled = false;
      inp.value = '';
      inp.classList.remove('error','success','filled');
    }
  }
  const timerWrap = document.getElementById('otpTimerWrap');
  if(timerWrap) timerWrap.classList.remove('expired');
  const st = document.getElementById('otpStatus');
  if(st){ st.className = 'otp-status'; st.innerHTML = ''; st.style.display = 'none'; }
  const btn = document.getElementById('otpVerifyBtn');
  if(btn) btn.setAttribute('disabled','');

  const result = await sendOtpCode(p.phone, p.name, ui.otpCode);
  startOtpTimer();

  if(result.auto){
    toast('✅ تم إرسال رمز جديد تلقائيًا 📱','gold');
  } else if(result.ok){
    toast('تم إرسال رمز جديد 📱','gold');
  } else {
    toast('تعذر إرسال الرمز','err');
  }
  playSound('notify');
  setTimeout(() => {
    const first = document.getElementById('otp_0');
    if(first) first.focus();
  }, 200);
}

function cancelOtp(){
  if(otpTimerHandle){ clearInterval(otpTimerHandle); otpTimerHandle = null; }
  ui.regStep = 'form';
  ui.pendingRegData = null;
  ui.otpCode = '';
  ui.otpExpiresAt = 0;
  ui.otpAttempts = 0;
  ui.otpVerified = false;
  render();
}

function finalizeRegistration(){
  const p = ui.pendingRegData;
  if(!p){ toast('حدث خطأ — أعد المحاولة','err'); cancelOtp(); return; }

  const s = {
    id: uid(),
    name: p.name,
    phone: p.phone,
    password: p.password,
    parentPhone: p.parentPhone,
    stage: p.stage,
    track: p.track,
    parentApprovalPassword: '',
    status: 'pending',
    plan: null,
    payment: null,
    paymentHistory: [],
    homework: [], exams: [], evaluations: [], autoEvaluations: [], schedule: [], certificates: [], privateNotes: [],
    activityLog: [{ id: uid(), type: 'other', title: 'تم إنشاء الحساب', meta: 'التحقق من الرقم بنجاح', at: Date.now() }],
    progressLog: [],
    notifications: [{
      id: uid(),
      text: '👋 مرحبًا بك في منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية — تم التحقق من رقم هاتفك بنجاح. يرجى إتمام عملية الدفع لتفعيل حسابك.',
      date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
    }],
    subscription: null,
    phoneVerified: true,
    verifiedAt: Date.now(),
    enrolledAt: Date.now(),
    expiresAt: null,
    createdAt: Date.now()
  };
  db.students.push(s);
  saveDB();

  try{
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_TOKEN, s.id);
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STUDENT_PHONE, s.phone);
  }catch(e){}
  saveLoginCreds('student', s.phone, p.password);
  ui.studentToken = s.id;
  ui.screen = 'student';
  ui.stuTab = 'overview';
  ui.regStep = 'form';
  ui.pendingRegData = null;
  ui.otpCode = '';
  ui.otpExpiresAt = 0;
  ui.otpVerified = false;
  ui.otpAttempts = 0;

  render();
  toast('✅ تم إنشاء الحساب بنجاح — يرجى إتمام الدفع','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

function studentRegister(){
  startOtpRegistration();
}

/* ============ اختر الخطة والدفع ============ */
function selectPlan(planId){
  ui.selectedPlanId = planId;
  render();
  setTimeout(() => {
    const el = document.getElementById('paymentSection');
    if(el) el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'});
  }, 100);
}

function submitPayment(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const planId = ui.selectedPlanId;
  if(!planId){ toast('اختر خطة الاشتراك أولاً','warn'); return; }
  const plan = db.settings.prices.plans.find(p => p.id === planId);
  if(!plan){ toast('الخطة غير موجودة','warn'); return; }

  const txnId = val('payTxnId');
  const note = val('payNote');
  const imgData = window._paymentScreenshot;
  if(!txnId && !imgData){ toast('أدخل رقم العملية أو ارفع صورة','warn'); return; }
  if(!imgData){ toast('ارفع صورة التحويل','warn'); return; }

  s.payment = {
    planId: plan.id,
    planName: plan.name,
    amount: plan.price,
    duration: plan.duration,
    method: 'vodafone_cash',
    transactionId: txnId,
    note: note,
    screenshot: imgData,
    submittedAt: Date.now(),
    status: 'pending',
    reviewedAt: null,
    rejectionReason: ''
  };
  s.plan = { id: plan.id, name: plan.name, price: plan.price, duration: plan.duration };

  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `📸 تم استلام بيانات الدفع (${plan.name} — ${plan.price} ${getCurrency().symbol}) — بانتظار مراجعة المعلم.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'payment', 'طلب دفع جديد', plan.name);

  delete window._paymentScreenshot;
  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('تم إرسال بيانات الدفع ✓ — بانتظار تفعيل المعلم','gold');
  playSound('success');

  const now = new Date();
  const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {weekday:'long', year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'});
  const timeStr = getNowTime12();
  const msg = `💰 *طلب دفع جديد*\nمن منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية للغة العربية\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n👤 *الطالب:* ${s.name}\n📱 *الهاتف:* ${s.phone}\n📚 *المرحلة:* ${s.stage}\n🎓 *المسار:* ${s.track||'عام'}\n\n📋 *الخطة:* ${plan.name}\n💵 *المبلغ:* ${plan.price} ${getCurrency().symbol}\n🔢 *رقم العملية:* ${txnId || '—'}\n📝 *ملاحظة:* ${note || 'لا يوجد'}\n\n🕐 *تاريخ الإرسال:* ${dateStr} — ${timeStr}\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nيرجى المراجعة والتفعيل من لوحة التحكم.`;
  setTimeout(() => sendWhatsApp(db.settings.whatsapp, msg, {
    recipientName: 'المعلم (طلب دفع)',
    recipientType: 'custom',
    category: 'payment',
    title: `طلب دفع من ${s.name}`,
    studentId: s.id
  }), 800);
}

function handlePaymentScreenshot(event){
  const file = event.target.files[0]; if(!file) return;
  if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){ toast('الرجاء اختيار صورة','warn'); return; }
  if(file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024){ toast('حجم الصورة كبير (الحد 10MB)','warn'); return; }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    window._paymentScreenshot = e.target.result;
    const zone = document.getElementById('payUploadZone');
    if(zone){
      zone.classList.add('has-img');
      zone.innerHTML = `
        <div class="img-preview-wrap">
          <img src="${e.target.result}" alt="معاينة">
          <div class="img-preview-info">
            <div class="ipi-name">✓ تم اختيار الصورة</div>
            <div class="ipi-actions">
              <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();document.getElementById('payScreenshot').click()">تغيير</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="payScreenshot" accept="image/*" onchange="handlePaymentScreenshot(event)">
      `;
    }
    toast('تم رفع صورة التحويل ✓','gold');
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function approvePayment(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!s.payment){ toast('لا يوجد طلب دفع','warn'); return; }
  const plan = s.payment;
  const now = Date.now();
  let expiresAt = now;
  const dur = (plan.duration||'').toLowerCase();
  if(dur.includes('شهر')) expiresAt = now + 30*24*3600*1000;
  else if(dur.includes('4')) expiresAt = now + 120*24*3600*1000;
  else if(dur.includes('عام') || dur.includes('سنة')) expiresAt = now + 365*24*3600*1000;

  s.payment.status = 'approved';
  s.payment.reviewedAt = now;
  s.status = 'active';
  s.expiresAt = expiresAt;

  s.paymentHistory = s.paymentHistory || [];
  s.paymentHistory.push({
    id: uid(),
    planId: plan.planId, planName: plan.planName,
    amount: plan.amount, method: 'vodafone_cash',
    transactionId: plan.transactionId,
    approvedAt: now, expiresAt
  });

  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `✅ تم تفعيل حسابك — خطة "${plan.planName}" سارية حتى ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(expiresAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: now
  });
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'payment', 'تفعيل الحساب', plan.planName);

  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('تم تفعيل الحساب ✓','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');

  const msg = `🎉 مرحبًا ${s.name}\n\nتم تفعيل حسابك في منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية للغة العربية.\n\n📋 الخطة: ${plan.planName}\n💵 المبلغ: ${plan.amount} ${getCurrency().symbol}\n📅 صالح حتى: ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(expiresAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}\n\n🔐 يمكنك الآن الدخول برقم هاتفك وكلمة المرور.\n\nبالتوفيق 🌟`;
  setTimeout(() => sendWhatsApp(s.phone, msg, {
    recipientName: s.name,
    recipientType: 'student',
    category: 'payment',
    title: 'تفعيل الحساب',
    studentId: s.id
  }), 600);
}

function rejectPayment(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const reason = prompt('سبب رفض الدفع:', 'الصورة غير واضحة أو المبلغ غير مطابق');
  if(reason === null) return;
  if(!s.payment) return;
  s.payment.status = 'rejected';
  s.payment.rejectionReason = reason;
  s.payment.reviewedAt = Date.now();
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `❌ تم رفض طلب الدفع — السبب: ${reason}. يرجى إعادة الرفع.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('تم رفض الطلب','warn');
  playSound('warn');
}

function suspendStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('إيقاف حساب هذا الطالب؟')) return;
  s.status = 'suspended';
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم إيقاف الحساب','warn');
}
function activateStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  s.status = 'active';
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم تفعيل الحساب','gold');
  playSound('ok');
}

/* ============ حساب العملة ============ */
function getCurrency(){
  const c = (db && db.settings && db.settings.currency) || 'EGP';
  return CURRENCIES[c] || CURRENCIES.EGP;
}
function currencySymbol(){ return getCurrency().symbol; }
function fmtPrice(n){ return `${n} ${currencySymbol()}`; }
/* ============ نظام الدورات الشهرية (Subscription) ============ */
function normalizeSubscription(sub){
  const defaultCur = (db && db.settings && db.settings.currency) || 'EGP';
  if(!sub || typeof sub !== 'object') return {
    startDate: '', startDay: '', endDate: '', cycleNumber: 1, sessionsPerCycle: 12, weeksPerCycle: 4,
    sessions: [], notes: '', active: false, monthlyFee: 0, feeNotes: '', showFees: true,
    currency: defaultCur, feePaid: false, paidAt: null, completedCycles: [],
    updatedAt: 0
  };
  return {
    startDate: sub.startDate || '',
    startDay: sub.startDay || '',
    endDate: sub.endDate || '',
    cycleNumber: sub.cycleNumber || 1,
    sessionsPerCycle: sub.sessionsPerCycle || 12,
    weeksPerCycle: sub.weeksPerCycle || 4,
    sessions: Array.isArray(sub.sessions) ? sub.sessions : [],
    notes: sub.notes || '',
    active: !!sub.active,
    monthlyFee: typeof sub.monthlyFee === 'number' ? sub.monthlyFee : (parseFloat(sub.monthlyFee) || 0),
    feeNotes: sub.feeNotes || '',
    showFees: sub.showFees === false ? false : true,
    currency: sub.currency || defaultCur,
    feePaid: sub.feePaid === true,
    paidAt: sub.paidAt || null,
    completedCycles: Array.isArray(sub.completedCycles) ? sub.completedCycles : [],
    updatedAt: typeof sub.updatedAt === 'number' ? sub.updatedAt : 0
  };
}

function getCurrentCycleStart(student){
  if(!student.subscription) return null;
  return student.subscription.startDate || null;
}

function getMonthlySessionsData(student, startDateStr){
  if(!startDateStr) return null;
  const start = new Date(startDateStr);
  if(isNaN(start)) return null;

  const sub = student.subscription || {};
  let end;
  if(sub.endDate){
    end = new Date(sub.endDate);
    end.setHours(23, 59, 59);
    if(isNaN(end) || end <= start){
      end = new Date(start);
      end.setDate(end.getDate() + 28);
      end.setHours(23, 59, 59);
    }
  } else {
    end = new Date(start);
    end.setDate(end.getDate() + 28);
    end.setHours(23, 59, 59);
  }

  const scheduledSessions = [];
  const dayMap = {0:'الأحد',1:'الاثنين',2:'الثلاثاء',3:'الأربعاء',4:'الخميس',5:'الجمعة',6:'السبت'};

  const current = new Date(start);
  while(current <= end){
    const dayName = dayMap[current.getDay()];
    const daySessions = (student.schedule || []).filter(x => x.day === dayName);
    daySessions.forEach(session => {
      scheduledSessions.push({
        date: current.toISOString().slice(0,10),
        dayName: dayName,
        subject: session.subject || 'حصة',
        from: session.from,
        to: session.to,
        location: session.location
      });
    });
    current.setDate(current.getDate() + 1);
  }

  const evaluations = (student.evaluations || []).filter(e => {
    const d = new Date(e.date);
    return d >= start && d <= end;
  });

  const subSessions = student.subscription && student.subscription.sessions ?
    student.subscription.sessions.filter(s => {
      const d = new Date(s.date);
      return d >= start && d <= end;
    }) : [];

  const allRecorded = [...evaluations];
  const attended = allRecorded.filter(e => e.attend === 'حاضر').length;
  const late = allRecorded.filter(e => e.attend === 'متأخر').length;
  const absent = allRecorded.filter(e => e.attend === 'غائب').length;

  const totalSessions = (sub.sessionsPerCycle && parseInt(sub.sessionsPerCycle) > 0)
    ? parseInt(sub.sessionsPerCycle)
    : (scheduledSessions.length || 12);

  if(scheduledSessions.length === 0 && totalSessions > 0){
    const dayNames = ['الأحد','الاثنين','الثلاثاء','الأربعاء','الخميس','الجمعة','السبت'];
    for(let i = 0; i < totalSessions; i++){
      const d = new Date(start);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + i * 2);
      scheduledSessions.push({
        date: d.toISOString().slice(0,10),
        dayName: dayNames[d.getDay()],
        subject: 'حصة ' + (i+1),
        from: '', to: '', location: '',
        isPlaceholder: true
      });
    }
  }

  const recordedCount = allRecorded.length;
  const attendanceRate = recordedCount > 0 ? Math.round(((attended + late * 0.5) / recordedCount) * 100) : 0;
  const cycleComplete = recordedCount >= totalSessions && totalSessions > 0;

  return {
    startDate: start,
    endDate: end,
    startDateStr: fmtDateWithDay(start),
    endDateStr: fmtDateWithDay(end),
    startHijri: toHijri(start),
    endHijri: toHijri(end),
    monthName: start.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {month:'long', year:'numeric'}),
    scheduledSessions: scheduledSessions,
    evaluations: evaluations,
    subSessions: subSessions,
    totalSessions: totalSessions,
    recordedCount: recordedCount,
    attended: attended,
    late: late,
    absent: absent,
    attendanceRate: attendanceRate,
    progress: totalSessions > 0 ? Math.round((recordedCount / totalSessions) * 100) : 0,
    cycleComplete: cycleComplete
  };
}

function archiveCurrentCycle(studentId, reason){
