  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.subscription) return null;
  const sub = s.subscription;
  const startDate = sub.startDate;
  if(!startDate) return null;

  const monthlyData = getMonthlySessionsData(s, startDate);
  if(!monthlyData) return null;

  const evaluationsInCycle = (s.evaluations || []).filter(e => {
    const d = new Date(e.date);
    return d >= monthlyData.startDate && d <= monthlyData.endDate;
  });

  const cycle = {
    id: uid(),
    cycleNumber: sub.cycleNumber || 1,
    startDate: monthlyData.startDate.toISOString().slice(0,10),
    endDate: monthlyData.endDate.toISOString().slice(0,10),
    startDateStr: monthlyData.startDateStr,
    endDateStr: monthlyData.endDateStr,
    startHijri: monthlyData.startHijri,
    endHijri: monthlyData.endHijri,
    monthName: monthlyData.monthName,
    totalSessions: monthlyData.totalSessions,
    recordedCount: monthlyData.recordedCount,
    attended: monthlyData.attended,
    late: monthlyData.late,
    absent: monthlyData.absent,
    attendanceRate: monthlyData.attendanceRate,
    progress: monthlyData.progress,
    cycleComplete: monthlyData.cycleComplete,
    sessionsPerCycle: sub.sessionsPerCycle,
    weeksPerCycle: sub.weeksPerCycle,
    monthlyFee: sub.monthlyFee,
    currency: sub.currency,
    feePaid: sub.feePaid,
    feeNotes: sub.feeNotes,
    notes: sub.notes,
    reason: reason || 'reset',
    archivedAt: Date.now(),
    archivedDate: todayISO(),
    sessions: Array.isArray(sub.sessions) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sub.sessions)) : [],
    evaluations: evaluationsInCycle.map(e => ({
      date: e.date,
      session: e.session,
      attend: e.attend,
      mastered: (e.mastered||[]).map(x => x.text),
      notMastered: (e.notMastered||[]).map(x => x.text),
      upcoming: (e.upcoming||[]).map(x => x.text),
      notes: e.notes
    }))
  };

  if(!sub.completedCycles) sub.completedCycles = [];
  sub.completedCycles.push(cycle);
  sub.updatedAt = Date.now();
  return cycle;
}

function startNewCycleForStudent(studentId, silent){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return;
  if(!s.subscription) s.subscription = normalizeSubscription(null);

  archiveCurrentCycle(studentId, 'auto_new_cycle');

  const preservedSessionsPerCycle = parseInt(s.subscription.sessionsPerCycle) || 12;
  const preservedWeeksPerCycle = parseInt(s.subscription.weeksPerCycle) || 4;

  s.subscription.cycleNumber = (s.subscription.cycleNumber || 1) + 1;
  s.subscription.sessions = [];
  s.subscription.startDate = todayISO();
  s.subscription.endDate = '';
  s.subscription.startDay = DAYS_JS[new Date().getDay()];
  s.subscription.active = true;
  s.subscription.feePaid = false;
  s.subscription.paidAt = null;
  s.subscription.sessionsPerCycle = preservedSessionsPerCycle;
  s.subscription.weeksPerCycle = preservedWeeksPerCycle;
  s.subscription.updatedAt = Date.now();

  saveDB(); render();
  if(!silent) toast('تم أرشفة الدورة الحالية وبدء دورة جديدة ✓','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

function resetCycleForStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('⚠️ سيتم أرشفة الدورة الحالية في سجل الدورات المكتملة، ثم بدء دورة جديدة.\n\nهل أنت متأكد؟')) return;
  if(!confirm('تأكيد نهائي: سيتم تصفير الدورة الحالية والبدء من جديد.')) return;

  archiveCurrentCycle(studentId, 'manual_reset');

  const startDate = getCurrentCycleStart(s);
  if(startDate && s.subscription){
    const start = new Date(startDate);
    const end = s.subscription.endDate ? new Date(s.subscription.endDate) : new Date(start.getTime() + 28*24*60*60*1000);
    end.setHours(23, 59, 59);

    s.evaluations = s.evaluations.filter(e => {
      const d = new Date(e.date);
      return !(d >= start && d <= end);
    });
    if(s.subscription.sessions){
      s.subscription.sessions = s.subscription.sessions.filter(ss => {
        const d = new Date(ss.date);
        return !(d >= start && d <= end);
      });
    }
    s.subscription.startDate = todayISO();
    s.subscription.endDate = '';
    s.subscription.startDay = DAYS_JS[new Date().getDay()];
    s.subscription.cycleNumber = (s.subscription.cycleNumber || 1) + 1;
    s.subscription.feePaid = false;
    s.subscription.paidAt = null;
    s.subscription.updatedAt = Date.now();
  }

  saveDB();
  closeModal();
  render();
  toast('تم أرشفة الدورة الحالية في السجل وبدء دورة جديدة ✓','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

function checkCycleCompletion(s){
  const startDate = getCurrentCycleStart(s);
  if(!startDate) return;
  const data = getMonthlySessionsData(s, startDate);
  if(!data || !data.cycleComplete) return;
  setTimeout(() => {
    if(confirm(`🎉 اكتملت دورة "${s.name}"! (${data.recordedCount}/${data.totalSessions} حصة).\nهل تريد أرشفة الدورة وبدء دورة جديدة تلقائيًا؟`)){
      startNewCycleForStudent(s.id, true);
    }
  }, 600);
}

function getRecordedCountCategory(recordedCount){
  if(!recordedCount || recordedCount <= 0) return 'empty';
  if(recordedCount >= 1 && recordedCount <= 5) return 'low';
  if(recordedCount >= 6 && recordedCount <= 10) return 'mid';
  if(recordedCount >= 11 && recordedCount <= 15) return 'high';
  return 'top';
}

function getRecordedCategoryInfo(category){
  const map = {
    empty: { label: 'بدون حصص', icon: '⭕' },
    low:   { label: '1-5 حصص', icon: '🌱' },
    mid:   { label: '6-10 حصص', icon: '🌿' },
    high:  { label: '11-15 حصة', icon: '🌳' },
    top:   { label: '16+ حصة', icon: '⭐' }
  };
  return map[category] || map.empty;
}

/* ============ نظام التحصيل التلقائي ============ */
function getAutoEvalLevel(percentage){
  if(percentage >= 90) return { label: 'ممتاز', cls: 'good', icon: '🌟' };
  if(percentage >= 80) return { label: 'جيد جداً', cls: 'good', icon: '💎' };
  if(percentage >= 70) return { label: 'جيد', cls: 'good', icon: '👍' };
  if(percentage >= 60) return { label: 'مقبول', cls: 'warn', icon: '📈' };
  if(percentage >= 50) return { label: 'يحتاج تحسين', cls: 'warn', icon: '⚠️' };
  return { label: 'يحتاج دعم', cls: 'bad', icon: '🔴' };
}

function getStudentPerformanceCategory(overall){
  if(overall >= 90) return { key: 'perfect', label: 'متميز', icon: '⭐', color: '#d97706' };
  if(overall >= 80) return { key: 'excellent', label: 'ممتاز', icon: '🏆', color: '#16a34a' };
  if(overall >= 60) return { key: 'good', label: 'جيد جدًا', icon: '✅', color: '#2563eb' };
  if(overall >= 40) return { key: 'fair', label: 'مقبول', icon: '⚠️', color: '#d97706' };
  return { key: 'poor', label: 'يحتاج تحسين', icon: '🚨', color: '#dc2626' };
}

function createAutoEvaluation(studentId, payload){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return null;
  if(!s.autoEvaluations) s.autoEvaluations = [];
  const total = parseFloat(payload.total) || 0;
  const score = parseFloat(payload.score) || 0;
  const percentage = total > 0 ? Math.round((score / total) * 100) : 0;
  const level = getAutoEvalLevel(percentage).label;
  const existing = s.autoEvaluations.find(x => x.itemId === payload.itemId && x.type === payload.type);
  if(existing){
    existing.score = score;
    existing.total = total;
    existing.percentage = percentage;
    existing.level = level;
    existing.itemTitle = payload.itemTitle || existing.itemTitle;
    existing.subject = payload.subject || existing.subject;
    existing.date = payload.date || existing.date;
    if(payload.notes !== undefined) existing.notes = payload.notes;
    existing.updatedAt = Date.now();
    return existing;
  }
  const entry = {
    id: uid(),
    type: payload.type,
    itemId: payload.itemId,
    itemTitle: payload.itemTitle || '',
    subject: payload.subject || '',
    score: score,
    total: total,
    percentage: percentage,
    level: level,
    date: payload.date || todayISO(),
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    updatedAt: null,
    editedBy: '',
    notes: payload.notes || ''
  };
  s.autoEvaluations.push(entry);
  return entry;
}

function getUnsolvedClosedItems(s){
  if(!s) return [];
  const unsolved = [];

  const getTotal = (item) => {
    if(item.total) return parseFloat(item.total) || 0;
    if(item.maxScore) return parseFloat(item.maxScore) || 0;
    if(item.questions && item.questions.length){
      return item.questions.reduce((a,q) => a + (q.points || 1), 0);
    }
    return 0;
  };

  (s.homework || []).forEach(h => {
    if(isItemClosed(h) && !isItemSolved(h)){
      unsolved.push({
        id: 'unsolved_hw_' + h.id,
        sourceItemId: h.id,
        sourceKind: 'homework',
        type: 'homework',
        itemTitle: h.title,
        subject: h.subject || '',
        score: 0,
        total: getTotal(h),
        percentage: 0,
        level: 'لم يتم الحل',
        date: h.closeDate || h.due || todayISO(),
        createdAt: h.closedAt || h.updatedAt || Date.now(),
        isUnsolved: true,
        closedReason: h.closedReason || ''
      });
    }
  });

  (s.exams || []).forEach(e => {
    if(isItemClosed(e) && !isItemSolved(e)){
      unsolved.push({
        id: 'unsolved_ex_' + e.id,
        sourceItemId: e.id,
        sourceKind: 'exams',
        type: 'exam',
        itemTitle: e.title,
        subject: e.subject || '',
        score: 0,
        total: getTotal(e),
        percentage: 0,
        level: 'لم يتم الحل',
        date: e.closeDate || e.date || todayISO(),
        createdAt: e.closedAt || e.updatedAt || Date.now(),
        isUnsolved: true,
        closedReason: e.closedReason || ''
      });
    }
  });

  return unsolved;
}

function getAutoStats(s){
  const realAutoEvals = s.autoEvaluations || [];
  const unsolvedItems = getUnsolvedClosedItems(s);
  const unsolvedIds = new Set(unsolvedItems.map(u => u.sourceItemId));
  const filteredRealAuto = realAutoEvals.filter(e => !unsolvedIds.has(e.itemId));
  const allItems = [...filteredRealAuto, ...unsolvedItems];

  const hwEvals = allItems.filter(e => e.type === 'homework');
  const exEvals = allItems.filter(e => e.type === 'exam');

  const hwAvg = hwEvals.length ? Math.round(hwEvals.reduce((a,e) => a + e.percentage, 0) / hwEvals.length) : 0;
  const exAvg = exEvals.length ? Math.round(exEvals.reduce((a,e) => a + e.percentage, 0) / exEvals.length) : 0;
  const overallAvg = allItems.length ? Math.round(allItems.reduce((a,e) => a + e.percentage, 0) / allItems.length) : 0;

  return {
    autoEvals: allItems,
    realAutoEvals: filteredRealAuto,
    unsolvedItems: unsolvedItems,
    unsolvedHwCount: unsolvedItems.filter(u => u.type === 'homework').length,
    unsolvedExCount: unsolvedItems.filter(u => u.type === 'exam').length,
    hwEvals, exEvals,
    hwAvg, exAvg, overallAvg,
    hwLevel: getAutoEvalLevel(hwAvg),
    exLevel: getAutoEvalLevel(exAvg),
    overallLevel: getAutoEvalLevel(overallAvg)
  };
}

function autoEvalCardHTML(e, variant, studentId){
  if(e.isUnsolved){
    const typeLabel = e.type === 'homework' ? '📝 واجب' : '📊 اختبار';
    const typeCls = e.type === 'homework' ? 'b-info' : 'b-purple';
    const reasonText = e.closedReason === 'timeout' ? '⏰ انتهت المدة الزمنية'
      : e.closedReason === 'end_date' ? '📅 انتهى تاريخ الإغلاق'
      : e.closedReason === 'manual_close' ? '🔒 أُغلق يدويًا'
      : '🔒 مغلق';
    return `
      <div class="auto-eval-card bad" style="border-color:#fca5a5;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef2f2,#fff)">
        <div class="auto-eval-head">
          <div class="auto-eval-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f87171,#b91c1c)">❌</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div class="auto-eval-title">${esc(e.itemTitle)}</div>
            <div class="auto-eval-meta">
              <span class="badge ${typeCls}">${typeLabel}</span>
              ${e.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(e.subject)}</span>` : ''}
              <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</span>
              <span class="badge b-bad" style="font-size:10px">${reasonText}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="auto-eval-score" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fee2e2,#fff);border-color:#fca5a5">
            <div class="aes-num" style="color:#b91c1c">0<span class="aes-total">/${e.total}</span></div>
            <div class="aes-pct bad">لم يُحل</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="auto-eval-body">
          <div class="auto-eval-level bad">
            <span>❌</span>
            <span>الحالة: <b>لم يتم الحل قبل الإغلاق</b></span>
          </div>
          <div class="auto-eval-bar"><div class="aeb-fill bad" style="width:0%"></div></div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }

  const level = getAutoEvalLevel(e.percentage);
  const typeLabel = e.type === 'homework' ? '📝 واجب' : '📊 اختبار';
  const typeCls = e.type === 'homework' ? 'b-info' : 'b-purple';
  return `
    <div class="auto-eval-card ${level.cls}">
      <div class="auto-eval-head">
        <div class="auto-eval-ico">${e.type === 'homework' ? '📝' : '📊'}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div class="auto-eval-title">${esc(e.itemTitle)}</div>
          <div class="auto-eval-meta">
            <span class="badge ${typeCls}">${typeLabel}</span>
            ${e.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(e.subject)}</span>` : ''}
            <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</span>
            ${e.updatedAt ? `<span class="badge b-warn" style="font-size:10px">✏️ معدّل</span>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="auto-eval-score">
          <div class="aes-num">${e.score}<span class="aes-total">/${e.total}</span></div>
          <div class="aes-pct ${level.cls}">${e.percentage}%</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-eval-body">
        <div class="auto-eval-level ${level.cls}">
          <span>${level.icon}</span>
          <span>المستوى: <b>${level.label}</b></span>
        </div>
        <div class="auto-eval-bar">
          <div class="aeb-fill ${level.cls}" style="width:${e.percentage}%"></div>
        </div>
        ${e.notes ? `<div class="auto-eval-notes">📝 ${esc(e.notes)}</div>` : ''}
      </div>
      ${variant === 'teacher' ? `
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:11px">
          <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editAutoEval('${studentId}','${e.id}')">✏️ تعديل</button>
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteAutoEval('${studentId}','${e.id}')">🗑 حذف</button>
        </div>
      ` : ''}
    </div>`;
}

function editAutoEval(studentId, evalId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.autoEvaluations) return;
  const e = s.autoEvaluations.find(x => x.id === evalId);
  if(!e) return;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--purple-d)">✏️ تعديل التقييم التلقائي</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">يمكنك تعديل الدرجة والملاحظات.</div>
    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="field"><label>الدرجة</label><input type="number" id="aeScore" value="${e.score}" min="0" step="0.5" inputmode="decimal"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>الدرجة الكاملة</label><input type="number" id="aeTotal" value="${e.total}" min="0" step="0.5" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>ملاحظات المعلم</label><textarea id="aeNotes" style="min-height:70px">${esc(e.notes||'')}</textarea></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveEditAutoEval('${studentId}','${evalId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveEditAutoEval(studentId, evalId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.autoEvaluations) return;
  const e = s.autoEvaluations.find(x => x.id === evalId);
  if(!e) return;
  const score = parseFloat(val('aeScore'));
  const total = parseFloat(val('aeTotal'));
  if(isNaN(score) || score < 0){ toast('أدخل درجة صحيحة','warn'); return; }
  if(isNaN(total) || total <= 0){ toast('أدخل درجة كاملة صحيحة','warn'); return; }
  e.score = score;
  e.total = total;
  e.percentage = Math.round((score / total) * 100);
  e.level = getAutoEvalLevel(e.percentage).label;
  e.notes = val('aeNotes');
  e.updatedAt = Date.now();
  e.editedBy = 'teacher';
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB();
  closeModal();
  render();
  toast('تم حفظ التعديلات ✓','gold');
  playSound('ok');
}

function deleteAutoEval(studentId, evalId){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا التقييم التلقائي؟')) return;
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.autoEvaluations) return;
  s.autoEvaluations = s.autoEvaluations.filter(x => x.id !== evalId);
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حذف التقييم','warn');
}

function clearAutoEvals(studentId){
  if(!confirm('تصفير كل سجل التحصيل التلقائي؟')) return;
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s) return;
  s.autoEvaluations = [];
  saveDB();
  render();
  toast('تم تصفير السجل','warn');
}

/* ============ الأسئلة التفاعلية ============ */
function addDraftQuestion(){
  saveFormDraft('exam');
  ui.draftQuestions.push({ id: uid(), type: 'mcq', text: '', options: ['', '', '', ''], correct: 0, correctTF: true, correctShort: '', points: 1, image: '', imageName: '' });
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('exam'), 50);
}
function removeDraftQuestion(qid){
  saveFormDraft('exam');
  ui.draftQuestions = ui.draftQuestions.filter(q => q.id !== qid);
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('exam'), 50);
}
function updateQuestionField(qid, field, value){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q[field] = value;
}
function updateQuestionOption(qid, idx, value){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.options[idx] = value;
}
function changeQuestionType(qid, type){
  saveFormDraft('exam');
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.type = type;
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('exam'), 50);
}
function setQuestionCorrect(qid, idx){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.correct = idx;
  render();
}
function setQuestionTF(qid, valBool){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.correctTF = valBool;
  render();
}
function addQuestionOption(qid){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(!q || q.options.length >= 6) return;
  q.options.push('');
  render();
}
function removeQuestionOption(qid, idx){
  const q = ui.draftQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(!q || q.options.length <= 2) return;
  q.options.splice(idx, 1);
  if(q.correct >= q.options.length) q.correct = 0;
  render();
}

function addDraftHwQuestion(){
  saveFormDraft('hw');
  ui.draftHwQuestions.push({ id: uid(), type: 'mcq', text: '', options: ['', '', '', ''], correct: 0, correctTF: true, correctShort: '', points: 1, image: '', imageName: '' });
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('hw'), 50);
}
function removeDraftHwQuestion(qid){
  saveFormDraft('hw');
  ui.draftHwQuestions = ui.draftHwQuestions.filter(q => q.id !== qid);
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('hw'), 50);
}
function updateHwQField(qid, field, value){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q[field] = value;
}
function updateHwQOption(qid, idx, value){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.options[idx] = value;
}
function changeHwQType(qid, type){
  saveFormDraft('hw');
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.type = type;
  render();
  setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft('hw'), 50);
}
function setHwQCorrect(qid, idx){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.correct = idx;
  render();
}
function setHwQTF(qid, valBool){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(q) q.correctTF = valBool;
  render();
}
function addHwQOption(qid){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(!q || q.options.length >= 6) return;
  q.options.push('');
  render();
}
function removeHwQOption(qid, idx){
  const q = ui.draftHwQuestions.find(x => x.id === qid);
  if(!q || q.options.length <= 2) return;
  q.options.splice(idx, 1);
  if(q.correct >= q.options.length) q.correct = 0;
  render();
}

/* ============ معاينة الأسئلة ============ */
function draftQuestionHTML(q, idx, ctx){
  const isHw = ctx === 'hw';
  const fns = isHw ? {
    changeType: 'changeHwQType', updateField: 'updateHwQField', updateOption: 'updateHwQOption',
    setCorrect: 'setHwQCorrect', setTF: 'setHwQTF', addOpt: 'addHwQOption', removeOpt: 'removeHwQOption', remove: 'removeDraftHwQuestion'
  } : {
    changeType: 'changeQuestionType', updateField: 'updateQuestionField', updateOption: 'updateQuestionOption',
    setCorrect: 'setQuestionCorrect', setTF: 'setQuestionTF', addOpt: 'addQuestionOption', removeOpt: 'removeQuestionOption', remove: 'removeDraftQuestion'
  };
  const types = [['mcq','🔘 اختيار من متعدد'],['tf','✅ صح / خطأ'],['short','✏️ إجابة قصيرة']];
  let contentHTML = '';
  if(q.type === 'mcq'){
    contentHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px;margin-top:8px">
        ${q.options.map((opt, i) => `
          <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center">
            <button onclick="${fns.setCorrect}('${q.id}',${i})" style="width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid ${q.correct===i?'var(--ok)':'var(--border)'};background:${q.correct===i?'var(--ok)':'#fff'};color:${q.correct===i?'#fff':'transparent'};cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0;font-weight:900">✓</button>
            <input value="${esc(opt)}" placeholder="الخيار ${i+1}" oninput="${fns.updateOption}('${q.id}',${i},this.value)" style="flex:1;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13.5px">
            ${q.options.length > 2 ? `<button onclick="${fns.removeOpt}('${q.id}',${i})" style="background:transparent;border:none;color:#94a3b8;cursor:pointer;font-size:15px;padding:0 6px">✕</button>` : ''}
          </div>
        `).join('')}
        ${q.options.length < 6 ? `<button onclick="${fns.addOpt}('${q.id}')" style="background:#f1f5f9;border:1.5px dashed #cbd5e1;border-radius:10px;padding:7px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px">+ إضافة خيار</button>` : ''}
      </div>`;
  } else if(q.type === 'tf'){
    contentHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;margin-top:8px">
        <button onclick="${fns.setTF}('${q.id}',true)" style="flex:1;border:2px solid ${q.correctTF===true?'var(--ok)':'var(--border)'};background:${q.correctTF===true?'var(--ok-l)':'#fff'};border-radius:12px;padding:12px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:${q.correctTF===true?'#15803d':'var(--muted)'}">✅ صح</button>
        <button onclick="${fns.setTF}('${q.id}',false)" style="flex:1;border:2px solid ${q.correctTF===false?'var(--danger)':'var(--border)'};background:${q.correctTF===false?'var(--danger-l)':'#fff'};border-radius:12px;padding:12px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:${q.correctTF===false?'#b91c1c':'var(--muted)'}">❌ خطأ</button>
      </div>`;
  } else if(q.type === 'short'){
    contentHTML = `
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:8px">
        <label style="font-size:12px">الإجابة الصحيحة</label>
        <input value="${esc(q.correctShort||'')}" placeholder="اكتب الإجابة الصحيحة..." oninput="${fns.updateField}('${q.id}','correctShort',this.value)">
      </div>`;
  }
  const imgHTML = q.image
    ? `<div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px;background:#f0fdf4;border-radius:11px;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0"><img src="${esc(q.image)}" style="max-width:100px;max-height:80px;border-radius:9px;border:1.5px solid #86efac"><div style="flex:1;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#15803d">✓ صورة السؤال مرفقة</div><button onclick="${fns.updateField}('${q.id}','image','')" style="background:var(--danger-l);color:var(--danger-d);border:none;border-radius:9px;padding:7px 11px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-weight:700;font-size:12px">🗑 حذف</button></div>`
    : `<div style="margin-top:10px;border:2px dashed #bae6fd;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0f9ff,#fff)" onclick="document.getElementById('qimg_${q.id}_${ctx}').click()">
        <div style="font-size:24px;margin-bottom:6px">🖼️</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#1e40af">📷 اضغط لإرفاق صورة مع السؤال (اختياري)</div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#3b82f6;font-weight:600;margin-top:3px">JPG / PNG بحد أقصى 3MB</div>
        <input type="file" id="qimg_${q.id}_${ctx}" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="handleQuestionImage('${ctx}','${q.id}',event)">
      </div>`;
  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:15px;background:#fff;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <div style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-weight:900;font-size:14px;flex-shrink:0">${idx+1}</div>
        <select onchange="${fns.changeType}('${q.id}',this.value)" style="flex:1;min-width:130px;max-width:200px;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;font-weight:700">
          ${types.map(([k,l]) => `<option value="${k}" ${q.type===k?'selected':''}>${l}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
        <button onclick="${fns.remove}('${q.id}')" style="background:var(--danger-l);color:var(--danger-d);border:none;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 12px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-weight:700;font-size:12.5px">🗑 حذف</button>
      </div>
      <div class="field">
        <label style="font-size:12px">نص السؤال</label>
        <textarea placeholder="اكتب نص السؤال..." style="min-height:60px" oninput="${fns.updateField}('${q.id}','text',this.value)">${esc(q.text)}</textarea>
      </div>
      ${imgHTML}
      ${contentHTML}
      <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin-top:12px;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px dashed var(--border)">
        <span style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">⭐ الدرجة:</span>
        <input type="number" min="1" value="${q.points}" oninput="${fns.updateField}('${q.id}','points',Math.max(1,parseInt(this.value)||1))" style="width:70px;text-align:center;font-weight:800;padding:7px 9px;font-size:13px">
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function handleQuestionImage(ctx, qid, event){
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){ toast('اختر صورة صالحة','warn'); return; }
  if(file.size > 3 * 1024 * 1024){ toast('حجم الصورة كبير (الحد 3MB)','warn'); return; }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    const arr = ctx === 'hw' ? ui.draftHwQuestions : ui.draftQuestions;
    const q = arr.find(x => x.id === qid);
    if(q){ q.image = e.target.result; q.imageName = file.name; }
    saveFormDraft(ctx === 'hw' ? 'hw' : 'exam');
    render();
    setTimeout(() => restoreFormDraft(ctx === 'hw' ? 'hw' : 'exam'), 50);
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function validateQuestions(questions){
  for(let i = 0; i < questions.length; i++){
    const q = questions[i];
    if(!q.text.trim()){ toast(`السؤال ${i+1} بدون نص`,'warn'); return false; }
    if(q.type === 'mcq'){
      const filled = q.options.filter(o => o.trim() !== '');
      if(filled.length < 2){ toast(`السؤال ${i+1} يحتاج خيارين على الأقل`,'warn'); return false; }
      if(!q.options[q.correct] || !q.options[q.correct].trim()){ toast(`حدد إجابة صحيحة للسؤال ${i+1}`,'warn'); return false; }
    }
    if(q.type === 'short' && !q.correctShort.trim()){ toast(`اكتب الإجابة الصحيحة للسؤال ${i+1}`,'warn'); return false; }
  }
  return true;
}

function isAnswerCorrect(q, ans){
  if(ans === undefined || ans === null || ans === '') return false;
  if(q.type === 'mcq') return parseInt(ans) === parseInt(q.correct);
  if(q.type === 'tf') return String(ans) === (q.correctTF ? 'true' : 'false');
  if(q.type === 'short') return String(ans).trim().toLowerCase() === (q.correctShort||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  return false;
}

/* ============ حفظ / استرجاع مسودات النماذج ============ */
function saveFormDraft(prefix){
  const fields = {
    hw: ['hwTitle','hwType','hwDue','hwMax','hwDesc','hwUrl','hwStartDate','hwStartTime','hwCloseDate','hwCloseTime','hwDuration'],
    exam: ['exTitle','exType','exDate','exMax','exNotes','exUrl','exScore','exStartDate','exStartTime','exCloseDate','exCloseTime','exDuration'],
    lib: ['libTitle','libDesc','libType','libMax','libDue','libUrl','libStartDate','libStartTime','libLocation','libDuration'],
    note: ['nTitle','nSubject','nType','nDesc','nUrl'],
    lesson: ['lTitle','lType','lDuration','lUrl','lDesc','lStartDate','lStartTime'],
    live: ['lvTitle','lvDur','lvDate','lvTime','lvLocation','lvUrl','lvDesc']
  };
  const list = fields[prefix] || [];
  const draft = {};
  list.forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el && el.type !== 'file') draft[id] = el.value;
  });
  if(!ui.libraryDraft) ui.libraryDraft = {};
  ui.libraryDraft[prefix + '_draft'] = draft;
}

function restoreFormDraft(prefix){
  const draft = (ui.libraryDraft || {})[prefix + '_draft'];
  if(!draft) return;
  Object.keys(draft).forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el && el.type !== 'file') el.value = draft[id] || '';
  });
}

/* ============ Quiz Form HTML ============ */
function quizFormHTML(item, kind){
  const isHw = kind === 'hw';
  const total = (item.questions||[]).reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);
  const displayMode = item.displayMode || 'steps';

  let timerHTML = '';
  if(item.durationMinutes && item.durationMinutes > 0){
    let remaining = getQuizRemainingSeconds(item);
    if(remaining === null){ remaining = item.durationMinutes * 60; }
    const cls = remaining <= 60 ? 'danger' : (remaining <= 180 ? 'warning' : '');
    timerHTML = `<div class="quiz-timer ${cls}">
      <span class="qt-ico">⏱️</span>
      <span>الوقت:</span>
      <span class="qt-val" id="quizTimerValue">${formatCountdownMMSS(remaining)}</span>
    </div>`;
  }

  if(displayMode === 'scroll'){
    return `
      <div class="quiz-wrap">
        <div class="quiz-head">
          <div class="qh-ico">${isHw?'📝':'📊'}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <h3>${esc(item.title)}</h3>
            <p>أجب على جميع الأسئلة ثم اضغط "تسليم"</p>
          </div>
          ${timerHTML}
          <div class="quiz-progress">⭐ ${total} درجة</div>
        </div>
        ${item.questions.map((q,i) => quizQuestionHTML(q, i)).join('')}
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px;padding-top:14px;border-top:2px dashed var(--border);margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="${isHw?'cancelHwQuiz()':'cancelQuiz()'}">إلغاء</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="${isHw?`submitHwQuiz('${item.id}')`:`submitQuiz('${item.id}')`}">📤 تسليم</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }

  const stepIdx = ui.quizStepIndex || 0;
  const q = item.questions[stepIdx];
  if(!q) return '';
  const answered = getAnsweredMap(item);
  return `
    <div class="quiz-wrap">
      <div class="quiz-head">
        <div class="qh-ico">${isHw?'📝':'📊'}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <h3>${esc(item.title)}</h3>
          <p>سؤال ${stepIdx+1} من ${item.questions.length}</p>
        </div>
        ${timerHTML}
        <div class="quiz-progress">⭐ ${total} درجة</div>
      </div>
      <div class="quiz-question">
        <div class="quiz-question-head">
          <div class="quiz-question-num">${stepIdx+1}</div>
          <div class="quiz-question-text">${esc(q.text)}</div>
          <span class="quiz-q-badge">${q.points||1} درجة</span>
        </div>
        ${q.image ? `<div class="quiz-question-image"><img src="${esc(q.image)}" alt="صورة السؤال"></div>` : ''}
        ${q.type === 'mcq' ? `
          <div class="quiz-options">
            ${q.options.map((opt, i) => `
              <label class="quiz-opt" onclick="markSelected(this)">
                <input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="${i}">
                <span class="qo-circle"></span>
                <span class="qo-label">${esc(opt)}</span>
              </label>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        ` : q.type === 'tf' ? `
          <div class="quiz-tf-row">
            <label class="quiz-tf-opt" onclick="markSelectedTF(this)">
              <input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="true" style="display:none">✅ صح
            </label>
            <label class="quiz-tf-opt" onclick="markSelectedTF(this)">
              <input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="false" style="display:none">❌ خطأ
            </label>
          </div>
        ` : `
          <div class="quiz-short-input">
            <input type="text" id="ans_${q.id}" placeholder="اكتب إجابتك...">
          </div>
        `}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding-top:14px;border-top:2px dashed var(--border);margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="${isHw?'cancelHwQuiz()':'cancelQuiz()'}">إلغاء</button>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          ${item.questions.map((_, i) => `<div style="width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:50%;background:${i===stepIdx?'linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary))':(answered[i]?'var(--ok-l)':'#e2e8f0')};color:${i===stepIdx?'#fff':(answered[i]?'#15803d':'#64748b')};display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:900;cursor:pointer" onclick="goQuizStep(${i})">${i+1}</div>`).join('')}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          ${stepIdx > 0 ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="prevQuizStep()">⬅ السابق</button>` : ''}
          ${stepIdx < item.questions.length - 1
            ? `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="nextQuizStep()">التالي ➡</button>`
            : `<button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="${isHw?`submitHwQuiz('${item.id}')`:`submitQuiz('${item.id}')`}">📤 تسليم</button>`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function getAnsweredMap(item){
  const map = {};
  (item.questions||[]).forEach((q, i) => {
    if(q.type === 'mcq' || q.type === 'tf'){
      const c = document.querySelector(`input[name="q_${q.id}"]:checked`);
      map[i] = !!c;
    } else {
      const inp = document.getElementById('ans_' + q.id);
      map[i] = !!(inp && inp.value.trim());
    }
  });
  return map;
}

function quizQuestionHTML(q, idx){
  const typeBadge = q.type === 'mcq' ? 'اختيار' : q.type === 'tf' ? 'صح/خطأ' : 'إجابة قصيرة';
  let inputHTML = '';
  if(q.type === 'mcq'){
    inputHTML = `<div class="quiz-options">${q.options.map((opt, i) => `
      <label class="quiz-opt" onclick="markSelected(this)">
        <input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="${i}">
        <span class="qo-circle"></span>
        <span class="qo-label">${esc(opt)}</span>
      </label>`).join('')}</div>`;
  } else if(q.type === 'tf'){
    inputHTML = `<div class="quiz-tf-row">
      <label class="quiz-tf-opt" onclick="markSelectedTF(this)"><input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="true" style="display:none">✅ صح</label>
      <label class="quiz-tf-opt" onclick="markSelectedTF(this)"><input type="radio" name="q_${q.id}" value="false" style="display:none">❌ خطأ</label>
    </div>`;
  } else {
    inputHTML = `<div class="quiz-short-input"><input type="text" id="ans_${q.id}" placeholder="اكتب إجابتك..."></div>`;
  }
  return `
    <div class="quiz-question">
      <div class="quiz-question-head">
        <div class="quiz-question-num">${idx+1}</div>
        <div class="quiz-question-text">${esc(q.text)}</div>
        <span class="quiz-q-badge">${typeBadge} • ${q.points||1} د</span>
      </div>
      ${q.image ? `<div class="quiz-question-image"><img src="${esc(q.image)}" alt="صورة السؤال"></div>` : ''}
      ${inputHTML}
    </div>`;
}

function markSelected(el){
  const parent = el.parentElement;
  if(parent){ parent.querySelectorAll('.quiz-opt, .quiz-tf-opt').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('selected')); }
  el.classList.add('selected');
}
function markSelectedTF(el){
  const parent = el.parentElement;
  if(parent){ parent.querySelectorAll('.quiz-opt, .quiz-tf-opt').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('selected')); }
  el.classList.add('selected');
  const radio = el.querySelector('input[type="radio"]');
  if(radio) radio.checked = true;
}

function goQuizStep(i){
  ui.quizStepIndex = i;
  render();
  setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.quiz-wrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'}), 60);
}
function nextQuizStep(){
  ui.quizStepIndex = (ui.quizStepIndex || 0) + 1;
  render();
  setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.quiz-wrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'}), 60);
}
function prevQuizStep(){
  ui.quizStepIndex = Math.max(0, (ui.quizStepIndex || 0) - 1);
  render();
  setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.quiz-wrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'}), 60);
}

function startHwQuiz(hwId){
  ui.quizHwId = hwId;
  ui.quizStepIndex = 0;
  window._quizAnswers = {};
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
  const h = s ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId) : null;
  if(h) setQuizStartedAtIfNeeded(hwId, h.durationMinutes);
  render();
  setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.quiz-wrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'}), 100);
}
function cancelHwQuiz(){ ui.quizHwId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; window._quizAnswers = {}; render(); }

function startExam(examId){
  ui.quizExamId = examId;
  ui.quizStepIndex = 0;
  window._quizAnswers = {};
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
  const e = s ? s.exams.find(x => x.id === examId) : null;
  if(e) setQuizStartedAtIfNeeded(examId, e.durationMinutes);
  render();
  setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.quiz-wrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'}), 100);
}
function cancelQuiz(){ ui.quizExamId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; window._quizAnswers = {}; render(); }

function submitHwQuiz(hwId){
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken); if(!s) return;
  const hw = s.homework.find(h => h.id === hwId); if(!hw) return;
  if(hw.submitted){ toast('تم التسليم بالفعل','warn'); return; }
  if(isItemClosed(hw)){ toast('انتهى وقت الواجب','warn'); return; }
  const answers = {};
  let missing = 0;
  hw.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(q.type === 'mcq' || q.type === 'tf'){
      const c = document.querySelector(`input[name="q_${q.id}"]:checked`);
      if(c) answers[q.id] = c.value;
      else if(window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id] !== undefined) answers[q.id] = window._quizAnswers[q.id];
      else missing++;
    } else {
      const inp = document.getElementById('ans_' + q.id);
      const v = inp ? inp.value.trim() : ((window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id]) || '');
      answers[q.id] = v;
      if(!v) missing++;
    }
  });
  if(missing > 0 && !confirm(`لديك ${missing} سؤال بدون إجابة. متابعة؟`)) return;
  let earned = 0, total = 0;
  hw.questions.forEach(q => {
    total += q.points || 1;
    if(isAnswerCorrect(q, answers[q.id])) earned += q.points || 1;
  });
  hw.answers = answers; hw.submitted = true; hw.submittedAt = Date.now();
  hw.score = earned; hw.total = total;
  hw.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  createAutoEvaluation(s.id, {
    type: 'homework',
    itemId: hw.id,
    itemTitle: hw.title,
    subject: hw.subject || '',
    score: earned,
    total: total,
    date: todayISO()
  });
  logStudentActivity(s.id, 'homework', 'إنجاز واجب: ' + hw.title, `${earned}/${total}`);
  logStudentProgress(s.id, 'homework', hw, earned, total);
  clearQuizStartedAt(hw.id);
  const pct = total ? Math.round((earned / total) * 100) : 0;
  ui.quizHwId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; window._quizAnswers = {};
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم التسليم — درجتك: ${earned}/${total} (${pct}%)`, pct >= 50 ? 'gold' : 'warn');
  playSound(pct >= 50 ? 'success' : 'warn');
}

function submitQuiz(examId){
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken); if(!s) return;
  const exam = s.exams.find(e => e.id === examId); if(!exam) return;
  if(exam.submitted){ toast('تم التسليم بالفعل','warn'); return; }
  if(isItemClosed(exam)){ toast('انتهى وقت الاختبار','warn'); return; }
  const answers = {};
  let missing = 0;
  exam.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(q.type === 'mcq' || q.type === 'tf'){
      const c = document.querySelector(`input[name="q_${q.id}"]:checked`);
      if(c) answers[q.id] = c.value;
      else if(window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id] !== undefined) answers[q.id] = window._quizAnswers[q.id];
      else missing++;
    } else {
      const inp = document.getElementById('ans_' + q.id);
      const v = inp ? inp.value.trim() : ((window._quizAnswers && window._quizAnswers[q.id]) || '');
      answers[q.id] = v;
      if(!v) missing++;
    }
  });
  if(missing > 0 && !confirm(`لديك ${missing} سؤال بدون إجابة. متابعة؟`)) return;
  let earned = 0, total = 0;
  exam.questions.forEach(q => {
    total += q.points || 1;
    if(isAnswerCorrect(q, answers[q.id])) earned += q.points || 1;
  });
  exam.answers = answers; exam.submitted = true; exam.submittedAt = Date.now();
  exam.score = earned; exam.total = total;
  exam.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  createAutoEvaluation(s.id, {
    type: 'exam',
    itemId: exam.id,
    itemTitle: exam.title,
    subject: exam.subject || '',
    score: earned,
    total: total,
    date: todayISO()
  });
  logStudentActivity(s.id, 'exam', 'إنجاز اختبار: ' + exam.title, `${earned}/${total}`);
  logStudentProgress(s.id, 'exam', exam, earned, total);
  clearQuizStartedAt(exam.id);
  const pct = total ? Math.round((earned / total) * 100) : 0;
  ui.quizExamId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; window._quizAnswers = {};
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم التسليم — درجتك: ${earned}/${total} (${pct}%)`, pct >= 50 ? 'gold' : 'warn');
  playSound(pct >= 50 ? 'success' : 'warn');
}

/* ============ مراجعة الأسئلة بعد التسليم ============ */
function openQuizReview(studentId, examId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  const e = s ? s.exams.find(x => x.id === examId) : null;
  if(!e) return;
  openReviewModal(e, e.title);
}
function openHwReview(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  const h = s ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId) : null;
  if(!h) return;
  openReviewModal(h, h.title);
}
function openReviewModal(item, title){
  const pct = item.total ? Math.round((item.score / item.total) * 100) : 0;
  const correctCount = (item.questions||[]).filter(q => isAnswerCorrect(q, item.answers[q.id])).length;
  const wrongCount = (item.questions||[]).length - correctCount;
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title">👁 مراجعة إجابات — ${esc(title)}</div>
    <div class="quiz-summary-box ${pct < 50 ? 'fail' : ''}" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <span class="qsb-ico">${pct >= 50 ? '🎉' : '💪'}</span>
      <div class="qsb-score">${item.score} / ${item.total}</div>
      <div class="qsb-pct">${pct}%</div>
      <div class="qsb-label">${pct >= 50 ? 'نتيجة جيدة' : 'تحتاج مراجعة'}</div>
      <div class="quiz-summary-stats">
        <div class="quiz-summary-stat correct"><div class="qss-num">${correctCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✓ صحيحة</div></div>
        <div class="quiz-summary-stat wrong"><div class="qss-num">${wrongCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✗ خاطئة</div></div>
        <div class="quiz-summary-stat empty"><div class="qss-num">${(item.questions||[]).length}</div><div class="qss-lbl">📝 إجمالي</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding-inline-end:5px">
      ${item.questions.map((q,i) => reviewQuestionHTML(q, i, item.answers[q.id])).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function reviewQuestionHTML(q, idx, studentAns){
  const correct = isAnswerCorrect(q, studentAns);
  let bodyHTML = '';
  if(q.type === 'mcq'){
    bodyHTML = `<div class="quiz-options">${q.options.map((opt, i) => {
      const isCorrect = i === q.correct;
      const isChosen = parseInt(studentAns) === i;
      let cls = '';
      if(isCorrect) cls = 'correct-ans';
      else if(isChosen) cls = 'wrong-ans';
      return `<div class="quiz-opt ${cls}">
        <span class="qo-circle"></span>
        <span class="qo-label">${esc(opt)}</span>
        ${isCorrect ? '<span class="badge b-ok" style="font-size:10px">✓ صحيحة</span>' : ''}
        ${!isCorrect && isChosen ? '<span class="badge b-bad" style="font-size:10px">✗ إجابتك</span>' : ''}
      </div>`;
    }).join('')}</div>`;
  } else if(q.type === 'tf'){
    const correctAns = q.correctTF;
    const chosen = studentAns === 'true' ? true : studentAns === 'false' ? false : null;
    bodyHTML = `<div class="quiz-tf-row">
      <div class="quiz-tf-opt ${correctAns===true?'correct-ans':(chosen===true?'wrong-ans':'')}">✅ صح ${correctAns===true?'✓':(chosen===true?'✗':'')}</div>
      <div class="quiz-tf-opt ${correctAns===false?'correct-ans':(chosen===false?'wrong-ans':'')}">❌ خطأ ${correctAns===false?'✓':(chosen===false?'✗':'')}</div>
    </div>`;
  } else {
    bodyHTML = `<div class="quiz-short-input">
      <div style="padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:7px">
        <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11.5px">إجابتك:</span> <span style="color:${correct?'#15803d':'#b91c1c'}">${esc(studentAns) || '<i style="color:#94a3b8">— فارغ —</i>'}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;background:#f0fdf4;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:#15803d">
        <span style="font-size:11.5px">الإجابة الصحيحة:</span> ${esc(q.correctShort)}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }
  return `
    <div class="quiz-question ${correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}">
      <div class="quiz-question-head">
        <div class="quiz-question-num">${idx+1}</div>
        <div class="quiz-question-text">${esc(q.text)}</div>
        <span class="quiz-q-badge" style="background:${correct?'var(--ok-l)':'var(--danger-l)'};color:${correct?'#15803d':'#b91c1c'}">${correct?'✓ صحيحة':'✗ خاطئة'}</span>
      </div>
      ${q.image ? `<div class="quiz-question-image"><img src="${esc(q.image)}" alt="صورة السؤال"></div>` : ''}
      ${bodyHTML}
    </div>`;
}

/* ============ Preview Questions ============ */
function openPreviewQuestions(studentId, examId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  const e = s ? s.exams.find(x => x.id === examId) : null;
  if(!e) return;
  openPreviewQuestionsFromArray(e.questions || [], e.title);
}
function openPreviewHwQuestions(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  const h = s ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId) : null;
  if(!h) return;
  openPreviewQuestionsFromArray(h.questions || [], h.title);
}
function openPreviewQuestionsFromArray(qs, title){
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title">👁 معاينة الأسئلة — ${esc(title)}</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${qs.length} سؤال • إجمالي الدرجات: ${qs.reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0)}</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding-inline-end:5px">
      ${qs.map((q,i) => `
        <div class="quiz-question">
          <div class="quiz-question-head">
            <div class="quiz-question-num">${i+1}</div>
            <div class="quiz-question-text">${esc(q.text) || '<i style="color:#94a3b8">— بدون نص —</i>'}</div>
            <span class="quiz-q-badge">${q.points||1} درجة</span>
          </div>
          ${q.image ? `<div class="quiz-question-image"><img src="${esc(q.image)}" alt="صورة السؤال"></div>` : ''}
          ${q.type === 'mcq' ? `<div class="quiz-options">${q.options.map((o,idx) => `<div class="quiz-opt ${idx===q.correct?'correct-ans':''}"><span class="qo-circle"></span><span class="qo-label">${esc(o)}</span>${idx===q.correct ? '<span class="badge b-ok" style="font-size:10px">صحيحة</span>' : ''}</div>`).join('')}</div>` :
            q.type === 'tf' ? `<div class="quiz-tf-row"><div class="quiz-tf-opt ${q.correctTF===true?'correct-ans':''}">✅ صح</div><div class="quiz-tf-opt ${q.correctTF===false?'correct-ans':''}">❌ خطأ</div></div>` :
            `<div class="quiz-short-input"><div style="background:#f0fdf4;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:11px;padding:11px 13px;font-weight:700;color:#15803d;font-size:13.5px">✏️ ${esc(q.correctShort)}</div></div>`}
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
    </div>`);
}

/* ============ إعادة تعيين التسليم من المعلم ============ */
function teacherResetHw(studentId, hwId){
  if(!confirm('إعادة تعيين حل الواجب ليتمكن الطالب من الحل مرة أخرى؟')) return;
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const h = s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId);
  if(!h) return;
  h.submitted = false; h.answers = {}; h.score = ''; h.submittedAt = null;
  h.closedAt = null; h.closedReason = ''; h.autoSubmitted = false;
  clearQuizStartedAt(hwId);
  h.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `🔄 أعاد المعلم فتح الواجب "${h.title}" — يمكنك الحل من جديد.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم إعادة التعيين — يمكن للطالب الحل الآن','gold');
  playSound('ok');
}

function teacherResetExam(studentId, examId){
  if(!confirm('إعادة تعيين حل الاختبار ليتمكن الطالب من الحل مرة أخرى؟')) return;
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const e = s.exams.find(x => x.id === examId);
  if(!e) return;
  e.submitted = false; e.answers = {}; e.score = ''; e.submittedAt = null;
  e.closedAt = null; e.closedReason = ''; e.autoSubmitted = false;
  clearQuizStartedAt(examId);
  e.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `🔄 أعاد المعلم فتح الاختبار "${e.title}" — يمكنك الحل من جديد.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم إعادة التعيين','gold');
  playSound('ok');
}

function teacherQuickReopen(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s){ toast('الطالب غير موجود','err'); return; }
  const item = kind === 'homework'
    ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId)
    : s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if(!item){ toast('العنصر غير موجود','err'); return; }
  if(!isItemClosed(item)){ toast('هذا العنصر مفتوح بالفعل ✓','warn'); return; }

  const reasonLabel = item.closedReason === 'timeout' ? 'انتهاء المدة الزمنية'
    : item.closedReason === 'end_date' ? 'انتهاء تاريخ الإغلاق'
    : item.closedReason === 'manual_close' ? 'إغلاق يدوي'
    : 'إغلاق';
  const wasSolved = isItemSolved(item);
  const kindLabel = kind === 'homework' ? 'الواجب' : 'الاختبار';

  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--primary-d)">🔓 إعادة فتح ${kindLabel}</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">"${esc(item.title)}" — للطالب ${esc(s.name)}</div>

    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 14px;margin-bottom:14px;font-size:12.5px;color:#78350f;font-weight:700;line-height:1.8">
      <div>📌 <b>سبب الإغلاق:</b> ${reasonLabel}</div>
      <div style="margin-top:4px">📊 <b>حالة الحل:</b> ${wasSolved ? '<span style="color:#15803d">✅ تم الحل</span>' : '<span style="color:#b91c1c">❌ لم يتم الحل</span>'}</div>
    </div>

    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <label>نوع إعادة الفتح *</label>
      <select id="reopenAction" style="font-weight:800">
        <option value="reopen_fresh">🔄 إعادة فتح من الصفر (حذف الإجابات السابقة)</option>
        <option value="reopen_keep">🔓 إعادة فتح مع الاحتفاظ بالإجابات</option>
        <option value="extend">⏰ تمديد وقت الإغلاق فقط</option>
      </select>
    </div>

    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <div class="field">
        <label>📅 تاريخ الإغلاق الجديد *</label>
        <input type="date" id="reopenCloseDate" value="${todayISO()}" min="${todayISO()}">
      </div>
      <div class="field">
        <label>🕐 وقت الإغلاق</label>
        <input type="time" id="reopenCloseTime" value="23:59">
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <label>⏱️ المدة الزمنية (بالدقائق) — اختياري</label>
      <input type="number" id="reopenDuration" value="${item.durationMinutes || 0}" min="0" max="600" inputmode="numeric">
      <span class="hint">0 = بدون حد زمني</span>
    </div>

    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="executeQuickReopen('${studentId}','${kind}','${itemId}')">🔓 تأكيد</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function executeQuickReopen(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s){ toast('الطالب غير موجود','err'); return; }
  const item = kind === 'homework'
    ? s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId)
    : s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if(!item){ toast('العنصر غير موجود','err'); return; }

  const action = val('reopenAction') || 'reopen_fresh';
  const newCloseDate = val('reopenCloseDate');
  const newCloseTime = val('reopenCloseTime') || '23:59';
  const newDuration = Math.max(0, parseInt(val('reopenDuration')) || 0);

  if(!newCloseDate){ toast('حدد تاريخ الإغلاق','warn'); return; }
  const newCloseAt = combineToTimestamp(newCloseDate, newCloseTime);
  if(!newCloseAt){ toast('تاريخ غير صالح','warn'); return; }
  if(newCloseAt <= Date.now()){ toast('التاريخ يجب أن يكون في المستقبل','warn'); return; }

  item.closedAt = null;
  item.closedReason = '';
  item.autoSubmitted = false;
  item.closeDate = newCloseDate;
  item.closeTime = newCloseTime;
  item.closeAt = newCloseAt;
  item.durationMinutes = newDuration;

  if(action === 'reopen_fresh'){
    if(item.type === 'questions'){
      item.submitted = false;
      item.answers = {};
      item.score = '';
      item.submittedAt = null;
    } else if(item.type === 'link'){
      item.submission = null;
      item.approved = false;
      item.rejected = false;
      item.score = '';
      item.total = item.maxScore || '';
      item.submittedAt = null;
    } else {
      item.score = '';
    }
  }

  clearQuizStartedAt(itemId);
  item.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();

  if(!s.notifications) s.notifications = [];
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `🔓 أعاد المعلم فتح "${item.title}" — الموعد الجديد: ${fmtDateWithDay(newCloseDate)}${newCloseTime ? ' الساعة ' + fmtTime12(newCloseTime) : ''}${newDuration > 0 ? ' — المدة: ' + newDuration + ' دقيقة' : ''}`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });

  logStudentActivity(s.id, kind === 'homework' ? 'homework' : 'exam',
    `أعاد المعلم فتح ${kind === 'homework' ? 'واجب' : 'اختبار'}: "${item.title}"`,
    `الموعد الجديد: ${fmtDateWithDay(newCloseDate)}`);

  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast(`تم إعادة فتح "${item.title}" ✓`, 'gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

/* ============ لوحة المعلم الرئيسية ============ */
function viewTeacher(){
  if(ui.openStudentId){
    const s = getStudent(ui.openStudentId);
    if(!s){ ui.openStudentId = null; return viewTeacher(); }
    return detailTeacher(s);
  }
  const totalStudents = db.students.length;
  const activeStudents = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length;
  const pendingPayments = db.students.filter(s => s.payment && s.payment.status === 'pending').length;
  const pendingStudents = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'pending').length;
  const totalLessons = db.lessons.length;
  const totalNotes = db.notes.length;
  const totalHW = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.homework.length,0);
  const totalEx = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.exams.length,0);
  const totalLectures = db.liveSessions.length;
  const totalEvals = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.length,0);
  const totalAutoEvals = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.autoEvaluations||[]).length,0);
  const totalNotifs = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.notifications.length,0);
  const totalCerts = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.certificates.length,0);
  const totalWA = (db.waLog||[]).length;
  const totalCompletedCycles = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+((s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles.length : 0),0);
  const totalAutoEvalPending = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+getUnsolvedClosedItems(s).length,0);

  const tabs = [
    ['students','👥 الطلاب'],
    ['homework','📝 الواجبات'],
    ['exams','📊 الاختبارات'],
    ['evals','🎯 التقييمات'],
    ['schedule','📅 الجدول'],
    ['overview','📈 النظرة العامة']
  ];

  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(46)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          لوحة المعلم
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">أ. ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)} — اللغة العربية</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()" title="اضغط لإعادة الاتصال"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
      ${soundToggleHTML()}
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="openRestoreModal()" title="استرداد نسخة">♻️</button>
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="backup()" title="تنزيل نسخة">💾</button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="logout()">خروج</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  ${pendingStudents > 0 ? `
    <div class="notif-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d;cursor:pointer" onclick="go('pendingAccounts')">
      <span class="ico-b">⏳</span>
      <div class="txt-b" style="color:#78350f">لديك <b>${pendingStudents}</b> حساب بانتظار التفعيل — <u>اضغط للمراجعة</u></div>
    </div>
  ` : ''}

  ${pendingPayments > 0 ? `
    <div class="notif-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d;cursor:pointer" onclick="go('pendingAccounts')">
      <span class="ico-b">💰</span>
      <div class="txt-b" style="color:#78350f">لديك <b>${pendingPayments}</b> طلب دفع بانتظار المراجعة</div>
    </div>` : ''}

  ${totalAutoEvalPending > 0 ? `
    <div class="notif-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fee2e2,#fef2f2);border-color:#fca5a5;cursor:pointer" onclick="openAutoCollectReportModal()">
      <span class="ico-b">❌</span>

    </div>` : ''}

  <div class="big-actions-grid anim-in">

      <div class="bab-body">

      </div>
      <div class="bab-badge">${totalEvals}</div>
    </button>
    <button class="big-action-btn bab-library" onclick="openLibraryModal()">
      <div class="bab-ico">📚</div>
      <div class="bab-body">
        <div class="bab-title">المكتبة الجماعية</div>
        <div class="bab-sub">إضافة وتوزيع للطلاب / الصفوف / المحددين</div>
      </div>
      <div class="bab-badge">${db.library.homework.length + db.library.exams.length}</div>
    </button>
    <button class="big-action-btn bab-reports" onclick="openReportsCenterModal()">
      <div class="bab-ico">📄</div>
      <div class="bab-body">
        <div class="bab-title">مركز التقارير</div>
        <div class="bab-sub">تقارير شاملة قابلة للطباعة والحفظ كصور</div>
      </div>
      <div class="bab-badge">📊</div>
    </button>
    <button class="big-action-btn bab-achievements" onclick="openAchievementsViewer()">
      <div class="bab-ico">🏆</div>
      <div class="bab-body">
        <div class="bab-title">التحصيل الدراسي</div>

        <div class="bab-sub">سجل الدورات المؤرشفة لكل طالب</div>
      </div>
      <div class="bab-badge">${totalCompletedCycles}</div>
    </button>
    <button class="big-action-btn bab-notifs" onclick="go('notificationsCenter')">
      <div class="bab-ico">🔔</div>
      <div class="bab-body">
        <div class="bab-title">مركز الإشعارات</div>
        <div class="bab-sub">إرسال جماعي • حذف الكل • لطالب محدد</div>

      <div class="bab-badge">${totalNotifs}</div>

        <div class="bab-sub">للطلاب / الأولياء / الصفوف / المحددين</div>
      </div>
      <div class="bab-badge">${totalWA}</div>
    </button>
    <button class="big-action-btn" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0891b2,#0e7490)" onclick="openWAArchiveModal()">
      <div class="bab-ico">📨</div>

      <div class="bab-badge">${totalWA}</div>
    </button>
  </div>

  ${renderAutoCollectPanel()}

  <div class="grid g4 anim-in" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="document.getElementById('studentsList')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'})">
      <span class="ico-stat">👥</span><div class="num">${totalStudents}</div><div class="lbl">إجمالي الطلاب</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat ok clickable" onclick="openAllActiveAccountsModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">✅</span><div class="num">${activeStudents}</div><div class="lbl">حسابات مفعّلة</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat gold clickable" onclick="go('pendingAccounts')">
      <span class="ico-stat">⏳</span><div class="num">${pendingStudents}</div><div class="lbl">بانتظار التفعيل</div>

    <div class="stat purple clickable" onclick="ui.screen='teacherLessons';render()">
      <span class="ico-stat">📹</span><div class="num">${totalLessons}</div><div class="lbl">دروس</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat info clickable" onclick="ui.screen='teacherNotes';render()">
      <span class="ico-stat">📄</span><div class="num">${totalNotes}</div><div class="lbl">مذكرات</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fef2f2,#fee2e2);border-color:#fecaca" onclick="ui.screen='teacherLive';render()">
      <span class="ico-stat">🎥</span>
      <div class="num" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f87171,#dc2626);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${totalLectures}</div>
      <div class="lbl">محاضرات أونلاين</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="openAllHomeworkModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">📝</span><div class="num">${totalHW}</div><div class="lbl">واجبات مرسلة</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="openAllExamsModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">📊</span><div class="num">${totalEx}</div><div class="lbl">اختبارات مرسلة</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="openEvalViewerModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">🎯</span><div class="num">${totalEvals}</div><div class="lbl">تقييمات</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable purple" onclick="openAutoCollectReportModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">📈</span><div class="num">${totalAutoEvals}</div><div class="lbl">تقييم تلقائي</div>

    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="openAllNotifsModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">🔔</span><div class="num">${totalNotifs}</div><div class="lbl">إشعارات</div>

    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="openAllCertificatesModal()">
      <span class="ico-stat">🎓</span><div class="num">${totalCerts}</div><div class="lbl">شهادات</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat clickable gold" onclick="openCompletedCyclesCenter()">
      <span class="ico-stat">🏆</span><div class="num">${totalCompletedCycles}</div><div class="lbl">دورات مكتملة</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="tabs" style="margin-top:16px">
    ${tabs.map(([k,l]) => `<button class="${ui.teacherHomeTab===k?'active':''}" onclick="ui.teacherHomeTab='${k}';render()">${l}</button>`).join('')}
    <button onclick="go('teacherMyData')">📋 بياناتي</button>
    <button onclick="ui.screen='teacherLessons';render()">📹 إدارة الدروس</button>
    <button onclick="ui.screen='teacherNotes';render()">📄 إدارة المذكرات</button>
    <button onclick="ui.screen='teacherLive';render()">🎥 محاضرات أونلاين</button>
    <button onclick="ui.screen='teacherCertificates';render()">🎓 الشهادات الجماعية</button>
    <button onclick="ui.screen='teacherStats';render()">📊 إحصائيات</button>
    <button onclick="openPricesModal()">💵 الأسعار</button>
    <button onclick="openLibraryModal()">📚 المكتبة</button>
    <button onclick="openWAArchiveModal()">📨 الأرشيف</button>
    <button onclick="openTeacherSettingsModal()">⚙️ الإعدادات</button>
  </div>

  <div>${renderTeacherHomeTab()}</div>`;
}

function renderTeacherHomeTab(){
  switch(ui.teacherHomeTab){
    case 'students': return teacherTabStudents();
    case 'homework': return teacherTabHomework();
    case 'exams': return teacherTabExams();
    case 'evals': return teacherTabEvals();
    case 'schedule': return teacherTabSchedule();
    case 'overview': return teacherTabOverview();
  }
  return teacherTabStudents();
}

/* ============ لوحة التحصيل التلقائي ============ */
function getFilteredAutoCollectStudents(){
  const f = ui.autoCollectFilter;
  let list = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active').map(s => ({
    student: s,
    stats: computeStudentStats(s),
    category: getStudentPerformanceCategory(computeStudentStats(s).overall || 0)
  }));

  if(f.search){
    const q = f.search.toLowerCase();
    list = list.filter(x =>
      x.student.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      x.student.phone.includes(q) ||
      (x.student.parentPhone||'').includes(q)
    );
  }
  if(f.stage){
    list = list.filter(x => x.student.stage === f.stage);
  }
  if(f.category && f.category !== 'all'){
    list = list.filter(x => x.category.key === f.category);
  }

  const sortBy = f.sortBy || 'overall';
  if(sortBy === 'overall') list.sort((a, b) => (b.stats.overall || 0) - (a.stats.overall || 0));
  else if(sortBy === 'name') list.sort((a, b) => a.student.name.localeCompare(b.student.name, 'ar'));
  else if(sortBy === 'recent') list.sort((a, b) => (b.student.enrolledAt || 0) - (a.student.enrolledAt || 0));

  return list;
}

function renderAutoCollectPanel(){
  if(!db.settings.showAutoCollect) return '';
  if(!ui.autoCollectVisible) return '';

  const allStudents = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  if(!allStudents.length) return '';

  const list = getFilteredAutoCollectStudents();
  const f = ui.autoCollectFilter;

  const categoryCounts = {
    all: allStudents.length,
    perfect: 0, excellent: 0, good: 0, fair: 0, poor: 0
  };
  allStudents.forEach(s => {
    const cat = getStudentPerformanceCategory(computeStudentStats(s).overall || 0);
    categoryCounts[cat.key]++;
  });

  const categoryChips = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'الكل', icon: '👥', color: '#64748b' },
    { key: 'perfect', label: 'متميز', icon: '⭐', color: '#d97706' },
    { key: 'excellent', label: 'ممتاز', icon: '🏆', color: '#16a34a' },
    { key: 'good', label: 'جيد جدًا', icon: '✅', color: '#2563eb' },
    { key: 'fair', label: 'مقبول', icon: '⚠️', color: '#d97706' },
    { key: 'poor', label: 'يحتاج تحسين', icon: '🚨', color: '#dc2626' }
  ];

  return `
  <div class="auto-progress-hero anim-in" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="aph-head">
      <div class="aph-ico">📈</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div class="aph-title">التحصيل التلقائي — مستوى تقدم الطلاب</div>
        <div class="aph-sub">نظرة لحظية على أداء جميع الطلاب المفعّلين مع تصنيفات احترافية</div>
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="ui.autoCollectVisible=false;render()" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);color:#fff;border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.3)">👁️ إخفاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="openAutoCollectReportModal()">📄 تقرير شامل</button>
    </div>

    <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.1);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);border-radius:14px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:14px">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم أو الهاتف..." value="${esc(f.search)}" oninput="ui.autoCollectFilter.search=this.value;render()" style="flex:1;min-width:180px;padding:9px 14px;font-size:13px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);border-radius:10px;border:none">
        <select onchange="ui.autoCollectFilter.stage=this.value;render()" style="min-width:140px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);border-radius:10px;border:none;font-weight:700">
          <option value="">كل الصفوف</option>
          ${getAllStages().map(st => `<option ${f.stage===st?'selected':''}>${esc(st)}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
        <select onchange="ui.autoCollectFilter.sortBy=this.value;render()" style="min-width:140px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);border-radius:10px;border:none;font-weight:700">
          <option value="overall" ${f.sortBy==='overall'?'selected':''}>🔽 الأعلى تقدمًا</option>
          <option value="name" ${f.sortBy==='name'?'selected':''}>🔤 أبجدي</option>
          <option value="recent" ${f.sortBy==='recent'?'selected':''}>🆕 الأحدث</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${categoryChips.map(c => `
          <button onclick="ui.autoCollectFilter.category='${c.key}';render()" style="border:1.5px solid ${f.category===c.key?'transparent':'rgba(255,255,255,.3)'};background:${f.category===c.key?c.color:'rgba(255,255,255,.15)'};color:#fff;font-family:inherit;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;padding:7px 13px;border-radius:99px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s;white-space:nowrap;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px">
            ${c.icon} ${c.label}
            <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.35);padding:1px 7px;border-radius:99px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:900">${categoryCounts[c.key] || 0}</span>
          </button>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="auto-progress-stats">
      <div class="auto-progress-stat">
        <div class="aps-num">${allStudents.length}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">👥 طلاب نشطون</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat good">
        <div class="aps-num">${categoryCounts.perfect + categoryCounts.excellent}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">⭐ متميزون</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat">
        <div class="aps-num">${list.length}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">📊 في الفلتر</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat bad">
        <div class="aps-num">${categoryCounts.poor}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">🚨 يحتاج دعم</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  ${list.length ? `
  <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px">
    ${list.slice(0, 24).map(({student: s, stats: st, category: cat}) => {
      const overall = st.overall || 0;
      const color = cat.color;
      return `
      <div onclick="openStudentDetail('${s.id}')" style="background:#fff;border:2px solid ${color}40;border-radius:16px;padding:14px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s" onmouseover="this.style.transform='translateY(-3px)';this.style.boxShadow='0 12px 30px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'" onmouseout="this.style.transform='none';this.style.boxShadow='none'">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px">
          ${avatar(s.name, 38)}
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:900;color:var(--text);line-height:1.3">${esc(s.name)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:2px">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')}</div>
          </div>
          <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:900;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;background:${color}22;color:${color}">${cat.icon} ${cat.label}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">
          <div>
            <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:900;color:${color};line-height:1">${overall}%</div>
            <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:800;margin-top:3px">التقدم العام</div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px">
            <div style="height:6px;background:#f1f5f9;border-radius:99px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:${st.hwCompletion || 0}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb);border-radius:99px"></div></div>
            <div style="height:6px;background:#f1f5f9;border-radius:99px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:${st.exAvgScore || 0}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed);border-radius:99px"></div></div>
            <div style="height:6px;background:#f1f5f9;border-radius:99px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:${st.attendanceRate || 0}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#4ade80,#16a34a);border-radius:99px"></div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `}).join('')}
  </div>
  ${list.length > 24 ? `<div style="text-align:center;padding:12px;color:var(--muted);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700">يتم عرض 24 من ${list.length} — استخدم البحث للتصفية</div>` : ''}
  ` : `
  <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:30px">
    <div style="font-size:44px;margin-bottom:12px;opacity:.5">🔍</div>
    <div style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">لا توجد نتائج مطابقة للفلاتر</div>
  </div>`}
  `;
}
/* ============ ملف الطالب الرئيسي ============ */
function detailTeacher(s){
  const unread = s.notifications.filter(n => !n.read).length;
  const pending = s.payment && s.payment.status === 'pending';
  const autoEvalsCount = (s.autoEvaluations || []).length;
  const activityCount = (s.activityLog || []).length;
  const completedCyclesCount = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles.length : 0;

  const tabs = [
    ['overview','🏠 نظرة عامة'],
    ['report','📄 التقرير'],
    ['achievements','🏆 التحصيل'],
    ['autoProgress','📈 التحصيل التلقائي'],
    ['cycles','🏆 الدورات'],
    ['payment','💰 الدفع'],
    ['lessons','📹 الدروس'],
    ['liveSessions','🎥 المحاضرات'],
    ['notes','📄 المذكرات'],
    ['homework','📝 الواجبات'],
    ['exams','📊 الاختبارات'],
    ['evals','🎯 التقييم'],
    ['schedule','📅 الجدول'],
    ['stats','📈 الأداء'],
    ['certificates','🎓 الشهادات'],
    ['activity','📊 السجل'],
    ['privateNotes','📝 ملاحظاتي'],
    ['notifs','🔔 الإشعارات'],
    ['settings','⚙️ البيانات']
  ];

  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="backToTeacherList()" title="رجوع">→</button>
      <span style="font-size:15px">ملف الطالب</span>
      <span class="sync-status"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openReportModal('${s.id}')">📄 تقرير</button>
      <button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openStudentWAArchive('${s.id}')">📨 أرشيف الطالب</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="waStudentOrParent('${s.id}')">💬 واتساب</button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteStudentConfirm('${s.id}')">🗑 حذف</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}
  <div class="stu-hero">
    ${avatar(s.name, 62)}
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
      <div class="name">${esc(s.name)}</div>
      <div class="sub">${s.stage ? '📚 ' + esc(s.stage) : ''} • 📱 ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
      <div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${(s.track||'عام') === 'بكالوريا' ? '<span class="track-tag bac">🎓 بكالوريا</span>' : '<span class="track-tag">📘 عام</span>'}
        ${s.phoneVerified ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#ede9fe;color:#6d28d9">🔐 رقم موثّق</span>' : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    ${s.status === 'active' ? '<span class="active-badge">✓ مفعّل</span>' : s.status === 'suspended' ? '<span class="suspended-badge">⛔ موقوف</span>' : '<span class="pending-badge">⏳ بانتظار</span>'}
  </div>
  ${pending ? `
    <div class="notif-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d;margin-top:14px">
      <span class="ico-b">💰</span>
      <div class="txt-b" style="color:#78350f">هذا الطالب لديه طلب دفع بانتظار المراجعة</div>
      <button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" onclick="approvePayment('${s.id}')">✓ تفعيل</button>
    </div>` : ''}
  <div class="tabs">
    ${tabs.map(([k,l]) => `<button class="${ui.detailTab===k?'active':''}" onclick="ui.detailTab='${k}';render()">${l}${k==='notifs'&&unread?`<span class="badge-dot">${unread}</span>`:''}${k==='activity'&&activityCount?`<span class="badge-dot" style="background:var(--purple);color:#fff">${Math.min(99,activityCount)}</span>`:''}${k==='cycles'&&completedCyclesCount?`<span class="badge-dot">${Math.min(99,completedCyclesCount)}</span>`:''}${k==='autoProgress'&&autoEvalsCount?`<span class="badge-dot" style="background:#a78bfa;color:#fff">${Math.min(99,autoEvalsCount)}</span>`:''}</button>`).join('')}
  </div>
  <div>${detailBodyTeacher(s)}</div>`;
}

function backToTeacherList(){ ui.openStudentId = null; render(); }

function openStudentDetail(id){
  ui.openStudentId = id;
  ui.detailTab = 'overview';
  render();
}

function detailBodyTeacher(s){
  switch(ui.detailTab){
    case 'overview': return tabOverviewTeacher(s);
    case 'report': return `<div style="text-align:center;padding:22px"><button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg" onclick="openReportModal('${s.id}')">📄 فتح التقرير الكامل</button></div>`;
    case 'achievements': return openStudentAchievementsTab(s);
    case 'autoProgress': return tabAutoProgressTeacher(s);
    case 'cycles': return tabCompletedCyclesTeacher(s);
    case 'payment': return tabPaymentTeacher(s);
    case 'lessons': return tabLessonsTeacher(s);
    case 'liveSessions': return tabLiveTeacher(s);
    case 'notes': return tabNotesTeacher(s);
    case 'homework': return tabHomeworkTeacher(s);
    case 'exams': return tabExamsTeacher(s);
    case 'evals': return tabEvalsTeacher(s);
    case 'schedule': return tabScheduleTeacher(s);
    case 'stats': return tabStatsTeacher(s);
    case 'certificates': return tabCertificatesTeacher(s);
    case 'activity': return tabActivityTeacher(s);
    case 'privateNotes': return tabPrivateNotesTeacher(s);
    case 'notifs': return tabNotifsTeacher(s);
    case 'settings': return tabSettingsTeacher(s);
  }
  return '';
}
