}

function saveStudentOverview(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const newPhone = cleanPhone(val('ovPhone'));
  if(!newPhone){ toast('رقم الهاتف مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const other = getStudentByPhone(newPhone);
  if(other && other.id !== studentId){ toast('الرقم مستخدم لطالب آخر','err'); return; }
  const newParent = cleanPhone(val('ovParent'));
  if(!newParent || newParent.length < 10){ toast('رقم ولي الأمر مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  s.name = val('ovName') || s.name;
  s.phone = newPhone;
  s.parentPhone = newParent;
  s.password = val('ovPass') || s.password;
  s.stage = val('ovStage');
  s.track = val('ovTrack');
  s.notes = val('ovNotes');
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ بيانات الطالب ✓','gold');
  playSound('success');
}

function regenerateStudentPassword(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('توليد كلمة مرور جديدة لهذا الطالب؟')) return;
  const newPass = genTempPassword();
  s.password = newPass;
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `🔑 تم تحديث كلمة مرورك من المعلم — تواصل معه للحصول عليها.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`كلمة المرور الجديدة: ${newPass}`,'gold');
  playSound('ok');
}

function sendCredentialsToStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const msg = `🎓 بيانات دخولك في منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية للغة العربية:\n\n📱 اسم الدخول: ${s.phone}\n🔑 كلمة المرور: ${s.password}\n\n🔗 رابط المنصة مرفق\n\nبالتوفيق 🌟`;
  sendWhatsApp(s.phone, msg, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: 'student', category: 'credentials', title: 'بيانات الدخول', studentId: s.id });
  toast('تم فتح واتساب','gold');
}

function readScheduleFields(prefix){
  const d = val(prefix + 'StartDate');
  const t = val(prefix + 'StartTime');
  const durationEl = document.getElementById(prefix + 'DurationMinutes');
  const closeDateEl = document.getElementById(prefix + 'CloseDate');
  const closeTimeEl = document.getElementById(prefix + 'CloseTime');

  const durationMinutes = durationEl ? Math.max(0, parseInt(durationEl.value) || 0) : 0;
  const closeDate = closeDateEl ? (closeDateEl.value || '') : '';
  const closeTime = closeTimeEl ? (closeTimeEl.value || '') : '';

  let startAt = null;
  if(d){
    startAt = combineToTimestamp(d, t || '00:00');
    if(!startAt){ toast('تاريخ الفتح غير صالح','warn'); return { error: true }; }
  }

  let closeAt = null;
  if(closeDate){
    closeAt = combineToTimestamp(closeDate, closeTime || '23:59');
    if(!closeAt){ toast('تاريخ الإغلاق غير صالح','warn'); return { error: true }; }
    if(startAt && closeAt <= startAt){ toast('تاريخ الإغلاق يجب أن يكون بعد الفتح','warn'); return { error: true }; }
  }

  return {
    startDate: d || '',
    startTime: t || '',
    startAt: startAt,
    durationMinutes: durationMinutes,
    closeDate: closeDate,
    closeTime: closeTime,
    closeAt: closeAt
  };
}

        إرسال رسالة واتساب عند الحفظ (اختياري)

function addHomework(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const title = val('hwTitle');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const sch = readScheduleFields('hw');
  if(sch.error) return;

  const base = {
    id: uid(),
    title,
    type: ui.hwType,
    subject: val('hwSubject'),
    desc: val('hwDesc'),
    due: val('hwDue'),
    maxScore: parseFloat(val('hwMax')) || 0,
    total: parseFloat(val('hwMax')) || 0,
    startDate: sch.startDate,
    startTime: sch.startTime,
    startAt: sch.startAt,
    durationMinutes: sch.durationMinutes,
    closeDate: sch.closeDate,
    closeTime: sch.closeTime,
    closeAt: sch.closeAt,
    closedAt: null,
    closedReason: '',
    autoSubmitted: false,
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    submitted: false,
    updatedAt: Date.now()
  };

  if(ui.hwType === 'questions'){
    const dmEl = document.getElementById('hwDisplayMode');
    base.displayMode = dmEl ? dmEl.value : 'steps';
    if(!ui.draftHwQuestions.length){ toast('أضف سؤالًا واحدًا على الأقل','warn'); return; }
    const valid = validateQuestions(ui.draftHwQuestions);
    if(!valid) return;
    base.questions = ui.draftHwQuestions.map(q => ({...q, id: uid()}));
    base.total = base.questions.reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);
    base.answers = {};
  }

  if(ui.hwType === 'link'){
    base.url = val('hwUrl');
    if(!base.url){ toast('الرابط مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  } else if(ui.hwType === 'pdf'){
    const pdfInput = document.getElementById('hwPdf');
    if(!pdfInput || !pdfInput.files[0]){ toast('ارفع ملف PDF أولًا','warn'); return; }
    const file = pdfInput.files[0];
    if(file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES){ toast(`حجم الملف كبير (الحد ${MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB}MB)`,'warn'); return; }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = e => {
      base.pdfData = e.target.result;
      base.pdfName = file.name;
      s.homework.push(base);
      ui.draftHwQuestions = [];
      logStudentActivity(studentId, 'homework', 'إضافة واجب جديد: ' + title, val('hwSubject') || '');
      s.updatedAt = Date.now();
      saveDB(); render();
      toast('تم حفظ الواجب ✓','gold'); playSound('success');
      if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent) sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'homework', base);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    return;
  }

  s.homework.push(base);
  ui.draftHwQuestions = [];
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'homework', 'إضافة واجب جديد: ' + title, val('hwSubject') || '');
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ الواجب ✓','gold'); playSound('success');
  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent) sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'homework', base);
}

/* ============ بطاقة واجب (للمعلم) ============ */
function hwCardTeacher(h, studentId){
  checkAutoClose(h, studentId);
  const type = h.type || 'simple';
  const typeBadge = type === 'link' ? '<span class="badge b-info">🔗 رابط</span>'
    : type === 'questions' ? '<span class="badge b-warn">📝 أسئلة</span>'
    : type === 'pdf' ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📄 PDF</span>'
    : '<span class="badge b-gray">📋 نصي</span>';
  const available = isItemAvailable(h);
  const closed = isItemClosed(h);
  const total = (h.questions||[]).reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);

  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px">${esc(h.title)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:6px">
            ${typeBadge}
            ${h.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(h.subject)}</span>` : ''}
            ${h.due ? `<span>📅 التسليم: ${fmtDateWithDay(h.due)}</span>` : ''}
            ${h.maxScore ? `<span>⭐ ${h.maxScore} درجة</span>` : ''}
            ${!available && !closed ? `<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ مغلق حتى ${fmtDateTime12(h.startDate, h.startTime)}</span>` : ''}
            ${itemMetaChipsHTML(h)}
            ${itemStatusBadgeHTML(h)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-self:center">
          <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="resendTaskWA('${studentId}','homework','${h.id}')" title="إرسال واتساب">📱</button>
          <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editTaskModal('${studentId}','homework','${h.id}')">✏️</button>
          ${closed ? `<button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" onclick="teacherQuickReopen('${studentId}','homework','${h.id}')" title="إعادة فتح">🔓</button>` : ''}
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteHomeworkConfirm('${studentId}','${h.id}')">🗑</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${h.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-bottom:10px">${esc(h.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      ${type === 'link' && h.url ? `<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(h.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(h.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🔍 فتح الرابط</button></div>` : ''}
      ${type === 'pdf' && (h.pdfData || h.pdfUrl) ? `<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openHomeworkPDFInline('${studentId}','${h.id}')">📄 عرض PDF</button></div>` : ''}
      ${type === 'questions' ? `
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
          <span class="badge b-gray">❓ ${(h.questions||[]).length} سؤال</span>
          <span class="badge b-gray">⭐ ${total} درجة</span>
          ${h.submitted ? `<span class="badge b-ok">✓ سلّم الطالب — ${h.score}/${h.total}</span>` : '<span class="badge b-warn">لم يسلّم</span>'}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="openPreviewHwQuestions('${studentId}','${h.id}')">👁 معاينة</button>
          ${h.submitted ? `
            <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="openHwReview('${studentId}','${h.id}')">📋 مراجعة الإجابات</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="teacherResetHw('${studentId}','${h.id}')">🔄 إعادة الحل</button>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
      ` : ''}
    </div>`;
}

function deleteHomeworkConfirm(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا الواجب؟')) return;
  s.homework = s.homework.filter(x => x.id !== hwId);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ تبويب الاختبارات للمعلم ============ */
function tabExamsTeacher(s){
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">➕</span> إضافة اختبار</div>
    <div class="exam-type-tabs" style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:6px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button onclick="handleExTypeChange('questions')" style="flex:1;min-width:130px;font-family:inherit;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:800;border:none;background:${ui.examType==='questions'?'linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary))':'transparent'};color:${ui.examType==='questions'?'#fff':'var(--muted)'};padding:11px 14px;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer">📝 أسئلة داخلية</button>
      <button onclick="handleExTypeChange('link')" style="flex:1;min-width:130px;font-family:inherit;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:800;border:none;background:${ui.examType==='link'?'linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary))':'transparent'};color:${ui.examType==='link'?'#fff':'var(--muted)'};padding:11px 14px;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer">🔗 رابط خارجي</button>
      <button onclick="handleExTypeChange('simple')" style="flex:1;min-width:130px;font-family:inherit;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:800;border:none;background:${ui.examType==='simple'?'linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary))':'transparent'};color:${ui.examType==='simple'?'#fff':'var(--muted)'};padding:11px 14px;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer">📊 إدخال درجة</button>
    </div>

    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="field"><label>عنوان الاختبار <span class="req">*</span></label><input id="exTitle" placeholder="مثال: اختبار النحو"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>المادة</label><input id="exSubject" placeholder="النحو - الصرف"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>التاريخ</label><input type="date" id="exDate" value="${todayISO()}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>الدرجة الكاملة</label><input type="number" id="exMax" placeholder="20" inputmode="numeric"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>ملاحظات</label><textarea id="exNotes" placeholder="تعليمات أو وصف..."></textarea></div>

    ${ui.examType === 'link' ? `
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>رابط الاختبار <span class="req">*</span></label><input id="exUrl" placeholder="https://forms.google.com/..."></div>
    ` : ''}

    ${ui.examType === 'simple' ? `
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>درجة الطالب (اختياري)</label><input type="number" id="exScore" placeholder="اتركه فارغًا إن لم تُصحّح" inputmode="numeric"></div>
    ` : ''}

    ${ui.examType === 'questions' ? examsQuestionsBuilderHTML() : ''}

    ${scheduleFieldsHTML('ex')}

    ${waSendBoxHTML()}

    <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:16px;width:100%" onclick="addExam('${s.id}')">💾 حفظ الاختبار</button>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📊</span> الاختبارات <span class="sec-sub">${s.exams.length}</span></div>
    ${s.exams.length ? s.exams.slice().reverse().map(e => examCardTeacher(e, s.id)).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد اختبارات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function examsQuestionsBuilderHTML(){
  return `
    <div style="margin-top:16px;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#eef2ff,#fff);border:1.5px solid #c7d2fe;border-radius:14px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#4338ca;margin-bottom:12px">
        <span>📝</span><span>أسئلة الاختبار (${ui.draftQuestions.length})</span>
      </div>
      ${ui.draftQuestions.map((q, i) => draftQuestionHTML(q, i, 'exam')).join('')}
      <button onclick="addDraftQuestion()" style="width:100%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#fff);border:1.5px dashed #6366f1;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:#4338ca">➕ إضافة سؤال</button>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:16px;padding:12px 14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0f9ff,#fff);border:1.5px solid #bae6fd;border-radius:12px">
      <label style="color:#0c4a6e">🎨 طريقة عرض الأسئلة للطالب</label>
      <select id="exDisplayMode" style="margin-top:6px;font-weight:800">
        <option value="steps" ${ui.examDisplayMode==='steps'?'selected':''}>🔢 سؤال بسؤال (التالي / السابق)</option>
        <option value="scroll" ${ui.examDisplayMode==='scroll'?'selected':''}>📜 سحب جميع الأسئلة</option>
      </select>
    </div>`;
}

function addExam(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const title = val('exTitle');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const sch = readScheduleFields('ex');
  if(sch.error) return;

  const base = {
    id: uid(),
    title,
    type: ui.examType,
    subject: val('exSubject'),
    date: val('exDate') || todayISO(),
    notes: val('exNotes'),
    maxScore: parseFloat(val('exMax')) || 0,
    total: parseFloat(val('exMax')) || 0,
    startDate: sch.startDate,
    startTime: sch.startTime,
    startAt: sch.startAt,
    durationMinutes: sch.durationMinutes,
    closeDate: sch.closeDate,
    closeTime: sch.closeTime,
    closeAt: sch.closeAt,
    closedAt: null,
    closedReason: '',
    autoSubmitted: false,
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    submitted: false,
    updatedAt: Date.now()
  };

  if(ui.examType === 'questions'){
    const dmEl = document.getElementById('exDisplayMode');
    base.displayMode = dmEl ? dmEl.value : 'steps';
    if(!ui.draftQuestions.length){ toast('أضف سؤالًا واحدًا على الأقل','warn'); return; }
    const valid = validateQuestions(ui.draftQuestions);
    if(!valid) return;
    base.questions = ui.draftQuestions.map(q => ({...q, id: uid()}));
    base.total = base.questions.reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);
    base.answers = {};
  } else if(ui.examType === 'link'){
    base.url = val('exUrl');
    if(!base.url){ toast('الرابط مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  } else if(ui.examType === 'simple'){
    base.score = val('exScore') !== '' ? parseFloat(val('exScore')) : '';
    if(!base.total) base.total = base.score || 0;
  }

  s.exams.push(base);
  ui.draftQuestions = [];
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'exam', 'إضافة اختبار جديد: ' + title, val('exSubject') || '');
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ الاختبار ✓','gold'); playSound('success');
  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent) sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'exam', base);
}

function examCardTeacher(e, studentId){
  checkAutoClose(e, studentId);
  const type = e.type || 'simple';
  const typeBadge = type === 'link' ? '<span class="badge b-info">🔗 رابط</span>'
    : type === 'questions' ? '<span class="badge b-warn">📝 أسئلة</span>'
    : '<span class="badge b-gray">📊 درجة</span>';
  const available = isItemAvailable(e);
  const closed = isItemClosed(e);
  const total = (e.questions||[]).reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);

  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px">${esc(e.title)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:6px">
            ${typeBadge}
            ${e.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(e.subject)}</span>` : ''}
            <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</span>
            ${e.maxScore ? `<span>⭐ ${e.maxScore} درجة</span>` : ''}
            ${!available && !closed ? `<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ مغلق حتى ${fmtDateTime12(e.startDate, e.startTime)}</span>` : ''}
            ${itemMetaChipsHTML(e)}
            ${itemStatusBadgeHTML(e)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-self:center">
          <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="resendTaskWA('${studentId}','exam','${e.id}')">📱</button>
          <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editTaskModal('${studentId}','exam','${e.id}')">✏️</button>
          ${closed ? `<button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" onclick="teacherQuickReopen('${studentId}','exams','${e.id}')" title="إعادة فتح">🔓</button>` : ''}
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteExamConfirm('${studentId}','${e.id}')">🗑</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${e.notes ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-bottom:10px">${esc(e.notes)}</div>` : ''}
      ${type === 'link' && e.url ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(e.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(e.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🔍 فتح الرابط</button>` : ''}
      ${type === 'questions' ? `
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
          <span class="badge b-gray">❓ ${(e.questions||[]).length} سؤال</span>
          <span class="badge b-gray">⭐ ${total} درجة</span>
          ${e.submitted ? `<span class="badge b-ok">✓ سلّم الطالب — ${e.score}/${e.total}</span>` : '<span class="badge b-warn">لم يسلّم</span>'}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="openPreviewQuestions('${studentId}','${e.id}')">👁 معاينة</button>
          ${e.submitted ? `
            <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="openQuizReview('${studentId}','${e.id}')">📋 مراجعة الإجابات</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="teacherResetExam('${studentId}','${e.id}')">🔄 إعادة الحل</button>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
      ` : ''}
      ${type === 'simple' && e.score !== '' && e.score !== undefined ? `<div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px;color:var(--primary-d);font-weight:800">الدرجة: ${e.score}${e.total ? ' / ' + e.total : ''}</div>` : ''}
    </div>`;
}

function deleteExamConfirm(studentId, exId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا الاختبار؟')) return;
  s.exams = s.exams.filter(x => x.id !== exId);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ تبويب التقييمات للمعلم ============ */
function tabEvalsTeacher(s){
  const sortedEvals = (s.evaluations || []).slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = combineToTimestamp(a.date) || a.createdAt || 0;
    const tb = combineToTimestamp(b.date) || b.createdAt || 0;
    return tb - ta;
  });
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎯</span> تقييم حصة</div>
    <div class="grid g3" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="field"><label>التاريخ</label><input type="date" id="evDate" value="${todayISO()}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>اسم الحصة</label><input id="evSession" placeholder="مثال: حصة النحو"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>الحضور</label>
        <select id="evAttend">${ATTEND.map(a => `<option>${a}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="field"><label>الدرجة العامة (اختياري)</label><input type="number" id="evScore" placeholder="10" min="0" max="100" inputmode="numeric"></div>

    ${draftSectionHTML('mastered','✅','المهام المتقنة','مثال: التمييز بين المفاهيم')}
    ${draftSectionHTML('notMastered','❌','المهام غير المتقنة','مثال: أخطاء في الإعراب')}
    ${draftSectionHTML('upcoming','📋','المطلوب حتى الحصة القادمة','مثال: حل تمارين ص 45')}

    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>ملاحظات عامة</label><textarea id="evNotes" placeholder="ملاحظات إضافية..."></textarea></div>

    <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:14px">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#78350f;margin-bottom:8px">🔐 كلمة اعتماد ولي الأمر لهذا الطالب</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <input type="text" id="studentApprovalPwd" value="${esc(s.parentApprovalPassword || '')}" placeholder="مثال: 1234" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;text-align:center;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:2px">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="saveStudentApprovalPwd('${s.id}')">💾 حفظ</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    ${waSendBoxHTML()}

    <div class="row" style="margin-top:16px">
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="addEval('${s.id}')">💾 حفظ التقييم</button>
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="clearDraft()">🔄 تفريغ</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📈</span> سجل التقييمات <span class="sec-sub">${sortedEvals.length}</span></div>
    ${sortedEvals.length ? sortedEvals.map(e => evalCardTeacher(e, s.id)).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد تقييمات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function saveStudentApprovalPwd(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const pwd = normalizeDigits(val('studentApprovalPwd'));
  s.parentApprovalPassword = pwd;
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ كلمة الاعتماد للطالب ✓ — تُطبق على التقييمات القادمة','gold');
}

function draftSectionHTML(key, icon, title, placeholder){
  const cls = key === 'mastered' ? '#f0fdf4' : key === 'notMastered' ? '#fef2f2' : '#fffbeb';
  const border = key === 'mastered' ? '#bbf7d0' : key === 'notMastered' ? '#fecaca' : '#fde68a';
  const color = key === 'mastered' ? '#166534' : key === 'notMastered' ? '#991b1b' : '#92400e';
  return `
  <div style="border-radius:16px;padding:16px;border:1.5px solid ${border};background:${cls};margin-top:12px">
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;color:${color}">
      <span style="width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:9px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:15px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.7)">${icon}</span>
      <span>${title}</span>
      <span id="draftCount_${key}" style="margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 9px;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.8)">${ui.draft[key].length}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
      <input id="draft_${key}" placeholder="${placeholder}" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();draftAdd('${key}')}" style="padding:10px 13px;font-size:13.5px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.9)">
      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="draftAdd('${key}')" style="flex-shrink:0">➕ إضافة</button>
    </div>
    <div id="draftList_${key}" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;margin-top:10px">${draftListHTML(key)}</div>
  </div>`;
}

function draftListHTML(key){
  const list = ui.draft[key];
  if(!list.length) return `<div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#94a3b8;padding:11px 12px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.6);border-radius:11px;text-align:center;border:1px dashed rgba(148,163,184,.4);font-weight:600">لم تُضف مهام بعد</div>`;
  return list.map(x => `
    <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.7;padding:9px 12px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.85);border-radius:11px">
      <span style="flex:1;font-weight:600;color:#1e293b">${esc(x.text)}</span>
      <button onclick="draftRemove('${key}','${x.id}')" style="background:transparent;border:none;color:#94a3b8;cursor:pointer;font-size:15px;padding:0 4px;font-family:inherit">✕</button>
    </div>`).join('');
}
function draftAdd(key){
  const input = document.getElementById('draft_' + key);
  const text = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب النص أولًا','warn'); return; }
  ui.draft[key].push({ id: uid(), text });
  if(input){ input.value = ''; input.focus(); }
  renderDraftList(key);
}
function draftRemove(key, id){
  ui.draft[key] = ui.draft[key].filter(x => x.id !== id);
  renderDraftList(key);
}
function renderDraftList(key){
  const el = document.getElementById('draftList_' + key);
  if(el) el.innerHTML = draftListHTML(key);
  const cnt = document.getElementById('draftCount_' + key);
  if(cnt) cnt.textContent = ui.draft[key].length;
}
function clearDraft(){
  if(!ui.draft.mastered.length && !ui.draft.notMastered.length && !ui.draft.upcoming.length){ toast('القوائم فارغة','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm('تفريغ جميع القوائم؟')) return;
  ui.draft = { mastered:[], notMastered:[], upcoming:[] };
  render();
}

function addEval(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const d = ui.draft;
  const hasContent = d.mastered.length || d.notMastered.length || d.upcoming.length;
  if(!hasContent){ toast('أضف مهمة واحدة على الأقل','warn'); return; }

  const parentPassword = normalizeDigits((s.parentApprovalPassword || '').trim());

  const evalItem = {
    id: uid(),
    date: val('evDate') || todayISO(),
    session: val('evSession') || 'تقييم',
    attend: val('evAttend'),
    score: val('evScore') !== '' ? parseFloat(val('evScore')) : null,
    mastered: d.mastered.map(x => ({ text: x.text })),
    notMastered: d.notMastered.map(x => ({ text: x.text })),
    upcoming: d.upcoming.map(x => ({ text: x.text })),
    notes: val('evNotes'),
    parentPassword,
    parentApproved: false,
    parentApprovedAt: null,
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    updatedAt: Date.now()
  };
  s.evaluations.push(evalItem);
  ui.draft = { mastered:[], notMastered:[], upcoming:[] };
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'eval', 'تقييم جديد', evalItem.session);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ التقييم ✓','gold'); playSound('success');
  checkCycleCompletion(s);
  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
    evalItem.title = evalItem.session;
    sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'eval', evalItem);
  }
}

function evalCardTeacher(e, studentId){
  const hasTasks = (e.mastered && e.mastered.length) || (e.notMastered && e.notMastered.length) || (e.upcoming && e.upcoming.length);
  const parentBadge = e.parentApproved
    ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✅ معتمد من ولي الأمر</span>'
    : (e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() ? '<span class="badge b-warn">⏳ بانتظار ولي الأمر</span>' : '');
  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px">${esc(e.session || 'حصة')} — ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:5px">
            <span class="badge ${e.attend === 'حاضر' ? 'b-ok' : e.attend === 'متأخر' ? 'b-warn' : 'b-bad'}">${esc(e.attend)}</span>
            ${e.score !== null && e.score !== undefined ? `<span class="badge b-info">⭐ ${e.score}</span>` : ''}
            ${parentBadge}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteEvalConfirm('${studentId}','${e.id}')">🗑</button>
      </div>
      ${hasTasks ? `
        ${e.mastered && e.mastered.length ? evalBlockHTML('mastered','✅','المهام المتقنة', e.mastered) : ''}
        ${e.notMastered && e.notMastered.length ? evalBlockHTML('not','❌','تحتاج تدريب', e.notMastered) : ''}
        ${e.upcoming && e.upcoming.length ? evalBlockHTML('upcoming','📋','المطلوب حتى الحصة القادمة', e.upcoming) : ''}
      ` : ''}
      ${e.notes ? `<div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;margin-top:10px;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--muted)"><b style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">ملاحظات: </b>${esc(e.notes)}</div>` : ''}
      ${e.parentPassword ? `
        <div style="margin-top:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#eef2ff,#fff);border:1.5px dashed #a5b4fc;border-radius:11px;padding:11px 14px">
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#4338ca;font-weight:800;margin-bottom:5px">🔐 كلمة اعتماد ولي الأمر (سرية)</div>
          <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#4f46e5;letter-spacing:3px">${esc(e.parentPassword)}</div>
        </div>
      ` : ''}
      <div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editEvalModal('${studentId}','${e.id}')">✏️ تعديل</button>
        <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="resendTaskWA('${studentId}','eval','${e.id}')">💬 إعادة إرسال</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function evalBlockHTML(type, icon, title, items){
  const bg = type === 'mastered' ? '#f0fdf4' : type === 'not' ? '#fef2f2' : '#fffbeb';
  const border = type === 'mastered' ? '#bbf7d0' : type === 'not' ? '#fecaca' : '#fde68a';
  const color = type === 'mastered' ? '#166534' : type === 'not' ? '#991b1b' : '#92400e';
  return `
    <div style="border-radius:12px;padding:11px 13px;border:1.5px solid ${border};background:${bg};margin-top:8px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;color:${color};margin-bottom:8px">
        <span>${icon}</span><span>${title}</span>
        <span style="margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:11px;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.85)">${items.length}</span>
      </div>
      <ul style="list-style:none;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px">
        ${items.map(it => `<li style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.7;color:#1e293b;font-weight:600;padding-inline-start:16px;position:relative"><span style="position:absolute;right:0;top:9px;width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${color}"></span>${esc(it.text)}</li>`).join('')}
      </ul>
    </div>`;
}

function deleteEvalConfirm(studentId, evId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا التقييم؟')) return;
  s.evaluations = s.evaluations.filter(x => x.id !== evId);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

function tabScheduleTeacher(s){
  return `
  <div class="card">

    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="field"><label>اليوم <span class="req">*</span></label>
        <select id="scDay">${DAYS.map(d => `<option>${d}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>نوع المحاضرة <span class="req">*</span></label>
        <select id="scType">${SESSION_TYPES.map(t => `<option value="${t.key}">${t.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>من (الساعة)</label><input type="time" id="scFrom"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>إلى (الساعة)</label><input type="time" id="scTo"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>المادة / الموضوع</label><input id="scSubject" placeholder="مثال: شرح النصوص"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>عنوان / مكان المحاضرة</label><input id="scLocation" placeholder="قاعة 3 — أو رابط الحضور"></div>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:16px;width:100%" onclick="addSchedule('${s.id}')">➕ إضافة للجدول</button>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📅</span> الجدول الأسبوعي <span class="sec-sub">${s.schedule.length}</span></div>
    ${s.schedule.length ? DAYS.filter(d => s.schedule.some(x => x.day === d)).map(day => `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fff,#f8fafc);border:2px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:14px;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:6px;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary-d))"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;padding-inline-start:8px">
          <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:var(--primary-d)">${day}</span>
          <span style="background:var(--primary-l);color:var(--primary-d);font-size:11px;font-weight:900;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px">${s.schedule.filter(x => x.day === day).length} حصة</span>
        </div>
        ${s.schedule.filter(x => x.day === day).map(x => `
          <div style="padding:12px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:var(--primary-d);background:#f0fdfa;padding:6px 11px;border-radius:9px;direction:ltr;min-width:100px;text-align:center">🕐 ${fmtTime12(x.from)}<br>${fmtTime12(x.to)}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:3px">${esc(x.subject || sessionTypeLabel(x.type))}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
                <span style="background:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)}22;color:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)};padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px">${sessionTypeLabel(x.type)}</span>
                ${x.location ? `<span>📍 ${esc(x.location)}</span>` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteScheduleItemConfirm('${s.id}','${x.id}')">🗑</button>
          </div>`).join('')}
      </div>`).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>الجدول فارغ</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function addSchedule(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const day = val('scDay');
  if(!day){ toast('اختر اليوم','warn'); return; }
  s.schedule.push({
    id: uid(),
    day,
    type: val('scType'),
    from: val('scFrom'),
    to: val('scTo'),
    subject: val('scSubject'),
    location: val('scLocation'),
    createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم الإضافة للجدول ✓','gold');
}
function deleteScheduleItemConfirm(studentId, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه الحصة؟')) return;
  s.schedule = s.schedule.filter(x => x.id !== itemId);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}
