  { id:'emerald', name:'💎 زمردي فاخر', color:'#0d9488', bg1:'#ccfbf1', bg2:'#f0fdfa' },
  { id:'royal_purple', name:'👑 ملكي بنفسجي', color:'#7c3aed', bg1:'#ede9fe', bg2:'#f5f3ff' },
  { id:'modern_blue', name:'🌊 أزرق عصري', color:'#2563eb', bg1:'#dbeafe', bg2:'#eff6ff' },
  { id:'crimson', name:'🔥 قرمزي أنيق', color:'#dc2626', bg1:'#fee2e2', bg2:'#fef2f2' },
  { id:'midnight', name:'🌙 ليلي فخم', color:'#0f172a', bg1:'#1e293b', bg2:'#0f172a', dark:true }
];

function tabCertificatesTeacher(s){
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎓</span> إصدار شهادة</div>
    <div class="field"><label>عنوان الشهادة</label><input id="certTitle" value="شهادة إتمام"></div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>وصف الشهادة</label>
      <textarea id="certDesc">أتم الطالب بنجاح دورة اللغة العربية وحصل على تقدير ممتاز</textarea>
    </div>
    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
      <div class="field"><label>التقدير</label>
        <select id="certGrade">
          <option>ممتاز</option>
          <option>جيد جدًا</option>
          <option>جيد</option>
          <option>مقبول</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>تصميم الشهادة</label>
        <select id="certDesign">${CERT_DESIGNS.map(d => `<option value="${d.id}">${d.name}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-gold" style="margin-top:16px;width:100%" onclick="issueCertificate('${s.id}')">🎓 إصدار الشهادة</button>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📜</span> الشهادات الصادرة <span class="sec-sub">${s.certificates.length}</span></div>
    ${s.certificates.length ? s.certificates.slice().reverse().map(c => `
      <div class="item" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div class="item-title">${esc(c.title)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta"><span>${esc(c.grade)}</span> • <span>🎨 ${esc((CERT_DESIGNS.find(d=>d.id===c.design)||CERT_DESIGNS[0]).name)}</span> • <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(c.issuedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</span></div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="saveCertificateAsImage('${s.id}','${c.id}')">💾 صورة</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="printCertificate('${s.id}','${c.id}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
          <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="resendTaskWA('${s.id}','certificate','${c.id}')">💬 واتساب</button>
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteCertificate('${s.id}','${c.id}')">🗑</button>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد شهادات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function issueCertificate(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const title = val('certTitle') || 'شهادة إتمام';
  const desc = val('certDesc');
  const grade = val('certGrade');
  const design = val('certDesign') || 'classic_gold';
  const c = {
    id: uid(),
    title, desc, grade, design,
    issuedAt: Date.now(),
    studentName: s.name,
    teacherName: db.settings.teacherName,
    stage: s.stage
  };
  s.certificates.push(c);
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `🎓 مبروك! تم إصدار شهادتك "${title}" بتقدير ${grade}.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'cert', 'إصدار شهادة', title);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم إصدار الشهادة ✓','gold'); playSound('celebrate');
  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
    sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'certificate', c);
  }
}

function deleteCertificate(studentId, certId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه الشهادة؟')) return;
  s.certificates = s.certificates.filter(x => x.id !== certId);
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
}

/* ============ تبويب الإشعارات ============ */
function tabNotifsTeacher(s){
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico">🔔</span> إرسال إشعار
      ${s.notifications.length ? `<button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" style="margin-inline-start:auto" onclick="deleteAllNotifsConfirm('${s.id}')">🗑 حذف الكل</button>` : ''}
    </div>
    <div class="field"><label>نص الإشعار <span class="req">*</span></label><textarea id="nfText" placeholder="اكتب رسالتك هنا..."></textarea></div>
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px">
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="sendNotifToStudent('${s.id}')">📤 إرسال للطالب</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp" onclick="sendWhatsAppToStudent('${s.id}')">📱 واتساب</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📨</span> الإشعارات <span class="sec-sub">${s.notifications.length}</span></div>
    ${s.notifications.length ? s.notifications.slice().reverse().map(n => `
      <div class="item">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text);font-weight:600">${esc(n.text)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:8px">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(n.date)} ${n.read ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✓</span>' : '<span class="badge b-warn">جديد</span>'}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteNotifConfirm('${s.id}','${n.id}')">🗑</button>
      </div>`).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد إشعارات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function sendNotifToStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const text = val('nfText');
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب نص الإشعار','warn'); return; }
  s.notifications.push({ id: uid(), text, date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now() });
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'other', 'إشعار من المعلم', text.slice(0, 60));
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم إرسال الإشعار ✓','gold'); playSound('notify');
}
function sendWhatsAppToStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const text = val('nfText');
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب نص الرسالة أولًا','warn'); return; }
  sendWhatsApp(s.phone, text, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: 'student', category: 'custom', title: 'رسالة مخصصة', studentId: s.id });
}
function deleteNotifConfirm(studentId, nid){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا الإشعار؟')) return;
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  s.notifications = s.notifications.filter(x => x.id !== nid);
  saveDB(); render();
}
function deleteAllNotifsConfirm(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف جميع الإشعارات؟')) return;
  s.notifications = [];
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حذف الإشعارات','warn');
}

/* ============ تبويب البيانات ============ */
function tabSettingsTeacher(s){
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">⚙️</span> بيانات الطالب الكاملة</div>
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdfa,#ccfbf1);border:1.5px solid #99f6e4;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;color:#0f766e;margin-bottom:10px">🔐 بيانات الدخول السرية</div>
      <div class="grid g2">
        <div><b style="font-size:12px;color:#0f766e">📱 اسم الدخول:</b><div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#115e59;direction:ltr;margin-top:3px">${esc(s.phone)}</div></div>
        <div><b style="font-size:12px;color:#0f766e">🔑 كلمة المرور:</b><div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#115e59;direction:ltr;margin-top:3px">${esc(s.password)}</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="field"><label>الاسم</label><input id="edName" value="${esc(s.name)}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>رقم الهاتف</label><input id="edPhone" value="${esc(s.phone)}" inputmode="tel" type="tel"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>رقم ولي الأمر</label><input id="edParent" value="${esc(s.parentPhone)}" inputmode="tel" type="tel"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>المرحلة</label>
        <select id="edStage">${STAGES.map(st => `<option ${st===s.stage?'selected':''}>${st}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>المسار</label>
        <select id="edTrack">${TRACKS.map(t => `<option ${t===(s.track||'عام')?'selected':''}>${t}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>كلمة المرور</label><input id="edPass" value="${esc(s.password)}"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>ملاحظات (يراها الطالب)</label><textarea id="edNotes">${esc(s.notes)}</textarea></div>
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:16px">
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveStudentSettings('${s.id}')">💾 حفظ</button>
      <button class="btn btn-warn-soft" onclick="regenerateStudentPassword('${s.id}')">🔄 توليد كلمة مرور</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function saveStudentSettings(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const newPhone = cleanPhone(val('edPhone'));
  const newParent = cleanPhone(val('edParent'));
  if(!newPhone || !newParent){ toast('الأرقام مطلوبة','warn'); return; }
  const other = getStudentByPhone(newPhone);
  if(other && other.id !== studentId){ toast('الرقم مستخدم','err'); return; }
  s.name = val('edName') || s.name;
  s.phone = newPhone;
  s.parentPhone = newParent;
  s.stage = val('edStage');
  s.track = val('edTrack');
  s.password = val('edPass') || s.password;
  s.notes = val('edNotes');
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم الحفظ ✓','gold');
}

/* ============ الملاحظات الخاصة ============ */
function tabPrivateNotesTeacher(s){
  const list = s.privateNotes || [];
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📝</span> إضافة ملاحظة خاصة</div>
    <div class="field">
      <label>نص الملاحظة (لا يراها الطالب)</label>
      <textarea id="pnText" placeholder="مثال: يحتاج متابعة في النحو..." style="min-height:110px"></textarea>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="addPrivateNote('${s.id}')">💾 حفظ الملاحظة</button>
  </div>
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📋</span> سجل الملاحظات <span class="sec-sub">${list.length}</span></div>
    ${list.length ? list.slice().reverse().map(n => `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;font-weight:800;font-size:12px;color:#92400e;margin-bottom:8px">📝 ملاحظة بتاريخ ${fmtDateWithDay(n.date)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.85;color:#1e293b;font-weight:600;white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(n.text)}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editPrivateNote('${s.id}','${n.id}')">✏️ تعديل</button>
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deletePrivateNote('${s.id}','${n.id}')">🗑 حذف</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد ملاحظات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function addPrivateNote(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const text = val('pnText');
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب نص الملاحظة','warn'); return; }
  s.privateNotes = s.privateNotes || [];
  s.privateNotes.push({ id: uid(), text, date: todayISO(), createdAt: Date.now(), updatedAt: null });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ الملاحظة ✓','gold');
}
function editPrivateNote(studentId, noteId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const n = (s.privateNotes||[]).find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!n) return;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#92400e">✏️ تعديل الملاحظة</div>
    <div class="field"><label>النص</label><textarea id="pnEditText" style="min-height:130px">${esc(n.text)}</textarea></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="savePrivateNoteEdit('${studentId}','${noteId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}
function savePrivateNoteEdit(studentId, noteId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const n = (s.privateNotes||[]).find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!n) return;
  n.text = val('pnEditText') || n.text;
  n.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم التعديل ✓','gold');
}
function deletePrivateNote(studentId, noteId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه الملاحظة؟')) return;
  s.privateNotes = (s.privateNotes||[]).filter(x => x.id !== noteId);
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ سجل الأنشطة ============ */
function tabActivityTeacher(s){
  const log = (s.activityLog || []).slice().reverse();
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#22d3ee,#0891b2);color:#fff">📊</span>
      سجل الأنشطة <span class="sec-sub">${log.length}</span>
      ${log.length ? `<button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" style="margin-inline-start:auto" onclick="clearStudentActivityLog('${s.id}')">🗑 مسح الكل</button>` : ''}
    </div>
    ${log.length ? log.slice(0, 100).map(a => {
      const info = getActivityTypeInfo(a.type);
      return `
        <div class="activity-log-item">
          <div class="ali-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${info.color},${info.color}cc)">${info.icon}</div>
          <div class="ali-body">
            <div class="ali-title">${esc(a.title)}</div>
            ${a.meta ? `<div class="ali-meta">${esc(a.meta)}</div>` : ''}
            <div class="ali-meta">
              <span>🕐 ${new Date(a.at).toLocaleString('ar-EG')}</span>
              <span style="background:#f0fdfa;color:#0f766e;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:10.5px">${info.label}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="ali-actions">
            <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editActivityLogItem('${s.id}','${a.id}')">✏️</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteActivityLogItem('${s.id}','${a.id}')">🗑</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا يوجد نشاط بعد</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function clearStudentActivityLog(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('مسح كل سجل الأنشطة؟')) return;
  s.activityLog = [];
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم مسح السجل','warn');
}
function editActivityLogItem(studentId, logId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const item = (s.activityLog||[]).find(x => x.id === logId); if(!item) return;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--info)">✏️ تعديل السجل</div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px"><label>العنوان</label><input id="alEditTitle" value="${esc(item.title)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>تفاصيل</label><textarea id="alEditMeta" style="min-height:70px">${esc(item.meta||'')}</textarea></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveActivityLogEdit('${studentId}','${logId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}
function saveActivityLogEdit(studentId, logId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const item = (s.activityLog||[]).find(x => x.id === logId); if(!item) return;
  item.title = val('alEditTitle') || item.title;
  item.meta = val('alEditMeta');
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم التعديل ✓','gold');
}
function deleteActivityLogItem(studentId, logId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا السجل؟')) return;
  s.activityLog = (s.activityLog||[]).filter(x => x.id !== logId);
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ حساب إحصائيات الطالب ============ */
function computeStudentStats(s){
  const hwQuiz = s.homework.filter(h => h.questions && h.questions.length);
  const hwDone = hwQuiz.filter(h => h.submitted);
  const hwCompletion = hwQuiz.length ? Math.round(hwDone.length / hwQuiz.length * 100) : null;
  const hwAvgScore = hwDone.length ? Math.round(hwDone.reduce((a,h) => a + (h.total ? (h.score/h.total)*100 : 0), 0) / hwDone.length) : null;

  const examResults = [];
  s.exams.forEach(e => {
    if(e.type === 'questions' && e.submitted && e.total) examResults.push((e.score / e.total) * 100);
    else if(e.type === 'simple' && e.score !== '' && e.total) examResults.push((parseFloat(e.score) / parseFloat(e.total)) * 100);
    else if(e.type === 'link' && e.total && e.score !== '') examResults.push((parseFloat(e.score) / parseFloat(e.total)) * 100);
  });
  const exAvgScore = examResults.length ? Math.round(examResults.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0) / examResults.length) : null;

  const evalsTotal = s.evaluations.length;
  const attended = s.evaluations.filter(e => e.attend === 'حاضر').length;
  const late = s.evaluations.filter(e => e.attend === 'متأخر').length;
  const absence = s.evaluations.filter(e => e.attend === 'غائب').length;
  const attendanceRate = evalsTotal ? Math.round(((attended + late * 0.5) / evalsTotal) * 100) : null;

  let masteredCount = 0, notMasteredCount = 0;
  s.evaluations.forEach(e => {
    masteredCount += (e.mastered || []).length;
    notMasteredCount += (e.notMastered || []).length;
  });
  const totalTasks = masteredCount + notMasteredCount;
  const masteryRate = totalTasks ? Math.round((masteredCount / totalTasks) * 100) : null;

  const comps = [];
  if(hwCompletion !== null) comps.push({ v: hwCompletion, w: 1.5 });
  if(hwAvgScore !== null) comps.push({ v: hwAvgScore, w: 1 });
  if(exAvgScore !== null) comps.push({ v: exAvgScore, w: 2 });
  if(attendanceRate !== null) comps.push({ v: attendanceRate, w: 1 });
  if(masteryRate !== null) comps.push({ v: masteryRate, w: 2 });
  let overall = null;
  if(comps.length){
    const sumW = comps.reduce((a,c) => a + c.w, 0);
    overall = Math.round(comps.reduce((a,c) => a + c.v * c.w, 0) / sumW);
  }
  return { hwQuizCount: hwQuiz.length, hwDoneCount: hwDone.length, hwCompletion, hwAvgScore, exAvgScore, exDoneCount: examResults.length, evalsTotal, attended, late, absence, attendanceRate, masteredCount, notMasteredCount, masteryRate, overall };
}

function progressDashboardHTML(s){
  const st = computeStudentStats(s);
  if(st.overall === null){
    return `
    <div class="prog-dash anim-in">
      <div class="prog-head">
        <div class="ico">📊</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><h3>إحصائيات الأداء</h3><p>ستظهر الإحصائيات بعد إضافة بيانات</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="empty" style="padding:28px"><span class="ico">📈</span>لا توجد بيانات كافية بعد</div>
    </div>`;
  }
  const overall = st.overall;
  const R = 60, C = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  const offset = C * (1 - overall / 100);
  const overallColor = overall >= 80 ? '#22c55e' : overall >= 60 ? '#14b8a6' : overall >= 40 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';
  const overallLabel = overall >= 80 ? 'ممتاز' : overall >= 60 ? 'جيد جدًا' : overall >= 40 ? 'جيد' : 'يحتاج تحسين';
  const overallBadge = overall >= 80 ? 'b-ok' : overall >= 60 ? 'b-info' : overall >= 40 ? 'b-warn' : 'b-bad';
  const bars = [];
  if(st.hwCompletion !== null) bars.push({ label: 'إنجاز الواجبات', v: st.hwCompletion, color: '#2563eb' });
  if(st.hwAvgScore !== null) bars.push({ label: 'أداء الواجبات', v: st.hwAvgScore, color: '#0891b2' });
  if(st.exAvgScore !== null) bars.push({ label: 'أداء الاختبارات', v: st.exAvgScore, color: '#7c3aed' });
  if(st.attendanceRate !== null) bars.push({ label: 'نسبة الحضور', v: st.attendanceRate, color: '#14b8a6' });
  if(st.masteryRate !== null) bars.push({ label: 'نسبة الإتقان', v: st.masteryRate, color: '#d97706' });
  return `
  <div class="prog-dash anim-in">
    <div class="prog-head">
      <div class="ico">📊</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><h3>إحصائيات الأداء التفصيلية</h3><p>نظرة شاملة على مستوى الطالب</p></div>
      <span class="badge ${overallBadge}" style="font-size:12px;padding:6px 13px">${overallLabel}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="prog-body">
      <div class="prog-ring">
        <svg width="150" height="150" viewBox="0 0 150 150" style="transform:rotate(-90deg)">
          <circle cx="75" cy="75" r="${R}" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="12"/>
          <circle cx="75" cy="75" r="${R}" fill="none" stroke="${overallColor}" stroke-width="12" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="${C.toFixed(2)}" stroke-dashoffset="${offset.toFixed(2)}" style="transition:stroke-dashoffset 1.3s cubic-bezier(.21,1.02,.73,1)"/>
        </svg>
        <div class="prog-center">
          <div class="pct" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${overallColor},${overallColor}bb);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${overall}%</div>
          <div class="lbl">التقدم العام</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="prog-bars">
        ${bars.length ? bars.map(b => `
          <div class="prog-bar">
            <div class="bl"><span class="bdot" style="background:${b.color}"></span>${b.label}</div>
            <div class="bt"><div class="bf" style="width:${b.v}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${b.color},${b.color}bb)"></div></div>
            <div class="bv" style="color:${b.color}">${b.v}%</div>
          </div>
        `).join('') : '<div class="empty" style="padding:20px;font-size:13px">لا توجد معايير قابلة للقياس بعد</div>'}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="prog-chips">
      <div class="prog-chip ${st.hwQuizCount && st.hwDoneCount === st.hwQuizCount ? 'ok' : ''}"><div class="v">${st.hwDoneCount}<span style="font-size:13px;opacity:.55">/${st.hwQuizCount}</span></div><div class="k">واجبات مُسلَّمة</div></div>
      <div class="prog-chip ${st.exAvgScore !== null && st.exAvgScore >= 60 ? 'ok' : (st.exAvgScore !== null ? 'warn' : '')}"><div class="v">${st.exAvgScore !== null ? st.exAvgScore + '%' : '—'}</div><div class="k">معدل الاختبارات</div></div>
      <div class="prog-chip ok"><div class="v">${st.attended}</div><div class="k">حضور</div></div>
      <div class="prog-chip ${st.absence > 0 ? 'bad' : ''}"><div class="v">${st.absence}</div><div class="k">غياب</div></div>
      <div class="prog-chip"><div class="v">${st.masteredCount}</div><div class="k">مهام متقنة</div></div>
      <div class="prog-chip ${st.notMasteredCount > 0 ? 'warn' : ''}"><div class="v">${st.notMasteredCount}</div><div class="k">تحتاج تدريب</div></div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ الرسم البياني الشامل (رادار) ============ */
function comprehensiveStudentChartHTML(s){
  const st = computeStudentStats(s);
  const attendance = st.attendanceRate !== null ? st.attendanceRate : 0;
  const homework = st.hwCompletion !== null ? st.hwCompletion : (st.hwAvgScore || 0);
  const exams = st.exAvgScore !== null ? st.exAvgScore : 0;
  const mastery = st.masteryRate !== null ? st.masteryRate : 0;
  const engagement = (s.homework.filter(h=>h.submitted).length + s.exams.filter(e=>e.submitted).length);
  const engagementRate = (s.homework.length + s.exams.length) ?
    Math.round(engagement / (s.homework.length + s.exams.length) * 100) : 0;

  const axes = [
    { key:'attendance', label:'الحضور',     value: attendance,     color:'#14b8a6', icon:'✅' },
    { key:'homework',   label:'الواجبات',   value: homework,       color:'#2563eb', icon:'📝' },
    { key:'exams',      label:'الاختبارات', value: exams,          color:'#7c3aed', icon:'📊' },
    { key:'mastery',    label:'الإتقان',    value: mastery,        color:'#d97706', icon:'🎯' },
    { key:'engagement', label:'التفاعل',    value: engagementRate, color:'#db2777', icon:'🔥' }
  ];
  const CX = 130, CY = 130, MAX_R = 100;
  const N = axes.length;
  const angleStep = (2 * Math.PI) / N;
  const gridLevels = [0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1];
  const gridPolygons = gridLevels.map(level => {
    const pts = [];
    for(let i = 0; i < N; i++){
      const a = -Math.PI/2 + i * angleStep;
      const x = CX + Math.cos(a) * MAX_R * level;
      const y = CY + Math.sin(a) * MAX_R * level;
      pts.push(`${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`);
    }
    return `<polygon points="${pts.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="1"/>`;
  }).join('');
  const axisLines = axes.map((_, i) => {
    const a = -Math.PI/2 + i * angleStep;
    const x = CX + Math.cos(a) * MAX_R;
    const y = CY + Math.sin(a) * MAX_R;
    return `<line x1="${CX}" y1="${CY}" x2="${x.toFixed(1)}" y2="${y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#cbd5e1" stroke-width="1"/>`;
  }).join('');
  const dataPoints = axes.map((ax, i) => {
    const a = -Math.PI/2 + i * angleStep;
    const r = (Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ax.value)) / 100) * MAX_R;
    const x = CX + Math.cos(a) * r;
    const y = CY + Math.sin(a) * r;
    return { x, y };
  });
  const dataPolygon = dataPoints.map(p => `${p.x.toFixed(1)},${p.y.toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
  const dataCircles = dataPoints.map((p, i) =>
    `<circle cx="${p.x.toFixed(1)}" cy="${p.y.toFixed(1)}" r="5" fill="${axes[i].color}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.5"/>`
  ).join('');
  const labels = axes.map((ax, i) => {
    const a = -Math.PI/2 + i * angleStep;
    const lx = CX + Math.cos(a) * (MAX_R + 24);
    const ly = CY + Math.sin(a) * (MAX_R + 24);
    return `
      <text x="${lx.toFixed(1)}" y="${ly.toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="800" fill="${ax.color}">${ax.label}</text>
      <text x="${lx.toFixed(1)}" y="${(ly + 13).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="900" fill="#0f172a">${ax.value}%</text>`;
  }).join('');
  const overall = st.overall || 0;
  const overallColor = overall >= 80 ? '#16a34a' : overall >= 60 ? '#14b8a6' : overall >= 40 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';
  const overallLabel = overall >= 80 ? 'ممتاز' : overall >= 60 ? 'جيد جدًا' : overall >= 40 ? 'جيد' : 'يحتاج تحسين';

  return `
  <div class="card anim-in" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fff,#f8fafc);overflow:hidden;position:relative">
    <div class="sec-title" style="position:relative;z-index:1">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#14b8a6,#0f766e);color:#fff">📊</span>
      الأداء الشامل في لمحة
      <span class="badge" style="margin-inline-start:auto;background:${overallColor}22;color:${overallColor}">${overallLabel} ${overall}%</span>
    </div>

    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:24px;align-items:center">
      <div style="text-align:center">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 260 260" width="260" height="260" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:0 auto">
          <defs>
            <radialGradient id="radarFill_${s.id}" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
              <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${overallColor}" stop-opacity="0.55"/>
              <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${overallColor}" stop-opacity="0.12"/>
            </radialGradient>
          </defs>
          ${gridPolygons}
          ${axisLines}
          <polygon points="${dataPolygon}" fill="url(#radarFill_${s.id})" stroke="${overallColor}" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
          ${dataCircles}
          ${labels}
        </svg>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;min-width:0">
        ${axes.map(ax => `
          <div style="background:#fff;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">
            <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:${ax.color}22;color:${ax.color};display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0">${ax.icon}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:5px">${ax.label}</div>
              <div style="height:7px;border-radius:99px;background:#f1f5f9;overflow:hidden">
                <div style="height:100%;width:${ax.value}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${ax.color},${ax.color}bb);border-radius:99px;transition:width 1s"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:${ax.color};min-width:46px;text-align:center">${ax.value}%</div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ تبويب التحصيل التلقائي للمعلم ============ */
function tabAutoProgressTeacher(s){
  const st = getAutoStats(s);
  const sorted = st.autoEvals.slice().sort((a,b) => (b.createdAt||0) - (a.createdAt||0));
  return `
  ${st.autoEvals.length ? `
  <div class="auto-progress-hero">
    <div class="aph-head">
      <div class="aph-ico">📈</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div class="aph-title">التحصيل التلقائي</div>
        <div class="aph-sub">مستوى التقدم التلقائي المبني من نتائج الواجبات والاختبارات</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="auto-progress-stats">
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.overallLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.overallAvg}%</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">المستوى العام</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.hwLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.hwEvals.length ? st.hwAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">📝 الواجبات (${st.hwEvals.length})</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.exLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.exEvals.length ? st.exAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">📊 الاختبارات (${st.exEvals.length})</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.unsolvedItems.length ? 'bad' : ''}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.unsolvedItems.length}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">❌ لم يتم حلها</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="auto-bars-grid">
    <div class="auto-bar-card hw">
      <div class="abc-head"><span class="abc-ico">📝</span><span>مستوى الواجبات</span></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-big">${st.hwAvg}%</div>
      <div class="auto-bar-track"><div class="fill hw" style="width:${st.hwAvg}%"></div></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-info"><span>المستوى: <b>${st.hwLevel.label}</b></span><span>${st.hwEvals.length} واجب</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="auto-bar-card ex">
      <div class="abc-head"><span class="abc-ico">📊</span><span>مستوى الاختبارات</span></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-big">${st.exAvg}%</div>
      <div class="auto-bar-track"><div class="fill ex" style="width:${st.exAvg}%"></div></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-info"><span>المستوى: <b>${st.exLevel.label}</b></span><span>${st.exEvals.length} اختبار</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  ` : ''}

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed);color:#fff">📈</span>
      سجل التحصيل التلقائي <span class="sec-sub">${st.autoEvals.length} تقييم</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">
      <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="saveAutoProgressAsImage('${s.id}')">📸 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="printAutoProgress('${s.id}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
      ${st.realAutoEvals.length ? `<button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="clearAutoEvals('${s.id}')">🗑 تصفير</button>` : ''}
    </div>
    ${sorted.length ? sorted.map(e => autoEvalCardHTML(e, 'teacher', s.id)).join('') : `
      <div class="empty" style="padding:30px">
        <span class="ico">📈</span>
        لا توجد تقييمات بعد
        <br><span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:8px;display:block">
          سيتم بناء التقييم تلقائيًا عند حل الطالب لأي واجب أو اختبار
        </span>
      </div>
    `}
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ تبويب الدورات المكتملة ============ */
function tabCompletedCyclesTeacher(s){
  const cycles = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles : [];
  const totalAttended = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.attended || 0), 0);
  const totalAbsent = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.absent || 0), 0);
  const totalSessions = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.recordedCount || 0), 0);
  const avgAttendance = cycles.length ? Math.round(cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.attendanceRate || 0), 0) / cycles.length) : 0;

  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#b45309);color:#fff">🏆</span>
      الدورات المكتملة والمؤرشفة
      <span class="sec-sub">${cycles.length} دورة</span>
    </div>

    ${cycles.length ? `
      <div class="grid g3" style="gap:9px;margin-bottom:14px">
        <div class="prog-chip"><div class="v">${cycles.length}</div><div class="k">🏆 دورات</div></div>
        <div class="prog-chip ok"><div class="v">${totalAttended}</div><div class="k">✅ حضور</div></div>
        <div class="prog-chip bad"><div class="v">${totalAbsent}</div><div class="k">❌ غياب</div></div>
        <div class="prog-chip"><div class="v">${totalSessions}</div><div class="k">📚 حصص</div></div>
        <div class="prog-chip ${avgAttendance >= 80 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><div class="v">${avgAttendance}%</div><div class="k">📊 متوسط الحضور</div></div>
      </div>

      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">
        <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="saveCompletedCyclesAsImage('${s.id}')">📸 حفظ السجل كصورة</button>
        <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="printCompletedCycles('${s.id}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="clearAllCompletedCycles('${s.id}')">🗑 حذف الكل (${cycles.length})</button>
      </div>

      <div style="max-height:65vh;overflow-y:auto">
        ${cycles.slice().reverse().map(c => completedCycleCardHTML(c, s.id, true)).join('')}
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border:2px dashed #fcd34d;border-radius:18px;padding:32px;text-align:center">
        <div style="font-size:56px;margin-bottom:12px;opacity:.7;animation:float 3s ease-in-out infinite">🏆</div>
        <h3 style="font-size:17px;font-weight:900;color:#78350f;margin-bottom:8px">لا توجد دورات مكتملة محفوظة بعد</h3>
        <p style="font-size:13px;color:#92400e;font-weight:600;line-height:1.8">
          عند تصفير أي دورة شهرية للطالب، سيتم أرشفتها تلقائيًا هنا مع كل تفاصيلها وإحصائياتها.
        </p>
      </div>
    `}
  </div>`;
}

function completedCycleCardHTML(cycle, studentId, isTeacher){
  const cur = getCurrency();
  const showFees = cycle.monthlyFee;
  const pct = cycle.attendanceRate || 0;
  return `
  <div class="completed-cycle-card">
    <div class="completed-cycle-head">
      <div class="completed-cycle-ico">🏆</div>
      <div class="completed-cycle-title">
        <div class="cct-t">دورة #${cycle.cycleNumber} — ${esc(cycle.monthName)}</div>
        <div class="cct-s">📅 ${esc(cycle.startDateStr)} ← ${esc(cycle.endDateStr)}</div>
        ${cycle.archivedDate ? `<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#92400e;font-weight:700;margin-top:2px">📦 تم الأرشفة: ${fmtDateWithDay(cycle.archivedDate)}</div>` : ''}
      </div>
      ${isTeacher ? `
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteCompletedCycle('${studentId}','${cycle.id}')">🗑</button>
        </div>` : ''}
    </div>

    <div class="completed-cycle-stats">
      <div class="completed-cycle-stat" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#fff);border-color:#86efac">
        <div class="ccst-v" style="color:#15803d">${cycle.attended}</div>
        <div class="ccst-l">✅ حضور</div>
      </div>
      <div class="completed-cycle-stat" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fff);border-color:#fcd34d">
        <div class="ccst-v" style="color:#b45309">${cycle.late}</div>
        <div class="ccst-l">⏰ متأخر</div>
      </div>
      <div class="completed-cycle-stat" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fee2e2,#fff);border-color:#fecaca">
        <div class="ccst-v" style="color:#b91c1c">${cycle.absent}</div>
        <div class="ccst-l">❌ غياب</div>
      </div>
      <div class="completed-cycle-stat">
        <div class="ccst-v" style="color:#0f766e">${cycle.recordedCount}/${cycle.totalSessions}</div>
        <div class="ccst-l">📚 حصص مسجلة</div>
      </div>
      <div class="completed-cycle-stat" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdfa,#fff);border-color:#99f6e4">
        <div class="ccst-v" style="color:${pct >= 80 ? '#15803d' : pct >= 60 ? '#b45309' : '#b91c1c'}">${pct}%</div>
        <div class="ccst-l">📊 نسبة الحضور</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    ${showFees ? `
      <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.75);border:1px dashed #fcd34d;border-radius:11px;padding:10px 13px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;color:#78350f">
          💰 الرسوم
          ${cycle.feePaid ? '<span style="background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;font-size:11px;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid #86efac;font-weight:900">✅ مستلم</span>' : '<span style="background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;font-size:11px;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid #f87171;font-weight:900">⏳ غير مستلم</span>'}
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#b45309">${cycle.monthlyFee} ${cur.symbol}</div>
      </div>
    ` : ''}

    ${cycle.notes ? `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#78350f;font-weight:700;margin-top:10px;padding:8px 12px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.6);border-radius:9px;border:1px dashed #fcd34d">📝 ${esc(cycle.notes)}</div>` : ''}
  </div>`;
}

function deleteCompletedCycle(studentId, cycleId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.subscription || !s.subscription.completedCycles){ toast('الطالب غير موجود','err'); return; }
  const cycle = s.subscription.completedCycles.find(c => c.id === cycleId);
  if(!cycle){ toast('الدورة غير موجودة','err'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`حذف دورة "${cycle.monthName}" من السجل نهائيًا؟`)) return;
  s.subscription.completedCycles = s.subscription.completedCycles.filter(c => c.id !== cycleId);
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حذف الدورة','warn');
}

function clearAllCompletedCycles(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId);
  if(!s || !s.subscription || !s.subscription.completedCycles){ toast('لا توجد دورات','warn'); return; }
  const count = s.subscription.completedCycles.length;
  if(!count){ toast('لا توجد دورات','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`حذف جميع الدورات (${count})؟`)) return;
  if(!confirm('تأكيد نهائي؟')) return;
  s.subscription.completedCycles = [];
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم حذف ${count} دورة`,'warn');
}

/* ============ تبويب الدروس/المذكرات/المحاضرات للطالب ============ */
function tabLessonsTeacher(s){
  const allLessons = db.lessons.filter(l => isTargetedToStudent(l, s));
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📹</span> الدروس الموجهة لهذا الطالب <span class="sec-sub">${allLessons.length}</span></div>
    ${allLessons.length ? renderFoldersView(allLessons, 'lesson', 'teacher') : '<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد دروس موجهة لهذا الطالب</div>'}
  </div>`;
}

function tabNotesTeacher(s){
  const allNotes = db.notes.filter(n => isTargetedToStudent(n, s));
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📄</span> المذكرات <span class="sec-sub">${allNotes.length}</span></div>
    ${allNotes.length ? renderFoldersView(allNotes, 'note', 'teacher') : '<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد مذكرات موجهة لهذا الطالب</div>'}
  </div>`;
}

function tabLiveTeacher(s){
  const allLive = db.liveSessions.filter(lv => isTargetedToStudent(lv, s));
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎥</span> المحاضرات الأونلاين <span class="sec-sub">${allLive.length}</span></div>
    ${allLive.length ? renderFoldersView(allLive, 'live', 'teacher') : '<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد محاضرات موجهة لهذا الطالب</div>'}
  </div>`;
}

function antiShareBanner(){
  return `
  <div class="anti-share-banner">
    <div class="asb-ico">🔒</div>
    <div class="asb-txt">
      المحتوى محمي بحقوق الملكية — يُمنع نسخ أو مشاركة أي رابط أو مادة مع أي شخص آخر.
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ الفلاتر والتصنيفات ============ */
function isTargetedToStudent(item, s){
  if(!item || !s) return false;
  const mode = item.targetMode || 'all';
  if(mode === 'all') return true;
  if(mode === 'stage'){
    if(item.targetStage && item.targetStage !== (s.stage||'')) return false;
    if(item.targetTrack && item.targetTrack !== (s.track||'عام')) return false;
    return true;
  }
  if(mode === 'students'){
    return (item.targetStudents||[]).includes(s.id);
  }
  return true;
}

function targetingSummaryHTML(item){
  const mode = item.targetMode || 'all';
  if(mode === 'all') return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;background:#eef2ff;color:#4338ca;border:1px solid #c7d2fe">👥 كل الطلاب</span>`;
  if(mode === 'stage'){
    const parts = [];
    if(item.targetStage) parts.push(item.targetStage);
    if(item.targetTrack) parts.push('مسار: ' + item.targetTrack);
    return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;background:#eef2ff;color:#4338ca;border:1px solid #c7d2fe">🎓 ${esc(parts.join(' • '))}</span>`;
  }
  if(mode === 'students'){
    return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;background:#eef2ff;color:#4338ca;border:1px solid #c7d2fe">🔍 ${(item.targetStudents||[]).length} طالب محدد</span>`;
  }
  return '';
}

/* ============ عرض المجلدات ============ */
function renderFoldersView(items, type, viewerMode){
  const f = ui.folderFilter;
  const filterKey = type === 'note' ? 'notesFolder' : type === 'lesson' ? 'lessonsFolder' : 'liveFolder';
  const currentFilter = f[filterKey] || 'all';

  const groups = {};
  items.forEach(item => {
    const folder = (item.folder || '').trim() || '__unfoldered__';
    if(!groups[folder]) groups[folder] = [];
    groups[folder].push(item);
  });

  const folderNames = Object.keys(groups).filter(k => k !== '__unfoldered__').sort();

  if(folderNames.length === 0){
    return items.slice().reverse().map(item => renderItemByType(item, type, viewerMode)).join('');
  }

  let html = '';

  html += `
    <div style="margin-bottom:20px">
      <div onclick="ui.folderFilter.${filterKey}='all';render()" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdfa,#ccfbf1);border:2px solid #99f6e4;border-radius:16px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:13px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#14b8a6,#0f766e);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:22px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(13,148,136,.35)">📂</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#0f766e;line-height:1.2">جميع المحتويات</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#0f766e;font-weight:700;margin-top:3px;opacity:.85">عرض كل العناصر</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:#fff;color:#0f766e;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;padding:6px 14px;border-radius:12px;border:1.5px solid #99f6e4;flex-shrink:0">${items.length}</div>
        <div style="font-size:20px;color:#0f766e;transition:transform .3s">${currentFilter === 'all' ? '▼' : '◀'}</div>
      </div>
      ${currentFilter === 'all' ? `
        <div style="margin-top:12px;padding-inline-start:18px;border-inline-start:3px dashed #99f6e4">
          ${items.slice().reverse().map(item => renderItemByType(item, type, viewerMode)).join('')}
        </div>
      ` : ''}
    </div>
  `;

  folderNames.forEach(folderName => {
    const folderItems = groups[folderName];
    const isOpen = currentFilter === folderName;
    html += `
      <div style="margin-bottom:20px">
        <div onclick="ui.folderFilter.${filterKey}='${esc(folderName).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}';render()" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border:2px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:16px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
          <div style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:13px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:22px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(217,119,6,.35)">📁</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#78350f;line-height:1.2">${esc(folderName)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#92400e;font-weight:700;margin-top:3px">${folderItems.length} عنصر</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:#fff;color:#d97706;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;padding:6px 14px;border-radius:12px;border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;flex-shrink:0">${folderItems.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:20px;color:#d97706;transition:transform .3s">${isOpen ? '▼' : '◀'}</div>
        </div>
        ${isOpen ? `
          <div style="margin-top:12px;padding-inline-start:18px;border-inline-start:3px dashed #fcd34d">
            ${folderItems.slice().reverse().map(item => renderItemByType(item, type, viewerMode)).join('')}
          </div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  });

  if(groups['__unfoldered__'] && groups['__unfoldered__'].length){
    const unItems = groups['__unfoldered__'];
    const isOpen = currentFilter === '__unfoldered__';
    html += `
      <div style="margin-bottom:20px">
        <div onclick="ui.folderFilter.${filterKey}='__unfoldered__';render()" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f1f5f9,#fff);border:2px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:16px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s">
          <div style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:13px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#94a3b8,#64748b);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:22px;flex-shrink:0">📄</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#475569;line-height:1.2">بدون مجلد</div>
            <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700;margin-top:3px">عناصر غير مصنّفة</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:#fff;color:#475569;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;padding:6px 14px;border-radius:12px;border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;flex-shrink:0">${unItems.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:20px;color:#64748b">${isOpen ? '▼' : '◀'}</div>
        </div>
        ${isOpen ? `
          <div style="margin-top:12px;padding-inline-start:18px;border-inline-start:3px dashed #cbd5e1">
            ${unItems.slice().reverse().map(item => renderItemByType(item, type, viewerMode)).join('')}
          </div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  return html;
}

function renderItemByType(item, type, viewerMode){
  if(type === 'note') return viewerMode === 'student' ? noteCardStudent(item) : noteCardTeacher(item);
  if(type === 'lesson') return viewerMode === 'student' ? lessonCardStudent(item) : lessonCardTeacher(item);
  if(type === 'live') return viewerMode === 'student' ? liveSessionCardStudent(item) : liveSessionCardTeacher(item);
  return '';
}
/* ============ إدارة الدروس للمعلم ============ */
function viewTeacherLessons(){
  const videoSource = ui.lessonVideoSource || 'link';
  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>

      <div>

        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">أضف وحرر وأدر جميع الدروس</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>${soundToggleHTML()}</div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">➕</span> إضافة درس جديد</div>

function lessonCardTeacher(l){
  const typeInfo = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === l.type) || SESSION_TYPES[1];
  const available = isItemAvailable(l);
  const isDevice = l.videoSource === 'device';
  return `
    <div class="lesson-card" style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;margin-bottom:14px;transition:all .25s">
      <div class="lesson-thumb" style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:16/9;display:grid;place-items:center;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,${typeInfo.color}55)">
        <span style="position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;background:${isDevice?'linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed)':'rgba(0,0,0,.55)'};color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:6px 13px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(8px);z-index:3">${isDevice?'📤 ':''}${typeInfo.label}</span>
        <div style="position:relative;z-index:2;width:70px;height:70px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);color:var(--primary-d);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:28px;box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(0,0,0,.4)">▶</div>
        ${l.duration ? `<span style="position:absolute;bottom:12px;left:12px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.65);color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px;z-index:3">${l.duration} د</span>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div style="padding:16px">
        <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px;line-height:1.5">${esc(l.title)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(l.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</span>
          ${isDevice ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📤 فيديو مرفوع</span>' : '<span class="badge b-info">🔗 رابط</span>'}
          ${!available ? `<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ مجدول: ${fmtDateTime12(l.startDate, l.startTime)}</span>` : '<span class="badge b-ok">✓ متاح</span>'}
          ${targetingSummaryHTML(l)}
        </div>
        ${l.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-top:10px">${esc(l.desc)}</div>` : ''}
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLessonPlayer('${l.id}')">▶ معاينة</button>
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteLessonConfirm('${l.id}')">🗑 حذف</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

async function handleLessonVideoSelect(event){
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if(!file){ return; }
  if(!file.type.startsWith('video/')){ toast('الرجاء اختيار ملف فيديو صالح','warn'); event.target.value=''; return; }
  if(file.size > MAX_VIDEO_SIZE_BYTES){ toast(`حجم الفيديو كبير (الحد ${MAX_VIDEO_SIZE_MB} ميجا)`,'warn'); event.target.value=''; return; }

  const zone = document.getElementById('lVideoUploadZone');
  const preview = document.getElementById('lVideoPreview');
  if(preview){
    preview.innerHTML = `
      <div class="video-upload-progress">
        <div class="vup-head"><span>📤 جاري تجهيز الفيديو...</span><span id="lVupPct">0%</span></div>
        <div class="vup-bar"><div class="vup-fill" id="lVupFill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
        <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-top:8px">${esc(file.name)} — ${fmtBytes(file.size)}</div>
      </div>`;
  }

  try{
    const blob = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onprogress = (e) => {
        if(e.lengthComputable){
          const pct = Math.round(e.loaded / e.total * 100);
          const fill = document.getElementById('lVupFill');
          const pctEl = document.getElementById('lVupPct');
          if(fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
          if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
        }
      };
      reader.onload = (e) => resolve(e.target.result);
      reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('فشل قراءة الملف'));
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    });

    ui.tempLessonVideo = { file, blob, name: file.name, size: file.size, type: file.type };

    if(preview){
      preview.innerHTML = `
        <div class="video-info-card">
          <div class="vic-ico">✓</div>
          <div class="vic-body">
            <div class="vic-name">${esc(file.name)}</div>
            <div class="vic-meta"><span>📦 ${fmtBytes(file.size)}</span><span>🎬 ${esc(file.type)}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="vic-actions">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();document.getElementById('lVideoFile').click()">تغيير</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();ui.tempLessonVideo=null;document.getElementById('lVideoPreview').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('lVideoFile').value=''">مسح</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
    if(zone) zone.classList.add('has-video');
    toast('تم تجهيز الفيديو — اضغط حفظ الدرس','gold');
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    toast('تعذر قراءة الفيديو','err');
    if(preview) preview.innerHTML = '';
    event.target.value = '';
  }
}

async function addLesson(){
  const title = val('lTitle');
  const type = val('lType');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const startDate = val('lStartDate');
  const startTime = val('lStartTime');
  const targeting = getTargetingFromForm('lesson');
  const folder = val('l_folderNew') || val('l_folderSelect');
  const folderValue = (folder && folder !== '__new__') ? folder : '';

  const base = {
    id: uid(),
    title, type,
    folder: folderValue,
    duration: val('lDuration'),
    desc: val('lDesc'),
    startDate, startTime,
    startAt: startDate ? combineToTimestamp(startDate, startTime || '00:00') : null,
    targetMode: targeting.targetMode,
    targetStage: targeting.targetStage,
    targetTrack: targeting.targetTrack,
    targetStudents: targeting.targetStudents,
    createdAt: Date.now()
  };

  if(ui.lessonVideoSource === 'device'){
    if(!ui.tempLessonVideo || !ui.tempLessonVideo.blob){
      toast('اختر ملف الفيديو أولًا','warn');
      return;
    }
    const videoId = 'vid_' + uid();
    try{
      toast('جارٍ رفع الفيديو إلى السحابة...', 'warn');
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_STORAGE_FOLDER + '/' + videoId);
      const blob = new Blob([ui.tempLessonVideo.blob], {type: ui.tempLessonVideo.type});
      const uploadTask = storageRef.put(blob);

      const preview = document.getElementById('lVideoPreview');
      if(preview){
        preview.innerHTML = `
          <div class="video-upload-progress">
            <div class="vup-head"><span>☁️ جاري الرفع...</span><span id="lCloudPct">0%</span></div>
            <div class="vup-bar"><div class="vup-fill" id="lCloudFill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
          </div>`;
      }

      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        uploadTask.on('state_changed',
          (snapshot) => {
            const pct = Math.round((snapshot.bytesTransferred / snapshot.totalBytes) * 100);
            const fill = document.getElementById('lCloudFill');
            const pctEl = document.getElementById('lCloudPct');
            if(fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
            if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
          },
          (error) => reject(error),
          () => resolve()
        );
      });

      base.videoSource = 'device';
      base.deviceVideoId = videoId;
      base.videoName = ui.tempLessonVideo.name;
      base.videoSize = ui.tempLessonVideo.size;
      base.url = '';
      toast('تم رفع الفيديو ✓','gold');
    }catch(e){
      console.error(e);
      toast('فشل رفع الفيديو','err');
      return;
    }
  } else {
    base.url = val('lUrl');
    base.videoSource = 'link';
    if(!base.url){ toast('رابط الفيديو مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  }

  db.lessons.push(base);
  const targets = getTargetsForItem(base);

  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
    targets.forEach((s, i) => {
      setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'lesson', base), i * 300);
    });
  } else {
    targets.forEach(s => {
      s.notifications.push({
        id: uid(),
        text: `📹 درس جديد: "${title}"${startDate ? ' — متاح من ' + fmtDateTime12(startDate, startTime) : ''}`,
        date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
      });
    });
  }

  ui.tempLessonVideo = null;
  ui.lessonVideoSource = 'link';
  ui.folderDraft.lesson = '';
  resetTargeting();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم إضافة الدرس ✓ — استهدف ${targets.length} طالب`,'gold');
  playSound('success');
}

async function deleteLessonConfirm(id){
  const lesson = db.lessons.find(l => l.id === id);
  if(!lesson) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا الدرس؟')) return;
  if(lesson.videoSource === 'device' && lesson.deviceVideoId){
    try{
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_STORAGE_FOLDER + '/' + lesson.deviceVideoId);
      await storageRef.delete();
    }catch(e){ console.warn('Could not delete video:', e); }
  }
  db.lessons = db.lessons.filter(x => x.id !== id);
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ إدارة المذكرات للمعلم ============ */
function viewTeacherNotes(){
  const isLink = ui.noteType === 'link';
  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(44)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2">إدارة المذكرات والملفات</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">رفع ملفات PDF أو إضافة روابط</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>${soundToggleHTML()}</div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">➕</span> إضافة ${isLink ? 'رابط' : 'مذكرة PDF'}</div>

      <div class="field"><label>عنوان المذكرة <span class="req">*</span></label><input id="nTitle" placeholder="مراجعة النحو الشاملة"></div>

    ${folderSelectorHTML('n', 'note')}
    ${targetingSelectorHTML('note')}
    ${waSendBoxHTML()}
    <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:16px;width:100%" onclick="addNote()">💾 ${isLink ? 'حفظ الرابط' : 'رفع المذكرة'}</button>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📄</span> المذكرات والملفات <span class="sec-sub">${db.notes.length}</span></div>
    ${db.notes.length ? renderFoldersView(db.notes, 'note', 'teacher') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد مذكرات بعد</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function noteCardTeacher(n){
  const isLink = (n.type === 'link');
  const gradient = isLink ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb)' : 'linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed)';
  const icon = isLink ? '🔗' : '📄';
  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:#fff">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:14px;background:${gradient};color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:24px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.2)">${icon}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px">${esc(n.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-top:6px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
            <span class="badge ${isLink?'b-info':'b-purple'}">${isLink?'🔗 رابط خارجي':'📄 PDF'}</span>
            ${n.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(n.subject)}</span>` : ''}
            <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(n.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</span>
            ${targetingSummaryHTML(n)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteNoteConfirm('${n.id}')">🗑</button>
      </div>
      ${n.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;line-height:1.8">${esc(n.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
        ${isLink
          ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(n.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(n.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">📖 معاينة</button>`
          : `<button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openNotePDF('${n.id}')">📄 معاينة</button>`}
        <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="editNoteModal('${n.id}')">✏️ تعديل</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function addNote(){
  const title = val('nTitle');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const targeting = getTargetingFromForm('note');
  const folder = val('n_folderNew') || val('n_folderSelect');
  const folderValue = (folder && folder !== '__new__') ? folder : '';
  const type = ui.noteType;

  if(type === 'link'){
    let url = val('nUrl');
    if(!url){ toast('الرابط مطلوب','warn'); return; }
    if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) url = 'https://' + url;
    const base = {
      id: uid(),
      type: 'link',
      title,
      folder: folderValue,
      url,
      subject: val('nSubject'),
      desc: val('nDesc'),
      targetMode: targeting.targetMode,
      targetStage: targeting.targetStage,
      targetTrack: targeting.targetTrack,
      targetStudents: targeting.targetStudents,
      createdAt: Date.now()
    };
    db.notes.push(base);
    const targets = getTargetsForItem(base);
    if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
      targets.forEach((s, i) => setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'note', base), i * 300));
    } else {
      targets.forEach(s => {
        s.notifications.push({ id: uid(), text: `📄 مذكرة جديدة: "${title}"`, date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now() });
      });
    }
    ui.folderDraft.note = '';
    resetTargeting();
    saveDB(); render();
    toast(`تم إضافة الرابط ✓ — استهدف ${targets.length} طالب`,'gold'); playSound('success');
    return;
  }

  const pdfInput = document.getElementById('nPdf');
  if(!pdfInput || !pdfInput.files[0]){ toast('ارفع ملف PDF أولًا','warn'); return; }
  const file = pdfInput.files[0];
  if(file.type !== 'application/pdf'){ toast('الملف يجب أن يكون PDF','warn'); return; }
  if(file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES){ toast(`حجم الملف كبير (الحد ${MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB}MB)`,'warn'); return; }

  const noteId = 'note_' + uid();
  const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_PDF_FOLDER + '/' + noteId);
  const uploadTask = storageRef.put(file);

  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--purple-d)">📄 جاري رفع المذكرة...</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${esc(title)} — ${fmtBytes(file.size)}</div>
    <div class="video-upload-progress" style="margin-top:14px">
      <div class="vup-head"><span>☁️ الرفع</span><span id="noteUploadPct">0%</span></div>
      <div class="vup-bar"><div class="vup-fill" id="noteUploadFill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
    </div>
  `);

  uploadTask.on('state_changed',
    (snapshot) => {
      const pct = Math.round((snapshot.bytesTransferred / snapshot.totalBytes) * 100);
      const fill = document.getElementById('noteUploadFill');
      const pctEl = document.getElementById('noteUploadPct');
      if(fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
      if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
    },
    (error) => {
      console.error('Upload error:', error);
      closeModal();
      toast('فشل رفع المذكرة','err');
    },
    async () => {
      try{
        const downloadURL = await uploadTask.snapshot.ref.getDownloadURL();
        const base = {
          id: noteId,
          type: 'pdf',
          title,
          folder: folderValue,
          subject: val('nSubject'),
          desc: val('nDesc'),
          pdfUrl: downloadURL,
          pdfName: file.name,
          pdfSize: file.size,
          targetMode: targeting.targetMode,
          targetStage: targeting.targetStage,
          targetTrack: targeting.targetTrack,
          targetStudents: targeting.targetStudents,
          createdAt: Date.now()
        };
        db.notes.push(base);
        const targets = getTargetsForItem(base);
        if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
          targets.forEach((s, i) => setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'note', base), i * 300));
        } else {
          targets.forEach(s => {
            s.notifications.push({ id: uid(), text: `📄 مذكرة جديدة: "${title}"`, date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now() });
          });
        }
        ui.folderDraft.note = '';
        resetTargeting();
        saveDB(); render();
        closeModal();
        toast(`تم رفع المذكرة ✓ — استهدف ${targets.length} طالب`,'gold'); playSound('success');
      }catch(e){
        console.error(e);
        closeModal();
        toast('تعذر إتمام العملية','err');
      }
    }
  );
}

function editNoteModal(noteId){
  const n = db.notes.find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!n) return;
  const isLink = n.type === 'link';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--purple-d)">✏️ تعديل المذكرة</div>
    <div class="field"><label>العنوان</label><input id="enTitle" value="${esc(n.title)}"></div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>المادة / الموضوع</label><input id="enSubject" value="${esc(n.subject||'')}"></div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>📁 المجلد</label><input id="enFolder" value="${esc(n.folder||'')}" placeholder="اسم المجلد (اختياري)"></div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>الوصف</label><textarea id="enDesc" style="min-height:80px">${esc(n.desc||'')}</textarea></div>
    ${isLink ? `<div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>الرابط</label><input id="enUrl" value="${esc(n.url||'')}"></div>` : ''}
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveNoteEdit('${noteId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveNoteEdit(noteId){
  const n = db.notes.find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!n) return;
  n.title = val('enTitle') || n.title;
  n.subject = val('enSubject');
  n.folder = val('enFolder');
  n.desc = val('enDesc');
  if(n.type === 'link') n.url = val('enUrl') || n.url;
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ التعديلات ✓','gold');
}

function deleteNoteConfirm(noteId){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه المذكرة؟')) return;
  const note = db.notes.find(n => n.id === noteId);
  if(note && note.pdfUrl && note.id.startsWith('note_')){
    try{
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_PDF_FOLDER + '/' + note.id);
      storageRef.delete().catch(e => console.warn('Could not delete PDF:', e));
    }catch(e){}
  }
  db.notes = db.notes.filter(x => x.id !== noteId);
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

        <div class="video-upload-zone" id="lvVideoUploadZone" onclick="document.getElementById('lvVideoFile').click()">

}

async function handleLiveVideoSelect(event){
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if(!file){ return; }
  if(!file.type.startsWith('video/')){ toast('الرجاء اختيار ملف فيديو صالح','warn'); event.target.value=''; return; }
  if(file.size > MAX_VIDEO_SIZE_BYTES){ toast(`حجم الفيديو كبير (الحد ${MAX_VIDEO_SIZE_MB} ميجا)`,'warn'); event.target.value=''; return; }

  const zone = document.getElementById('lvVideoUploadZone');
  const preview = document.getElementById('lvVideoPreview');
  if(preview){
    preview.innerHTML = `
      <div class="video-upload-progress">
        <div class="vup-head"><span>📤 جاري التجهيز...</span><span id="lvVupPct">0%</span></div>
        <div class="vup-bar"><div class="vup-fill" id="lvVupFill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
      </div>`;
  }

  try{
    const blob = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onprogress = (e) => {
        if(e.lengthComputable){
          const pct = Math.round(e.loaded / e.total * 100);
          const fill = document.getElementById('lvVupFill');
          const pctEl = document.getElementById('lvVupPct');
          if(fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
          if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
        }
      };
      reader.onload = (e) => resolve(e.target.result);
      reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('فشل قراءة الملف'));
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    });

    ui.tempLiveVideo = { file, blob, name: file.name, size: file.size, type: file.type };

    if(preview){
      preview.innerHTML = `
        <div class="video-info-card">
          <div class="vic-ico">✓</div>
          <div class="vic-body">
            <div class="vic-name">${esc(file.name)}</div>
            <div class="vic-meta"><span>📦 ${fmtBytes(file.size)}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="vic-actions">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();document.getElementById('lvVideoFile').click()">تغيير</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();ui.tempLiveVideo=null;document.getElementById('lvVideoPreview').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('lvVideoFile').value=''">مسح</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
    if(zone) zone.classList.add('has-video');
    toast('تم تجهيز الفيديو','gold');
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    toast('تعذر قراءة الفيديو','err');
    if(preview) preview.innerHTML = '';
    event.target.value = '';
  }
}

async function addLiveSession(){
  const title = val('lvTitle');
  const location = val('lvLocation');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  const startDate = val('lvDate');
  const startTime = val('lvTime');
  const targeting = getTargetingFromForm('live');
  const folder = val('lv_folderNew') || val('lv_folderSelect');
  const folderValue = (folder && folder !== '__new__') ? folder : '';

  const base = {
    id: uid(),
    title,
    folder: folderValue,
    location: location || '',
    duration: val('lvDur'),
    desc: val('lvDesc'),
    startDate, startTime,
    startAt: startDate ? combineToTimestamp(startDate, startTime || '00:00') : null,
    targetMode: targeting.targetMode,
    targetStage: targeting.targetStage,
    targetTrack: targeting.targetTrack,
    targetStudents: targeting.targetStudents,
    createdAt: Date.now()
  };

  if(ui.liveVideoSource === 'device'){
    if(!ui.tempLiveVideo || !ui.tempLiveVideo.blob){
      toast('اختر ملف الفيديو أولًا','warn');
      return;
    }
    const videoId = 'vid_' + uid();
    try{
      toast('جارٍ رفع الفيديو...', 'warn');
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_STORAGE_FOLDER + '/' + videoId);
      const blob = new Blob([ui.tempLiveVideo.blob], {type: ui.tempLiveVideo.type});
      const uploadTask = storageRef.put(blob);

      const preview = document.getElementById('lvVideoPreview');
      if(preview){
        preview.innerHTML = `
          <div class="video-upload-progress">
            <div class="vup-head"><span>☁️ الرفع...</span><span id="lvCloudPct">0%</span></div>
            <div class="vup-bar"><div class="vup-fill" id="lvCloudFill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
          </div>`;
      }

      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        uploadTask.on('state_changed',
          (snapshot) => {
            const pct = Math.round((snapshot.bytesTransferred / snapshot.totalBytes) * 100);
            const fill = document.getElementById('lvCloudFill');
            const pctEl = document.getElementById('lvCloudPct');
            if(fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
            if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
          },
          (error) => reject(error),
          () => resolve()
        );
      });

      base.videoSource = 'device';
      base.deviceVideoId = videoId;
      base.videoName = ui.tempLiveVideo.name;
      base.videoSize = ui.tempLiveVideo.size;
      base.url = '';
      if(!location){ base.location = 'فيديو مسجل'; }
      toast('تم رفع الفيديو ✓','gold');
    }catch(e){
      console.error(e);
      toast('فشل رفع الفيديو','err');
      return;
    }
  } else {
    base.url = val('lvUrl');
    base.videoSource = 'link';
    if(!location && !base.url){ toast('حدد مكان أو رابط','warn'); return; }
  }

  db.liveSessions.push(base);
  const targets = getTargetsForItem(base);
  if(ui.sendWA.toStudent || ui.sendWA.toParent){
    targets.forEach((s, i) => setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'live', base), i * 300));
  } else {
    targets.forEach(s => {
      s.notifications.push({
        id: uid(),
        text: `🎥 محاضرة أونلاين جديدة: "${title}"${startDate ? ' — ' + fmtDateTime12(startDate, startTime) : ''}`,
        date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
      });
    });
  }
  ui.tempLiveVideo = null;
  ui.liveVideoSource = 'link';
  ui.folderDraft.live = '';
  resetTargeting();
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم حفظ المحاضرة ✓ — استهدف ${targets.length} طالب`,'gold'); playSound('success');
}

function liveSessionCardTeacher(lv){
  const available = isItemAvailable(lv);
  const isDevice = lv.videoSource === 'device';
  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff,#fef2f2)">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#dc2626);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:24px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(220,38,38,.32)">🎥</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px">${esc(lv.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-top:6px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
            <span class="badge b-bad">🎥 محاضرة أونلاين</span>
            ${isDevice ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📤 فيديو مرفوع</span>' : ''}
            ${lv.startAt ? `<span>⏰ ${fmtDateTime12(lv.startDate, lv.startTime)}</span>` : ''}
            ${targetingSummaryHTML(lv)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteLiveSessionConfirm('${lv.id}')">🗑</button>
      </div>
      ${lv.location ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f1f5f9;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:11px;line-height:1.8;font-weight:700">📍 <b>المكان:</b> ${esc(lv.location)}</div>` : ''}
      ${lv.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#fff;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;line-height:1.8;margin-top:8px">${esc(lv.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
        ${available ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✓ متاح الآن</span>' : '<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ مجدول</span>'}
        ${isDevice ? `<button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openLivePlayerTeacher('${lv.id}')">📹 معاينة</button>` : ''}
        ${lv.url ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(lv.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(lv.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🔗 فتح الرابط</button>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function openLivePlayerTeacher(liveId){
  const lv = db.liveSessions.find(x => x.id === liveId);
  if(!lv) return;
  if(!lv.deviceVideoId){ toast('لا يوجد فيديو','warn'); return; }
  const viewerLabel = db.settings.teacherName || 'المعلم';
  playDeviceVideo(lv.deviceVideoId, lv.title, {color:'#dc2626', label:'🎥 محاضرة أونلاين'}, viewerLabel);
}

async function deleteLiveSessionConfirm(id){
  const lv = db.liveSessions.find(x => x.id === id);
  if(!lv) return;
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه المحاضرة؟')) return;
  if(lv.videoSource === 'device' && lv.deviceVideoId){
    try{
      const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_STORAGE_STORAGE_FOLDER || FIREBASE_STORAGE_FOLDER + '/' + lv.deviceVideoId);
      await storageRef.delete();
    }catch(e){ console.warn('Could not delete video:', e); }
  }
  db.liveSessions = db.liveSessions.filter(x => x.id !== id);
  saveDB(); render(); toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ الشهادات الجماعية ============ */
function viewTeacherCertificates(){
  const modes = [
    ['all','👥 جميع الطلاب'],
    ['stage','🎓 حسب الصف / المرحلة'],
    ['students','🔍 طلاب محددين']
  ];
  const students = db.students;
  const q = (ui.certSearch||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = students.filter(s => {
    if(!q) return true;
    return s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q);
  });
  let targets = [];
  if(ui.certMode === 'all') targets = students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  else if(ui.certMode === 'stage') targets = students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  else if(ui.certMode === 'students') targets = ui.certSelected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.status === 'active');

  return `

    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>

      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>${soundToggleHTML()}</div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎓</span> إعداد الشهادات الجماعية</div>

      <textarea id="bcCertDesc" style="min-height:90px">أتم الطالب بنجاح دورة اللغة العربية وحصل على تقدير ممتاز</textarea>

          <option>مقبول</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>تصميم الشهادة</label>
        <select id="bcCertDesign">${CERT_DESIGNS.map(d => `<option value="${d.id}">${d.name}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="sec-title" style="font-size:14px;margin-top:20px"><span class="ico" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:14px">🎯</span> الفئة المستهدفة</div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">

    </div>

    ${ui.certMode === 'stage' ? `

          <select id="bcCertStage">
            <option value="">كل المراحل</option>

          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label>المسار</label>
          <select id="bcCertTrack">
            <option value="">كل المسارات</option>
