function rendercertTargetList(){
  const q = (ui.certSearch||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = db.students.filter(s => !q || s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q));
  if(!list.length) return `<div class="empty" style="padding:20px;font-size:13px">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
  return list.slice(0, 300).map(s => {
    const sel = ui.certSelected.includes(s.id);
    return `
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;background:${sel?'#f0fdfa':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${sel?'#99f6e4':'transparent'};margin-bottom:4px">
        <input type="checkbox" ${sel?'checked':''} onchange="toggleCertStudent('${s.id}')" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--primary)">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')} • ${esc(s.phone)} ${s.status!=='active'?'• ⏳ غير مفعل':''}</div>
        </div>
      </label>`;
  }).join('');
}

function toggleCertStudent(id){
  const i = ui.certSelected.indexOf(id);
  if(i >= 0) ui.certSelected.splice(i, 1);
  else ui.certSelected.push(id);
  render();
}

function issueBulkCertificates(){
  const title = val('bcCertTitle') || 'شهادة إتمام';
  const desc = val('bcCertDesc');
  const grade = val('bcCertGrade');
  const design = val('bcCertDesign') || 'classic_gold';
  if(!title || !desc){ toast('العنوان والوصف مطلوبان','warn'); return; }

  let targets = [];
  if(ui.certMode === 'all'){
    targets = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  } else if(ui.certMode === 'stage'){
    const stage = document.getElementById('bcCertStage')?.value || '';
    const track = document.getElementById('bcCertTrack')?.value || '';
    targets = db.students.filter(s => {
      if(s.status !== 'active') return false;
      if(stage && s.stage !== stage) return false;
      if(track && (s.track||'عام') !== track) return false;
      return true;
    });
  } else if(ui.certMode === 'students'){
    targets = ui.certSelected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  }
  if(!targets.length){ toast('لا يوجد طلاب مستهدفون','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`سيتم إصدار شهادة "${title}" لـ ${targets.length} طالب. متابعة؟`)) return;

  const sendStudent = ui.sendWA.toStudent;
  const sendParent = ui.sendWA.toParent;

  targets.forEach(s => {
    const c = {
      id: uid(),
      title, desc, grade, design,
      issuedAt: Date.now(),
      studentName: s.name,
      teacherName: db.settings.teacherName,
      stage: s.stage
    };
    s.certificates.push(c);
    s.notifications.push({
      id: uid(),
      text: `🎓 مبروك! تم إصدار شهادتك "${title}" بتقدير ${grade}.`,
      date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
    });
    logStudentActivity(s.id, 'cert', 'إصدار شهادة', title);
    if(sendStudent && s.phone){
      setTimeout(() => {
        const msg = `🎓 *مبروك ${s.name}!*\n\nتم إصدار شهادتك من منصة الأستاذ ${db.settings.teacherName}.\n\n📜 العنوان: ${title}\n🏅 التقدير: ${grade}\n\nيرجى الدخول للمنصة لطباعة الشهادة.\n\nبالتوفيق 🌟`;
        sendWhatsApp(s.phone, msg, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: 'student', category: 'certificate', title, studentId: s.id });
      }, 300);
    }
    if(sendParent && s.parentPhone){
      setTimeout(() => {
        const msg = `🎓 *مبروك لولي أمر الطالب ${s.name}!*\n\nتم إصدار شهادة إتمام.\n\n📜 العنوان: ${title}\n🏅 التقدير: ${grade}\n\nبالتوفيق 🌟`;
        sendWhatsApp(s.parentPhone, msg, { recipientName: s.name + ' — ولي الأمر', recipientType: 'parent', category: 'certificate', title, studentId: s.id });
      }, 700);
    }
  });

  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم إصدار ${targets.length} شهادة ✓`,'gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

/* ============ الحسابات بانتظار التفعيل ============ */
function viewPendingAccounts(){
  const pending = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'pending');
  const q = (ui.pendingSearch||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  let filtered = pending.filter(s => {
    if(!q) return true;
    return s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q) || (s.parentPhone||'').includes(q);
  });
  if(ui.pendingFilter === 'withPayment') filtered = filtered.filter(s => s.payment && s.payment.status === 'pending');
  if(ui.pendingFilter === 'noPayment') filtered = filtered.filter(s => !s.payment);

  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(44)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2">حسابات بانتظار التفعيل</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">راجع الحسابات واطلب الدفع أو فعّل مباشرة</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
      ${soundToggleHTML()}
      <button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openWAArchiveModal()">📨 الأرشيف</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico">⏳</span> الحسابات المنتظرة
      <span class="sec-sub">${filtered.length} من ${pending.length}</span>
    </div>

    <div class="search-wrap">
      <span class="s-ico">🔍</span>
      <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم، الهاتف..." value="${esc(ui.pendingSearch)}" oninput="ui.pendingSearch=this.value;render()">
    </div>

    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">
      <button class="btn ${ui.pendingFilter==='all'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="ui.pendingFilter='all';render()">👥 الكل (${pending.length})</button>
      <button class="btn ${ui.pendingFilter==='withPayment'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="ui.pendingFilter='withPayment';render()">💰 طلبوا الدفع (${pending.filter(s=>s.payment&&s.payment.status==='pending').length})</button>
      <button class="btn ${ui.pendingFilter==='noPayment'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="ui.pendingFilter='noPayment';render()">💤 بدون دفع (${pending.filter(s=>!s.payment).length})</button>
    </div>

    ${filtered.length ? filtered.slice().reverse().map(s => {
      const hasPayment = s.payment && s.payment.status === 'pending';
      const paymentInfo = s.payment ? `طلب خطة "${esc(s.payment.planName)}" بمبلغ ${fmtPrice(s.payment.amount)}` : 'لم يطلب خطة بعد';
      return `
        <div class="item" style="flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch;gap:12px">
          <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;flex-wrap:wrap">
            ${avatar(s.name, 52)}
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div class="item-title" style="font-size:16px">${esc(s.name)}</div>
              <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:6px">
                <span>📱 ${esc(s.phone)}</span>
                ${s.parentPhone ? `<span>👨‍👩‍👦 ${esc(s.parentPhone)}</span>` : '<span style="color:var(--danger)">⚠️ لا يوجد ولي أمر</span>'}
                <span>📚 ${esc(s.stage||'—')}</span>
                <span class="track-tag${(s.track||'عام')==='بكالوريا'?' bac':''}">${esc(s.track||'عام')}</span>
                ${s.phoneVerified ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#ede9fe;color:#6d28d9">🔐 موثّق</span>' : ''}
              </div>
              <div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:${hasPayment?'#92400e':'#64748b'};background:${hasPayment?'#fef3c7':'#f1f5f9'};padding:8px 12px;border-radius:10px;display:inline-block">
                ${hasPayment ? '💰 ' + paymentInfo : '💤 ' + paymentInfo}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            ${hasPayment ? `
              <button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" onclick="approvePayment('${s.id}')">✓ تفعيل (تم الدفع)</button>
              <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="rejectPayment('${s.id}')">✗ رفض</button>
              <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="viewPaymentDetails('${s.id}')">🔍 التفاصيل</button>
            ` : `
              <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="activateStudent('${s.id}');render()">✓ تفعيل مباشر</button>
              <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="requestPaymentFromStudent('${s.id}')">💰 طلب الدفع</button>
            `}
            <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="waStudentOrParent('${s.id}')">💬 واتساب</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="openStudentDetail('${s.id}')">📄 فتح</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteStudentConfirm('${s.id}')">🗑</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🎉</span>لا توجد حسابات بانتظار التفعيل</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function viewPaymentDetails(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s || !s.payment) return;
  const p = s.payment;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#92400e">💰 تفاصيل الدفع</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${esc(s.name)} — ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
    <div class="grade-review-card pending">
      <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:11px">
        <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">الخطة</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(p.planName)}</div></div>
        <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">المبلغ</div><div class="gic-value">${fmtPrice(p.amount)}</div></div>
        <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">رقم العملية</div><div class="gic-value" style="font-size:14px">${esc(p.transactionId||'—')}</div></div>
        <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">التاريخ</div><div class="gic-value" style="font-size:14px">${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(p.submittedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div></div>
      </div>
      ${p.screenshot ? `<img src="${esc(p.screenshot)}" style="max-width:100%;max-height:280px;border-radius:11px;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;margin-top:10px;display:block" alt="صورة التحويل">` : ''}
      ${p.note ? `<div style="background:#fff;padding:11px 14px;border-radius:11px;margin-top:10px;font-size:13px"><b>ملاحظة:</b> ${esc(p.note)}</div>` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:14px">
      <button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="approvePayment('${s.id}');closeModal()">✓ تفعيل</button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger-soft" onclick="rejectPayment('${s.id}');closeModal()">✗ رفض</button>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}

function requestPaymentFromStudent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const plan = (db.settings.prices.plans || [])[0];
  const msg = `السلام عليكم ${s.name},\n\nلتأكيد اشتراكك في منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية للغة العربية، يرجى إتمام الدفع عبر فودافون كاش:\n\n📱 الرقم: ${db.settings.vodafoneCash}\n💵 الاشتراك الشهري: ${plan ? fmtPrice(plan.price) : '—'}\n\nبعد التحويل يرجى رفع صورة الإشعار في المنصة.\n\nبالتوفيق 🌟`;
  const target = s.parentPhone || s.phone;
  sendWhatsApp(target, msg, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: s.parentPhone ? 'parent' : 'student', category: 'payment', title: 'طلب الدفع', studentId: s.id });
  toast('تم فتح واتساب ✓','gold');
}

function waStudentOrParent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const hasParent = !!s.parentPhone;
  if(!hasParent){
    sendWhatsApp(s.phone, `مرحبًا ${s.name} — من منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية`, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: 'student', category: 'custom', studentId: s.id });
    return;
  }
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#128c7e">💬 إلى مَن تريد الإرسال؟</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">اختر المستقبل</div>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;color:#128c7e;font-size:14px">
        <input type="radio" name="waTarget" value="student" checked style="accent-color:#25d366;width:18px;height:18px">
        📱 الطالب — ${esc(s.phone)}
      </label>
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;color:#128c7e;font-size:14px">
        <input type="radio" name="waTarget" value="parent" style="accent-color:#25d366;width:18px;height:18px">
        👨‍👩‍👦 ولي الأمر — ${esc(s.parentPhone)}
      </label>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:14px">
      <label>نص الرسالة</label>
      <textarea id="waCustomText" style="min-height:100px;font-size:13.5px">مرحبًا ${esc(s.name)} — من منصة الأستاذ صلاح عطية للغة العربية</textarea>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp" onclick="doWaStudentOrParent('${s.id}')">📤 إرسال</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function doWaStudentOrParent(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const target = document.querySelector('input[name="waTarget"]:checked')?.value || 'student';
  const text = val('waCustomText');
  if(!text){ toast('النص فارغ','warn'); return; }
  const phone = target === 'parent' ? s.parentPhone : s.phone;
  sendWhatsApp(phone, text, {
    recipientName: s.name + (target === 'parent' ? ' — ولي الأمر' : ''),
    recipientType: target === 'parent' ? 'parent' : 'student',
    category: 'custom',
    title: 'رسالة مخصصة',
    studentId: s.id
  });
  closeModal();
  toast('تم فتح واتساب ✓','gold');
}

/* ============ إحصائيات المنصة للمعلم ============ */
function viewTeacherStats(){
  const stats = computeTeacherStats();
  const tabs = [
    ['overview','📊 نظرة عامة'],
    ['students','👥 الطلاب'],
    ['content','📚 المحتوى'],
    ['finance','💰 المالية'],
    ['activity','📈 النشاط']
  ];
  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(44)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2">إحصائيات المنصة</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">نظرة شاملة على جميع البيانات</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
      ${soundToggleHTML()}
      <button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openWAArchiveModal()">📨 الأرشيف</button>
      <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="openFullPlatformReportModal()">📄 تقرير شامل</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}
  <div class="tabs">
    ${tabs.map(([k,l]) => `<button class="${ui.teacherStatTab===k?'active':''}" onclick="ui.teacherStatTab='${k}';render()">${l}</button>`).join('')}
  </div>
  <div id="teacherStatsBody">${teacherStatsBody(stats)}</div>`;
}

function computeTeacherStats(){
  const s = db.students;
  const total = s.length;
  const active = s.filter(x => x.status === 'active').length;
  const pending = s.filter(x => x.status === 'pending').length;
  const suspended = s.filter(x => x.status === 'suspended').length;
  const trackAm = s.filter(x => (x.track||'عام') === 'عام').length;
  const trackBac = s.filter(x => (x.track||'عام') === 'بكالوريا').length;
  const byStage = {};
  STAGES.forEach(st => byStage[st] = s.filter(x => x.stage === st).length);

  const totalHW = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.homework.length,0);
  const totalEx = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.exams.length,0);
  const totalEvals = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.evaluations.length,0);
  const totalAutoEvals = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+(x.autoEvaluations||[]).length,0);
  const totalHWSubmitted = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.homework.filter(h=>h.submitted).length,0);
  const totalExSubmitted = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.exams.filter(e=>e.submitted).length,0);
  const hwSubmissionRate = totalHW ? Math.round(totalHWSubmitted / totalHW * 100) : 0;
  const exSubmissionRate = totalEx ? Math.round(totalExSubmitted / totalEx * 100) : 0;

  const attendedCount = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.evaluations.filter(e=>e.attend==='حاضر').length,0);
  const lateCount = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.evaluations.filter(e=>e.attend==='متأخر').length,0);
  const absentCount = s.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.evaluations.filter(e=>e.attend==='غائب').length,0);
  const attendanceRate = totalEvals ? Math.round((attendedCount + lateCount * 0.5) / totalEvals * 100) : 0;

  let revenue = 0;
  const historyRevenue = [];
  s.forEach(x => {
    (x.paymentHistory || []).forEach(p => {
      revenue += parseFloat(p.amount) || 0;
      historyRevenue.push({ at: p.approvedAt, amt: parseFloat(p.amount) || 0, student: x.name });
    });
  });
  const pendingRevenue = s.filter(x => x.payment && x.payment.status === 'pending')
    .reduce((a,x)=>a+(parseFloat(x.payment.amount)||0),0);
  const thisMonthStart = new Date(); thisMonthStart.setDate(1); thisMonthStart.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const monthRevenue = historyRevenue.filter(h => h.at >= thisMonthStart.getTime()).reduce((a,h)=>a+h.amt,0);

  const totalLessons = db.lessons.length;
  const totalNotes = db.notes.length;
  const totalLive = db.liveSessions.length;
  const totalCompletedCycles = s.reduce((a,x) => a + ((x.subscription && x.subscription.completedCycles) ? x.subscription.completedCycles.length : 0), 0);

  const topStudents = s.map(x => ({
    name: x.name, id: x.id,
    overall: computeStudentStats(x).overall,
    stage: x.stage, track: x.track
  })).filter(x => x.overall !== null).sort((a,b)=>b.overall - a.overall).slice(0, 10);

  const recentPayments = historyRevenue.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.at-a.at).slice(0, 8);

  return {
    total, active, pending, suspended, trackAm, trackBac, byStage,
    totalHW, totalEx, totalEvals, totalAutoEvals, totalHWSubmitted, totalExSubmitted,
    hwSubmissionRate, exSubmissionRate,
    attendedCount, lateCount, absentCount, attendanceRate,
    revenue, pendingRevenue, monthRevenue, recentPayments,
    totalLessons, totalNotes, totalLive, totalCompletedCycles, topStudents
  };
}

function teacherStatsBody(st){
  switch(ui.teacherStatTab){
    case 'overview': return statsOverview(st);
    case 'students': return statsStudents(st);
    case 'content': return statsContent(st);
    case 'finance': return statsFinance(st);
    case 'activity': return statsActivity(st);
  }
  return '';
}

      </div>
