function statsActivity(st){
  const activityList = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => {
    s.homework.forEach(h => { if(h.submittedAt) activityList.push({ type: 'واجب', student: s.name, at: h.submittedAt, extra: h.title, kind: 'homework', id: s.id }); });
    s.exams.forEach(e => { if(e.submittedAt) activityList.push({ type: 'اختبار', student: s.name, at: e.submittedAt, extra: e.title, kind: 'exam', id: s.id }); });
    s.evaluations.forEach(e => { activityList.push({ type: 'تقييم', student: s.name, at: e.createdAt, extra: e.session || 'حصة', kind: 'eval', id: s.id }); });
  });
  activityList.sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
  const recent = activityList.slice(0, 15);
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🔥</span> أحدث الأنشطة</div>
    ${recent.length ? recent.map(a => {
      const color = a.type === 'واجب' ? '#2563eb' : a.type === 'اختبار' ? '#7c3aed' : '#d97706';
      return `
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:11px 14px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer" onclick="openStudentDetail('${a.id}')">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
            <span style="display:inline-grid;place-items:center;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;background:${color}22;color:${color};font-size:14px">${a.type === 'واجب' ? '📝' : a.type === 'اختبار' ? '📊' : '🎯'}</span>
            <div>
              <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800">${esc(a.student)} — ${esc(a.type)}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">${esc(a.extra)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(a.at).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🌱</span>لا يوجد نشاط بعد</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ عرض الطالب الكامل ============ */
function viewStudent(){
  const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
  if(!s){ ui.screen = 'studentLogin'; return viewStudentLogin(); }

  const unread = s.notifications.filter(n => !n.read).length;
  const pendingApprovalEvals = s.evaluations.filter(e => e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() && !e.parentApproved);
  const pendingApprovalCount = pendingApprovalEvals.length;
  const autoEvalsCount = (s.autoEvaluations || []).length;
  const activityCount = (s.activityLog || []).length;
  const completedCyclesCount = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles.length : 0;

  const tabs = [
    ['overview','🏠 الرئيسية'],
    ['myData','👤 بياناتي'],
    ['achievements','🏆 التحصيل'],
    ['autoProgress','📈 تحصيلي'],
    ['lessons','📹 الدروس'],
    ['live','🎥 محاضرات'],
    ['notes','📄 المذكرات'],
    ['homework','📝 الواجبات'],
    ['exams','📊 الاختبارات'],
    ['evals','🎯 التقييم'],
    ['schedule','📅 الجدول'],
    ['stats','📈 أدائي'],
    ['activity','📊 سجلي'],
    ['notifs','🔔 الإشعارات'],
    ['certificates','🎓 شهاداتي']
  ];

  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(44)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2">منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">مرحبًا، ${esc(s.name)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
      ${soundToggleHTML()}
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="logout()">خروج</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML('student')}
  <div class="stu-hero anim-in">
    ${avatar(s.name, 62)}
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
      <div class="name">${esc(s.name)}</div>
      <div class="sub">${s.stage ? '📚 ' + esc(s.stage) : 'طالب'}</div>
      <div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${(s.track||'عام') === 'بكالوريا' ? '<span class="track-tag bac">🎓 بكالوريا</span>' : '<span class="track-tag">📘 عام</span>'}
        ${s.phoneVerified ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#ede9fe;color:#6d28d9">🔐 رقم موثّق</span>' : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    ${s.status === 'active' ? '<span class="active-badge">✓ مفعّل</span>' : s.status === 'suspended' ? '<span class="suspended-badge">⛔ موقوف</span>' : '<span class="pending-badge">⏳ بانتظار</span>'}
  </div>

  ${!s.status || s.status === 'pending' ? renderPaymentRequired(s) : ''}
  ${s.payment && s.payment.status === 'pending' ? `
    <div class="pending-notice" style="margin-top:14px">
      <div class="pn-title"><span class="pn-ico">⏳</span> بانتظار تفعيل حسابك</div>
      <div class="pn-note">
        ✅ استلمنا بيانات الدفع (${esc(s.payment.planName)} — ${fmtPrice(s.payment.amount)}) — بانتظار تفعيل المعلم.
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
  ${s.payment && s.payment.status === 'rejected' ? `
    <div class="pending-notice" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef2f2,#fff);border-color:#fecaca">
      <div class="pn-title" style="color:#991b1b"><span class="pn-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f87171,#dc2626)">❌</span> تم رفض طلب الدفع</div>
      <div class="pn-note" style="color:#991b1b;border-color:#fecaca">السبب: ${esc(s.payment.rejectionReason || '—')}</div>
    </div>` : ''}

  ${s.status === 'active' && pendingApprovalCount > 0 ? `
    <div class="parent-approval-alert" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border:2px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:18px;padding:16px 18px;cursor:pointer;position:relative;overflow:hidden" onclick="openPendingApprovalEval('${s.id}')">
      <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:5px;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#fbbf24,#d97706)"></div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">
        <div style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:13px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:22px;flex-shrink:0;animation:pulseRing 2s infinite">✍️</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-size:15.5px;font-weight:900;color:#78350f">تقييم بحاجة إلى اعتماد ولي الأمر</div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#92400e;font-weight:700;margin-top:3px">اضغط لعرض التقييم</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:#fff;color:#d97706;font-size:20px;font-weight:900;padding:7px 15px;border-radius:13px;border:1.5px solid #fcd34d">${pendingApprovalCount}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  ` : ''}

  ${s.status === 'active' ? `
    <div class="tabs">
      ${tabs.map(([k,l]) => `<button class="${ui.stuTab===k?'active':''}" onclick="setStudentTab('${k}')">${l}${k==='notifs'&&unread?`<span class="badge-dot">${unread}</span>`:''}${k==='activity'&&activityCount?`<span class="badge-dot" style="background:var(--purple);color:#fff">${Math.min(99,activityCount)}</span>`:''}${k==='autoProgress'&&autoEvalsCount?`<span class="badge-dot" style="background:#a78bfa;color:#fff">${Math.min(99,autoEvalsCount)}</span>`:''}</button>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="protect-content" id="studentProtectedContent">${stuBody(s)}</div>
  ` : ''}`;
}

function setStudentTab(tab){
  ui.stuTab = tab;
  if(tab === 'notifs'){
    const s = getStudent(ui.studentToken);
    if(s){
      let changed = false;
      s.notifications.forEach(n => { if(!n.read){ n.read = true; changed = true; } });
      if(changed) saveDB();
    }
  }
  if(tab === 'homework'){ ui.quizHwId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; }
  if(tab === 'exams'){ ui.quizExamId = null; ui.quizStepIndex = 0; }
  render();
}

function openPendingApprovalEval(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  ui.stuTab = 'evals';
  render();
  setTimeout(() => {
    const firstPending = s.evaluations.find(e => e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() && !e.parentApproved);
    if(firstPending){
      const el = document.getElementById('eval_' + firstPending.id);
      if(el){
        el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'});
        el.style.transition = 'box-shadow .5s';
        el.style.boxShadow = '0 0 0 3px #fbbf24, 0 12px 30px rgba(217,119,6,.35)';
        setTimeout(() => { el.style.boxShadow = ''; }, 3000);
      }
    }
  }, 200);
}

function renderPaymentRequired(s){
  const plans = db.settings.prices.plans || [];
  const selected = ui.selectedPlanId;
  const selectedPlan = selected ? plans.find(p => p.id === selected) : null;
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#fff">💰</span>
      اختر خطة الاشتراك
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:13px;color:#78350f;font-weight:700;line-height:1.85;margin-bottom:14px">
      لتفعيل حسابك، اختر الخطة المناسبة وأتمم الدفع عبر فودافون كاش.
    </div>
    <div class="grid g3" style="margin-bottom:20px">
      ${plans.map(p => `
        <div style="background:#fff;border:2px solid ${selected===p.id?'var(--gold-2)':'var(--border)'};border-radius:18px;padding:18px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s;position:relative;overflow:hidden;${selected===p.id?'box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(217,119,6,.25)':''}" onclick="selectPlan('${p.id}')">
          ${p.featured ? '<div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#451a03;font-size:11px;font-weight:900;padding:5px 14px;border-bottom-left-radius:14px">⭐ الأكثر اختيارًا</div>' : ''}
          <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">${esc(p.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:32px;font-weight:900;color:var(--primary-d);line-height:1;margin-bottom:6px">${esc(p.price)}<span style="font-size:14px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-inline-start:5px">${currencySymbol()}</span></div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px">📅 ${esc(p.duration)}</div>
          <ul style="list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0 0 14px">
            ${(p.features||[]).map(f => `<li style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;font-weight:600;padding:5px 0;padding-inline-start:20px;position:relative"><span style="position:absolute;right:0;color:var(--ok);font-weight:900">✓</span>${esc(f)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
          ${selected === p.id ? `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:var(--ok);background:#dcfce7;padding:8px;border-radius:10px">✓ تم الاختيار</div>` : `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;color:var(--primary-d)">اضغط للاختيار</div>`}
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  </div>

  ${selectedPlan ? `
    <div id="paymentSection" class="vodafone-box anim-in" style="margin-top:14px">
      <div class="vb-head">
        <div class="vb-ico">📱</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <h3>الدفع عبر فودافون كاش</h3>
          <p>اتبع الخطوات التالية لإتمام الدفع</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="vodafone-number" onclick="copyText('${VODAFONE_CASH_NUMBER}')">
        <div>
          <small>رقم فودافون كاش</small>
          ${VODAFONE_CASH_NUMBER}
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:18px">📋</span>
      </div>
      <div class="vodafone-steps">
        <ol>
          <li>افتح محفظة فودافون كاش</li>
          <li>اختر "تحويل" ثم أدخل الرقم: <b style="color:#dc2626">${VODAFONE_CASH_NUMBER}</b></li>
          <li>أدخل المبلغ: <b style="color:#dc2626">${fmtPrice(selectedPlan.price)}</b></li>
          <li>أكمل التحويل واحتفظ برقم العملية</li>
          <li>ارفع صورة واضحة للإشعار مع كتابة رقم العملية</li>
        </ol>
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px">
        <label>رقم العملية (اختياري)</label>
        <input id="payTxnId" placeholder="مثال: 1234567890" inputmode="numeric">
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px">
        <label>ملاحظة إضافية</label>
        <input id="payNote" placeholder="مثال: تم التحويل من رقم 01xxxxxxxxx">
      </div>
      <div class="img-upload-zone" id="payUploadZone" onclick="document.getElementById('payScreenshot').click()">
        <span class="iuz-ico">📷</span>
        <div class="iuz-txt">اضغط لرفع صورة إشعار التحويل</div>
        <div class="iuz-sub">صورة واضحة JPG / PNG</div>
        <input type="file" id="payScreenshot" accept="image/*" onchange="handlePaymentScreenshot(event)">
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-vodafone btn-lg btn-block" style="margin-top:16px" onclick="submitPayment('${s.id}')">📤 إرسال بيانات الدفع</button>
    </div>
  ` : ''}`;
}

/* ============ جسم الطالب (التبويبات) ============ */
function stuBody(s){
  switch(ui.stuTab){
    case 'overview': return stuOverview(s);
    case 'myData': return stuMyData(s);
    case 'achievements': return openStudentAchievementsTab(s);
    case 'autoProgress': return stuAutoProgress(s);
    case 'lessons': return stuLessons(s);
    case 'live': return stuLiveSessions(s);
    case 'notes': return stuNotes(s);
    case 'homework': return stuHomework(s);
    case 'exams': return stuExams(s);
    case 'evals': return stuEvals(s);
    case 'schedule': return stuSchedule(s);
    case 'stats': return `<div style="margin-top:4px">${comprehensiveStudentChartHTML(s)}${progressDashboardHTML(s)}</div>`;
    case 'activity': return stuActivity(s);
    case 'notifs': return stuNotifs(s);
    case 'certificates': return stuCertificates(s);
  }
  return '';
}

function stuActivity(s){
  const log = (s.activityLog || []).slice().reverse();
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#22d3ee,#0891b2);color:#fff">📊</span>
      سجل أنشطتي <span class="sec-sub">${log.length}</span>
      <span class="sec-sub" style="color:var(--danger)">🔒 للعرض فقط</span>
    </div>
    ${log.length ? log.map(a => {
      const info = getActivityTypeInfo(a.type);
      return `
        <div class="activity-log-item">
          <div class="ali-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${info.color},${info.color}cc)">${info.icon}</div>
          <div class="ali-body">
            <div class="ali-title">${esc(a.title)}</div>
            ${a.meta ? `<div class="ali-meta">${esc(a.meta)}</div>` : ''}
            <div class="ali-meta">
              <span>🕐 ${new Date(a.at).toLocaleString('ar-EG')}</span>
              <span style="background:#f0fdfa;color:#0f766e;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:10.5px">${info.label}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا يوجد نشاط</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function stuMyData(s){
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico">👤</span> بياناتي الشخصية
      <span class="sec-sub" style="color:var(--danger)">🔒 للعرض فقط</span>
    </div>
    <div class="notif-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d">
      <span class="ico-b">ℹ️</span>
      <div class="txt-b" style="color:#78350f">لتعديل أي بيانات، تواصل مع المعلم.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:14px">
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">👤 الاسم</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(s.name)}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">📱 رقم الهاتف</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(s.phone)} ${s.phoneVerified ? '<span style="color:#6d28d9;font-size:11px">✓ موثّق</span>' : ''}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">👨‍👩‍👦 رقم ولي الأمر</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(s.parentPhone || '—')}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">📚 المرحلة</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(s.stage || '—')}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">🎓 المسار</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(s.track || 'عام')}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">💰 الخطة</div><div class="gic-value">${s.plan ? esc(s.plan.name) : '—'}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">📅 تاريخ الانتهاء</div><div class="gic-value">${s.expiresAt ? fmtDateWithDay(new Date(s.expiresAt).toISOString().slice(0,10)) : '—'}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">✅ الحالة</div><div class="gic-value">${s.status === 'active' ? 'مفعّل' : s.status === 'pending' ? 'بانتظار' : 'موقوف'}</div></div>
    </div>
    ${s.notes ? `
      <div style="margin-top:14px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:14px">
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:800;margin-bottom:6px">📝 ملاحظات المعلم</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.85;color:var(--text);font-weight:600">${esc(s.notes)}</div>
      </div>
    ` : ''}
  </div>`;
}

function stuOverview(s){
  const recent = s.notifications.slice(-1)[0];
  const upcomingEval = latestUpcoming(s);
  const lessonsCount = db.lessons.filter(l => isTargetedToStudent(l, s)).length;
  const notesCount = db.notes.filter(n => isTargetedToStudent(n, s)).length;
  const liveCount = db.liveSessions.filter(lv => isTargetedToStudent(lv, s)).length;
  return `
  <div class="grid g4 anim-in">
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="setStudentTab('lessons')"><span class="ico-stat">📹</span><div class="num">${lessonsCount}</div><div class="lbl">درس متاح</div></div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="setStudentTab('live')"><span class="ico-stat">🎥</span><div class="num">${liveCount}</div><div class="lbl">محاضرة أونلاين</div></div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="setStudentTab('notes')"><span class="ico-stat">📄</span><div class="num">${notesCount}</div><div class="lbl">مذكرة</div></div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="setStudentTab('homework')"><span class="ico-stat">📝</span><div class="num">${s.homework.length}</div><div class="lbl">واجبات</div></div>
    <div class="stat clickable" onclick="setStudentTab('exams')"><span class="ico-stat">📊</span><div class="num">${s.exams.length}</div><div class="lbl">اختبارات</div></div>
  </div>
  ${comprehensiveStudentChartHTML(s)}
  ${progressDashboardHTML(s)}
  ${upcomingEval ? renderUpcomingWidget(upcomingEval) : ''}
  ${recent ? `
  <div class="card anim-in-d2" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🔔</span> آخر إشعار</div>
    <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;color:var(--text);background:#f8fafc;padding:14px;border-radius:12px;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--gold)">
      ${esc(recent.text)}
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:8px;font-weight:600">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(recent.date)}</div>
    </div>
  </div>` : ''}
  <div class="card anim-in-d3" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📅</span> حصص اليوم</div>
    ${todaySessionsHTML(s)}
  </div>`;
}

function latestUpcoming(s){
  for(let i = s.evaluations.length - 1; i >= 0; i--){
    const e = s.evaluations[i];
    if(e.upcoming && e.upcoming.length) return e;
  }
  return null;
}
function renderUpcomingWidget(evalItem){
  return `
  <div class="upcoming-widget" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="uw-head">
      <span class="uw-ico">📋</span>
      <div style="flex:1">
        <div>المهام المطلوبة منك</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:#b45309;margin-top:2px">من تقييم: ${esc(evalItem.session || 'حصة')} — ${fmtDateWithDay(evalItem.date)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <ul style="list-style:none;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
      ${evalItem.upcoming.map((u, i) => `<li style="display:flex;gap:11px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:14px;padding:11px 13px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.75);border-radius:11px;font-weight:600;color:#1e293b;border-inline-start:3px solid #d97706"><span style="width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;background:#d97706;color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;flex-shrink:0">${i+1}</span><span>${esc(u.text)}</span></li>`).join('')}
    </ul>
  </div>`;
}

function todaySessionsHTML(s){
  const jsDay = new Date().getDay();
  const map = {0:'الأحد',1:'الاثنين',2:'الثلاثاء',3:'الأربعاء',4:'الخميس',5:'الجمعة',6:'السبت'};
  const today = map[jsDay];
  const list = s.schedule.filter(x => x.day === today);
  if(!list.length) return `<div class="empty" style="padding:24px"><span class="ico">☕</span>لا توجد حصص اليوم (${today})</div>`;
  return list.map(x => `
    <div class="item">
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div class="item-title">${esc(x.subject || sessionTypeLabel(x.type))}</div>
        <div class="item-meta">
          <span class="badge" style="background:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)}22;color:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)}">${sessionTypeLabel(x.type)}</span>
          <span>🕐 ${fmtTime12(x.from)} - ${fmtTime12(x.to)}</span>
          ${x.location ? `<span>📍 ${esc(x.location)}</span>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
      ${x.location && x.location.startsWith('http') ? `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(x.location).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(x.subject||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🚀 دخول</button>` : ''}
    </div>`).join('');
}

/* ============ تبويب تحصيلي التلقائي للطالب ============ */
function stuAutoProgress(s){
  const st = getAutoStats(s);
  const sorted = st.autoEvals.slice().sort((a,b) => (b.createdAt||0) - (a.createdAt||0));
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#5b21b6;font-weight:700;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f5f3ff,#fff);padding:11px 14px;border-radius:11px;border:1px dashed #c4b5fd;line-height:1.8">
      🔒 <b>هذا السجل للعرض فقط</b> — يعرض تقييماتك المبنية تلقائيًا من نتائج واجباتك واختباراتك، بما فيها العناصر المُغلقة دون حل (تُحسب بصفر وتؤثر على معدلك).
    </div>
  </div>

  ${st.autoEvals.length ? `
  <div class="auto-progress-hero" style="margin-top:14px">
    <div class="aph-head">
      <div class="aph-ico">📈</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div class="aph-title">مستوى تقدمك</div>
        <div class="aph-sub">${esc(s.name)} — تقييم مبني تلقائيًا</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="auto-progress-stats">
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.overallLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.overallAvg}%</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">المستوى العام</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.hwLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.hwEvals.length ? st.hwAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">📝 الواجبات (${st.hwEvals.length})</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.exLevel.cls}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.exEvals.length ? st.exAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">📊 الاختبارات (${st.exEvals.length})</div>
      </div>
      <div class="auto-progress-stat ${st.unsolvedItems.length ? 'bad' : ''}">
        <div class="aps-num">${st.unsolvedItems.length}</div>
        <div class="aps-lbl">❌ لم يتم حلها</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="auto-bars-grid" style="margin-top:14px">
    <div class="auto-bar-card hw">
      <div class="abc-head"><span class="abc-ico">📝</span><span>مستوى الواجبات</span></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-big">${st.hwAvg}%</div>
      <div class="auto-bar-track"><div class="fill hw" style="width:${st.hwAvg}%"></div></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-info"><span>المستوى: <b>${st.hwLevel.label}</b></span><span>${st.hwEvals.length} واجب</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="auto-bar-card ex">
      <div class="abc-head"><span class="abc-ico">📊</span><span>مستوى الاختبارات</span></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-big">${st.exAvg}%</div>
      <div class="auto-bar-track"><div class="fill ex" style="width:${st.exAvg}%"></div></div>
      <div class="auto-bar-info"><span>المستوى: <b>${st.exLevel.label}</b></span><span>${st.exEvals.length} اختبار</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card anim-in-d1" style="margin-top:14px">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed);color:#fff">📈</span>
      سجل تقييماتك <span class="sec-sub">${st.autoEvals.length} تقييم</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">
      <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="saveAutoProgressAsImage('${s.id}')">📸 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="printAutoProgress('${s.id}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>
    ${sorted.map(e => autoEvalCardHTML(e, 'student', s.id)).join('')}
  </div>
  ` : `
  <div class="card anim-in-d1" style="margin-top:14px">
    <div class="empty" style="padding:40px">
      <span class="ico">📈</span>
      لا توجد تقييمات تلقائية بعد
      <br><span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:8px;display:block">
        حل واجبًا أو اختبارًا ليظهر تقييمك هنا تلقائيًا ✨
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>
  `}`;
}

/* ============ تبويب الدروس للطالب ============ */
function stuLessons(s){
  const allLessons = db.lessons.filter(l => isTargetedToStudent(l, s));
  const activeLessons = allLessons.filter(l => isItemAvailable(l));
  const lockedLessons = allLessons.filter(l => !isItemAvailable(l));
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📹</span> الدروس المتاحة <span class="sec-sub">${activeLessons.length}</span></div>
    ${activeLessons.length ? renderFoldersView(activeLessons, 'lesson', 'student') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🎬</span>لا توجد دروس متاحة حاليًا</div>`}
  </div>
  ${lockedLessons.length ? `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">⏰</span> دروس مجدولة <span class="sec-sub">${lockedLessons.length}</span></div>
    ${lockedLessons.map(l => lessonCardStudent(l)).join('')}
  </div>` : ''}`;
}

function lessonCardStudent(l){
  const typeInfo = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === l.type) || SESSION_TYPES[1];
  const available = isItemAvailable(l);
  const isDevice = l.videoSource === 'device';
  return `
    <div class="lesson-card" style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;margin-bottom:14px">
      <div style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:16/9;display:grid;place-items:center;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,${typeInfo.color}55);cursor:${available?'pointer':'default'}" ${available?`onclick="openLessonPlayer('${l.id}')"`:''}>
        <span style="position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;background:${isDevice?'linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed)':'rgba(0,0,0,.55)'};color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:6px 13px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(8px);z-index:3">${isDevice?'📤 ':''}${typeInfo.label}</span>
        ${available ? `<div style="position:relative;z-index:2;width:70px;height:70px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);color:var(--primary-d);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:28px;box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(0,0,0,.4)">▶</div>` : `<div style="position:relative;z-index:2;font-size:64px">🔒</div>`}
        ${l.duration ? `<span style="position:absolute;bottom:12px;left:12px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.65);color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px;z-index:3">${l.duration} د</span>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div style="padding:16px">
        <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px;line-height:1.5">${esc(l.title)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(l.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</span>
          ${isDevice ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📤 فيديو مرفوع</span>' : ''}
          ${!available ? `<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ متاح: ${fmtDateTime12(l.startDate, l.startTime)}</span>` : '<span class="badge b-ok">✓ متاح الآن</span>'}
        </div>
        ${l.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-top:10px">${esc(l.desc)}</div>` : ''}
        ${available ? `<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openLessonPlayer('${l.id}')">▶ مشاهدة الدرس</button></div>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function stuLiveSessions(s){
  const sessions = db.liveSessions.filter(lv => isTargetedToStudent(lv, s));
  const active = sessions.filter(lv => isItemAvailable(lv));
  const locked = sessions.filter(lv => !isItemAvailable(lv));
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎥</span> المحاضرات المتاحة <span class="sec-sub">${active.length}</span></div>
    ${active.length ? renderFoldersView(active, 'live', 'student') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🎬</span>لا توجد محاضرات متاحة حاليًا</div>`}
  </div>
  ${locked.length ? `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">⏰</span> محاضرات مجدولة <span class="sec-sub">${locked.length}</span></div>
    ${locked.map(lv => liveSessionCardStudent(lv)).join('')}
  </div>` : ''}`;
}

function liveSessionCardStudent(lv){
  const available = isItemAvailable(lv);
  const isDevice = lv.videoSource === 'device';
  return `
    <div class="lesson-card" style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;margin-bottom:14px">
      <div style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:16/9;display:grid;place-items:center;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#7f1d1d,#dc262655)">
        <span style="position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;background:${isDevice?'linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed)':'rgba(220,38,38,.9)'};color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:6px 13px;border-radius:99px;z-index:3">${isDevice?'📤 ':''}🎥 محاضرة</span>
        ${available ? `<div style="position:relative;z-index:2;width:70px;height:70px;border-radius:50%;background:${isDevice?'#7c3aed':'#dc2626'};color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:28px;box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(0,0,0,.4)">${isDevice?'📹':'🎥'}</div>` : `<div style="position:relative;z-index:2;font-size:64px">🔒</div>`}
        ${lv.duration ? `<span style="position:absolute;bottom:12px;left:12px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.65);color:#fff;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px;z-index:3">${lv.duration} د</span>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div style="padding:16px">
        <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px;line-height:1.5">${esc(lv.title)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          ${isDevice ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📤 فيديو مرفوع</span>' : ''}
          ${!available ? `<span class="badge b-warn">⏰ متاح: ${fmtDateTime12(lv.startDate, lv.startTime)}</span>` : '<span class="badge b-ok">✓ متاح الآن</span>'}
        </div>
        ${lv.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-top:10px">${esc(lv.desc)}</div>` : ''}
        ${lv.location ? `<div style="margin-top:10px;background:#f1f5f9;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#475569">📍 <b>المكان:</b> ${esc(lv.location)}</div>` : ''}
        ${!available ? countdownBoxHTML(lv, 'student') : ''}
        ${available ? `
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
            ${isDevice ? `<button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openLivePlayer('${lv.id}')">📹 مشاهدة الفيديو</button>` : ''}
            ${lv.url ? `<button class="btn btn-vodafone btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(lv.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(lv.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🚀 انضم للمحاضرة</button>` : ''}
          </div>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function openLivePlayer(liveId){
  const lv = db.liveSessions.find(x => x.id === liveId);
  if(!lv) return;
  if(!lv.deviceVideoId){ toast('لا يوجد فيديو','warn'); return; }
  const s = ui.studentToken ? getStudent(ui.studentToken) : null;
  const viewerLabel = s ? (s.name + ' • ' + s.phone) : (db.settings.teacherName || 'المعلم');
  if(s){
    logStudentActivity(s.id, 'live', 'شاهد محاضرة: ' + lv.title, '');
    saveDB();
  }
  playDeviceVideo(lv.deviceVideoId, lv.title, {color:'#dc2626', label:'🎥 محاضرة أونلاين'}, viewerLabel);
}

function stuNotes(s){
  const notes = db.notes.filter(n => isTargetedToStudent(n, s)).slice().reverse();
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📄</span> المذكرات والملفات <span class="sec-sub">${notes.length}</span></div>
    ${notes.length ? renderFoldersView(notes, 'note', 'student') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد مذكرات بعد</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function noteCardStudent(n){
  const isLink = n.type === 'link';
  const gradient = isLink ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb)' : 'linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed)';
  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:#fff">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:14px;background:${gradient};color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:24px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.2)">${isLink?'📖':'📄'}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15.5px">${esc(n.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-top:6px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center">
            <span class="badge ${isLink?'b-info':'b-purple'}">${isLink?'📖 كتاب / مذكرة':'📄 PDF'}</span>
            ${n.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(n.subject)}</span>` : ''}
            <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(n.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${n.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;line-height:1.8">${esc(n.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
        ${isLink
          ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(n.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(n.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">📖 فتح المذكرة</button>`
          : `<button class="btn btn-purple btn-sm" onclick="openNotePDF('${n.id}')">📄 عرض المذكرة (مع الكتابة)</button>`}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function stuHomework(s){
  const sortedHW = (s.homework || []).slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = combineToTimestamp(a.due) || a.createdAt || 0;
    const tb = combineToTimestamp(b.due) || b.createdAt || 0;
    return tb - ta;
  }).reverse();
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📝</span> الواجبات <span class="sec-sub">${s.homework.length}</span></div>
    ${sortedHW.length ? sortedHW.map(h => hwCardStudent(h, s.id)).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🎉</span>لا توجد واجبات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function hwCardStudent(h, studentId){
  checkAutoClose(h, studentId);
  const type = h.type || 'simple';
  const typeBadge = type === 'link' ? '<span class="badge b-info">🔗 رابط</span>'
    : type === 'questions' ? '<span class="badge b-warn">📝 أسئلة</span>'
    : type === 'pdf' ? '<span class="badge b-purple">📄 PDF</span>'
    : '<span class="badge b-gray">📋 نصي</span>';
  const available = isItemAvailable(h);
  const closed = isItemClosed(h);
  const isOpen = ui.quizHwId === h.id;
  const extraChips = itemMetaChipsHTML(h);
  let body = '';

  if(closed){
    body = closedBannerHTML(h, 'student');
    if(type === 'questions' && h.submitted){
      const pct = h.total ? Math.round((h.score / h.total) * 100) : 0;
      const correctCount = (h.questions||[]).filter(q => isAnswerCorrect(q, h.answers[q.id])).length;
      const wrongCount = (h.questions||[]).length - correctCount;
      body += `
        <div class="quiz-summary-box ${pct < 50 ? 'fail' : ''}" style="margin-top:12px">
          <span class="qsb-ico">${pct >= 50 ? '🎉' : '💪'}</span>
          <div class="qsb-score">${h.score} / ${h.total}</div>
          <div class="qsb-pct">${pct}%</div>
          <div class="qsb-label">${h.autoSubmitted ? '⚡ تم التسليم تلقائيًا' : (pct >= 50 ? 'أحسنت!' : 'يحتاج مراجعة')}</div>
          <div class="quiz-summary-stats">
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat correct"><div class="qss-num">${correctCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✓ صحيحة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat wrong"><div class="qss-num">${wrongCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✗ خاطئة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat empty"><div class="qss-num">${(h.questions||[]).length}</div><div class="qss-lbl">📝 إجمالي</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openHwReview('${studentId}','${h.id}')">👁 مراجعة إجاباتك</button>
        </div>`;
    } else if(type === 'link'){
      body += `<div class="item-desc" style="margin-top:8px">${h.desc ? esc(h.desc) : ''}</div>`;
    }
  }
  else if(!available){
    body = `
      ${countdownBoxHTML(h, 'student')}
      <div class="locked-banner">
        <div class="lb-ico">🔒</div>
        <div class="lb-txt">هذا الواجب مغلق حاليًا<small>سيُفتح في الموعد المحدد</small></div>
      </div>`;
  }
  else if(type === 'link'){
    if(h.submitted){
      body = `
        <div class="pending-notice" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#fff);border-color:#bbf7d0">
          <div class="pn-title" style="color:#166534"><span class="pn-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4ade80,#16a34a)">✓</span>تم التسليم</div>
          <div class="pn-note" style="color:#166534;border-color:#bbf7d0">تم رفع النتيجة — بانتظار اعتماد المعلم</div>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      body = `
        ${scheduleInfoBoxHTML(h)}
        ${h.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13.5px;color:#334155;margin-bottom:11px;line-height:1.85;background:#f0f9ff;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--info)">${esc(h.desc)}</div>` : ''}
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-info" onclick="openLinkPreview('${esc(h.url||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}', '${esc(h.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}', {studentView:true})">🚀 افتح الواجب</button>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border:2px solid #fde68a;border-radius:16px;padding:18px;margin-top:14px">
          <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#92400e;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;margin-bottom:12px">📸 ارفع نتيجة واجبك</div>
          <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#92400e;font-weight:600;background:rgba(255,255,255,.7);padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;line-height:1.8;margin-bottom:14px;border:1px dashed #fcd34d">
            🔒 التقط صورة واضحة لنتيجتك وارفعها هنا مع كتابة الدرجة.
          </div>
          <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:12px">
            <div class="field"><label>الدرجة التي حصلت عليها *</label><input type="number" id="sub_score_${h.id}" placeholder="مثال: 9" inputmode="numeric"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>الدرجة الكاملة</label><input type="number" value="${esc(h.maxScore||'')}" readonly style="background:#f8fafc;cursor:not-allowed"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px"><label>ملاحظة (اختياري)</label><input id="sub_note_${h.id}" placeholder="مثال: الوقت لم يكفِ..."></div>
          <div class="img-upload-zone" id="uploadZone_${h.id}" onclick="document.getElementById('imgInput_${h.id}').click()">
            <span class="iuz-ico">📷</span>
            <div class="iuz-txt">اضغط لاختيار صورة النتيجة</div>
            <div class="iuz-sub">صورة واضحة JPG / PNG</div>
            <input type="file" id="imgInput_${h.id}" accept="image/*" onchange="handleImageUpload(event,'${h.id}','hw')">
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:12px">
            <button class="btn btn-ok btn-block" onclick="submitExternalGrade('${studentId}','hw','${h.id}')">📤 رفع النتيجة</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
  }
  else if(type === 'pdf' && (h.pdfData || h.pdfUrl)){
    body = `
      ${scheduleInfoBoxHTML(h)}
      ${h.desc ? `<div style="font-size:13.5px;color:#334155;margin-bottom:11px;line-height:1.85;background:#faf5ff;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--purple)">${esc(h.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      <button class="btn btn-purple" style="margin-bottom:11px" onclick="openHomeworkPDFInline('${studentId}','${h.id}')">📄 فتح ملف PDF</button>
      ${!h.submitted ? `<div style="margin-top:11px"><button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="markHwDone('${studentId}','${h.id}')">✓ تم إنجاز الواجب</button></div>` : '<div class="badge b-ok" style="padding:8px 14px">✓ تم الإنجاز</div>'}`;
  }
  else if(type === 'questions'){
    const total = (h.questions||[]).reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);
    if(h.submitted){
      const pct = h.total ? Math.round((h.score / h.total) * 100) : 0;
      const correctCount = (h.questions||[]).filter(q => isAnswerCorrect(q, h.answers[q.id])).length;
      const wrongCount = (h.questions||[]).length - correctCount;
      body = `
        <div class="quiz-summary-box ${pct < 50 ? 'fail' : ''}">
          <span class="qsb-ico">${pct >= 50 ? '🎉' : '💪'}</span>
          <div class="qsb-score">${h.score} / ${h.total}</div>
          <div class="qsb-pct">${pct}%</div>
          <div class="qsb-label">${h.autoSubmitted ? '⚡ تم التسليم تلقائيًا' : (pct >= 50 ? 'أحسنت!' : 'يحتاج مراجعة')}</div>
          <div class="quiz-summary-stats">
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat correct"><div class="qss-num">${correctCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✓ صحيحة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat wrong"><div class="qss-num">${wrongCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✗ خاطئة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat empty"><div class="qss-num">${(h.questions||[]).length}</div><div class="qss-lbl">📝 إجمالي</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openHwReview('${studentId}','${h.id}')">👁 مراجعة إجاباتك</button>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      body = `
        ${scheduleInfoBoxHTML(h)}
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
          <span class="badge b-gray">❓ ${(h.questions||[]).length} سؤال</span>
          <span class="badge b-gray">⭐ ${total} درجة</span>
          ${extraChips}
        </div>
        ${!isOpen ? `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="startHwQuiz('${h.id}')">🚀 ابدأ الحل</button>` : `<div class="quiz-wrap">${quizFormHTML(h, 'hw')}</div>`}`;
    }
  }
  else {
    body = `
      ${scheduleInfoBoxHTML(h)}
      ${h.desc ? `<div class="item-desc" style="margin-top:0;border-top:none;padding-top:0">${esc(h.desc)}</div>` : ''}
      ${!h.submitted ? `<div style="margin-top:11px"><button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="markHwDone('${studentId}','${h.id}')">✓ تم إنجاز الواجب</button></div>` : '<div class="badge b-ok" style="padding:8px 14px">✓ تم الإنجاز</div>'}`;
  }

  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:#fff" data-item-id="${h.id}">
      <div style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px;margin-bottom:6px">${esc(h.title)}</div>
        <div class="item-meta">
          ${typeBadge}
          ${h.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(h.subject)}</span>` : ''}
          ${h.due ? `<span>📅 التسليم: ${fmtDateWithDay(h.due)}</span>` : ''}
          ${h.maxScore ? `<span>⭐ ${h.maxScore} درجة</span>` : ''}
          ${!available && !closed ? miniCountdownChipHTML(h) : ''}
          ${extraChips}
          ${itemStatusBadgeHTML(h)}
        </div>
      </div>
      ${body}
    </div>`;
}

function markHwDone(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const h = s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId); if(!h) return;
  if(h.submitted){ toast('تم التسليم','warn'); return; }
  h.submitted = true;
  h.submittedAt = Date.now();
  h.score = h.maxScore || 0;
  h.total = h.maxScore || 0;
  h.updatedAt = Date.now();
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'homework', 'إنجاز واجب: ' + h.title, `${h.score}/${h.total}`);
  logStudentProgress(studentId, 'homework', h, h.score, h.total);
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم تسجيل الإنجاز ✓','gold');
  playSound('success');
}

function handleImageUpload(event, itemId, kind){
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){ toast('اختر صورة','warn'); return; }
  if(file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024){ toast('حجم الصورة كبير (الحد 5MB)','warn'); return; }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    const dataUrl = e.target.result;
    window._tempImages = window._tempImages || {};
    window._tempImages[itemId + '_' + kind] = dataUrl;
    const zone = document.getElementById('uploadZone_' + itemId);
    if(zone){
      zone.classList.add('has-img');
      zone.innerHTML = `
        <div class="img-preview-wrap">
          <img src="${dataUrl}" alt="معاينة">
          <div class="img-preview-info">
            <div class="ipi-name">✓ تم اختيار الصورة</div>
            <div class="ipi-actions">
              <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();document.getElementById('imgInput_${itemId}').click()">تغيير</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="imgInput_${itemId}" accept="image/*" onchange="handleImageUpload(event,'${itemId}','${kind}')">
      `;
    }
    toast('تم رفع الصورة ✓','gold');
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function submitExternalGrade(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const item = kind === 'exam' ? s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId) : s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if(!item) return;
  if(!isItemAvailable(item)){ toast('هذا العنصر مغلق','warn'); return; }
  if(isItemClosed(item)){ toast('هذا العنصر مغلق','warn'); return; }
  if(item.submitted){ toast('تم التسليم','warn'); return; }

  const scoreInput = document.getElementById('sub_score_' + itemId);
  const noteInput = document.getElementById('sub_note_' + itemId);
  const scoreVal = scoreInput ? parseFloat(scoreInput.value) : NaN;
  if(isNaN(scoreVal) || scoreVal < 0){ toast('أدخل الدرجة','warn'); return; }
  if(item.maxScore && scoreVal > parseFloat(item.maxScore)){
    if(!confirm(`الدرجة أكبر من الكاملة. متابعة؟`)) return;
  }

  const imgKey = itemId + '_' + kind;
  const imgData = window._tempImages ? window._tempImages[imgKey] : null;
  if(!imgData){ toast('ارفع صورة النتيجة','warn'); return; }

  const now = Date.now();
  item.submission = {
    studentScore: scoreVal,
    note: noteInput ? noteInput.value.trim() : '',
    image: imgData,
    date: todayISO(),
    submittedAt: now
  };
  item.submitted = true;
  item.submittedAt = now;
  item.updatedAt = now;
  s.updatedAt = now;

  if(window._tempImages) delete window._tempImages[imgKey];

  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `📸 تم رفع نتيجة في "${item.title}" — الدرجة: ${scoreVal}${item.maxScore ? ' / ' + item.maxScore : ''} (بانتظار الاعتماد)`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: now
  });

  logStudentActivity(studentId, kind === 'exam' ? 'exam' : 'homework',
    `رفع نتيجة ${kind === 'exam' ? 'اختبار' : 'واجب'}: "${item.title}"`,
    `الدرجة المُدخلة: ${scoreVal}`);

  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم رفع النتيجة وإرسالها للمعلم ✓','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

function stuExams(s){
  const sortedEx = (s.exams || []).slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = combineToTimestamp(a.date) || a.createdAt || 0;
    const tb = combineToTimestamp(b.date) || b.createdAt || 0;
    return tb - ta;
  });
  return `
  ${antiShareBanner()}
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📊</span> الاختبارات <span class="sec-sub">${s.exams.length}</span></div>
    ${sortedEx.length ? sortedEx.map(e => examCardStudent(e, s.id)).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد اختبارات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function examCardStudent(e, studentId){
  checkAutoClose(e, studentId);
  const type = e.type || 'simple';
  const typeBadge = type === 'link' ? '<span class="badge b-info">🔗 رابط</span>'
    : type === 'questions' ? '<span class="badge b-warn">📝 أسئلة</span>'
    : '<span class="badge b-gray">📊 درجة</span>';
  const available = isItemAvailable(e);
  const closed = isItemClosed(e);
  const isOpen = ui.quizExamId === e.id;
  const extraChips = itemMetaChipsHTML(e);
  let body = '';

  if(closed){
    body = closedBannerHTML(e, 'student');
    if(type === 'questions' && e.submitted){
      const pct = e.total ? Math.round((e.score / e.total) * 100) : 0;
      body += `
        <div class="quiz-summary-box ${pct < 50 ? 'fail' : ''}" style="margin-top:12px">
          <span class="qsb-ico">${pct >= 50 ? '🎉' : '💪'}</span>
          <div class="qsb-score">${e.score} / ${e.total}</div>
          <div class="qsb-pct">${pct}%</div>
        </div>`;
    }
  }
  else if(!available){
    body = `
      ${countdownBoxHTML(e, 'student')}
      <div class="locked-banner">
        <div class="lb-ico">🔒</div>
        <div class="lb-txt">هذا الاختبار مغلق حاليًا<small>سيُفتح في الموعد المحدد</small></div>
      </div>`;
  }
  else if(type === 'questions'){
    const total = (e.questions||[]).reduce((a,q)=>a+(q.points||1),0);
    if(e.submitted){
      const pct = e.total ? Math.round((e.score / e.total) * 100) : 0;
      const correctCount = (e.questions||[]).filter(q => isAnswerCorrect(q, e.answers[q.id])).length;
      const wrongCount = (e.questions||[]).length - correctCount;
      body = `
        <div class="quiz-summary-box ${pct < 50 ? 'fail' : ''}">
          <span class="qsb-ico">${pct >= 50 ? '🎉' : '💪'}</span>
          <div class="qsb-score">${e.score} / ${e.total}</div>
          <div class="qsb-pct">${pct}%</div>
          <div class="qsb-label">${e.autoSubmitted ? '⚡ تم التسليم تلقائيًا' : (pct >= 50 ? 'أحسنت!' : 'يحتاج مراجعة')}</div>
          <div class="quiz-summary-stats">
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat correct"><div class="qss-num">${correctCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✓ صحيحة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat wrong"><div class="qss-num">${wrongCount}</div><div class="qss-lbl">✗ خاطئة</div></div>
            <div class="quiz-summary-stat empty"><div class="qss-num">${(e.questions||[]).length}</div><div class="qss-lbl">📝 إجمالي</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openQuizReview('${studentId}','${e.id}')">👁 مراجعة إجاباتك</button>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      body = `
        ${scheduleInfoBoxHTML(e)}
        <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
          <span class="badge b-gray">❓ ${(e.questions||[]).length} سؤال</span>
          <span class="badge b-gray">⭐ ${total} درجة</span>
          ${extraChips}
        </div>
        ${!isOpen ? `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="startExam('${e.id}')">🚀 ابدأ الاختبار</button>` : `<div class="quiz-wrap">${quizFormHTML(e, 'exam')}</div>`}`;
    }
  }
  else if(type === 'link'){
    if(e.submitted){
      body = `
        <div class="pending-notice" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#fff);border-color:#bbf7d0">
          <div class="pn-title" style="color:#166534">✓ تم التسليم</div>
          <div class="pn-note" style="color:#166534;border-color:#bbf7d0">بانتظار اعتماد المعلم</div>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      body = `
        ${e.notes ? `<div style="font-size:13.5px;color:#334155;line-height:1.85;background:#f8fafc;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;margin-bottom:11px">${esc(e.notes)}</div>` : ''}
        <button class="btn btn-info" style="margin-bottom:11px" onclick="openLinkPreview('${esc(e.url||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}', '${esc(e.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}', {studentView:true})">🚀 افتح الاختبار</button>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border:2px solid #fde68a;border-radius:16px;padding:18px">
          <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#92400e;margin-bottom:12px">📸 ارفع نتيجة اختبارك</div>
          <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:12px">
            <div class="field"><label>الدرجة *</label><input type="number" id="sub_score_${e.id}" placeholder="18" inputmode="numeric"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>الكاملة</label><input type="number" value="${esc(e.maxScore||'')}" readonly style="background:#f8fafc"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px"><label>ملاحظة</label><input id="sub_note_${e.id}"></div>
          <div class="img-upload-zone" id="uploadZone_${e.id}" onclick="document.getElementById('imgInput_${e.id}').click()">
            <span class="iuz-ico">📷</span>
            <div class="iuz-txt">اضغط لاختيار صورة النتيجة</div>
            <input type="file" id="imgInput_${e.id}" accept="image/*" onchange="handleImageUpload(event,'${e.id}','exam')">
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn btn-ok btn-block" onclick="submitExternalGrade('${studentId}','exam','${e.id}')">📤 رفع النتيجة</button></div>
        </div>`;
    }
  }
  else if(type === 'simple'){
    const hasScore = e.score !== '' && e.score !== null && e.score !== undefined;
    body = hasScore
      ? `<span class="badge b-info" style="padding:8px 14px;font-size:13px">الدرجة: ${esc(e.score)}${e.total?' / '+esc(e.total):''}</span>`
      : `<span class="badge b-gray" style="padding:8px 14px;font-size:13px">لم تُرصد بعد</span>`;
  }

  return `
    <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:#fff" data-item-id="${e.id}">
      <div style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px;margin-bottom:6px">${esc(e.title)}</div>
        <div class="item-meta">
          ${typeBadge}
          ${e.subject ? `<span>📚 ${esc(e.subject)}</span>` : ''}
          <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</span>
          ${e.maxScore ? `<span>⭐ ${e.maxScore} درجة</span>` : ''}
          ${!available && !closed ? miniCountdownChipHTML(e) : ''}
          ${extraChips}
          ${itemStatusBadgeHTML(e)}
        </div>
      </div>
      ${body}
    </div>`;
}

/* ============ تبويب تقييمات الطالب ============ */
function stuEvals(s){
  const sortedEvals = (s.evaluations || []).slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = combineToTimestamp(a.date) || a.createdAt || 0;
    const tb = combineToTimestamp(b.date) || b.createdAt || 0;
    return tb - ta;
  });
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎯</span> تقييماتي <span class="sec-sub">${s.evaluations.length}</span></div>
    ${sortedEvals.length ? sortedEvals.map(e => evalCardStudent(e, s.id)).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد تقييمات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function evalCardStudent(e, studentId){
  const hasTasks = (e.mastered && e.mastered.length) || (e.notMastered && e.notMastered.length) || (e.upcoming && e.upcoming.length);
  const parentBox = renderParentApprovalStudent(studentId, e);
  return `
    <div id="eval_${e.id}" style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;background:#fff">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:15px">${esc(e.session || 'حصة')} — ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:5px">
            <span class="badge ${e.attend === 'حاضر' ? 'b-ok' : e.attend === 'متأخر' ? 'b-warn' : 'b-bad'}">${esc(e.attend)}</span>
            ${e.parentApproved ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✅ معتمد من ولي الأمر</span>' : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${hasTasks ? `
        ${e.mastered && e.mastered.length ? evalBlockHTML('mastered','✅','ما أتقنته', e.mastered) : ''}
        ${e.notMastered && e.notMastered.length ? evalNotMasteredProminentHTML(e.notMastered) : ''}
        ${e.upcoming && e.upcoming.length ? evalBlockHTML('upcoming','📋','المطلوب حتى الحصة القادمة', e.upcoming) : ''}
      ` : ''}
      ${e.notes ? `<div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:#334155;background:#f8fafc;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:11px;margin-top:10px;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--muted)"><b style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">ملاحظة المعلم: </b>${esc(e.notes)}</div>` : ''}
      ${parentBox}
    </div>`;
}

function evalNotMasteredProminentHTML(items){
  return `
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fee2e2,#fef2f2);border:2px solid #ef4444;border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin-top:10px;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
      <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:6px;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#ef4444,#991b1b)"></div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;font-weight:900;font-size:14px;color:#991b1b;margin-bottom:10px">
        <span style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#b91c1c);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;flex-shrink:0">⚠️</span>
        يحتاج إلى تدريب عاجل (${items.length})
      </div>
      <ul style="list-style:none;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px">
        ${items.map((it, i) => `<li style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.7;color:#7f1d1d;font-weight:800;background:rgba(255,255,255,.85);padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;border-inline-start:3px solid #ef4444;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px"><span style="width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:50%;background:#ef4444;color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:900;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px">${i+1}</span><span>${esc(it.text)}</span></li>`).join('')}
      </ul>
      <div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#7f1d1d;background:rgba(255,255,255,.85);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:9px;text-align:center">
        📌 يرجى التركيز على هذه النقاط قبل الحصة القادمة
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function renderParentApprovalStudent(studentId, evalItem){
  const hasPassword = !!(evalItem.parentPassword && evalItem.parentPassword.trim());
  const approved = !!evalItem.parentApproved;
  if(!hasPassword) return '';
  if(approved){
    return `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#fff);border:2px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;margin-top:12px">
        <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#166534;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
          <span style="font-size:20px">✅</span> تم اعتماد هذا التقييم من ولي الأمر بتاريخ ${fmtDateWithDay(evalItem.parentApprovedAt || evalItem.date)}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
  return `
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#eef2ff,#fff);border:2px dashed #a5b4fc;border-radius:14px;padding:16px;margin-top:12px">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#4338ca;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <span style="font-size:18px">✍️</span> يعتمد ولي الأمر
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#4f46e5;font-weight:600;line-height:1.8;background:rgba(255,255,255,.7);padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:12px;border:1px dashed #c7d2fe">
        اطلب من ولي أمرك كتابة الكلمة السرية في الحقل أدناه، ثم اضغط "اعتماد".
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <input type="text" id="pasInput_${evalItem.id}" placeholder="اكتب كلمة الاعتماد..." autocomplete="off" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;text-align:center;letter-spacing:2px;font-weight:800;font-size:15px;border-color:#a5b4fc;background:#fff">
        <button class="btn btn-ok" onclick="submitParentApproval('${studentId}','${evalItem.id}')">✓ اعتماد</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function submitParentApproval(studentId, evalId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const e = s.evaluations.find(x => x.id === evalId); if(!e) return;
  if(e.parentApproved){ toast('تم الاعتماد','warn'); return; }
  const input = document.getElementById('pasInput_' + evalId);
  const pwd = normalizeDigits(input ? input.value.trim() : '');
  if(!pwd){ toast('اكتب كلمة الاعتماد','warn'); return; }
  if(pwd !== normalizeDigits((e.parentPassword || '').trim())){
    toast('كلمة الاعتماد غير صحيحة','err');
    playSound('error');
    if(input){ input.value = ''; input.focus(); }
    return;
  }
  e.parentApproved = true;
  e.parentApprovedAt = todayISO();
  e.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.notifications.push({
    id: uid(),
    text: `✅ تم اعتماد تقييم حصة "${e.session || 'حصة'}" من ولي الأمر.`,
    date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
  });
  logStudentActivity(studentId, 'approval', 'اعتماد تقييم من ولي الأمر', e.session);
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم الاعتماد بنجاح ✓','gold');
  playSound('celebrate');
}

function stuSchedule(s){
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📅</span> جدولي الأسبوعي</div>
    ${s.schedule.length ? DAYS.filter(d => s.schedule.some(x => x.day === d)).map(day => `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fff,#f8fafc);border:2px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:14px;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:6px;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary-d))"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;padding-inline-start:8px">
          <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:var(--primary-d)">${day}</span>
          <span style="background:var(--primary-l);color:var(--primary-d);font-size:11px;font-weight:900;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px">${s.schedule.filter(x => x.day === day).length} حصة</span>
        </div>
        ${s.schedule.filter(x => x.day === day).map(x => `
          <div style="padding:12px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:var(--primary-d);background:#f0fdfa;padding:6px 11px;border-radius:9px;direction:ltr;min-width:100px;text-align:center">🕐 ${fmtTime12(x.from)}<br>${fmtTime12(x.to)}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:3px">${esc(x.subject || sessionTypeLabel(x.type))}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
                <span style="background:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)}22;color:${sessionTypeColor(x.type)};padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px">${sessionTypeLabel(x.type)}</span>
                ${x.location ? `<span>📍 ${esc(x.location)}</span>` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
            ${x.location && x.location.startsWith('http') ? `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="openLinkInline('${esc(x.location).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(x.subject||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🚀 دخول</button>` : ''}
          </div>`).join('')}
      </div>`).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>الجدول فارغ</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function stuNotifs(s){
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico">🔔</span> إشعاراتي <span class="sec-sub">${s.notifications.length}</span>
      <span class="sec-sub" style="color:var(--danger)">🔒 للعرض فقط</span>
    </div>
    ${s.notifications.length ? s.notifications.slice().reverse().map(n => `
      <div class="item" style="${n.read?'':'background:linear-gradient(90deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border-color:#fcd34d'}">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.85;color:var(--text);font-weight:600">${esc(n.text)}</div>
          <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:8px">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(n.date)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🔕</span>لا توجد إشعارات</div>`}
  </div>`;
}

function stuCertificates(s){
  return `
  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">🎓</span> شهاداتي <span class="sec-sub">${s.certificates.length}</span></div>
    ${s.certificates.length ? s.certificates.slice().reverse().map(c => {
      const design = CERT_DESIGNS.find(d => d.id === c.design) || CERT_DESIGNS[0];
      const isDark = design.dark;
      return `
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${design.bg1},${design.bg2});border:3px double ${design.color};border-radius:20px;padding:32px 24px;text-align:center;position:relative;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:var(--shadow-lg);margin-bottom:14px;${isDark?'color:#f1f5f9':''}">
        <div style="font-size:40px;margin-bottom:8px">🏆</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:22px;font-weight:700;color:${design.color};margin-bottom:6px">${esc(c.title)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:${isDark?'#94a3b8':design.color};margin-bottom:20px;letter-spacing:3px">CERTIFICATE OF COMPLETION</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:28px;font-weight:700;color:${design.color};margin:14px 0">${esc(c.studentName)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:13px;${isDark?'color:#cbd5e1':''};line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:20px">${esc(c.desc)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:${design.color};margin:10px 0">التقدير: ${esc(c.grade)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:${isDark?'#94a3b8':'#92400e'};margin-top:14px">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(c.issuedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div>
        <div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="saveCertificateAsImage('${s.id}','${c.id}')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
          <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="printCertificate('${s.id}','${c.id}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('') : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">📭</span>لا توجد شهادات بعد</div>`}
  </div>`;
}
/* ============ عارض الدروس (Lesson Player) ============ */
function openLessonPlayer(lessonId){
  const lesson = db.lessons.find(l => l.id === lessonId);
  if(!lesson) return;
  const s = ui.studentToken ? getStudent(ui.studentToken) : null;
  if(s){
    if(s.status !== 'active'){ toast('حسابك غير مفعّل','warn'); return; }
    if(!isTargetedToStudent(lesson, s)){ toast('هذا الدرس غير موجه لحسابك','warn'); return; }
    if(!isItemAvailable(lesson)){ toast('هذا الدرس مجدول','warn'); return; }
    logStudentActivity(s.id, 'lesson', 'شاهد الدرس: ' + lesson.title, '');
    saveDB();
  }
  const typeInfo = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === lesson.type) || SESSION_TYPES[1];
  showContentLoader(
    lesson.title,
    typeInfo.label + (lesson.duration ? ' • ' + lesson.duration + ' دقيقة' : ''),
    `proceedToLessonPlayer('${lesson.id}')`,
    getViewerLabel(),
    lesson.type === 'recorded' ? '📹' : '🏫'
  );
  setTimeout(() => {
    const root = document.getElementById('iapRoot');
    if(root && root.innerHTML.includes('جاري تجهيز المحتوى')){
      proceedToLessonPlayer(lesson.id);
    }
  }, 1800);
}

function getViewerLabel(){
  const s = ui.studentToken ? getStudent(ui.studentToken) : null;
  return s ? (s.name + ' • ' + s.phone) : (db.settings.teacherName || 'المعلم');
}

  const html = `

          <div style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:6px;background:rgba(20,184,166,.16);border:1px solid rgba(94,234,212,.32);color:#5eead4;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 12px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(8px);margin-top:12px">
            <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:#5eead4;box-shadow:0 0 8px #5eead4;animation:pulseRing 1.6s infinite"></span>
            جاري تجهيز المحتوى
          </div>

        <div style="padding:28px 24px;text-align:center;background:#fff">

          <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;line-height:1.8;max-width:400px;margin:0 auto">
            يتم عرض المحتوى داخل المنصة في نافذة محمية.<br>
            <span style="font-size:11.5px;opacity:.9">🔒 الرابط مخفي — يُمنع نسخه</span>

            <span style="font-size:14px">👤</span>
            <span>محتوى مخصص لـ ${esc(viewerLabel)}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:14px;display:flex;gap:10px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()">إلغاء</button>
            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="${nextActionJs}">🚀 فتح الآن</button>
