function proceedToLessonPlayer(lessonId){
  const lesson = db.lessons.find(l => l.id === lessonId);
  if(!lesson) return;
  const s = ui.studentToken ? getStudent(ui.studentToken) : null;
  const viewerLabel = s ? (s.name + ' • ' + s.phone) : (db.settings.teacherName || 'المعلم');
  const typeInfo = SESSION_TYPES.find(x => x.key === lesson.type) || SESSION_TYPES[1];

  if(lesson.videoSource === 'device' && lesson.deviceVideoId){
    return playDeviceVideo(lesson.deviceVideoId, lesson.title, typeInfo, viewerLabel);
  }

  const embedUrl = buildEmbedURL(lesson.url, lesson.type);
  const playerId = 'player_' + Date.now();

  let mediaHTML;
  if(!embedUrl){
    mediaHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100%;gap:16px;padding:30px;text-align:center;color:#fff">
        <div style="font-size:64px">🎥</div>
        <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:900">المحاضرة الأونلاين جاهزة</div>
        <button class="btn btn-gold btn-lg" onclick="openExternalLink('${esc(lesson.url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(lesson.title).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🚀 انضم للمحاضرة</button>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    mediaHTML = `<iframe id="${playerId}" src="${esc(embedUrl)}" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allow="autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture; clipboard-read; clipboard-write" allowfullscreen webkitallowfullscreen mozallowfullscreen frameborder="0" oncontextmenu="return false"></iframe>`;
  }

  const html = `
    <div class="iap-overlay" id="lessonOverlay">
      <div class="iap-window">
        <div class="iap-header" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#134e4a);color:#fff;flex-shrink:0">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:${typeInfo.color};display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0">${lesson.type === 'recorded' ? '📹' : '🏫'}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${esc(lesson.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#5eead4;font-weight:700">محتوى خاص بـ ${esc(viewerLabel)}</div>
          <button class="iap-btn close" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.85);border:none;color:#fff;border-radius:9px;width:36px;height:36px;display:grid;place-items:center;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;font-size:15px">✕</button>
        </div>
        <div class="iap-body" style="flex:1;background:#000;overflow:hidden;position:relative" oncontextmenu="return false">
          <div style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:16/9;background:#000;border-radius:0;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:10;pointer-events:none;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:flex-start;padding:20px">
              <span style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);color:rgba(255,255,255,.55);font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 12px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</span>
            </div>
            ${mediaHTML}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="iap-footer" style="padding:10px 16px;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700">🔒 محتوى محمي — منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)}</div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            ${embedUrl ? `<button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="requestPlayerFullscreen('${playerId}')">⛶ شاشة كاملة</button>` : ''}
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()">إغلاق</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = html;
  startPrivacyProtection();
}

async function playDeviceVideo(videoId, title, typeInfo, viewerLabel){
  document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = `
    <div class="iap-overlay">
      <div class="iap-window" style="max-width:520px;height:auto">
        <div class="note-preload-hero" style="padding:30px 24px">
          <div style="position:relative;width:86px;height:86px;margin:0 auto 18px">
            <div style="position:absolute;inset:0;border-radius:50%;border:4px solid #ccfbf1;border-top-color:var(--primary);animation:spin 1.1s linear infinite"></div>
            <div style="position:absolute;inset:14px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdfa,#ccfbf1);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:26px">📹</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:17px;font-weight:900;color:#fff">جاري تحميل الفيديو...</div>
        </div>
        <div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;background:#fff">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()" style="min-width:200px">← العودة</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  try{
    const storageRef = storage.ref(FIREBASE_STORAGE_FOLDER + '/' + videoId);
    const downloadURL = await storageRef.getDownloadURL();
    const playerId = 'devicePlayer_' + Date.now();
    const html = `
      <div class="iap-overlay" id="lessonOverlay">
        <div class="iap-window">
          <div class="iap-header" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#134e4a);color:#fff;flex-shrink:0">
            <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0">📹</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${esc(title)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#5eead4;font-weight:700">محتوى خاص بـ ${esc(viewerLabel)}</div>
            <button class="iap-btn close" onclick="closeLessonPlayer(true)" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.85);border:none;color:#fff;border-radius:9px;width:36px;height:36px;display:grid;place-items:center;cursor:pointer">✕</button>
          </div>
          <div class="iap-body" style="flex:1;background:#000;overflow:hidden;position:relative" oncontextmenu="return false">
            <div style="position:relative;width:100%;height:100%;background:#000">
              <div style="position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:10;pointer-events:none;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:flex-start;padding:20px">
                <span style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);color:rgba(255,255,255,.55);font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 12px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</span>
              </div>
              <video id="${playerId}" style="width:100%;height:100%;background:#000;display:block;object-fit:contain" controls controlsList="nodownload noremoteplayback nofullscreen" disablePictureInPicture disableRemotePlayback playsinline preload="metadata" oncontextmenu="return false" onloadeddata="this.play()">
                <source src="${downloadURL}" type="video/mp4">
                متصفحك لا يدعم تشغيل الفيديو
              </video>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="iap-footer" style="padding:10px 16px;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700">🔒 فيديو محمي</div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
              <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="requestPlayerFullscreen('${playerId}')">⛶ شاشة كاملة</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeLessonPlayer(true)">إغلاق</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = html;
    attachVideoSecurity(playerId);
    startPrivacyProtection();
    playSound('notify');
  }catch(e){
    console.error('Device video load error:', e);
    document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = `
      <div class="iap-overlay">
        <div class="iap-window" style="max-width:520px;height:auto">
          <div class="note-preload-hero" style="padding:30px 24px">
            <div style="font-size:56px;margin-bottom:10px">⚠️</div>
            <div style="font-size:17px;font-weight:900;color:#fff">تعذر تحميل الفيديو</div>
          </div>
          <div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;background:#fff">
            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()" style="min-width:200px">← العودة</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    toast('تعذر تحميل الفيديو','err');
  }
}

function attachVideoSecurity(playerId){
  const video = document.getElementById(playerId);
  if(!video) return;
  video.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());
  video.addEventListener('enterpictureinpicture', e => {
    try{ document.exitPictureInPicture(); }catch(err){}
    toast('وضع PiP معطّل 🔒','warn');
  });
  const handleVisibility = () => {
    if(document.visibilityState === 'hidden'){
      try{ video.pause(); }catch(e){}
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
  const handleBlur = () => { try{ video.pause(); }catch(e){} };
  window.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur);
  window._videoSecurityCleanup = () => {
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
    window.removeEventListener('blur', handleBlur);
  };
}

function requestPlayerFullscreen(playerId){
  const player = document.getElementById(playerId);
  const wrap = player ? player.closest('.iap-body') : document.querySelector('.iap-body');
  const target = wrap || player;
  if(!target){ toast('غير مدعوم','warn'); return; }
  const el = target;
  try{
    if(el.requestFullscreen) el.requestFullscreen();
    else if(el.webkitRequestFullscreen) el.webkitRequestFullscreen();
    else if(el.msRequestFullscreen) el.msRequestFullscreen();
    else if(player && player.requestFullscreen) player.requestFullscreen();
    else toast('الشاشة الكاملة غير مدعومة','warn');
  }catch(e){ toast('تعذر تفعيل الشاشة الكاملة','warn'); }
}

function closeLessonPlayer(isDevice){
  const v = document.querySelector('#iapRoot video');
  if(v){ try{ v.pause(); v.src = ''; v.load(); }catch(e){} }
  if(typeof window._videoSecurityCleanup === 'function'){
    try{ window._videoSecurityCleanup(); }catch(e){}
    window._videoSecurityCleanup = null;
  }
  document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = '';
  stopPrivacyProtection();
  if(document.fullscreenElement){ try{ document.exitFullscreen(); }catch(e){} }
}

function openExternalLink(url, title){
  if(!url){ toast('لا يوجد رابط','warn'); return; }
  if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) url = 'https://' + url;
  try{ window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer'); }catch(e){}
  toast('تم فتح الرابط','gold');
}

/* ============ عارض PDF ============ */
let pdfDrawingEnabled = true;

function openPDFViewerInline(title, pdfData, viewerLabel, uniqueKey){
  let pdfSource = pdfData;
  if(!pdfSource){ toast('لا يوجد ملف','warn'); return; }

  const pdfKey = 'pdf_annot_' + b64encode((uniqueKey || title) + '_' + viewerLabel).slice(0,60);
  pdfDrawingEnabled = true;

  const html = `
    <div class="iap-overlay" id="lessonOverlay">
      <div class="iap-window">
        <div class="iap-header" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#4c1d95);color:#fff;flex-shrink:0">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:var(--purple);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0">📄</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${esc(title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#c4b5fd;font-weight:700">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</div>
          <button class="iap-btn close" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.85);border:none;color:#fff;border-radius:9px;width:36px;height:36px;display:grid;place-items:center;cursor:pointer">✕</button>
        </div>
        <div style="padding:8px 14px;background:#f8fafc;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;color:#475569">🎨 أدوات:</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" id="pdfToolHighlight" onclick="setPdfTool('highlight')">🖍️ تظليل</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="pdfToolPen" onclick="setPdfTool('pen')">✏️ قلم</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="pdfToolEraser" onclick="setPdfTool('eraser')">🧽 ممحاة</button>
          <input type="color" id="pdfColor" value="#fbbf24" style="width:40px;height:34px;padding:2px;cursor:pointer;border-radius:8px;border:1.5px solid var(--border)">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" id="pdfToggleDraw" onclick="togglePdfDrawing()">✏️ الكتابة: مفعلة</button>
          <span style="margin-inline-start:auto;display:flex;gap:6px">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-ok-soft btn-sm" onclick="savePdfAnnotations('${pdfKey}')">💾 حفظ</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="clearPdfAnnotations()">🗑 مسح</button>
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="iap-body" id="pdfBodyContainer" style="flex:1;background:#525659;overflow:auto;padding:10px;position:relative" oncontextmenu="return false">
          <div id="pdfScrollHint" style="display:none;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:20;background:rgba(16,185,129,.95);color:#fff;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:10px;align-items:center;gap:8px;justify-content:center">
            <span>👆</span><span>اسحب للتنقل بين الصفحات</span>
          </div>
          <div style="position:absolute;top:20px;right:20px;z-index:11;pointer-events:none">
            <span style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);color:rgba(255,255,255,.7);font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 12px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</span>
          </div>
          <div id="pdfPagesContainer" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:12px;position:relative;z-index:2">
            <div style="color:#fff;padding:30px;text-align:center;font-weight:700">📄 جاري تحميل الملف...</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="iap-footer" style="padding:10px 16px;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700">🔒 ملف PDF — الكتابة محفوظة لحسابك</div>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()">إغلاق</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = html;

  window._pdfCtx = {
    pdfKey,
    currentTool: 'highlight',
    currentColor: '#fbbf24',
    savedAnnotations: localStorage.getItem(pdfKey) || ''
  };

  pdfDrawingEnabled = true;
  const pdfBody = document.getElementById('pdfBodyContainer');
  if(pdfBody){
    pdfBody.classList.add('drawing-enabled');
    pdfBody.classList.remove('drawing-disabled');
  }

  renderPdfPages(pdfSource, window._pdfCtx.savedAnnotations);
  startPrivacyProtection();
}

function togglePdfDrawing(){
  pdfDrawingEnabled = !pdfDrawingEnabled;
  const btn = document.getElementById('pdfToggleDraw');
  const pdfBody = document.getElementById('pdfBodyContainer');
  const hint = document.getElementById('pdfScrollHint');

  if(btn){
    btn.className = pdfDrawingEnabled ? 'btn btn-ok btn-sm' : 'btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm';
    btn.textContent = pdfDrawingEnabled ? '✏️ الكتابة: مفعلة' : '🔒 الكتابة: معطلة';
  }
  if(pdfBody){
    if(pdfDrawingEnabled){
      pdfBody.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      pdfBody.style.touchAction = 'none';
    } else {
      pdfBody.style.overflow = 'auto';
      pdfBody.style.touchAction = 'pan-y';
    }
  }
  if(hint) hint.style.display = pdfDrawingEnabled ? 'none' : 'flex';

  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-overlay').forEach(ov => {
    ov.style.cursor = pdfDrawingEnabled ? 'crosshair' : 'default';
    ov.style.pointerEvents = pdfDrawingEnabled ? 'auto' : 'none';
  });
  toast(pdfDrawingEnabled ? '✅ تم تفعيل الكتابة' : '🔓 تم تعطيل الكتابة', pdfDrawingEnabled ? 'ok' : 'warn');
}

async function renderPdfPages(pdfSource, savedAnnotations){
  const container = document.getElementById('pdfPagesContainer');
  if(!container) return;
  if(typeof pdfjsLib === 'undefined'){
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#fff;padding:30px;text-align:center">⚠️ مكتبة PDF.js غير محمّلة</div>';
    return;
  }
  try{
    let arrayBuffer;
    if(typeof pdfSource === 'string' && pdfSource.startsWith('data:')){
      const blob = dataURItoBlob(pdfSource);
      if(!blob) throw new Error('invalid dataURI');
      arrayBuffer = await blob.arrayBuffer();
    } else if(typeof pdfSource === 'string'){
      const res = await fetch(pdfSource);
      arrayBuffer = await res.arrayBuffer();
    } else {
      arrayBuffer = await pdfSource.arrayBuffer();
    }

    const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data: arrayBuffer}).promise;

    let annotations = {};
    try{ annotations = JSON.parse(savedAnnotations || '{}'); }catch(e){ annotations = {}; }

    container.innerHTML = '';

    for(let pageNum = 1; pageNum <= pdf.numPages; pageNum++){
      const page = await pdf.getPage(pageNum);
      const viewport = page.getViewport({scale: 1.5});
      const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
      wrapper.style.cssText = 'position:relative;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.35);border-radius:6px;overflow:hidden;max-width:100%';
      wrapper.dataset.page = pageNum;

      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = viewport.width;
      canvas.height = viewport.height;
      canvas.style.cssText = 'display:block;max-width:100%;height:auto';
      wrapper.appendChild(canvas);

      const overlay = document.createElement('canvas');
      overlay.width = viewport.width;
      overlay.height = viewport.height;
      overlay.className = 'pdf-overlay';
      overlay.dataset.page = pageNum;
      overlay.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;cursor:crosshair;touch-action:none';
      wrapper.appendChild(overlay);

      container.appendChild(wrapper);

      await page.render({canvasContext: canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport}).promise;

      const ctx = overlay.getContext('2d');
      if(annotations[pageNum]){
        const img = new Image();
        img.onload = () => ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, overlay.width, overlay.height);
        img.src = annotations[pageNum];
      }

      attachOverlayEvents(overlay);
    }
  }catch(e){
    console.error('PDF render error:', e);
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#fff;padding:30px;text-align:center">تعذر عرض الملف</div>';
  }
}

function attachOverlayEvents(overlay){
  const ctx = overlay.getContext('2d');
  let drawing = false;
  let lastX = 0, lastY = 0;

  const getPos = (e) => {
    const rect = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scaleX = overlay.width / rect.width;
    const scaleY = overlay.height / rect.height;
    const clientX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    const clientY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    return { x: (clientX - rect.left) * scaleX, y: (clientY - rect.top) * scaleY };
  };

  const start = (e) => {
    if(!pdfDrawingEnabled) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const tool = window._pdfCtx?.currentTool || 'highlight';
    if(tool === 'eraser'){
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, overlay.width, overlay.height);
      return;
    }
    drawing = true;
    const p = getPos(e);
    lastX = p.x; lastY = p.y;
  };

  const move = (e) => {
    if(!drawing) return;
    if(!pdfDrawingEnabled){ drawing = false; return; }
    e.preventDefault();
    const tool = window._pdfCtx?.currentTool || 'highlight';
    const color = window._pdfCtx?.currentColor || '#fbbf24';
    const p = getPos(e);

    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

    if(tool === 'highlight'){
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
      ctx.strokeStyle = color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 24;
    } else {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.strokeStyle = color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    }

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(lastX, lastY);
    ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
    ctx.stroke();
    lastX = p.x; lastY = p.y;
  };

  const end = () => { drawing = false; ctx.globalAlpha = 1; };

  overlay.addEventListener('mousedown', start);
  overlay.addEventListener('mousemove', move);
  overlay.addEventListener('mouseup', end);
  overlay.addEventListener('mouseleave', end);
  overlay.addEventListener('touchstart', start, {passive:false});
  overlay.addEventListener('touchmove', move, {passive:false});
  overlay.addEventListener('touchend', end);
}

function setPdfTool(tool){
  if(!window._pdfCtx) return;
  window._pdfCtx.currentTool = tool;
  ['highlight','pen','eraser'].forEach(t => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('pdfTool' + t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1));
    if(btn) btn.className = 'btn ' + (t === tool ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-ghost') + ' btn-sm';
  });
}

function savePdfAnnotations(pdfKey){
  if(!window._pdfCtx) return;
  const annotations = {};
  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-overlay').forEach(ov => {
    annotations[ov.dataset.page] = ov.toDataURL('image/png');
  });
  try{
    localStorage.setItem(pdfKey, JSON.stringify(annotations));
    window._pdfCtx.savedAnnotations = JSON.stringify(annotations);
    toast('تم حفظ الكتابة ✓','gold');
  }catch(e){
    toast('تعذر الحفظ — مساحة ممتلئة','err');
  }
}

function clearPdfAnnotations(){
  if(!confirm('مسح كل الكتابات؟')) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-overlay').forEach(ov => {
    const ctx = ov.getContext('2d');
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, ov.width, ov.height);
  });
  if(window._pdfCtx){
    try{ localStorage.removeItem(window._pdfCtx.pdfKey); }catch(e){}
    window._pdfCtx.savedAnnotations = '';
  }
  toast('تم المسح','warn');
}

document.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
  if(e.target && e.target.id === 'pdfColor' && window._pdfCtx){
    window._pdfCtx.currentColor = e.target.value;
  }
});

function openHomeworkPDFInline(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const hw = s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId); if(!hw) return;
  const src = hw.pdfData || hw.pdfUrl;
  if(!src){ toast('لا يوجد ملف','warn'); return; }
  showContentLoader(hw.title, '📄 ملف PDF', `proceedHomeworkPDF('${studentId}','${hwId}')`, getViewerLabel(), '📄');
  setTimeout(() => {
    if(document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML.includes('جاري تجهيز المحتوى')){
      proceedHomeworkPDF(studentId, hwId);
    }
  }, 1500);
}

function proceedHomeworkPDF(studentId, hwId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const hw = s.homework.find(x => x.id === hwId); if(!hw) return;
  const viewerLabel = ui.studentToken ? getViewerLabel() : (db.settings.teacherName + ' (المعلم)');
  const src = hw.pdfData || hw.pdfUrl;
  if(!src) return;
  openPDFViewerInline(hw.title, src, viewerLabel, 'hw_' + studentId + '_' + hwId);
}

function openNotePDF(noteId){
  const note = db.notes.find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!note) return;
  showContentLoader(note.title, note.subject ? '📚 ' + note.subject : '📄 مذكرة', `proceedNotePDF('${noteId}')`, getViewerLabel(), '📄');
  setTimeout(() => {
    if(document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML.includes('جاري تجهيز المحتوى')){
      proceedNotePDF(noteId);
    }
  }, 1500);
}

function proceedNotePDF(noteId){
  const note = db.notes.find(x => x.id === noteId); if(!note) return;
  const viewerLabel = getViewerLabel();
  const src = note.pdfData || note.pdfUrl;
  if(!src){ toast('لا يوجد ملف','warn'); return; }
  openPDFViewerInline(note.title, src, viewerLabel, 'note_' + noteId);
}

function openLinkInline(url, title){
  if(!url){ toast('لا يوجد رابط','warn'); return; }
  if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) url = 'https://' + url;
  showContentLoader(title || 'فتح الرابط', '🔗 محتوى خارجي محمي', `proceedLinkInline('${esc(url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(title||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')`, getViewerLabel(), '🔗');
  setTimeout(() => {
    if(document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML.includes('جاري تجهيز المحتوى')){
      proceedLinkInline(url, title);
    }
  }, 1500);
}

function proceedLinkInline(url, title){
  const viewerLabel = getViewerLabel();
  const embedUrl = buildEmbedURL(url);
  const playerId = 'linkPlayer_' + Date.now();
  let mediaHTML;
  if(!embedUrl){
    mediaHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100%;gap:16px;padding:30px;text-align:center;color:#fff">
        <div style="font-size:64px">🔗</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900">هذا الرابط لا يقبل التضمين</div>
        <button class="btn btn-gold btn-lg" onclick="openExternalLink('${esc(url).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${esc(title||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">🚀 فتح الرابط</button>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    mediaHTML = `<iframe id="${playerId}" src="${esc(embedUrl)}" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allow="autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture; clipboard-read; clipboard-write" allowfullscreen webkitallowfullscreen mozallowfullscreen frameborder="0" oncontextmenu="return false"></iframe>`;
  }
  const html = `
    <div class="iap-overlay" id="lessonOverlay">
      <div class="iap-window">
        <div class="iap-header" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#1e40af);color:#fff;flex-shrink:0">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0">🔗</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${esc(title || 'محتوى')}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#93c5fd;font-weight:700">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</div>
          <button class="iap-btn close" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.85);border:none;color:#fff;border-radius:9px;width:36px;height:36px;display:grid;place-items:center;cursor:pointer">✕</button>
        </div>
        <div class="iap-body" style="flex:1;background:#1e293b;overflow:hidden;position:relative" oncontextmenu="return false">
          <div style="position:absolute;top:20px;right:20px;z-index:11;pointer-events:none">
            <span style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);color:rgba(255,255,255,.7);font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 12px;border-radius:99px;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">👤 ${esc(viewerLabel)}</span>
          </div>
          ${mediaHTML}
        </div>
        <div class="iap-footer" style="padding:10px 16px;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center">
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700">🔒 محتوى محمي</div>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeLessonPlayer()">إغلاق</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML = html;
  startPrivacyProtection();
}

/* ============ حماية الخصوصية ============ */
let privacyHandlers = {};

function startPrivacyProtection(){
  if(privacyHandlers.key) return;
  privacyHandlers.key = (e) => {
    if(!document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML && !document.querySelector('.protect-content')) return;
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && ['s','p','c','u','a'].includes(k)){
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      toast('🔒 محتوى محمي','warn');
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('keydown', privacyHandlers.key, true);

  privacyHandlers.keyup = (e) => {
    if(e.key === 'PrintScreen' || e.code === 'PrintScreen' || e.keyCode === 44){
      try{ navigator.clipboard.writeText('🔒 محتوى محمي'); }catch(err){}
      document.body.style.transition = 'filter .2s';
      document.body.style.filter = 'blur(40px) saturate(0)';
      toast('🔒 لقطات الشاشة ممنوعة','err');
      setTimeout(() => { document.body.style.filter = 'none'; }, 2000);
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('keyup', privacyHandlers.keyup, true);

  privacyHandlers.copy = (e) => {
    if(document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML || (e.target && e.target.closest('.protect-content'))){
      e.preventDefault();
      if(e.clipboardData) e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', '🔒 محتوى محمي');
      toast('🔒 النسخ غير مسموح','warn');
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('copy', privacyHandlers.copy, true);
  document.addEventListener('cut', privacyHandlers.copy, true);

  privacyHandlers.blur = () => {
    const iap = document.getElementById('iapRoot');
    const protect = document.querySelector('.protect-content');
    const isHidden = document.visibilityState === 'hidden' || !document.hasFocus();
    if(iap && iap.innerHTML) iap.style.filter = isHidden ? 'blur(40px) saturate(0)' : 'none';
    if(protect) protect.style.filter = isHidden ? 'blur(30px) saturate(0)' : 'none';
  };
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', privacyHandlers.blur);
  window.addEventListener('blur', privacyHandlers.blur);
  window.addEventListener('focus', privacyHandlers.blur);
}

function stopPrivacyProtection(){
  if(privacyHandlers.key) document.removeEventListener('keydown', privacyHandlers.key, true);
  if(privacyHandlers.keyup) document.removeEventListener('keyup', privacyHandlers.keyup, true);
  if(privacyHandlers.copy){
    document.removeEventListener('copy', privacyHandlers.copy, true);
    document.removeEventListener('cut', privacyHandlers.copy, true);
  }
  if(privacyHandlers.blur){
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', privacyHandlers.blur);
    window.removeEventListener('blur', privacyHandlers.blur);
    window.removeEventListener('focus', privacyHandlers.blur);
  }
  privacyHandlers = {};
}

/* ============ الشهادات - توليد وطباعة ============ */
function generateCertificateHTML(student, cert){
  const design = CERT_DESIGNS.find(d => d.id === cert.design) || CERT_DESIGNS[0];
  const isDark = design.dark;
  const textColor = isDark ? '#f1f5f9' : '#1e293b';
  const mutedColor = isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';
  const borderColor = design.color;
  const bg1 = design.bg1;
  const bg2 = design.bg2;
  const goldColor = isDark ? '#fcd34d' : design.color;

  return `
    <div class="cert" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${bg1},${bg2});border:14px double ${borderColor};border-radius:12px;padding:40px 30px;text-align:center;position:relative;box-shadow:0 25px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.2);color:${textColor};max-width:900px;margin:0 auto;font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif">
      <div style="position:absolute;top:20px;right:20px;font-size:48px;opacity:.35;color:${goldColor}">❋</div>
      <div style="position:absolute;top:20px;left:20px;font-size:48px;opacity:.35;color:${goldColor}">❋</div>
      <div style="position:absolute;bottom:20px;right:20px;font-size:48px;opacity:.35;color:${goldColor}">❋</div>
      <div style="position:absolute;bottom:20px;left:20px;font-size:48px;opacity:.35;color:${goldColor}">❋</div>
      <div style="width:90px;height:90px;margin:0 auto 15px;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;background:linear-gradient(135deg,${borderColor},${borderColor}cc);color:#fff;font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:52px;font-weight:700;box-shadow:0 12px 30px ${borderColor}66">ص</div>
      <div style="font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:42px;font-weight:700;color:${goldColor};margin-bottom:6px;letter-spacing:2px">${esc(cert.title)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:${mutedColor};letter-spacing:10px;margin-bottom:26px">CERTIFICATE OF COMPLETION</div>
      <div style="margin-bottom:18px;color:${textColor};font-size:16px;font-weight:600">تشهد منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)} للغة العربية بأن</div>
      <div style="font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:52px;font-weight:700;color:${goldColor};margin:22px 0;line-height:1.3;letter-spacing:1px">${esc(cert.studentName)}</div>
      <div style="width:70%;height:3px;margin:0 auto 22px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,${goldColor},transparent)"></div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;color:${textColor};line-height:2;margin:22px auto;max-width:680px;font-weight:500">${esc(cert.desc)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:900;color:${goldColor};margin:18px 0;letter-spacing:2px">التقدير: ${esc(cert.grade)}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;margin-top:50px;padding-top:30px;border-top:3px dashed ${borderColor};flex-wrap:wrap;gap:20px">
        <div style="text-align:center;flex:1;min-width:180px">
          <div style="width:200px;max-width:100%;border-top:2px solid ${textColor};margin:0 auto 10px"></div>
          <div style="font-size:14px;color:${textColor};font-weight:800">${esc(cert.teacherName)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:${mutedColor};font-weight:600;margin-top:3px">معلم اللغة العربية</div>
        </div>
        <div style="width:100px;height:100px;border-radius:50%;border:4px solid ${goldColor};display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:'Amiri',serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${goldColor};text-align:center;line-height:1.3;padding:8px;background:${isDark ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.05)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.65)'}">
          شهادة<br>معتمدة<br>${new Date(cert.issuedAt).getFullYear()}
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:${mutedColor};font-weight:800;text-align:center;min-width:200px">
          <div>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(cert.issuedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:8px">رقم الشهادة: ${cert.id.slice(-8).toUpperCase()}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function printCertificate(studentId, certId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const c = s.certificates.find(x => x.id === certId); if(!c) return;
  const certHtml = generateCertificateHTML(s, c);
  const w = window.open('', '_blank');
  if(!w){ toast('اسمح بالنوافذ المنبثقة','warn'); return; }
  w.document.write(`
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html dir="rtl" lang="ar">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>${esc(c.title)}</title>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&family=Amiri:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
      <style>
        *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
        @page{size:A4 landscape;margin:0}
        body{font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;background:#f1f5f9;padding:20px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:100vh}
        @media print{body{background:#fff;padding:0}.cert{box-shadow:none!important;border-radius:0!important;width:100vw!important;height:100vh!important;max-width:none!important}}
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>${certHtml}<script>setTimeout(()=>window.print(),500);<\/script></body>
    </html>`);
  w.document.close();
}

function saveCertificateAsImage(studentId, certId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const c = s.certificates.find(x => x.id === certId); if(!c) return;
  if(typeof html2canvas === 'undefined'){ toast('مكتبة الصور غير جاهزة','warn'); return; }
  toast('جاري التجهيز...','gold');
  const container = document.createElement('div');
  container.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:-10000px;left:0;z-index:-1;background:#fff';
  container.innerHTML = generateCertificateHTML(s, c);
  document.body.appendChild(container);
  setTimeout(async () => {
    try{
      const el = container.querySelector('.cert');
      const canvas = await html2canvas(el, {scale: 2, backgroundColor: '#fff', useCORS: true, logging: false});
      const dataURL = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.download = `شهادة-${s.name}-${todayISO()}.png`;
      link.href = dataURL;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      toast('تم حفظ الشهادة كصورة ✓','gold');
      playSound('success');
    }catch(e){
      console.error(e);
      toast('تعذر الحفظ','err');
    }finally{
      document.body.removeChild(container);
    }
  }, 200);
}

/* ============ حفظ كصورة عام ============ */
async function saveElementAsImage(elementId, filename){
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
  if(!el){ toast('العنصر غير موجود','err'); return; }
  try{
    if(typeof html2canvas === 'undefined'){
      toast('مكتبة الصور غير محمّلة','warn');
      printElement(elementId);
      return;
    }
    toast('جارٍ تجهيز الصورة...','warn');
    if(document.fonts && document.fonts.ready){ try{ await document.fonts.ready; }catch(e){} }
    const canvas = await html2canvas(el, {
      scale: 2,
      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
      useCORS: true,
      logging: false,
      windowWidth: el.scrollWidth,
      windowHeight: el.scrollHeight
    });
    const dataURL = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.download = (filename || 'report') + '-' + todayISO() + '.png';
    link.href = dataURL;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    toast('تم حفظ الصورة ✓','gold');
    playSound('success');
  }catch(e){
    console.error('saveElementAsImage error:', e);
    toast('تعذر حفظ الصورة — سيتم فتح الطباعة','warn');
    printElement(elementId);
  }
}

function printElement(elementId){
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
  if(!el){ toast('العنصر غير موجود','err'); return; }
  const w = window.open('', '_blank');
  if(!w){ toast('اسمح بالنوافذ المنبثقة','warn'); return; }
  const styleText = document.querySelector('style') ? document.querySelector('style').textContent : '';
  w.document.write(`
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html dir="rtl" lang="ar">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>طباعة</title>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;500;600;700;800;900&family=Amiri:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
      <style>${styleText}</style>
      <style>
        *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
        @page{size:A4;margin:10mm}
        body{font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;background:#fff;padding:0;color:#0f172a;direction:rtl;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}
        .report-canvas{width:100%!important;min-height:auto!important;box-shadow:none!important;border:2px solid #0f766e!important;padding:12mm!important;margin:0!important;border-radius:8px!important}
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>${el.outerHTML}<script>setTimeout(()=>window.print(),600);<\/script></body>
    </html>`);
  w.document.close();
}

function shareElementTextWhatsApp(text){
  if(!text){ toast('لا يوجد نص','warn'); return; }
  const wa = db.settings.whatsapp || WHATSAPP_NUMBER;
  const cleanWa = waIntlNumber(wa);
  const url = `https://wa.me/${cleanWa}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
  try{ window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener'); }catch(e){}
  toast('تم فتح واتساب ✓','gold');
}

function copyText(t){
  if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(() => toast('تم النسخ ✓'), () => toast(t));
  } else {
    const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
    ta.value = t; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
    try{ document.execCommand('copy'); toast('تم النسخ ✓'); }catch(e){ toast(t); }
    document.body.removeChild(ta);
  }
  playSound('click');
}

/* ============ نسخة احتياطية واسترداد ============ */
function backup(){
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(db, null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = 'salah-arabic-backup-' + todayISO() + '.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  toast('تم تنزيل النسخة الاحتياطية ✓','gold');
}

function openRestoreModal(){
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--info)">♻️ استرداد نسخة احتياطية</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">ارفع ملف JSON سبق تنزيله</div>
    <div class="img-upload-zone" onclick="document.getElementById('restoreFile').click()" style="border-color:#93c5fd;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#eff6ff,#fff)">
      <span class="iuz-ico">📁</span>
      <div class="iuz-txt" style="color:#1d4ed8">اضغط لاختيار الملف</div>
      <div class="iuz-sub">ملف JSON فقط</div>
      <input type="file" id="restoreFile" accept=".json,application/json" onchange="handleRestoreFile(event)">
    </div>
    <div class="notif-banner" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d">
      <span class="ico-b">⚠️</span>
      <div class="txt-b" style="color:#78350f">سيتم استبدال كل البيانات الحالية</div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button></div>
  `);
}

function handleRestoreFile(event){
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  if(!confirm('سيتم استبدال كل البيانات. متابعة؟')) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (e) => {
    try{
      const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
      if(!data || typeof data !== 'object'){ toast('ملف غير صالح','err'); return; }
      db = normalizeDB(data);
      saveDB();
      closeModal();
      render();
      toast('تم استرداد النسخة ✓','gold');
      playSound('celebrate');
    }catch(err){
      console.error(err);
      toast('فشل قراءة الملف','err');
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

/* ============ قوائم الفئات المستهدفة ============ */
function targetingSelectorHTML(prefix){
  const t = ui.targeting;
  return `
    <div style="margin-top:16px;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#eef2ff,#fff);border:1.5px solid #c7d2fe;border-radius:14px">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#4338ca;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        <span>🎯</span><span>الفئة المستهدفة</span>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button type="button" class="btn ${t.mode==='all'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="setTargetingMode('${prefix}','all')">👥 كل الطلاب</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn ${t.mode==='stage'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="setTargetingMode('${prefix}','stage')">🎓 حسب الصف</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn ${t.mode==='students'?'btn-primary':'btn-ghost'} btn-sm" onclick="setTargetingMode('${prefix}','students')">🔍 طلاب محددين</button>
      </div>
      <div id="${prefix}_tgt_stage" style="display:${t.mode==='stage'?'':'none'}">
        <div class="grid g2">
          <div class="field"><label>المرحلة</label>
            <select id="${prefix}_tgtStage">
              <option value="">كل المراحل</option>
              ${getAllStages().map(s => `<option value="${esc(s)}" ${t.stage===s?'selected':''}>${esc(s)}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="field"><label>المسار</label>
            <select id="${prefix}_tgtTrack">
              <option value="">كل المسارات</option>
              ${TRACKS.map(tr => `<option value="${esc(tr)}" ${t.track===tr?'selected':''}>${esc(tr)}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="${prefix}_tgt_students" style="display:${t.mode==='students'?'':'none'}">
        <div class="field">
          <label>ابحث بالاسم أو الهاتف</label>
          <input id="${prefix}_tgtSearch" placeholder="ابحث..." oninput="ui.targeting.search=this.value;renderTargetStudentList('${prefix}')" value="${esc(t.search||'')}">
        </div>
        <div style="max-height:240px;overflow-y:auto;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:10px;margin-top:8px;background:#fff" id="${prefix}_tgtList">${targetStudentListHTML(prefix)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:8px" id="${prefix}_tgtCount">تم تحديد: <b style="color:var(--primary-d)">${t.selectedStudents.length}</b> طالب</div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function targetStudentListHTML(prefix){
  const q = (ui.targeting.search||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = db.students.filter(s => !q || s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q));
  if(!list.length) return `<div class="empty" style="padding:20px;font-size:13px">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
  return list.slice(0, 300).map(s => {
    const checked = ui.targeting.selectedStudents.includes(s.id);
    return `
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;background:${checked?'#f0fdfa':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${checked?'#99f6e4':'transparent'};margin-bottom:4px">
        <input type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="toggleTargetStudent('${prefix}','${s.id}')" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--primary)">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')} • ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
        </div>
      </label>`;
  }).join('');
}

function renderTargetStudentList(prefix){
  const el = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgtList`);
  if(el) el.innerHTML = targetStudentListHTML(prefix);
  const cntEl = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgtCount`);
  if(cntEl) cntEl.innerHTML = `تم تحديد: <b style="color:var(--primary-d)">${ui.targeting.selectedStudents.length}</b> طالب`;
}

function toggleTargetStudent(prefix, id){
  const i = ui.targeting.selectedStudents.indexOf(id);
  if(i >= 0) ui.targeting.selectedStudents.splice(i, 1);
  else ui.targeting.selectedStudents.push(id);
  renderTargetStudentList(prefix);
}

function setTargetingMode(prefix, mode){
  ui.targeting.mode = mode;
  const stageBox = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgt_stage`);
  const studentsBox = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgt_students`);
  if(stageBox) stageBox.style.display = mode === 'stage' ? '' : 'none';
  if(studentsBox) studentsBox.style.display = mode === 'students' ? '' : 'none';
  const container = document.querySelector(`[onclick="setTargetingMode('${prefix}','all')"]`)?.parentElement;
  if(container){
    container.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
      const txt = btn.textContent;
      let btnMode = '';
      if(txt.includes('كل الطلاب')) btnMode = 'all';
      else if(txt.includes('حسب الصف')) btnMode = 'stage';
      else if(txt.includes('طلاب محددين')) btnMode = 'students';
      if(btnMode) btn.className = 'btn ' + (btnMode === mode ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-ghost') + ' btn-sm';
    });
  }
}

function getTargetingFromForm(prefix){
  const result = { targetMode: 'all', targetStage: '', targetTrack: '', targetStudents: [] };
  const mode = ui.targeting.mode || 'all';
  if(mode === 'stage'){
    result.targetMode = 'stage';
    const s = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgtStage`);
    const t = document.getElementById(`${prefix}_tgtTrack`);
    result.targetStage = s ? s.value : '';
    result.targetTrack = t ? t.value : '';
  } else if(mode === 'students'){
    result.targetMode = 'students';
    result.targetStudents = [...ui.targeting.selectedStudents];
  }
  return result;
}

function resetTargeting(){
  ui.targeting = { mode: 'all', stage: '', track: '', search: '', selectedStudents: [] };
}

/* ============ selector المجلدات ============ */
function folderSelectorHTML(prefix, kind){
  const folders = getAllFolders(kind);
  const draft = ui.folderDraft[kind] || '';
  return `

      <label>📁 المجلد (اختياري)</label>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
        <select id="${prefix}_folderSelect" style="flex:1;min-width:180px" onchange="ui.folderDraft.${kind}=this.value">
          <option value="">— بدون مجلد —</option>
          ${folders.map(f => `<option value="${esc(f)}" ${draft===f?'selected':''}>📁 ${esc(f)}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
        <input id="${prefix}_folderNew" placeholder="مجلد جديد" style="flex:1;min-width:150px" oninput="ui.folderDraft.${kind}=this.value">
      </div>
      <span class="hint">سيظهر المحتوى داخل مجلد بالاسم المحدد</span>
    </div>`;
}

/* ============ تعديل المهام والتقييمات ============ */
function editTaskModal(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const arr = kind === 'homework' ? s.homework : s.exams;
  const item = arr.find(x => x.id === itemId); if(!item) return;
  const isHW = kind === 'homework';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--primary-d)">✏️ تعديل ${isHW ? 'الواجب' : 'الاختبار'}</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${esc(s.name)}</div>
    <div class="field"><label>العنوان</label><input id="editTitle" value="${esc(item.title||'')}"></div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>الوصف / التعليمات</label><textarea id="editDesc" style="min-height:80px">${esc(item.desc || item.notes || '')}</textarea></div>
    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
      <div class="field"><label>تاريخ التسليم</label><input type="date" id="editDue" value="${esc(item.due||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>الدرجة الكاملة</label><input type="number" id="editMax" value="${esc(item.maxScore||item.total||'')}" inputmode="numeric"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>📅 تاريخ الفتح</label><input type="date" id="editStartDate" value="${esc(item.startDate||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>🕐 وقت الفتح</label><input type="time" id="editStartTime" value="${esc(item.startTime||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>📅 تاريخ الإغلاق</label><input type="date" id="editCloseDate" value="${esc(item.closeDate||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>🕐 وقت الإغلاق</label><input type="time" id="editCloseTime" value="${esc(item.closeTime||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>⏱️ المدة (دقيقة)</label><input type="number" id="editDuration" value="${esc(item.durationMinutes||0)}" min="0" max="600" inputmode="numeric"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveTaskEdit('${studentId}','${kind}','${itemId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveTaskEdit(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const arr = kind === 'homework' ? s.homework : s.exams;
  const item = arr.find(x => x.id === itemId); if(!item) return;
  item.title = val('editTitle') || item.title;
  if(kind === 'homework') item.desc = val('editDesc');
  else item.notes = val('editDesc');
  item.due = val('editDue');
  const mx = parseFloat(val('editMax')) || 0;
  if(mx) { item.maxScore = mx; item.total = mx; }
  item.startDate = val('editStartDate');
  item.startTime = val('editStartTime');
  item.startAt = item.startDate ? combineToTimestamp(item.startDate, item.startTime || '00:00') : null;
  item.closeDate = val('editCloseDate');
  item.closeTime = val('editCloseTime');
  item.closeAt = item.closeDate ? combineToTimestamp(item.closeDate, item.closeTime || '23:59') : null;
  item.durationMinutes = Math.max(0, parseInt(val('editDuration')) || 0);
  item.updatedAt = Date.now();
  s.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم حفظ التعديلات ✓','gold');
}

function editEvalModal(studentId, evalId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const e = s.evaluations.find(x => x.id === evalId); if(!e) return;
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#d97706">✏️ تعديل التقييم</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${esc(s.name)}</div>
    <div class="field"><label>اسم الحصة</label><input id="eeSession" value="${esc(e.session||'')}"></div>
    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
      <div class="field"><label>التاريخ</label><input type="date" id="eeDate" value="${esc(e.date||todayISO())}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>الحضور</label>
        <select id="eeAttend">${ATTEND.map(a => `<option ${a===e.attend?'selected':''}>${a}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label>الدرجة (اختياري)</label><input type="number" id="eeScore" value="${esc(e.score!=null?e.score:'')}" inputmode="numeric"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>ملاحظات</label><textarea id="eeNotes" style="min-height:80px">${esc(e.notes||'')}</textarea></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveEvalEdit('${studentId}','${evalId}')">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveEvalEdit(studentId, evalId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const e = s.evaluations.find(x => x.id === evalId); if(!e) return;
  e.session = val('eeSession') || e.session;
  e.date = val('eeDate') || e.date;
  e.attend = val('eeAttend');
  const sc = val('eeScore');
  e.score = sc !== '' ? parseFloat(sc) : null;
  e.notes = val('eeNotes');
  e.updatedAt = Date.now();
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم الحفظ ✓','gold');
}

/* ============ استرداد إعادة الإرسال واتساب ============ */
function resendTaskWA(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  let item;
  if(kind === 'homework') item = s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'exam') item = s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'eval') item = s.evaluations.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'certificate') item = s.certificates.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if(!item){ toast('العنصر غير موجود','warn'); return; }
  openResendWAModal(s, kind, item);
}

function openResendWAModal(student, kind, item){
  const teacherSender = db.settings.teacherSenderPhone || db.settings.teacherPhone;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#128c7e">💬 إرسال رسالة واتساب</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">أرسل رسالة احترافية عن "${esc(item.title || item.session || 'العنصر')}"</div>
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:14px;font-size:12.5px;color:#128c7e;font-weight:700">
      📤 سيتم الإرسال من رقم: <b style="font-size:14px;letter-spacing:1px">${esc(teacherSender)}</b>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:9px;margin-bottom:14px">
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:12px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px;color:#128c7e">
        <input type="checkbox" id="resendStudent" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:#25d366">
        📱 الطالب — ${esc(student.phone)}
      </label>
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:12px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px;color:#128c7e">
        <input type="checkbox" id="resendParent" ${student.parentPhone?'checked':''} ${student.parentPhone?'':'disabled'} style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:#25d366">
        👨‍👩‍👦 ولي الأمر — ${esc(student.parentPhone || 'غير مسجل')}
      </label>
    </div>
    <div class="field">
      <label>نص الرسالة (يمكنك تعديله)</label>
      <textarea id="resendText" style="min-height:180px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7">${esc(buildTaskWAMessage(kind, item, db.settings.teacherName || 'صلاح عطية'))}</textarea>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp" onclick="doResendWA('${student.id}','${kind}','${item.id}')">📤 إرسال</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function doResendWA(studentId, kind, itemId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const text = val('resendText');
  const toStudent = document.getElementById('resendStudent')?.checked;
  const toParent = document.getElementById('resendParent')?.checked;
  if(!toStudent && !toParent){ toast('حدد مستقبلًا','warn'); return; }
  if(!text){ toast('النص فارغ','warn'); return; }
  let item = null;
  if(kind === 'homework') item = s.homework.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'exam') item = s.exams.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'eval') item = s.evaluations.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  else if(kind === 'certificate') item = s.certificates.find(x => x.id === itemId);
  const title = item ? (item.title || item.session || '') : '';
  if(toStudent && s.phone){
    setTimeout(() => sendWhatsApp(s.phone, text, { recipientName: s.name, recipientType: 'student', category: kind || 'custom', title, studentId: s.id }), 100);
  }
  if(toParent && s.parentPhone){
    setTimeout(() => sendWhatsApp(s.parentPhone, text, { recipientName: s.name + ' — ولي الأمر', recipientType: 'parent', category: kind || 'custom', title, studentId: s.id }), 700);
  }
  closeModal();
  toast('تم فتح واتساب ✓','gold');
}

/* ============ تحصيل تلقائي - صور وطباعة ============ */
function buildAutoProgressHTML(s){
  const st = getAutoStats(s);
  const sorted = st.autoEvals.slice().sort((a,b) => (b.createdAt||0) - (a.createdAt||0));
  const teacherName = db.settings.teacherName || 'صلاح عطية';
  return `
    <div style="width:820px;background:#fff;padding:32px;font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;direction:rtl;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:16px">

        <div style="position:absolute;top:-40%;right:-10%;width:300px;height:300px;background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(167,139,250,.45),transparent 70%);border-radius:50%"></div>
        <div style="position:relative;z-index:1;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <div style="width:72px;height:72px;border-radius:20px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#c4b5fd,#7c3aed);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:36px;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.3);border:2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25)">📈</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;line-height:1.2">تقرير التحصيل التلقائي</div>

            <div style="font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px">📱 ${esc(s.phone)} • 📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);border:1px solid rgba(196,181,253,.3);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 18px;text-align:center">

        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#b45309">${st.hwEvals.length ? st.hwAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e;margin-top:4px">📝 متوسط الواجبات</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ede9fe,#fff);border:1.5px solid #c4b5fd;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#7c3aed">${st.exEvals.length ? st.exAvg + '%' : '—'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#5b21b6;margin-top:4px">📊 متوسط الاختبارات</div>

        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #86efac;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">

              </div>
            </div>

      <div style="margin-top:22px;padding-top:16px;border-top:2px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700;line-height:1.8">
        منصة الأستاذ <b style="color:#0f766e">${esc(teacherName)}</b> — 📞 ${esc(db.settings.teacherPhone)}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

async function saveAutoProgressAsImage(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  if(typeof html2canvas === 'undefined'){ toast('مكتبة الصور غير محمّلة','err'); return; }
  toast('جاري التجهيز...','gold');
  const container = document.createElement('div');
  container.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:-99999px;left:0;z-index:-1';
  container.innerHTML = buildAutoProgressHTML(s);
  document.body.appendChild(container);
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
  try{
    const target = container.firstElementChild;
    const canvas = await html2canvas(target, {scale: 2, backgroundColor:'#ffffff', useCORS:true, logging:false, windowWidth:900});
    canvas.toBlob(blob => {
      if(!blob){ if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container); toast('تعذر إنشاء الصورة','err'); return; }
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = `التحصيل-${(s.name||'طالب').replace(/\s+/g,'-')}-${todayISO()}.png`;
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
      setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);
      if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container);
      toast('تم حفظ التقرير كصورة ✓','gold');
      playSound('success');
    }, 'image/png', 0.95);
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container);
    toast('تعذر الحفظ','err');
  }
}

function printAutoProgress(studentId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const win = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=1000,height=700');
  if(!win){ toast('المتصفح منع فتح النافذة','warn'); return; }
  const html = buildAutoProgressHTML(s);
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html dir="rtl" lang="ar"><head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>تقرير التحصيل — ${esc(s.name)}</title>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;500;600;700;800;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>body{font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;padding:20px;background:#f1f5f9;margin:0}@media print{body{padding:0;background:#fff}}</style>
  </head><body>${html}
  <script>window.onload=function(){setTimeout(()=>window.print(),500)}<\/script>
  </body></html>`);
  win.document.close();
}

/* ============ الدورات المكتملة - صور وطباعة ============ */
function buildCompletedCyclesReportHTML(s){
  const teacherName = db.settings.teacherName || 'صلاح عطية';
  const cycles = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles : [];
  const totalAttended = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.attended || 0), 0);
  const totalAbsent = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.absent || 0), 0);
  const totalSessions = cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.recordedCount || 0), 0);
  const avgAttendance = cycles.length ? Math.round(cycles.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.attendanceRate || 0), 0) / cycles.length) : 0;
  return `
    <div style="width:820px;background:#fff;padding:32px;font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;direction:rtl;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:16px">
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#4c1d95,#7c3aed);color:#fff;border-radius:20px;padding:24px;margin-bottom:20px;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="position:relative;z-index:1;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <div style="width:72px;height:72px;border-radius:20px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#b45309);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:36px;flex-shrink:0">🏆</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;line-height:1.2">سجل الدورات المكتملة</div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;color:#c4b5fd;font-weight:700;margin-top:5px">${esc(s.name)} ${s.stage ? '— ' + esc(s.stage) : ''}</div>
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:12px;margin-bottom:18px">
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#b45309">${cycles.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e;margin-top:4px">🏆 دورات</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #86efac;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#15803d">${totalAttended}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#166534;margin-top:4px">✅ حضور</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fee2e2,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fecaca;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#b91c1c">${totalAbsent}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#991b1b;margin-top:4px">❌ غياب</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f5f3ff,#fff);border:1.5px solid #c4b5fd;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;color:#5b21b6">${avgAttendance}%</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#5b21b6;margin-top:4px">📊 متوسط الحضور</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900;color:#5b21b6;margin-bottom:12px">📋 تفاصيل الدورات:</div>
      ${cycles.length ? cycles.slice().reverse().map(c => `
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:12px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:10px;border-inline-start:5px solid #b45309">
          <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900;color:#78350f;margin-bottom:8px">دورة #${c.cycleNumber} — ${esc(c.monthName)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#b45309;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">📅 ${esc(c.startDateStr)} ← ${esc(c.endDateStr)}</div>
          <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:8px">
            <div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px;text-align:center">
              <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#15803d">${c.attended}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e">✅ حضور</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px;text-align:center">
              <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#b45309">${c.late}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e">⏰ متأخر</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px;text-align:center">
              <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#b91c1c">${c.absent}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e">❌ غياب</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px;text-align:center">
              <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#0f766e">${c.recordedCount}/${c.totalSessions}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e">📚 حصص</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px;text-align:center">
              <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:${c.attendanceRate>=80?'#15803d':c.attendanceRate>=60?'#b45309':'#b91c1c'}">${c.attendanceRate}%</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e">📊 حضور</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('') : '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:700">لا توجد دورات</div>'}
      
      <div style="margin-top:22px;padding-top:16px;border-top:2px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#64748b;font-weight:700;line-height:1.8">
        منصة الأستاذ <b style="color:#0f766e">${esc(teacherName)}</b> — 📞 ${esc(db.settings.teacherPhone)}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  const cycles = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles : [];

  if(typeof html2canvas === 'undefined'){ toast('مكتبة الصور غير محمّلة','err'); return; }
  toast('جاري التجهيز...','gold');
  const container = document.createElement('div');
  container.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:-99999px;left:0;z-index:-1';
  container.innerHTML = buildCompletedCyclesReportHTML(s);
  document.body.appendChild(container);
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
  try{
    const target = container.firstElementChild;
    const canvas = await html2canvas(target, {scale: 2, backgroundColor:'#ffffff', useCORS:true, logging:false, windowWidth:900});
    canvas.toBlob(blob => {
      if(!blob){ if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container); toast('تعذر الحفظ','err'); return; }
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = `سجل-الدورات-${(s.name||'طالب').replace(/\s+/g,'-')}-${todayISO()}.png`;
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
      setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);
      if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container);
      toast('تم الحفظ ✓','gold');
      playSound('success');
    }, 'image/png', 0.95);
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    if(container.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(container);
    toast('تعذر الحفظ','err');

}
