/* ============ مركز الدورات المكتملة (لكل الطلاب) ============ */
function openCompletedCyclesCenter(){
  const rows = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => {
    const cycles = (s.subscription && s.subscription.completedCycles) ? s.subscription.completedCycles : [];
    cycles.forEach(c => rows.push({ student: s, cycle: c }));
  });
  if(!rows.length){ toast('لا توجد دورات مكتملة','warn'); return; }
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#b45309">🏆 سجل الدورات المكتملة</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${rows.length} دورة مؤرشفة لجميع الطلاب</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px">
      ${rows.slice().reverse().map(({student: s, cycle: c}) => `
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fff);border:1.5px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:10px;cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${s.id}');setTimeout(()=>{ui.detailTab='cycles';render()},200)">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            ${avatar(s.name, 42)}
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900;color:#78350f">${esc(s.name)} — دورة #${c.cycleNumber}</div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;color:#b45309;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px">📅 ${esc(c.startDateStr)} ← ${esc(c.endDateStr)}</div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:6px">
                <span class="badge b-ok">✅ ${c.attended}</span>
                <span class="badge b-warn">⏰ ${c.late}</span>
                <span class="badge b-bad">❌ ${c.absent}</span>
                <span class="badge b-info">📊 ${c.attendanceRate}%</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}

/* ============ عارض التقييمات الشامل ============ */
function openEvalViewerModal(){
  ui.evalViewer = { filter: 'all', search: '', stage: '', approvalStatus: 'all' };
  renderEvalViewerModal();
}

function getFilteredEvals(){
  const f = ui.evalViewer;
  const rows = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => {
    s.evaluations.forEach(e => rows.push({ student: s, item: e }));
  });
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = combineToTimestamp(a.item.date) || a.item.createdAt || 0;
    const tb = combineToTimestamp(b.item.date) || b.item.createdAt || 0;
    return tb - ta;
  });
  let list = rows;
  if(f.search){
    const q = f.search.toLowerCase();
    list = list.filter(r => r.student.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.student.phone.includes(q) || (r.item.session || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }
  if(f.stage) list = list.filter(r => r.student.stage === f.stage);
  if(f.approvalStatus === 'approved') list = list.filter(r => r.item.parentApproved);
  else if(f.approvalStatus === 'pending') list = list.filter(r => r.item.parentPassword && r.item.parentPassword.trim() && !r.item.parentApproved);
  else if(f.approvalStatus === 'noPwd') list = list.filter(r => !r.item.parentPassword || !r.item.parentPassword.trim());
  return list;
}

function renderEvalViewerModal(){
  const f = ui.evalViewer;
  const list = getFilteredEvals();
  const stages = getAllStages();
  const total = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.length,0);
  const approved = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.filter(e=>e.parentApproved).length,0);
  const pending = db.students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.filter(e=>e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() && !e.parentApproved).length,0);

  const itemsHtml = list.length ? list.map(r => {
    const s = r.student, e = r.item;
    const parentBadge = e.parentApproved ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✅ معتمد</span>' : (e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() ? '<span class="badge b-warn">⏳ بانتظار</span>' : '<span class="badge b-gray">بدون اعتماد</span>');
    return `
      <div class="wa-archive-item" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${s.id}');setTimeout(()=>{ui.detailTab='evals';render()},200)">
        <div class="wai-head">
          <div class="wai-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706)">🎯</div>
          <div class="wai-meta">
            <div class="wai-name">${esc(e.session || 'حصة')}</div>
            <div class="wai-phone">👤 ${esc(s.name)} • 📚 ${esc(s.stage||'—')} • 📱 ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="wai-date">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0">
          <span class="badge ${e.attend === 'حاضر' ? 'b-ok' : e.attend === 'متأخر' ? 'b-warn' : 'b-bad'}">${esc(e.attend)}</span>
          ${parentBadge}
          ${e.score != null ? `<span class="badge b-info">⭐ ${e.score}</span>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('') : `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:700"><div style="font-size:52px;margin-bottom:12px;opacity:.5">📭</div>لا توجد تقييمات مطابقة</div>`;

  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#d97706">
      <span style="font-size:22px">🎯</span> عارض التقييمات الشامل
      <span style="margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:12px;background:#fef3c7;color:#78350f;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:99px;font-weight:800">${list.length} / ${total}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-sub">تصفح وحلل جميع تقييمات الطلاب بتصنيفات متعددة</div>

    <div class="grid g3" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <div class="grade-info-cell" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d">
        <div class="gic-label">إجمالي التقييمات</div>
        <div class="gic-value" style="color:#d97706">${total}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#f0fdf4);border-color:#86efac">
        <div class="gic-label">معتمدة</div>
        <div class="gic-value" style="color:#15803d">${approved}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fef3c7,#fff);border-color:#fde68a">
        <div class="gic-label">بانتظار</div>
        <div class="gic-value" style="color:#92400e">${pending}</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="search-wrap">
      <span class="s-ico">🔍</span>
      <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم، الهاتف، اسم الحصة..." value="${esc(f.search)}" oninput="ui.evalViewer.search=this.value;refreshEvalViewer()">
    </div>

    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <div class="field"><label style="font-size:11.5px">الصف</label>
        <select onchange="ui.evalViewer.stage=this.value;refreshEvalViewer()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="">كل الصفوف</option>
          ${stages.map(st => `<option ${f.stage===st?'selected':''}>${esc(st)}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label style="font-size:11.5px">حالة الاعتماد</label>
        <select onchange="ui.evalViewer.approvalStatus=this.value;refreshEvalViewer()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="all" ${f.approvalStatus==='all'?'selected':''}>الكل</option>
          <option value="approved" ${f.approvalStatus==='approved'?'selected':''}>✅ معتمد</option>
          <option value="pending" ${f.approvalStatus==='pending'?'selected':''}>⏳ بانتظار</option>
          <option value="noPwd" ${f.approvalStatus==='noPwd'?'selected':''}>بدون اعتماد</option>
        </select>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div id="evalViewerList" style="max-height:480px;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px">${itemsHtml}</div>

    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info" onclick="saveElementAsImage('evalViewerList','تقرير-التقييمات')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="printElement('evalViewerList')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function refreshEvalViewer(){
  const el = document.getElementById('evalViewerList');
  if(!el){ renderEvalViewerModal(); return; }
  const list = getFilteredEvals();
  el.innerHTML = list.length ? list.map(r => {
    const s = r.student, e = r.item;
    const parentBadge = e.parentApproved ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✅ معتمد</span>' : (e.parentPassword && e.parentPassword.trim() ? '<span class="badge b-warn">⏳ بانتظار</span>' : '<span class="badge b-gray">بدون اعتماد</span>');
    return `
      <div class="wa-archive-item" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${s.id}');setTimeout(()=>{ui.detailTab='evals';render()},200)">
        <div class="wai-head">
          <div class="wai-ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706)">🎯</div>
          <div class="wai-meta">
            <div class="wai-name">${esc(e.session || 'حصة')}</div>
            <div class="wai-phone">👤 ${esc(s.name)} • 📚 ${esc(s.stage||'—')}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="wai-date">📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(e.date)}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0">
          <span class="badge ${e.attend === 'حاضر' ? 'b-ok' : e.attend === 'متأخر' ? 'b-warn' : 'b-bad'}">${esc(e.attend)}</span>
          ${parentBadge}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('') : `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:700">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
}

/* ============ التقرير الشامل للمنصة ============ */
function openFullPlatformReportModal(){
  const students = db.students;
  const total = students.length;
  const active = students.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length;
  const pending = students.filter(s => s.status === 'pending').length;
  const totalHW = students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.homework.length,0);
  const totalEx = students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.exams.length,0);
  const totalEvals = students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.length,0);
  const totalLessons = db.lessons.length;
  const totalNotes = db.notes.length;
  const totalLive = db.liveSessions.length;
  const totalCerts = students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.certificates.length,0);
  let totalRevenue = 0;
  students.forEach(s => (s.paymentHistory||[]).forEach(p => totalRevenue += parseFloat(p.amount) || 0));

  const reportId = 'fullPlatformReport';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--primary-d)">📊 التقرير الشامل للمنصة</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">نظرة شاملة على جميع البيانات</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:8px">
      <div id="${reportId}" class="report-canvas">
        <div class="rp-head">
          <div class="rp-logo">ص</div>
          <div class="rp-title">
            <h1>التقرير الشامل لمنصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)}</h1>
            <p>اللغة العربية للمرحلة الثانوية — ${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section">
          <h2>👥 الطلاب</h2>
          <div class="rp-stats-grid">
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${total}</div><div class="l">إجمالي الطلاب</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#16a34a">${active}</div><div class="l">مفعّل</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#d97706">${pending}</div><div class="l">بانتظار</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${students.filter(s=>(s.track||'عام')==='عام').length}</div><div class="l">مسار عام</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${students.filter(s=>(s.track||'عام')==='بكالوريا').length}</div><div class="l">بكالوريا</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section">
          <h2>📚 المحتوى التعليمي</h2>
          <div class="rp-stats-grid">
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalLessons}</div><div class="l">دروس</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalNotes}</div><div class="l">مذكرات</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalLive}</div><div class="l">محاضرات</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalHW}</div><div class="l">واجبات</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalEx}</div><div class="l">اختبارات</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalEvals}</div><div class="l">تقييمات</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${totalCerts}</div><div class="l">شهادات</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section">
          <h2>💰 المالية</h2>
          <div class="rp-stats-grid">
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#16a34a">${fmtPrice(totalRevenue)}</div><div class="l">إجمالي الإيرادات</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-footer">
          © ${new Date().getFullYear()} — منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)} للغة العربية
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info" onclick="saveElementAsImage('${reportId}','التقرير-الشامل')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="printElement('${reportId}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function shareFullReportWhatsApp(){
  const students = db.students;
  const total = students.length;
  const active = students.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length;
  const text = `📊 *التقرير الشامل للمنصة*\nمن منصة الأستاذ ${db.settings.teacherName}\n📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())}\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n👥 إجمالي الطلاب: ${total}\n✅ مفعّل: ${active}\n⏳ بانتظار: ${students.filter(s=>s.status==='pending').length}\n\n📚 الدروس: ${db.lessons.length}\n📄 المذكرات: ${db.notes.length}\n🎥 المحاضرات: ${db.liveSessions.length}\n📝 الواجبات: ${students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.homework.length,0)}\n📊 الاختبارات: ${students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.exams.length,0)}\n🎯 التقييمات: ${students.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.evaluations.length,0)}\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━`;
  shareElementTextWhatsApp(text);
}

/* ============ تقرير التحصيل التلقائي لكل الطلاب ============ */
function openAutoCollectReportModal(){
  const active = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  if(!active.length){ toast('لا يوجد طلاب مفعّلون','warn'); return; }

  const studentsWithStats = active.map(s => ({
    student: s,
    stats: getAutoStats(s),
    category: getStudentPerformanceCategory(computeStudentStats(s).overall || 0)
  })).sort((a, b) => (b.stats.overallAvg || 0) - (a.stats.overallAvg || 0));

  const catCounts = { perfect: 0, excellent: 0, good: 0, fair: 0, poor: 0 };
  studentsWithStats.forEach(x => catCounts[x.category.key]++);

  const reportId = 'autoCollectReport';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--primary-d)">📈 تقرير التحصيل التلقائي الشامل</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${studentsWithStats.length} طالب مفعّل — مرتبين حسب النسبة الإجمالية</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:8px">
      <div id="${reportId}" class="report-canvas">
        <div class="rp-head">
          <div class="rp-logo">📈</div>
          <div class="rp-title">
            <h1>تقرير التحصيل التلقائي</h1>
            <p>منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)} — ${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section">
          <h2>📊 ملخص التصنيفات</h2>
          <div class="rp-stats-grid">
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#d97706">${catCounts.perfect}</div><div class="l">⭐ متميز (90%+)</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#16a34a">${catCounts.excellent}</div><div class="l">🏆 ممتاز (80-89%)</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#2563eb">${catCounts.good}</div><div class="l">✅ جيد جدًا (60-79%)</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#d97706">${catCounts.fair}</div><div class="l">⚠️ مقبول (40-59%)</div></div>
            <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n" style="color:#dc2626">${catCounts.poor}</div><div class="l">🚨 يحتاج دعم</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section">
          <h2>🏆 ترتيب الطلاب</h2>
          ${studentsWithStats.map((r, i) => {
            const s = r.student, st = r.stats, cat = r.category;
            const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : '';
            return `
              <div style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:9px;border-inline-start:5px solid ${cat.color}">
                <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
                  <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                    <div style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:900">${medal} ${i+1}. ${esc(s.name)}</div>
                    <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;margin-top:4px">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')} • 📱 ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div style="text-align:center;flex-shrink:0">
                    <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:900;color:${cat.color}">${st.overallAvg}%</div>
                    <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:900;color:${cat.color};margin-top:2px">${cat.icon} ${cat.label}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>`;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
        <div class="rp-footer">© ${new Date().getFullYear()} — منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info" onclick="saveElementAsImage('${reportId}','التحصيل-التلقائي')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="printElement('${reportId}')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

/* ============ عارض التحصيل الدراسي ============ */
function openAchievementsViewer(){
  ui.achievement = {
    filter: 'all', search: '', stage: '', track: '', studentId: '', viewMode: 'students',
    category: 'all', sortBy: 'overall', expandedStudentId: null
  };
  renderAchievementsModal();
}

function renderAchievementsModal(){
  const f = ui.achievement;
  const stages = getAllStages();
  const allStudents = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  let totalHW = 0, totalEx = 0, totalScore = 0, totalMaxScore = 0;
  allStudents.forEach(s => {
    s.homework.forEach(h => { totalHW++; if(h.total){ totalScore += parseFloat(h.score||0); totalMaxScore += parseFloat(h.total||0); } });
    s.exams.forEach(e => { totalEx++; if(e.total){ totalScore += parseFloat(e.score||0); totalMaxScore += parseFloat(e.total||0); } });
  });
  const avgScore = totalMaxScore ? Math.round(totalScore / totalMaxScore * 100) : 0;

  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#2563eb">
      <span style="font-size:22px">🏆</span> التحصيل الدراسي
      <span style="margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:12px;background:#dbeafe;color:#1d4ed8;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:99px;font-weight:800">${allStudents.length} طالب</span>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-sub">عرض احترافي لإنجازات الطلاب — قابل للطباعة والحفظ كصورة</div>

    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1e293b,#0f172a);color:#fff;border-radius:22px;padding:26px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 20px 44px rgba(0,0,0,.28);margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="position:relative;z-index:1;display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:18px">
        <div style="width:68px;height:68px;border-radius:20px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:34px;box-shadow:0 10px 28px rgba(217,119,6,.5);flex-shrink:0">🏆</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;line-height:1.25">إنجازات المنصة</div>
          <div style="font-size:13px;color:#5eead4;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px">${fmtDateWithDay(todayISO())} • ${allStudents.length} طالب نشط</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(120px,1fr));gap:10px">
        <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#f59e0b);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${totalHW}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">📝 واجبات</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#f59e0b);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${totalEx}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">📊 اختبارات</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;text-align:center;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)">
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#f59e0b);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${avgScore}%</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">🎯 متوسط الأداء</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="search-wrap">
      <span class="s-ico">🔍</span>
      <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم أو الهاتف..." value="${esc(f.search)}" oninput="ui.achievement.search=this.value;refreshAchievementsList()">
    </div>

    <div class="grid g3" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <div class="field"><label style="font-size:11.5px">الصف</label>
        <select onchange="ui.achievement.stage=this.value;refreshAchievementsList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="">كل الصفوف</option>
          ${stages.map(st => `<option ${f.stage===st?'selected':''}>${esc(st)}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label style="font-size:11.5px">التصنيف</label>
        <select onchange="ui.achievement.category=this.value;refreshAchievementsList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="all" ${f.category==='all'?'selected':''}>📊 الكل</option>
          <option value="perfect" ${f.category==='perfect'?'selected':''}>⭐ متميز</option>
          <option value="excellent" ${f.category==='excellent'?'selected':''}>🏆 ممتاز</option>
          <option value="good" ${f.category==='good'?'selected':''}>✅ جيد جدًا</option>
          <option value="fair" ${f.category==='fair'?'selected':''}>⚠️ مقبول</option>
          <option value="poor" ${f.category==='poor'?'selected':''}>🚨 يحتاج دعم</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label style="font-size:11.5px">الترتيب</label>
        <select onchange="ui.achievement.sortBy=this.value;refreshAchievementsList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="overall" ${f.sortBy==='overall'?'selected':''}>📈 الأعلى أداء</option>
          <option value="name" ${f.sortBy==='name'?'selected':''}>🔤 أبجدي</option>
        </select>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div id="achievementsList" style="max-height:520px;overflow-y:auto">${renderAchievementsList()}</div>

    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info" onclick="saveElementAsImage('achievementsList','تقرير-التحصيل')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="printElement('achievementsList')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function renderAchievementsList(){
  const f = ui.achievement;
  const q = (f.search||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  let students = db.students.filter(s => {
    if(f.stage && s.stage !== f.stage) return false;
    if(!q) return true;
    return s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q);
  });

  if(f.category && f.category !== 'all'){
    students = students.filter(s => {
      const overall = computeStudentStats(s).overall || 0;
      return getStudentPerformanceCategory(overall).key === f.category;
    });
  }

  const sortBy = f.sortBy || 'overall';
  if(sortBy === 'overall') students.sort((a,b) => (computeStudentStats(b).overall||0) - (computeStudentStats(a).overall||0));
  else if(sortBy === 'name') students.sort((a,b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'ar'));

  if(!students.length){
    return `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8"><div style="font-size:52px;margin-bottom:12px;opacity:.5">📭</div><div>لا توجد نتائج</div></div>`;
  }

  return students.map(s => {
    const stats = computeStudentStats(s);
    const overall = stats.overall || 0;
    const cat = getStudentPerformanceCategory(overall);
    const color = cat.color;

    return `
      <div style="background:#fff;border:2px solid ${color}40;border-radius:18px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:14px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">
          ${avatar(s.name, 52)}
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:var(--text);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
              ${esc(s.name)}
              <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:900;padding:4px 11px;border-radius:99px;background:${color}22;color:${color}">${cat.icon} ${cat.label}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:3px">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')} • 📱 ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:center;padding:10px 18px;background:${color}15;border:2px solid ${color}40;border-radius:14px">
            <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:900;color:${color};line-height:1">${overall}%</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;color:${color};margin-top:3px">${cat.label}</div>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${s.id}')">📄 التفاصيل</button>
        </div>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px">
          <div style="background:#eff6ff;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;border-radius:10px;padding:10px;text-align:center">
            <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#2563eb">${s.homework.filter(h=>h.submitted).length}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;color:#1e40af;margin-top:3px">📝 واجبات</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:#f5f3ff;border:1px solid #ddd6fe;border-radius:10px;padding:10px;text-align:center">
            <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#7c3aed">${s.exams.filter(e=>e.submitted).length}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;color:#5b21b6;margin-top:3px">📊 اختبارات</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:#fef3c7;border:1px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:10px;padding:10px;text-align:center">
            <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#b45309">${s.evaluations.length}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e;margin-top:3px">🎯 تقييمات</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background:#f0fdf4;border:1px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:10px;padding:10px;text-align:center">
            <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#15803d">${s.certificates.length}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;color:#166534;margin-top:3px">🎓 شهادات</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function refreshAchievementsList(){
  const el = document.getElementById('achievementsList');
  if(el) el.innerHTML = renderAchievementsList();
}

function openStudentAchievementsTab(s){
  const stats = computeStudentStats(s);
  const overall = stats.overall || 0;
  const cat = getStudentPerformanceCategory(overall);
  const color = cat.color;

  const achievements = [];
  s.homework.filter(h => h.submitted).forEach(h => {
    achievements.push({ at: h.submittedAt||h.createdAt, type: 'homework', icon: '📝', color: '#2563eb', title: h.title, meta: 'واجب', score: h.score, total: h.total });
  });
  s.exams.filter(e => e.submitted).forEach(e => {
    achievements.push({ at: e.submittedAt||e.createdAt, type: 'exam', icon: '📊', color: '#7c3aed', title: e.title, meta: 'اختبار', score: e.score, total: e.total });
  });
  s.certificates.forEach(c => {
    achievements.push({ at: c.issuedAt, type: 'cert', icon: '🎓', color: '#d97706', title: c.title, meta: `شهادة — ${c.grade}`, score: null, total: null });
  });
  achievements.sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);

  return `
  <div class="card anim-in" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fff,#f8fafc)">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#fff">🏆</span>
      التحصيل الدراسي الكامل
      <span class="badge" style="margin-inline-start:auto;background:${color}22;color:${color}">${cat.icon} ${cat.label} ${overall}%</span>
    </div>
    
    <div class="grid g4" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="stat" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#eff6ff,#dbeafe);border-color:#bfdbfe">
        <span class="ico-stat">📝</span>
        <div class="num" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#60a5fa,#2563eb);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${s.homework.filter(h=>h.submitted).length}</div>
        <div class="lbl">واجبات مُسلَّمة</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#f5f3ff,#ede9fe);border-color:#ddd6fe">
        <span class="ico-stat">📊</span>
        <div class="num" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a78bfa,#7c3aed);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${s.exams.filter(e=>e.submitted).length}</div>
        <div class="lbl">اختبارات مُسلَّمة</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#fef3c7,#fffbeb);border-color:#fcd34d">
        <span class="ico-stat">🎯</span>
        <div class="num" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${s.evaluations.length}</div>
        <div class="lbl">تقييمات</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat" style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,#f0fdf4,#dcfce7);border-color:#bbf7d0">
        <span class="ico-stat">🎓</span>
        <div class="num" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4ade80,#16a34a);-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent">${s.certificates.length}</div>
        <div class="lbl">شهادات</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="sec-title" style="font-size:14px">
      <span class="ico" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:14px">📅</span>
      سجل الإنجازات الزمني
    </div>

    ${achievements.length ? `
      <div style="position:relative;padding-inline-start:32px">
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:12px;bottom:0;width:2px;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary-d))"></div>
        ${achievements.slice(0, 40).map(a => `
          <div style="position:relative;margin-bottom:14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px">
            <div style="position:absolute;top:20px;right:-24px;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary-d));border:3px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--primary-l)"></div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:11px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:8px">
              <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0;color:#fff;background:linear-gradient(135deg,${a.color},${a.color}cc)">${a.icon}</div>
              <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900">${esc(a.title)}</div>
                <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:2px">${esc(a.meta)} • ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(a.at).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div>
              </div>
              ${a.score !== null ? `<div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbbf24,#d97706);color:#fff;padding:8px 16px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:900;font-size:14px">${a.score}/${a.total}</div>` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    ` : `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🌱</span>لم يسجّل الطالب أي إنجاز بعد</div>`}

    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:16px">
      <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="saveElementAsImage('studentAchReport','تحصيل-${esc(s.name).replace(/\s+/g,'-')}')">💾 حفظ كصورة</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="printElement('studentAchReport')">🖨 طباعة</button>
    </div>

    <div id="studentAchReport" style="display:none">
      <div class="report-canvas">
        <div class="rp-head">
          <div class="rp-logo">🏆</div>
          <div class="rp-title">
            <h1>تقرير التحصيل الدراسي</h1>
            <p>${esc(s.name)} — ${esc(s.stage||'—')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-stats-grid">
          <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${overall}%</div><div class="l">${cat.label}</div></div>
          <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${s.homework.filter(h=>h.submitted).length}</div><div class="l">واجبات</div></div>
          <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${s.exams.filter(e=>e.submitted).length}</div><div class="l">اختبارات</div></div>
          <div class="rp-stat"><div class="n">${s.evaluations.length}</div><div class="l">تقييمات</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="rp-section" style="margin-top:20px">
          <h2>الإنجازات</h2>
          ${achievements.map(a => `
            <div class="rp-bar-row" style="grid-template-columns:1fr auto">
              <div class="lbl">${a.icon} ${esc(a.title)} — ${esc(a.meta)}</div>
              <div class="vl">${a.score !== null ? a.score + '/' + a.total : ''}</div>
            </div>
          `).join('')}
        </div>
        <div class="rp-footer">© ${new Date().getFullYear()} — منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

/* ============ أرشيف الواتساب ============ */
function openWAArchiveModal(){
  ui.waArchive = { search: '', category: 'all', dateFrom: '', dateTo: '', studentId: '' };
  renderWAArchiveModal();
}

function getFilteredWALog(){
  const f = ui.waArchive;
  const q = (f.search||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  let list = (db.waLog || []).slice().reverse();
  if(f.category && f.category !== 'all'){
    list = list.filter(x => x.category === f.category);
  }
  if(f.studentId){
    list = list.filter(x => x.studentId === f.studentId);
  }
  if(f.dateFrom){
    const t = new Date(f.dateFrom).getTime();
    if(!isNaN(t)) list = list.filter(x => x.sentAt >= t);
  }
  if(f.dateTo){
    const t = new Date(f.dateTo).getTime() + 24*3600*1000;
    if(!isNaN(t)) list = list.filter(x => x.sentAt < t);
  }
  if(q){
    list = list.filter(x =>
      (x.recipientName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (x.phone||'').includes(q) ||
      (x.text||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (x.title||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }
  return list;
}

function renderWAArchiveModal(){
  const f = ui.waArchive;
  const list = getFilteredWALog();
  const totalCount = (db.waLog || []).length;

  const catCounts = {};
  (db.waLog || []).forEach(m => { catCounts[m.category] = (catCounts[m.category] || 0) + 1; });

  const catsHtml = WA_CATEGORIES.map(c => {
    const n = c.key === 'all' ? totalCount : (catCounts[c.key] || 0);
    return `<button onclick="setWACategory('${c.key}')" style="border:1.5px solid ${f.category===c.key?'transparent':'var(--border)'};background:${f.category===c.key?'linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary-2),var(--primary))':'#fff'};color:${f.category===c.key?'#fff':'var(--muted)'};font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s;white-space:nowrap">
      ${c.icon} ${c.label} ${n > 0 ? `<span style="opacity:.7">(${n})</span>` : ''}
    </button>`;
  }).join('');

  const itemsHtml = list.length ? list.map(m => {
    const d = new Date(m.sentAt);
    const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric'});
    const timeStr = d.toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    const catLabel = waCatLabel(m.category);
    const catIcon = waCatIcon(m.category);
    const recType = m.recipientType === 'parent' ? 'ولي أمر' : (m.recipientType === 'group' ? 'مجموعة' : 'طالب');
    return `
      <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;position:relative;overflow:hidden" onclick="viewWALogDetail('${m.id}')">
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:4px;height:100%;background:${waCatColor(m.category)}"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:8px">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,${waCatColor(m.category)},${waCatColor(m.category)}cc);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;flex-shrink:0">${catIcon}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px">${esc(m.recipientName || 'غير محدد')} <span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">(${recType})</span></div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;direction:ltr;text-align:right">${esc(m.phone || '—')}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:center">
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">📅 ${dateStr}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10.5px;opacity:.7;font-weight:700">🕐 ${timeStr}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;background:${waCatColor(m.category)}22;color:${waCatColor(m.category)};display:inline-block;margin-bottom:6px">${catIcon} ${catLabel}</div>
        ${m.title ? `<div style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;color:var(--primary-d);margin-bottom:5px">📌 ${esc(m.title)}</div>` : ''}
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#334155;line-height:1.75;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;max-height:80px;overflow:hidden">${esc((m.text||'').slice(0, 300))}${(m.text||'').length > 300 ? '...' : ''}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:8px" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
          <button class="btn btn-whatsapp btn-sm" onclick="resendWALog('${m.id}')">💬 إعادة</button>
          <button class="btn btn-info-soft btn-sm" onclick="copyWALogText('${m.id}')">📋 نسخ</button>
          ${m.studentId ? `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${m.studentId}')">👤 ملف الطالب</button>` : ''}
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="deleteWALogEntry('${m.id}')">🗑</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('') : `
    <div style="text-align:center;padding:50px 20px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">
      <div style="font-size:52px;margin-bottom:12px;opacity:.5">📭</div>
      <div style="font-size:15px;margin-bottom:6px">لا توجد رسائل مطابقة</div>
    </div>`;

  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#128c7e">
      <span style="font-size:22px">📨</span> أرشيف رسائل الواتساب
      <span style="margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:12px;background:#dcfce7;color:#128c7e;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:99px;font-weight:800">
        ${list.length} من ${totalCount}
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-sub">سجل كامل بجميع الرسائل المرسلة</div>

    <div class="search-wrap" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <span class="s-ico">🔍</span>
      <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم، الهاتف، أو نص الرسالة..." value="${esc(f.search)}" oninput="ui.waArchive.search=this.value;refreshWAArchiveList()">
    </div>

    <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:8px;background:#f1f5f9;border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:12px">${catsHtml}</div>

    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div class="field" style="flex:1;min-width:150px">
        <label style="font-size:11.5px">من تاريخ</label>
        <input type="date" value="${esc(f.dateFrom)}" onchange="ui.waArchive.dateFrom=this.value;refreshWAArchiveList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="flex:1;min-width:150px">
        <label style="font-size:11.5px">إلى تاريخ</label>
        <input type="date" value="${esc(f.dateTo)}" onchange="ui.waArchive.dateTo=this.value;refreshWAArchiveList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="flex:1;min-width:150px">
        <label style="font-size:11.5px">الطالب</label>
        <select onchange="ui.waArchive.studentId=this.value;refreshWAArchiveList()" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <option value="">كل الطلاب</option>
          ${db.students.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" ${f.studentId===s.id?'selected':''}>${esc(s.name)}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:6px">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="resetWAArchiveFilters()">🔄</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="clearWAArchiveConfirm()">🗑</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div style="max-height:480px;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px" id="waArchiveList">${itemsHtml}</div>

    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}

function refreshWAArchiveList(){
  const el = document.getElementById('waArchiveList');
  if(!el){ renderWAArchiveModal(); return; }
  const list = getFilteredWALog();
  el.innerHTML = list.length ? list.map(m => {
    const d = new Date(m.sentAt);
    const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric'});
    const timeStr = d.toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    const catLabel = waCatLabel(m.category);
    const catIcon = waCatIcon(m.category);
    const recType = m.recipientType === 'parent' ? 'ولي أمر' : 'طالب';
    return `
      <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;position:relative" onclick="viewWALogDetail('${m.id}')">
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:4px;height:100%;background:${waCatColor(m.category)}"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:8px">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,${waCatColor(m.category)},${waCatColor(m.category)}cc);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;flex-shrink:0">${catIcon}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px">${esc(m.recipientName || 'غير محدد')}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;direction:ltr;text-align:right">${esc(m.phone || '')}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700">${dateStr} • ${timeStr}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;background:${waCatColor(m.category)}22;color:${waCatColor(m.category)};display:inline-block;margin-bottom:6px">${catIcon} ${catLabel}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;color:#334155;line-height:1.75;max-height:80px;overflow:hidden">${esc((m.text||'').slice(0, 300))}</div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('') : `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--muted)">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
}

function setWACategory(cat){ ui.waArchive.category = cat; renderWAArchiveModal(); }
function resetWAArchiveFilters(){ ui.waArchive = { search: '', category: 'all', dateFrom: '', dateTo: '', studentId: '' }; renderWAArchiveModal(); }

function viewWALogDetail(id){
  const m = (db.waLog || []).find(x => x.id === id);
  if(!m) return;
  const d = new Date(m.sentAt);
  const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG',{weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});
  const timeStr = d.toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:${waCatColor(m.category)}">${waCatIcon(m.category)} تفاصيل الرسالة</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${waCatLabel(m.category)} — ${dateStr} في ${timeStr}</div>
    <div class="grid g2" style="margin-bottom:14px">
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">المستقبل</div><div class="gic-value">${esc(m.recipientName || '—')}</div></div>
      <div class="grade-info-cell"><div class="gic-label">الرقم</div><div class="gic-value" style="direction:ltr;font-size:13px">${esc(m.phone || '—')}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="field"><label>نص الرسالة</label>
      <div style="background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:14px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.85;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;max-height:340px;overflow-y:auto;font-weight:600">${esc(m.text || '')}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="renderWAArchiveModal()">← رجوع</button>
      <button class="btn btn-info-soft" onclick="copyWALogText('${m.id}')">📋 نسخ</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp" onclick="resendWALog('${m.id}')">💬 إعادة الإرسال</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function copyWALogText(id){
  const m = (db.waLog || []).find(x => x.id === id);
  if(!m) return;
  copyText(m.text || '');
}
function resendWALog(id){
  const m = (db.waLog || []).find(x => x.id === id);
  if(!m) return;
  sendWhatsApp(m.phone, m.text || '');
  toast('تم فتح واتساب ✓','gold');
}
function deleteWALogEntry(id){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه الرسالة من الأرشيف؟')) return;
  db.waLog = (db.waLog || []).filter(x => x.id !== id);
  saveDB();
  toast('تم الحذف','warn');
  renderWAArchiveModal();
}
function clearWAArchiveConfirm(){
  if(!confirm('مسح جميع الرسائل؟ لا يمكن التراجع.')) return;
  db.waLog = [];
  saveDB();
  toast('تم مسح الأرشيف','warn');
  renderWAArchiveModal();
}

/* ============ إرسال واتساب جماعي ============ */
function openWhatsAppBroadcastModal(){
  ui.waBroadcast = { mode: 'allStudents', search: '', selected: [] };
  renderWaBroadcastModal();
}

function renderWaBroadcastModal(){
  const b = ui.waBroadcast;
  const totalStudents = db.students.length;
  const totalParents = db.students.filter(s => s.parentPhone).length;

  let recipients = [];
  let modeLabel = '';
  if(b.mode === 'allStudents'){ recipients = db.students.filter(s => s.phone); modeLabel = 'كل الطلاب'; }
  else if(b.mode === 'allParents'){ recipients = db.students.filter(s => s.parentPhone); modeLabel = 'كل أولياء الأمور'; }
  else if(b.mode === 'studentsSelected'){ recipients = b.selected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.phone); modeLabel = 'طلاب محددون'; }
  else if(b.mode === 'parentsSelected'){ recipients = b.selected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.parentPhone); modeLabel = 'أولياء محددون'; }

  const searchQ = (b.search||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const searchList = (b.mode === 'studentsSelected' || b.mode === 'parentsSelected')
    ? db.students.filter(s => {
        if(b.mode === 'parentsSelected' && !s.parentPhone) return false;
        if(!searchQ) return true;
        return s.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQ) || s.phone.includes(searchQ);
      })
    : [];

  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:#128c7e">
      <span style="font-size:22px">💬</span> إرسال واتساب جماعي
    </div>
    <div class="modal-sub">اختر مستقبلي الرسالة ثم اكتب النص</div>

    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#f0fdf4);border:1.5px solid #86efac;border-radius:13px;padding:12px 14px;font-size:12.5px;color:#128c7e;font-weight:800;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;margin-bottom:12px">
      <span style="width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:9px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:15px;flex-shrink:0">📤</span>
      <span>عدد المستقبلين: <b style="font-size:16px">${recipients.length}</b> — ${modeLabel}</span>
    </div>

    <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;padding:7px;background:#f1f5f9;border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:14px;overflow-x:auto">
      <button onclick="setWaBcMode('allStudents')" style="border:none;background:${b.mode==='allStudents'?'#fff':'transparent'};color:${b.mode==='allStudents'?'#128c7e':'var(--muted)'};font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;${b.mode==='allStudents'?'box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.06)':''}">📱 كل الطلاب (${totalStudents})</button>
      <button onclick="setWaBcMode('allParents')" style="border:none;background:${b.mode==='allParents'?'#fff':'transparent'};color:${b.mode==='allParents'?'#128c7e':'var(--muted)'};font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;${b.mode==='allParents'?'box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.06)':''}">👨‍👩‍👦 كل الأولياء (${totalParents})</button>
      <button onclick="setWaBcMode('studentsSelected')" style="border:none;background:${b.mode==='studentsSelected'?'#fff':'transparent'};color:${b.mode==='studentsSelected'?'#128c7e':'var(--muted)'};font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;${b.mode==='studentsSelected'?'box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.06)':''}">🔍 طلاب محددون</button>
      <button onclick="setWaBcMode('parentsSelected')" style="border:none;background:${b.mode==='parentsSelected'?'#fff':'transparent'};color:${b.mode==='parentsSelected'?'#128c7e':'var(--muted)'};font-family:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;${b.mode==='parentsSelected'?'box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.06)':''}">🔍 أولياء محددون</button>
    </div>

    ${(b.mode === 'studentsSelected' || b.mode === 'parentsSelected') ? `
      <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <label>🔎 ابحث واختر</label>
        <input placeholder="ابحث..." value="${esc(b.search)}" oninput="ui.waBroadcast.search=this.value;renderWaRecipientList()" id="waBcSearch">
      </div>
      <div style="max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:10px;background:#fff" id="waRecipientList">${waRecipientListHTML(searchList, b)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin:10px 0">تم تحديد: <b style="color:#128c7e">${b.selected.length}</b></div>
    ` : ''}

    <div class="field" style="margin-top:14px">
      <label>نص الرسالة <span class="req">*</span></label>
      <textarea id="waBcText" style="min-height:160px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.7">السلام عليكم ورحمة الله وبركاته،

من منصة الأستاذ ${esc(db.settings.teacherName || 'صلاح عطية')} للغة العربية.

📌 نود إعلامكم بالمستجدات والمواعيد المهمة.

بالتوفيق 🌟</textarea>
    </div>

    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-whatsapp" onclick="sendWhatsAppBroadcast()">📤 إرسال (${recipients.length})</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function waRecipientListHTML(list, b){
  if(!list.length) return `<div style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
  return list.slice(0, 300).map(s => {
    const sel = b.selected.includes(s.id);
    const phone = b.mode === 'parentsSelected' ? s.parentPhone : s.phone;
    return `
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:4px;background:${sel?'#dcfce7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${sel?'#86efac':'transparent'}">
        <input type="checkbox" ${sel?'checked':''} onchange="toggleWaRecipient('${s.id}')" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:#25d366">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-top:2px;direction:ltr;text-align:right">${esc(phone||'—')} • ${esc(s.stage||'—')}</div>
        </div>
      </label>`;
  }).join('');
}

function renderWaRecipientList(){
  const el = document.getElementById('waRecipientList');
  const b = ui.waBroadcast;
  if(!el) return;
  const q = (b.search||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = db.students.filter(s => {
    if(b.mode === 'parentsSelected' && !s.parentPhone) return false;
    if(!q) return true;
    return s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q);
  });
  el.innerHTML = waRecipientListHTML(list, b);
}

function toggleWaRecipient(id){
  const b = ui.waBroadcast;
  const i = b.selected.indexOf(id);
  if(i >= 0) b.selected.splice(i, 1); else b.selected.push(id);
  renderWaBroadcastModal();
}

function setWaBcMode(mode){
  ui.waBroadcast.mode = mode;
  ui.waBroadcast.selected = [];
  ui.waBroadcast.search = '';
  renderWaBroadcastModal();
}

function sendWhatsAppBroadcast(){
  const b = ui.waBroadcast;
  const text = val('waBcText');
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب نص الرسالة','warn'); return; }
  let recipients = [];
  if(b.mode === 'allStudents') recipients = db.students.filter(s => s.phone);
  else if(b.mode === 'allParents') recipients = db.students.filter(s => s.parentPhone);
  else if(b.mode === 'studentsSelected') recipients = b.selected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.phone);
  else if(b.mode === 'parentsSelected') recipients = b.selected.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean).filter(s => s.parentPhone);
  if(!recipients.length){ toast('لا يوجد مستقبلون','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`سيتم فتح واتساب لـ ${recipients.length} مستقبل. متابعة؟`)) return;

  closeModal();
  toast(`جارٍ فتح واتساب لـ ${recipients.length} مستقبل...`,'gold');
  playSound('message');
  recipients.forEach((s, i) => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const isParent = b.mode === 'parentsSelected' || b.mode === 'allParents';
      const phone = isParent ? s.parentPhone : s.phone;
      sendWhatsApp(phone, text, {
        recipientName: s.name + (isParent ? ' — ولي الأمر' : ''),
        recipientType: isParent ? 'parent' : 'student',
        category: 'broadcast',
        title: 'رسالة جماعية',
        studentId: s.id
      });
    }, i * 350);
  });
}

/* ============ قوائم إضافية ============ */
function openAllHomeworkModal(){ openAllItemsModal('homework'); }
function openAllExamsModal(){ openAllItemsModal('exam'); }
function openAllNotifsModal(){
  const rows = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => s.notifications.forEach(n => rows.push({ student: s, item: n })));
  rows.sort((a, b) => (b.item.createdAt || 0) - (a.item.createdAt || 0));
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title">🔔 جميع الإشعارات</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${rows.length} إشعار</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px">
      ${rows.length ? rows.map(r => `
        <div style="padding:11px 14px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${r.student.id}')">
          <div style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.8">${esc(r.item.text)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:6px">👤 ${esc(r.student.name)} • 📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(r.item.date)}</div>
        </div>
      `).join('') : '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8">لا توجد إشعارات</div>'}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}
function openAllCertificatesModal(){
  const rows = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => s.certificates.forEach(c => rows.push({ student: s, item: c })));
  rows.sort((a, b) => (b.item.issuedAt || 0) - (a.item.issuedAt || 0));
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title">🎓 جميع الشهادات</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${rows.length} شهادة</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px">
      ${rows.length ? rows.map(r => `
        <div style="padding:11px 14px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${r.student.id}')">
          <div style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:800">${esc(r.item.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:6px">👤 ${esc(r.student.name)} • 🏅 ${esc(r.item.grade)} • 📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(new Date(r.item.issuedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10))}</div>
        </div>
      `).join('') : '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8">لا توجد شهادات</div>'}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}
function openAllItemsModal(kind){
  const rows = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => {
    const arr = kind === 'homework' ? s.homework : s.exams;
    arr.forEach(item => rows.push({ student: s, item, type: kind }));
  });
  rows.sort((a,b) => (b.item.createdAt||0) - (a.item.createdAt||0));
  const title = kind === 'homework' ? '📝 جميع الواجبات' : '📊 جميع الاختبارات';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title">${title}</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">${rows.length} عنصر</div>
    <div style="max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:4px">
      ${rows.length ? rows.map(r => `
        <div style="padding:11px 14px;background:#f8fafc;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal();openStudentDetail('${r.student.id}');setTimeout(()=>{ui.detailTab='${kind === 'homework' ? 'homework' : 'exams'}';render()},200)">
          <div style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:800">${esc(r.item.title)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:6px">👤 ${esc(r.student.name)} ${r.item.submitted ? '• ✓ مُسلَّم' : '• ⏳ لم يُسلَّم'}</div>
        </div>
      `).join('') : '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#94a3b8">لا توجد عناصر</div>'}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button></div>
  `);
}

/* ============ مركز الإشعارات ============ */
function viewNotificationsCenter(){
  const allNotifs = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => s.notifications.forEach(n => allNotifs.push({ student: s, item: n })));
  allNotifs.sort((a, b) => (b.item.createdAt || 0) - (a.item.createdAt || 0));
  const total = allNotifs.length;
  const unread = allNotifs.filter(x => !x.item.read).length;

  return `
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="brand">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" onclick="go('teacher')">→</button>
      <span>${brandBlockHTML(44)}</span>
      <div>
        <div style="line-height:1.2">مركز الإشعارات</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">إدارة شاملة لجميع إشعارات الطلاب</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span class="sync-status" onclick="retryFirebase()"><span class="dot"></span><span>...</span></span>
      ${soundToggleHTML()}
    </div>
  </div>
  ${todayHeaderHTML()}

  <div class="grid g3 anim-in">
    <div class="stat info"><span class="ico-stat">🔔</span><div class="num">${total}</div><div class="lbl">إجمالي الإشعارات</div></div>
    <div class="stat gold"><span class="ico-stat">⚠️</span><div class="num">${unread}</div><div class="lbl">غير مقروءة</div></div>
    <div class="stat ok"><span class="ico-stat">✅</span><div class="num">${total - unread}</div><div class="lbl">مقروءة</div></div>
  </div>

  <div class="card anim-in" style="margin-top:16px">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📤</span> إرسال إشعار جديد</div>
    <div class="field">
      <label>نص الإشعار <span class="req">*</span></label>
      <textarea id="ncNotifText" placeholder="اكتب نص الإشعار..." style="min-height:100px"></textarea>
    </div>
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px;gap:8px">
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="sendNotifToAll()">📢 إرسال لجميع الطلاب</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card anim-in">
    <div class="sec-title">
      <span class="ico">🗑️</span> حذف الإشعارات
      <span class="sec-sub">${total} إشعار</span>
    </div>
    <div class="row" style="gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="deleteAllNotifsForAll()">🗑 حذف إشعارات جميع الطلاب</button>
      <button class="btn btn-warn-soft" onclick="deleteReadNotifsForAll()">🧹 حذف المقروءة فقط</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card anim-in-d1">
    <div class="sec-title"><span class="ico">📨</span> جميع الإشعارات <span class="sec-sub">${total}</span></div>
    <div class="search-wrap">
      <span class="s-ico">🔍</span>
      <input placeholder="ابحث بالاسم أو النص..." id="ncSearch" oninput="filterNotifsList(this.value)">
    </div>
    <div id="notifsList" style="max-height:520px;overflow-y:auto">${renderAllNotifsList(allNotifs)}</div>
  </div>`;
}

function renderAllNotifsList(list){
  if(!list.length) return `<div class="empty"><span class="ico">🔕</span>لا توجد إشعارات</div>`;
  return list.slice(0, 100).map(x => `
    <div class="item" style="${x.item.read ? '' : 'background:linear-gradient(90deg,#fffbeb,#fff);border-color:#fcd34d'}">
      ${avatar(x.student.name, 40)}
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text);font-weight:600">${esc(x.item.text)}</div>
        <div class="item-meta" style="margin-top:6px">
          <span>👤 ${esc(x.student.name)}</span>
          <span>📅 ${fmtDateWithDay(x.item.date)}</span>
          ${x.item.read ? '<span class="badge b-ok">✓ مقروء</span>' : '<span class="badge b-warn">جديد</span>'}
        </div>
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-icon-sm" onclick="deleteNotifById('${x.student.id}','${x.item.id}')">🗑</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function filterNotifsList(q){
  q = (q||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const allNotifs = [];
  db.students.forEach(s => s.notifications.forEach(n => allNotifs.push({ student: s, item: n })));
  allNotifs.sort((a, b) => (b.item.createdAt || 0) - (a.item.createdAt || 0));
  const filtered = q ? allNotifs.filter(x => x.student.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (x.item.text||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) : allNotifs;
  const el = document.getElementById('notifsList');
  if(el) el.innerHTML = renderAllNotifsList(filtered);
}

function sendNotifToAll(){
  const text = val('ncNotifText');
  if(!text){ toast('اكتب نص الإشعار','warn'); return; }
  const active = db.students.filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  if(!active.length){ toast('لا يوجد طلاب مفعّلون','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`إرسال لـ ${active.length} طالب؟`)) return;
  active.forEach(s => {
    s.notifications.push({ id: uid(), text, date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now() });
    logStudentActivity(s.id, 'other', 'إشعار من المعلم', text.slice(0, 60));
  });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast(`تم الإرسال لـ ${active.length} طالب ✓`,'gold');
  playSound('notify');
}

function deleteAllNotifsForAll(){
  if(!confirm('حذف جميع إشعارات جميع الطلاب؟ لا يمكن التراجع.')) return;
  db.students.forEach(s => { s.notifications = []; });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم حذف جميع الإشعارات','warn');
}
function deleteReadNotifsForAll(){
  if(!confirm('حذف الإشعارات المقروءة؟')) return;
  db.students.forEach(s => { s.notifications = s.notifications.filter(n => !n.read); });
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}
function deleteNotifById(studentId, notifId){
  const s = getStudent(studentId); if(!s) return;
  s.notifications = s.notifications.filter(n => n.id !== notifId);
  saveDB(); render();
  toast('تم الحذف','warn');
}

/* ============ أسعار الاشتراك ============ */
function openPricesModal(){
  const prices = db.settings.prices;
  const currentCurrency = db.settings.currency || 'EGP';
  openModalWide(`
    <div class="modal-title" style="color:var(--primary-d)">💵 إدارة الأسعار والخطط</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">حدّد أسعار الخطط وأسعار الحصص</div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <label>🌍 العملة</label>
      <select id="currencySelect">
        <option value="EGP" ${currentCurrency==='EGP'?'selected':''}>🇪🇬 جنيه مصري (ج.م)</option>
        <option value="SAR" ${currentCurrency==='SAR'?'selected':''}>🇸🇦 ريال سعودي (ر.س)</option>
      </select>
    </div>
    <div class="sec-title" style="font-size:14px"><span class="ico" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:14px">📋</span> خطط الاشتراك</div>
    <div id="plansList">
      ${prices.plans.map((p, i) => `
        <div style="border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:10px;background:#f8fafc">
          <div class="grid g3">
            <div class="field"><label>اسم الخطة</label><input id="planName_${i}" value="${esc(p.name)}"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>السعر</label><input type="number" id="planPrice_${i}" value="${esc(p.price)}" inputmode="numeric"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>المدة</label><input id="planDur_${i}" value="${esc(p.duration)}"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="field" style="margin-top:10px"><label>المميزات (كل ميزة في سطر)</label>
            <textarea id="planFeat_${i}" style="min-height:80px">${esc((p.features||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="deletePlan(${i})">🗑 حذف</button>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="addPlan()">➕ إضافة خطة</button>
    <div class="sec-title" style="font-size:14px;margin-top:20px"><span class="ico" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:14px">🎓</span> أسعار الحصص</div>
    <div class="grid g3">
      ${SESSION_TYPES.map(t => `
        <div class="field"><label>${t.label}</label>
          <input type="number" id="sessPrice_${t.key}" value="${esc(prices.sessionTypes[t.key] || 0)}" inputmode="numeric">
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions" style="margin-top:20px">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="savePrices()">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}
function addPlan(){
  db.settings.prices.plans.push({ id: uid(), name: 'خطة جديدة', price: 500, duration: 'شهر', features: ['ميزة جديدة'] });
  closeModal();
  setTimeout(openPricesModal, 100);
}
function deletePlan(i){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذه الخطة؟')) return;
  db.settings.prices.plans.splice(i, 1);
  closeModal();
  setTimeout(openPricesModal, 100);
}
function savePrices(){
  const plans = [];
  const count = db.settings.prices.plans.length;
  for(let i = 0; i < count; i++){
    const name = val('planName_' + i);
    const price = parseFloat(val('planPrice_' + i)) || 0;
    const duration = val('planDur_' + i);
    const features = (document.getElementById('planFeat_' + i).value || '').split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s);
    if(!name) continue;
    plans.push({ id: db.settings.prices.plans[i].id, name, price, duration, features, featured: db.settings.prices.plans[i].featured || false });
  }
  if(!plans.length){ toast('خطة واحدة على الأقل','warn'); return; }
  db.settings.prices.plans = plans;
  SESSION_TYPES.forEach(t => {
    const v = parseFloat(val('sessPrice_' + t.key)) || 0;
    db.settings.prices.sessionTypes[t.key] = v;
  });
  db.settings.currency = document.getElementById('currencySelect')?.value || 'EGP';
  saveDB();
  closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم الحفظ ✓','gold');
}

/* ============ المكتبة الجماعية ============ */
function openLibraryModal(){
  if(!ui.libraryTab) ui.libraryTab = 'homework';
  if(!ui.libraryTargetMode) ui.libraryTargetMode = 'stage';
  if(!Array.isArray(ui.librarySelectedStudents)) ui.librarySelectedStudents = [];
  if(!ui.libraryDraft) ui.libraryDraft = {};
  renderLibraryModal();
}

function renderLibraryModal(){
  const stages = getAllStages();
  const isHW = ui.libraryTab === 'homework';
  const isExam = ui.libraryTab === 'exams';
  const isMemo = ui.libraryTab === 'memos';
  const isLive = ui.libraryTab === 'live';
  const libHW = db.library.homework.length;
  const libEX = db.library.exams.length;
  const savedDraft = (ui.libraryDraft && ui.libraryDraft.form) || {};

    <div class="modal-sub">إرسال جماعي لمرحلة / مسار / طلاب محددين</div>

      <button type="button" class="${isExam?'active':''}" onclick="setLibraryTab('exams')">📊 اختبار</button>

    </div>

    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px">
      <label>عنوان <span class="req">*</span></label>
