    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px">
      <label>وصف / تعليمات</label>
      <textarea id="libDesc" placeholder="التعليمات...">${esc(savedDraft.libDesc||'')}</textarea>
    </div>

    ${isLive ? `
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>مكان / رابط المحاضرة <span class="req">*</span></label>
        <input id="libLocation" value="${esc(savedDraft.libLocation||'')}" placeholder="قاعة 3 — أو رابط">
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>رابط انضمام اختياري</label>
        <input id="libUrl" value="${esc(savedDraft.libUrl||'')}" placeholder="https://meet.google.com/...">
      </div>
      <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
        <div class="field"><label>📅 تاريخ الفتح</label><input type="date" id="libStartDate" value="${esc(savedDraft.libStartDate||todayISO())}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>🕐 وقت الفتح</label><input type="time" id="libStartTime" value="${esc(savedDraft.libStartTime||'')}"></div>
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
        <div class="field"><label>النوع</label>
          <select id="libType" onchange="ui.libraryDraft.form=ui.libraryDraft.form||{};ui.libraryDraft.form.libType=this.value;renderLibraryModal()">
            <option value="questions" ${(savedDraft.libType||'questions')==='questions'?'selected':''}>📝 أسئلة تفاعلية</option>
            <option value="link" ${savedDraft.libType==='link'?'selected':''}>🔗 رابط خارجي</option>
            <option value="simple" ${savedDraft.libType==='simple'?'selected':''}>📋 نصي بسيط</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label>الدرجة الكاملة</label><input type="number" id="libMax" value="${esc(savedDraft.libMax||'')}" placeholder="10" inputmode="numeric"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>تاريخ التسليم</label><input type="date" id="libDue" value="${esc(savedDraft.libDue||'')}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>📅 تاريخ الفتح</label><input type="date" id="libStartDate" value="${esc(savedDraft.libStartDate||'')}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>🕐 وقت الفتح</label><input type="time" id="libStartTime" value="${esc(savedDraft.libStartTime||'')}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>📅 تاريخ الإغلاق</label><input type="date" id="libCloseDate" value="${esc(savedDraft.libCloseDate||'')}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>🕐 وقت الإغلاق</label><input type="time" id="libCloseTime" value="${esc(savedDraft.libCloseTime||'')}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>⏱️ المدة (دقيقة)</label><input type="number" id="libDuration" value="${esc(savedDraft.libDuration||0)}" min="0" max="600" inputmode="numeric"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>رابط (إن وُجد)</label><input id="libUrl" value="${esc(savedDraft.libUrl||'')}" placeholder="https://forms.google.com/..."></div>
    `}

    <div class="sec-title" style="font-size:14px;margin-top:20px"><span class="ico" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:14px">🎯</span> الفئة المستهدفة</div>
    <div class="tabs" style="margin-top:0;background:#fff;border-color:#ddd6fe">
      <button type="button" class="${ui.libraryTargetMode==='stage'?'active':''}" onclick="setLibraryTargetMode('stage')">🎓 حسب المرحلة</button>
      <button type="button" class="${ui.libraryTargetMode==='students'?'active':''}" onclick="setLibraryTargetMode('students')">👥 طلاب محددين</button>
    </div>

    ${ui.libraryTargetMode === 'stage' ? `
      <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">
        <div class="field"><label>المرحلة</label>
          <select id="libStage">
            <option value="">كل المراحل</option>
            ${stages.map(s => `<option>${esc(s)}</option>`).join('')}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label>المسار</label>
          <select id="libTrack">
            <option value="">كل المسارات</option>
            ${TRACKS.map(t => `<option>${esc(t)}</option>`).join('')}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px">
        <label>ابحث واختر الطلاب</label>
        <input id="libStudentSearch" placeholder="ابحث..." oninput="ui.librarySearch=this.value;document.getElementById('libStudentList').innerHTML=libraryStudentListHTML()" value="${esc(ui.librarySearch||'')}">
      </div>
      <div id="libStudentList" style="max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:10px;margin-top:8px;background:#fff">${libraryStudentListHTML()}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin-top:8px">تم تحديد: <b style="color:var(--primary-d)">${ui.librarySelectedStudents.length}</b> طالب</div>
    `}

    <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dcfce7,#fff);border:2px dashed #25d366;border-radius:14px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#128c7e;margin-bottom:10px">
        <span style="width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:16px">💬</span>
        إرسال واتساب تلقائي
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <label style="flex:1;min-width:140px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:11px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:#128c7e">
          <input type="checkbox" id="libWaStudent" ${ui.sendWA.toStudent?'checked':''} onchange="ui.sendWA.toStudent=this.checked" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:#25d366"> 📱 للطلاب
        </label>
        <label style="flex:1;min-width:140px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:11px 14px;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:#128c7e">
          <input type="checkbox" id="libWaParent" ${ui.sendWA.toParent?'checked':''} onchange="ui.sendWA.toParent=this.checked" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:#25d366"> 👨‍👩‍👦 للأولياء
        </label>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="modal-actions" style="margin-top:20px">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-purple" onclick="sendLibraryItem()">📤 إرسال</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function setLibraryTab(tab){ ui.libraryTab = tab; renderLibraryModal(); }
function setLibraryTargetMode(mode){ ui.libraryTargetMode = mode; renderLibraryModal(); }

function libraryStudentListHTML(){
  const q = (ui.librarySearch||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = db.students.filter(s => !q || s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.phone.includes(q));
  if(!list.length) return `<div style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">لا توجد نتائج</div>`;
  return list.slice(0, 200).map(s => {
    const checked = ui.librarySelectedStudents.includes(s.id);
    return `
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;background:${checked?'#f0fdfa':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${checked?'#99f6e4':'transparent'};margin-bottom:4px">
        <input type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="toggleLibraryStudent('${s.id}')" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--primary)">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">${esc(s.stage||'—')} • ${esc(s.track||'عام')} • ${esc(s.phone)}</div>
        </div>
      </label>`;
  }).join('');
}

function toggleLibraryStudent(id){
  const i = ui.librarySelectedStudents.indexOf(id);
  if(i >= 0) ui.librarySelectedStudents.splice(i, 1);
  else ui.librarySelectedStudents.push(id);
  const el = document.getElementById('libStudentList');
  if(el) el.innerHTML = libraryStudentListHTML();
}

function sendLibraryItem(){
  const title = val('libTitle');
  if(!title){ toast('العنوان مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  let targets = [];
  if(ui.libraryTargetMode === 'stage'){
    const stage = val('libStage');
    const track = val('libTrack');
    targets = db.students.filter(s => {
      if(stage && s.stage !== stage) return false;
      if(track && (s.track||'عام') !== track) return false;
      return true;
    });
  } else {
    targets = ui.librarySelectedStudents.map(id => getStudent(id)).filter(Boolean);
  }
  if(!targets.length){ toast('لا يوجد طلاب مستهدفون','warn'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`سيتم الإرسال إلى ${targets.length} طالب. متابعة؟`)) return;

  const sendStudent = ui.sendWA.toStudent;
  const sendParent = ui.sendWA.toParent;

  if(ui.libraryTab === 'live'){
    const url = val('libUrl');
    const location = val('libLocation');
    const startDate = val('libStartDate');
    const startTime = val('libStartTime');
    const base = {
      id: uid(), title, url: url || '', location: location || '', desc: val('libDesc'),
      startDate, startTime,
      startAt: startDate ? combineToTimestamp(startDate, startTime || '00:00') : null,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      targetMode: ui.libraryTargetMode,
      targetStage: ui.libraryTargetMode === 'stage' ? val('libStage') : '',
      targetTrack: ui.libraryTargetMode === 'stage' ? val('libTrack') : '',
      targetStudents: ui.libraryTargetMode === 'students' ? [...ui.librarySelectedStudents] : []
    };
    db.liveSessions.push(base);
    targets.forEach(s => {
      s.notifications.push({ id: uid(), text: `🎥 محاضرة جديدة: "${title}"`, date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now() });
    });
    if(sendStudent || sendParent){
      targets.forEach((s, i) => setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, 'live', base), i * 300));
    }
    saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
    toast(`تم الإرسال إلى ${targets.length} طالب ✓`,'gold'); playSound('success');
    return;
  }

  const type = val('libType');
  const base = {
    id: uid(), title, type,
    desc: val('libDesc'),
    due: val('libDue'),
    maxScore: parseFloat(val('libMax')) || 0,
    total: parseFloat(val('libMax')) || 0,
    startDate: val('libStartDate'),
    startTime: val('libStartTime'),
    startAt: (function(){ const d = val('libStartDate'); return d ? combineToTimestamp(d, val('libStartTime') || '00:00') : null; })(),
    closeDate: val('libCloseDate'),
    closeTime: val('libCloseTime'),
    closeAt: (function(){ const d = val('libCloseDate'); return d ? combineToTimestamp(d, val('libCloseTime') || '23:59') : null; })(),
    durationMinutes: Math.max(0, parseInt(val('libDuration')) || 0),
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    submitted: false
  };
  if(type === 'link'){
    base.url = val('libUrl');
    if(!base.url){ toast('الرابط مطلوب','warn'); return; }
  }

  targets.forEach((s, idx) => {
    const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(base));
    copy.id = uid();
    const arr = ui.libraryTab === 'homework' ? s.homework : s.exams;
    arr.push(copy);
    s.notifications.push({
      id: uid(),
      text: `${ui.libraryTab === 'homework' ? '📝' : '📊'} ${ui.libraryTab === 'homework' ? 'واجب' : 'اختبار'} جديد: "${base.title}"`,
      date: todayISO(), read: false, createdAt: Date.now()
    });
    if(sendStudent || sendParent){
      setTimeout(() => sendTaskWhatsApp(s, ui.libraryTab, base), idx * 300);
    }
  });
  db.library[ui.libraryTab].push({ ...base, sentCount: targets.length, sentAt: Date.now() });
  ui.libraryDraft = {};
  ui.librarySelectedStudents = [];
  saveDB();
  closeModal(); render();
  toast(`تم الإرسال إلى ${targets.length} طالب ✓`,'gold');
  playSound('success');
}

/* ============ إعدادات المعلم ============ */

      <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:10px"><label>وصف دخول المعلم</label><input id="tsTeachLoginSubtitle" value="${esc(lt.teacherLoginSubtitle||'')}"></div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:10px"><label>عنوان دخول الطالب</label><input id="tsStuLoginTitle" value="${esc(lt.studentLoginTitle||'')}"></div>

        📊 عرض التحصيل التلقائي في الرئيسية
      </label>
    </div>

    <div style="margin-top:16px;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdfa,#ccfbf1);border:1.5px solid #99f6e4;border-radius:14px">

             <div style="flex:1;min-width:150px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
               <button class="btn btn-info btn-sm" onclick="document.getElementById('tsImg').click()">🔄 تغيير</button>
               <button class="btn btn-danger-soft btn-sm" onclick="removeTeacherImage()">🗑 حذف</button>
             </div>`
          : `<div style="width:90px;height:90px;border-radius:50%;border:2.5px dashed #94a3b8;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:30px;color:#94a3b8;background:#fff">🖼️</div>
             <div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="document.getElementById('tsImg').click()">📷 اختر صورة</button></div>`}
        <input type="file" id="tsImg" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="handleTeacherImage(event)">
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="modal-actions" style="margin-top:20px">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveTeacherSettings()">💾 حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function handleTeacherImage(event){
  const file = event.target.files[0]; if(!file) return;
  if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){ toast('اختر صورة','warn'); return; }
  if(file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024){ toast('حجم كبير (الحد 2MB)','warn'); return; }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    db.settings.teacherImage = e.target.result;
    saveDB();
    toast('تم رفع الصورة ✓','gold');
    closeModal();
    setTimeout(openTeacherSettingsModal, 100);
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function removeTeacherImage(){
  if(!confirm('حذف الصورة؟')) return;
  db.settings.teacherImage = '';
  saveDB();
  toast('تم الحذف','warn');
  closeModal();
  setTimeout(openTeacherSettingsModal, 100);
}

function saveTeacherSettings(){
  const name = val('tsName');
  const phone = cleanPhone(val('tsPhone'));
  if(!name || !phone){ toast('الاسم والهاتف مطلوبان','warn'); return; }
  db.settings.teacherName = name;
  db.settings.teacherPhone = phone;
  db.settings.vodafoneCash = cleanPhone(val('tsVodafone'));
  db.settings.whatsapp = cleanPhone(val('tsWhatsApp'));
  db.settings.contactPhone = cleanPhone(val('tsContactPhone')) || phone;
  db.settings.teacherSenderPhone = cleanPhone(val('tsSenderPhone')) || phone;
  if(!db.settings.loginTexts) db.settings.loginTexts = {};
  db.settings.loginTexts.heroTitle = val('tsHeroTitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.heroTitle;
  db.settings.loginTexts.heroSubtitle = val('tsHeroSubtitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.heroSubtitle;
  db.settings.loginTexts.heroDesc = val('tsHeroDesc') || db.settings.loginTexts.heroDesc;
  db.settings.loginTexts.teacherLoginTitle = val('tsTeachLoginTitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.teacherLoginTitle;
  db.settings.loginTexts.teacherLoginSubtitle = val('tsTeachLoginSubtitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.teacherLoginSubtitle;
  db.settings.loginTexts.studentLoginTitle = val('tsStuLoginTitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.studentLoginTitle;
  db.settings.loginTexts.studentLoginSubtitle = val('tsStuLoginSubtitle') || db.settings.loginTexts.studentLoginSubtitle;
  if(!db.settings.platformIntro) db.settings.platformIntro = {};
  db.settings.platformIntro.text = val('tsIntroText') || db.settings.platformIntro.text;
  db.settings.showAutoCollect = !!document.getElementById('tsShowAutoCollect')?.checked;
  saveDB(); closeModal(); render();
  toast('تم الحفظ ✓','gold');
}

function openChangePasswordModal(){
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-title">🔑 تغيير كلمة المرور</div>
    <div class="modal-sub">قم بتحديث كلمة مرور المعلم</div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px"><label>كلمة المرور الحالية</label>
      <div class="pw-wrap">
        <input type="password" id="pOld" placeholder="••••••••">
        <button type="button" class="pw-toggle" onclick="event.preventDefault();togglePasswordVisibility('pOld', this)">👁️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:12px"><label>كلمة المرور الجديدة</label>
      <div class="pw-wrap">
        <input type="password" id="pNew" placeholder="4 أحرف على الأقل">
        <button type="button" class="pw-toggle" onclick="event.preventDefault();togglePasswordVisibility('pNew', this)">👁️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="field"><label>تأكيد كلمة المرور</label>
      <div class="pw-wrap">
        <input type="password" id="pConfirm">
        <button type="button" class="pw-toggle" onclick="event.preventDefault();togglePasswordVisibility('pConfirm', this)">👁️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeModal()">إلغاء</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="savePasswordChange()">حفظ</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function savePasswordChange(){
  const oldP = val('pOld');
  const newP = val('pNew');
  const conf = val('pConfirm');
  if(oldP !== db.settings.teacherPassword){ toast('كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة','err'); return; }
  if(!newP || newP.length < 4){ toast('كلمة المرور قصيرة','warn'); return; }
  if(newP !== conf){ toast('غير متطابقتين','err'); return; }
  db.settings.teacherPassword = newP;
  saveDB(); closeModal();
  toast('تم التحديث ✓','gold');
  playSound('success');
}

/* ============ اختبار WhatsApp API ============ */
async function testWhatsAppAPI(){
  const myPhone = db.settings.teacherSenderPhone || db.settings.teacherPhone;
  if(!myPhone){ toast('لا يوجد رقم','warn'); return; }
  toast('جارٍ اختبار الإرسال...','warn');
  const testMsg = `✅ اختبار الاتصال\nمن منصة الأستاذ ${db.settings.teacherName}\nتم الربط بنجاح 🎉\n${new Date().toLocaleString('ar-EG')}`;
  const r = await sendWhatsAppViaAPI(myPhone, testMsg);
  if(r.ok){ toast(`✅ تم الإرسال عبر ${r.provider}`,'gold'); playSound('success'); }
  else toast(`❌ فشل: ${r.error}`,'err');
}

/* ============ Render الرئيسي ============ */
function captureFormState(){
  const state = {};
  document.querySelectorAll('input[id], select[id], textarea[id]').forEach(el => {
    if(el.type === 'file') return;
    if(el.id.startsWith('otp_') || el.id.startsWith('pasInput_')) return;
    if(el.type === 'checkbox' || el.type === 'radio'){
      state[el.id] = { type: el.type, checked: el.checked };
    } else {
      state[el.id] = { type: el.type, value: el.value };
    }
  });
  return state;
}

function restoreFormState(state){
  if(!state) return;
  Object.keys(state).forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el) return;
    const saved = state[id];
    if(!saved) return;
    if(saved.type === 'file') return;
    if(saved.type === 'checkbox' || saved.type === 'radio'){
      el.checked = saved.checked;
    } else {
      el.value = saved.value || '';
    }
  });
}

function render(){
  const app = document.getElementById('app');
  const formState = captureFormState();

  let html = '';
  if(ui.screen === 'home')                       html = viewHome();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherLogin')          html = viewTeacherLogin();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacher')               html = viewTeacher();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherMyData')         html = viewTeacherMyData();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherLessons')        html = viewTeacherLessons();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherNotes')          html = viewTeacherNotes();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherLive')           html = viewTeacherLive();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherStats')          html = viewTeacherStats();
  else if(ui.screen === 'teacherCertificates')   html = viewTeacherCertificates();
  else if(ui.screen === 'pendingAccounts')       html = viewPendingAccounts();
  else if(ui.screen === 'notificationsCenter')   html = viewNotificationsCenter();
  else if(ui.screen === 'studentLogin')          html = viewStudentLogin();
  else if(ui.screen === 'studentRegister')       html = viewStudentRegister();
  else if(ui.screen === 'student')               html = viewStudent();
  else html = viewHome();

  app.innerHTML = html;
  updateSyncBadge();
  restoreFormState(formState);

  if(!countdownTicker) startCountdownTicker();
  if(!liveClockTicker) startLiveClock();
  if(!quizTimerTicker) startQuizTimerTicker();

  if(ui._lastScreen && ui._lastScreen !== ui.screen){
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }
  ui._lastScreen = ui.screen;
}

/* ============ التهيئة الأولية ============ */
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if(e.key === 'Escape'){
    if(document.getElementById('iapRoot').innerHTML){ closeLessonPlayer(); return; }
    if(document.getElementById('modalRoot').innerHTML) closeModal();
  }
});

document.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
  const inVideo = e.target.closest('.iap-body, video');
  const inStudent = e.target.closest('.protect-content');
  if(inVideo || inStudent){
    e.preventDefault();
    toast('غير مسموح 🔒','warn');
  }
});

document.addEventListener('copy', (e) => {
  const inStudent = e.target.closest('.protect-content, .iap-body');
  if(inStudent){ e.preventDefault(); toast('غير مسموح 🔒','warn'); }
});
document.addEventListener('cut', (e) => {
  const inStudent = e.target.closest('.protect-content, .iap-body');
  if(inStudent){ e.preventDefault(); toast('غير مسموح 🔒','warn'); }
});
document.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
  const inStudent = e.target.closest('.protect-content, .iap-body');
  if(inStudent) e.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener('selectstart', (e) => {
  const inStudent = e.target.closest('.protect-content');
  if(inStudent) e.preventDefault();
});

document.addEventListener('focusout', () => {
  setTimeout(() => {
    const active = document.activeElement;
    const isTyping = active && (active.tagName === 'INPUT' || active.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || active.tagName === 'SELECT');
    if(!isTyping && pendingRender){
      pendingRender = false;
      render();
    }
  }, 180);
});

/* محاولة استعادة جلسة سابقة */
try{
  if(localStorage.getItem(LS_TEACHER_TOKEN) === 'ok'){
    ui.screen = 'teacher';
  } else {
    const sid = localStorage.getItem(LS_STUDENT_TOKEN);
    if(sid && getStudent(sid)){
      ui.studentToken = sid;
      ui.screen = 'student';
    }
  }
}catch(e){}
